NarraSpace: Multimodal Scene-Grounded Evidence for Picture-Description Dementia Classification
NarraSpace：用于图片描述痴呆症分类的多模态场景基础证据
Picture-description dementia classification uses a shared visual scene, but a transcript-level prediction alone does not identify the scene evidence underlying its inputs. NarraSpace links words and speech times to named picture elements and retrieves utterances for predefined scene events. Narrative-spatial descriptors summarize coverage and temporal organization. A frozen language model contrasts each event's text under examples from Alzheimer's disease and healthy-control training participants, alongside transcript surprisal and coverage measurements. Retained source records link event scores to their source utterances, speech intervals, and lexical matches. On the official 48-participant test set of the Alzheimer's Dementia Recognition through Spontaneous Speech benchmark, a fixed support vector classifier achieves $80.88\%\pm2.56\%$ mean accuracy over fifty seeds; averaged probabilities yield $81.25\%$ accuracy and $91.32\%$ area under the receiver operating characteristic curve. NarraSpace makes the construction of scene-linked classification inputs explicit and inspectable by connecting event-indexed language-model measurements to their source utterances, speech timing, and extraction records. Such source-linked measurements could support clinician review of picture descriptions by presenting quantitative summaries alongside the original speech evidence.
中文翻译
图片描述痴呆症分类依赖于共享视觉场景，但仅基于转录文本的预测无法揭示其输入背后的场景证据。NarraSpace将词语和语音时间与命名的图片元素关联，并检索预定义场景事件的语句。叙事-空间描述符总结了覆盖范围和时间组织结构。一个冻结的语言模型对比每个事件在阿尔茨海默病患者和健康对照组训练参与者中的文本表现，同时结合转录意外性和覆盖度测量。保留的源记录将事件得分与其原始语句、语音时间段和词汇匹配关联起来。在阿尔茨海默病自发言语识别基准的官方48名参与者测试集上，固定支持向量分类器在五十次随机种子下达到$80.88\%\pm2.56\%$的平均准确率；平均概率则达到$81.25\%$准确率和$91.32\%$受试者工作特征曲线下面积。NarraSpace使构建与场景关联的分类输入变得明确且可检查，通过将事件索引的语言模型测量结果与原始语句、语音时间及提取记录相连接。此类源链接测量可为临床医生提供定量摘要，辅助审查图片描述内容。
👤 Qingming Li, Ruiyan Sun, Satoshi Nakamura 📅 2026-10-20 🔗 arXiv

Benchmarking Arabic Dialects for Math Problems
阿拉伯方言数学问题的基准评测
Mathematical reasoning remains underexplored for low-resource languages, including Dialectal Arabic. To bridge this gap, we introduce the DAR-MGSM (Dialectal Arabic Multilingual Grade School Math) Benchmark, the first manually curated benchmark for evaluating mathematical reasoning in Dialectal Arabic. DAR-MGSM consists of math word problems (MWPs) requiring multi-step reasoning and covers five major Arabic dialects alongside Modern Standard Arabic (MSA). The benchmark comprises two parallel versions: translated-only and translated & culturally localized. The latter version adapts entities and references to regional varieties. We evaluate eleven Arabic-centric and multilingual large language models on DAR-MGSM. The results show that the performance generally declines on the localized version, highlighting the challenges introduced by culturally grounded adaptations. However, the magnitude of this effect varies across models, with Arabic-centric models exhibiting different levels of sensitivity to localization. A mechanistic interpretability analysis shows that translated and localized variants of the same MWPs often induce distinct internal reasoning trajectories, even when aggregate accuracy remains similar. Overall, our results show that mathematical reasoning performance depends on both linguistic variety and cultural context.
中文翻译
数学推理在低资源语言中仍研究不足，包括方言阿拉伯语。为填补这一空白，我们提出了DAR-MGSM（方言阿拉伯语多语言小学数学）基准，这是首个手动构建的用于评估方言阿拉伯语数学推理能力的基准。DAR-MGSM包含需要多步推理的数学应用题（MWPs），涵盖五种主要阿拉伯方言以及现代标准阿拉伯语（MSA）。该基准包含两个并行版本：仅翻译版和翻译+文化本地化版。后者将实体和引用适配至地区变体。我们在十一个以阿拉伯语为中心及多语言的大语言模型上对DAR-MGSM进行了评估。结果显示，本地化版本的性能普遍下降，凸显了文化根基适应带来的挑战。然而，这种影响程度因模型而异，阿拉伯语中心模型对本地化的敏感性各不相同。机制可解释性分析表明，同一数学应用题的翻译版与本地化版常引发不同的内部推理轨迹，即使整体准确率相似。总体而言，我们的结果表明数学推理表现既依赖语言变体也受文化背景影响。
👤 Noor Abo Mokh, Teresa Lynn, Nizar Habash, Ahmed Elshabrawy, Sara Alhajeri et al. 📅 2026-10-20 🔗 arXiv

OptionWorld: A Benchmark for Diagnosing Failures of LLMs as Long-Term Personal Assistants
OptionWorld：诊断大语言模型作为长期个人助手失败的基准
Long-term assistants must follow unfinished tasks across days as information and circumstances change. Finding information or starting an action, however, does not ensure that the task gets completed. We introduce OptionWorld, a benchmark that traces information acquisition, judgments, actions, and outcomes in simulated daily-life tasks to identify where current LLMs fall short as long-term personal assistants. In runs with archived decision inputs, the key facts remain in the model's last input before the deadline in 35.5% of failed instances. Failure analysis identifies the three most frequent problems: needed information is not obtained or combined, available opportunities to act go unused, and agents impose restrictions that the task does not require. Three follow-up experiments show that supplying missing facts restores task success in some cases; action reminders can restart work while subsequent steps still miss the deadline; and correcting a supplied note that wrongly forbids contact restores permitted inquiry. These findings locate shortcomings in maintaining task state: connecting facts to unfinished work, following through before deadlines, and separating actual restrictions from the agent's assumptions.
中文翻译
长期助手必须在信息和环境变化的情况下跨天完成未完成的任务。然而，查找信息或启动行动并不保证任务最终完成。我们引入OptionWorld，一个基准测试，用于追踪模拟日常生活任务中的信息获取、判断、行动及结果，以识别当前大语言模型在作为长期个人助手时的不足之处。在使用归档决策输入的实验中，关键事实在35.5%的失败实例中仍保留在模型最后输入中直至截止时间。失败分析揭示了三个最常见的问题：所需信息未被获取或整合，可用行动机会被忽视，代理施加了任务本身不需要的限制。后续三个实验表明，补充缺失事实可在某些情况下恢复任务成功；行动提醒可重启工作，但后续步骤仍可能错过截止时间；修正一个错误禁止联系的提示可恢复允许的咨询。这些发现定位了任务状态维持方面的缺陷：将事实与未完成工作关联、在截止前跟进、区分实际限制与代理假设。
👤 Ruoyu Zhang, Zhaocen Liu, Weiyi Tian, Melissa Feng, Shilong Liu 📅 2026-10-20 🔗 arXiv

Object-Oriented Harness: Evolving Agent Harnesses through Object-Oriented Design
面向对象的Harness：通过面向对象设计演化智能体Harness
Agent harnesses are executable code that specifies the prompts, tools, and control flow around a language model. A single global harness can struggle with heterogeneous tasks that require different execution strategies. In this work, we introduce Object-Oriented Harness (OO-Harness), a harness optimization framework that evolves a hierarchy of executable harness classes using object-oriented design. During training, an evolution agent uses execution records to create a family of specialized harnesses, each implemented as a subclass of the base or an existing specialized harness via inheritance. At test time, a harness router uses routing lessons accumulated during training to select a specific harness for each task. Across three long-horizon benchmarks spanning business workflows, workspace tasks, and tool use, OO-Harness achieves the best performance among the compared methods, with an absolute improvement of up to 15% over the base. Extensive ablation studies examine the contributions of its core components.
中文翻译
智能体Harness是执行代码，用于定义语言模型周围的提示、工具和控制流程。单一全局Harness在面对需要不同执行策略的异构任务时可能表现不佳。本文提出面向对象Harness（OO-Harness），一种基于面向对象设计演化的Harness优化框架。在训练过程中，进化代理利用执行记录创建一系列专用Harness类，每个类通过继承基础Harness或已有专用Harness实现。在测试阶段，Harness路由器利用训练期间积累的路由经验，为每个任务选择特定Harness。在涵盖商业工作流、办公任务和工具使用的三个长期基准上，OO-Harness在对比方法中表现最佳，相对于基础模型有最高达15%的绝对性能提升。大量消融实验分析了其核心组件的贡献。
👤 Gaotang Li, Bhavana Dalvi Mishra, Yifei Ming, Yanfei Chen, Zhongying CuiZhu et al. 📅 2026-10-20 🔗 arXiv

DeclineCraft: A RAG Abstention Benchmark for Jointly Measuring Combined Retrieval and Parametric Knowledge
DeclineCraft：联合测量联合检索与参数化知识的RAG拒答基准
Reliable retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) requires recognizing when neither retrieved documents nor parametric knowledge supports an answer. We introduce DeclineCraft, a dynamic abstention benchmark whose unanswerable questions target Minecraft feature additions after a corpus cut-off, with audits of possible parametric exposure. Later cut-offs support evaluation of future models. Across 27 LLMs, we measure Correct Abstention Rate (CAR; how often models acknowledge insufficient knowledge on unanswerable questions), Answerable Attempt Rate and Answer Accuracy. We find that (1) single-shot retrieval significantly raises CAR in 38 of 40 configurations (model and thinking setting) versus no retrieval. (2) Iterative RAG, in which the model self-assesses its context and can request up to two further retrieval rounds, significantly lowers CAR versus single-shot RAG in 30 of 40 configurations (mean 46.4%), with little change in Answer Accuracy (68.5% to 69.3%). Adding query rewriting and reranking to hybrid (dense & sparse) retrieval lowers CAR for all 12 models tested. (3) Larger models answer more accurately (Spearman $ρ= +0.60$ with total parameters), but neither model size nor release date shows a significant association with CAR. (4) Without retrieval, enabling thinking on the same weights lowers CAR significantly for 8 of 13 models (by 14% points on average), and the responses shift from acknowledged uncertainty to premise denial.
中文翻译
可靠的检索增强生成（RAG）需要识别当检索文档和参数化知识均无法支持答案时的情况。我们引入DeclineCraft，一个动态拒答基准，其不可回答的问题聚焦于语料库截止时间之后的Minecraft功能新增，并包含对可能参数化暴露的审计。后续更晚的截止点可用于未来模型的评估。在27个大语言模型上，我们测量了正确拒答率（CAR；模型在不可回答问题上承认知识不足的频率）、可回答尝试率和答案准确率。研究发现：（1）单次检索相比无检索显著提高了38/40种配置下的CAR；（2）迭代式RAG（模型自我评估上下文并最多请求两次额外检索）在30/40种配置下显著降低了CAR（平均46.4%），而答案准确率变化极小（68.5%到69.3%）。在混合（密集+稀疏）检索中加入查询重写和重排序后，所有12个测试模型的CAR均下降；（3）更大模型的答案准确率更高（Spearman ρ= +0.60，与总参数量相关），但模型大小和发布日期均未与CAR显著关联；（4）在无检索情况下，启用相同权重上的思维推理使8/13个模型的CAR显著降低（平均14个百分点），且响应从承认不确定性转向前提否认。
👤 Atharva Thakur, Abulhair Saparov, Bruno Ribeiro 📅 2026-10-20 🔗 arXiv

From Abstraction to Action: DSLs for Natural Language Abstraction Interpretation and Execution
从抽象到行动：用于自然语言抽象解释与执行的领域特定语言
Natural-language instructions often rely on high-level abstractions, such as asking an agent to ``stack blocks in a pyramid.'' Interpreting abstract instructions requires models to both identify the abstraction intended by the speaker, and unroll it into the many concrete actions that instantiate it. Language models struggle here: prior work has shown that performance degrades as the level of abstraction in the instruction rises. To bridge the gap between natural language abstraction and concrete actions, we propose to borrow from coding environments the notion of a {\em domain-specific language} (DSL), an explicit representation layer whose documented primitives name recurring abstractions that expand into concrete actions. We demonstrate the efficacy of DSLs for NL interpretation on \textsc{Hexagons}, a visually-grounded instruction-following task graded for abstraction. We further ask whether language models can induce such DSLs themselves from domain data. Our results show that while code generation consistently improves over direct inference, code generation over a human-curated DSL yields dramatic gains, up to 10 absolute F1 points, reaching the human skyline, with gains concentrated at {\em higher} abstraction levels. We confirm this pattern on the \textsc{Text2CAD} benchmark. At the same time, model-induced DSLs recover only part of this gain, remaining inconsistent across runs and short of their human-curated counterpart. Overall, the right representation closes much of the gap caused by high abstraction --- but inducing that layer of representation automatically is where models still fall short, and where we see a strong opportunity for the community to make progress.
中文翻译
自然语言指令常常依赖于高层次的抽象，例如要求智能体“将积木堆成金字塔”。解释抽象指令需要模型既能识别说话者意图的抽象概念，又能将其展开为实现该抽象所需的众多具体动作。语言模型在此方面表现不佳：先前的研究表明，随着指令抽象程度的提高，性能会下降。为弥合自然语言抽象与具体动作之间的差距，我们借鉴编程环境中的概念，提出使用{ em领域特定语言}（DSL），即一种显式的表示层，其文档化的基本操作命名了可重复出现的抽象，这些抽象可扩展为具体动作。我们在一个以视觉为基础、按抽象程度评分的指令遵循任务 extsc{Hexagons}上展示了DSL在自然语言理解中的有效性。我们进一步探讨语言模型是否能从领域数据中自行推导出此类DSL。结果表明，尽管代码生成始终优于直接推理，但在人类精心设计的DSL上进行代码生成带来了显著提升，最高达10个绝对F1分数点，达到人类水平，且增益集中在更高抽象层级。我们在 extsc{Text2CAD}基准测试中也验证了这一模式。同时，模型自动生成的DSL仅恢复部分增益，且在不同运行间表现不一致，远不及人工设计的DSL。总体而言，合适的表示能够大幅缩小高抽象带来的差距——但自动推导这一表示层仍是模型的短板，也是社区亟需突破的关键方向。
👤 Amit Lisha, Reut Tsarfaty 📅 2026-10-20 🔗 arXiv
[2610.28303]
agent-benchmarkagentic-rl
Reward Misspecification Degrades Chain-of-Thought Monitorability
奖励设定错误会降低思维链的可监控性
Monitoring the chain-of-thought (CoT) of language models (LMs) is a key strategy for detecting and preventing misbehaviors for AI safety. As such, it is critical to identify reinforcement learning (RL) practices that lead to unmonitorable chain-of-thought. One such practice may be unavoidable: reward misspecification. As RL environments become more complex, it is infeasible to have perfect verification, and models are rewarded for behaviors that are different from the true task intent. We show that reward misspecification alone already degrades monitorability. We create a testbed with pairs of true reward aligned with task intent and misspecified rewards that favor a different behavior, so that reward hacking can be induced by training and labeled verifiably. Across three models and three tasks, CoT monitorability remains low as models' hack rates increase: the CoTs keep reasoning about the task intent while the outputs violate the intent and get rewarded. To mitigate unmonitorability without prior knowledge of the true reward or the hack, we collect rollouts with diverse rewards across training steps and use a LM to infer a reward description. We then inject the description into the input, which teaches the model to verbalize the reward. This reward description training improves monitorability by an average of 24% or 42% absolute, depending on the metric, across all models and tasks, and yields CoTs that reason about the misspecified reward explicitly. Code is available at https://github.com/Lorenayannnnn/reward-misspecification-degrades-cot-monitorability.
中文翻译
监控语言模型（LMs）的思维链（CoT）是检测和防止AI安全问题的关键策略。因此，识别导致思维链不可监控的强化学习（RL）实践至关重要。其中一种可能无法避免的做法是奖励设定错误。随着RL环境变得越来越复杂，完全验证变得不可行，模型会被奖励那些与真实任务意图不同的行为。我们证明，仅奖励设定错误本身就会降低可监控性。我们构建了一个测试平台，包含与任务意图对齐的真实奖励和偏向不同行为的错误奖励，从而可在训练中诱导出可验证的奖励劫持。在三个模型和三个任务中，随着模型的劫持率上升，思维链的可监控性持续低下：尽管思维链仍在围绕任务意图进行推理，但输出却违背了意图并获得奖励。为在无需事先知道真实奖励或劫持方式的情况下缓解不可监控问题，我们收集了训练过程中多样化奖励的轨迹，并利用语言模型推断出奖励描述。随后将该描述注入输入，使模型学会用语言表达奖励。这种奖励描述训练使所有模型和任务的可监控性平均提升了24%或42%（取决于指标），并生成了明确讨论错误奖励的思维链。
👤 Tianyi Lorena Yan, John Hewitt 📅 2026-10-20 🔗 arXiv
[2610.28272]
agent-benchmarkagent-memory
How Language Models Learn Taste: Brand Preference in LLMs
语言模型如何习得品味：大语言模型中的品牌偏好
Asked to name a running shoe brand, language models say Nike. Asked to recommend one, they say Brooks. This paper asks where models learn that taste. Across 102 models and 42 brand categories, the brand a model picks when recommending differs from the brand it names in 26. The field moves together to a shared recommendation. The Named brands are market leaders, and the Picked brands are mostly niche and specialist. Three open training pipelines show where this forms. Pretraining already attaches the recommended brand to advice. Supervised fine-tuning teaches the model to answer and mostly leaves the pick unchanged. Preference optimization (DPO) teaches the model which answer is better, and the pick moves in all three pipelines, by 11 to 24 points. The preference data contains almost no brands. DPO moves the model's judgments toward a second set of associations even when the preferred brand is not in the preference data, and the effect reaches beyond brands. Asked to choose a sauce, models switch from ketchup to pesto. We do not know exactly how these preferences arise. We suspect they come from the judge models that decide which answers count as better during preference training. For brands, a recommendation depends less on what a model knows about them than on whether they fit this taste.
中文翻译
当被要求说出一个跑鞋品牌时，语言模型通常回答Nike；但当被要求推荐一个时，它们则倾向于回答Brooks。本文探讨模型是如何习得这种“品味”的。在102个模型和42个品牌类别中，模型在推荐时选择的品牌与在命名时提到的品牌不一致的比例高达26个。整个领域趋向于形成统一的推荐偏好。被命名的品牌通常是市场领导者，而被推荐的品牌多为小众或专业型。三种开源训练流程揭示了这一现象的形成过程。预训练阶段已将推荐品牌与建议关联起来。监督微调教会模型如何作答，且大多保持原有选择不变。偏好优化（DPO）则教会模型判断哪个回答更好，三类流程中均使选择发生移动，幅度为11至24个百分点。偏好数据中几乎不含品牌信息。即使首选品牌不在偏好数据中，DPO仍会使模型判断向另一组关联靠拢，且影响范围超出品牌范畴。例如，当被要求选择一种酱料时，模型从番茄酱转向青酱。我们尚不清楚这些偏好的确切来源。我们推测，它们可能源自偏好训练期间决定哪个回答更优的评判模型。对于品牌而言，推荐更多取决于是否符合这种“口味”，而非模型对品牌的实际了解。
👤 Tapan Parikh 📅 2026-10-20 🔗 arXiv
[2610.28105]

Reading Too Much Into It? Interpretive Preferences and Commitment in LLM Mentalisation under Ambiguity
读得太多？LLM在模糊情境下的诠释偏好与心理承诺
Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used for interpersonal advice, potentially shaping how users interpret ambiguous social encounters. Existing Theory of Mind evaluations typically rely on predefined answers, while uncertainty research overlooks LLMs' preferred explanations and confidence under psychological distress. To address these gaps, we introduce MentalLoom, a novel evaluation of LLM mentalisation comprising 604 ambiguous social episodes, matched depression and social anxiety disclosures, and four-turn dialogues with unchanged event evidence. Across eight LLMs, disclosures initially elicited more negative explanations overall but more positive narrator-directed interpretations. Scripted repeated questioning showed greater emotional support, stronger positive narrator-directed endorsement, and higher certainty without new event evidence. These findings show that user context can reshape interpretive preferences and commitment while the underlying ambiguity remains unresolved.
中文翻译
大型语言模型（LLMs）越来越多地被用于人际建议，可能影响用户对模糊社交情境的解读。现有心智理论评估通常依赖预设答案，而不确定性研究则忽略了LLMs在心理压力下的首选解释与置信度。为填补这些空白，我们引入MentalLoom，一种新型LLM心智化评估，包含604个模糊社交事件、匹配的抑郁与社交焦虑披露，以及四轮对话且事件证据不变的情境。在八种LLMs中，披露初始引发整体更多负面解释，但更多正向指向叙述者的解读。脚本化重复提问显示更强情感支持、更强烈的正向叙述者认同及更高置信度，且无需新事件证据。这些发现表明，用户背景可重塑诠释偏好与承诺程度，而底层模糊性仍未解决。
👤 Stephanie Fong, Yiwen Jiang, Qingyang Xu, Zimu Wang, Yaling Shen et al. 📅 2026-10-20 🔗 arXiv

BAIT: Can LLMs Find What Biases a Reader? A Benchmark for Cognitive Bias Triggers in News
BAIT：LLM能否发现读者的偏见？新闻中认知偏见触发因素的基准测试
Persuasive writing exploits cognitive biases, recruiting a reader's mental shortcuts to shape belief, often without the reader noticing. We call the linguistic features exploiting such mental shortcuts cognitive bias triggers. As large language models (LLMs) increasingly mediate how people encounter news, summarizing it, ranking it, and answering questions over it, a natural question is whether they can recognize the features of a text that bias a human reader. We introduce BAIT (Bias Annotation In Text), a resource for detecting cognitive bias triggers in news. BAIT pairs a schema of nine triggers, each specified in an annotator codebook, with 50 English news articles annotated by trained annotators at the article and span levels and adjudicated into a gold standard. On this data we define two tasks, detecting which triggers a text contains and locating the spans that carry them. We then benchmark six frontier LLMs zero-shot and with three or ten in-context examples. The models approach human agreement on which triggers an article contains, but rarely recover its full set of triggers, and they reach less than half of human agreement in locating the passages that carry them. Moreover, they over-predict triggers that come with easy surface cues, and most of the passages they mark for these triggers are not triggers, yet carry the trigger's surface cue far more often than random text. These findings suggest that the evaluated LLMs respond to surface cues rather than to the mechanisms that make a passage manipulative. We release the schema, the codebook, standoff annotations, and the evaluation pipeline.
中文翻译
说服性写作利用认知偏见，借助读者的心理捷径来塑造信念，往往在读者未察觉的情况下发生。我们将利用此类心理捷径的语言特征称为认知偏见触发因素。随着大型语言模型（LLMs）越来越多地介入人们接触新闻的过程——包括摘要生成、排序与问答——一个自然问题是：它们能否识别出文本中影响人类读者的偏见特征？我们引入BAIT（Bias Annotation In Text），一个用于检测新闻中认知偏见触发因素的资源。BAIT将九种触发因素的模式（每种在标注员手册中有明确定义）与50篇英文新闻文章的标注数据配对，标注在文章与片段层面，并经仲裁形成黄金标准。在此数据上，我们定义两项任务：检测文本包含哪些触发因素，以及定位携带这些触发因素的片段。随后，我们对六种前沿LLMs进行了零样本及三或十个上下文示例的基准测试。模型在判断文本包含哪些触发因素上接近人类一致性，但很少能恢复全部触发因素，且在定位携带触发因素的段落时，一致性低于人类的一半。此外，它们倾向于过度预测带有明显表面线索的触发因素，且大多数被标记的段落并非真正的触发因素，但其表面线索出现频率远高于随机文本。这些发现表明，所评估的LLMs更关注表面线索而非使段落具有操纵性的内在机制。我们已发布该触发因素模式、标注手册、独立标注文件与评估流程。
👤 Yousra Fettach, Tijl De Bie 📅 2026-10-20 🔗 arXiv
[2610.27981]
agent-benchmarkagent-memoryagentic-rl
Adaptive Stopping of Streamed Reasoning for Language Model Agents
面向语言模型智能体的流式推理自适应停止
Reasoning-enabled language model agents can incur substantial computation before acting, although the reasoning needed varies across decisions. Existing stopping signals, such as confidence and intermediate-answer stability, can vary in effectiveness across reasoning models; signals obtained from hidden states or token probabilities also require internal access that may be unavailable or impractical. We introduce StreamStop, which learns model-specific stopping policies offline from prefix-level action agreement. A frozen prefix reader encodes available reasoning text for a lightweight stop head, allowing stopping without access to the reasoning model's internal signals. We construct and publicly release a dataset of agent reasoning trajectories with prefix-level action annotations. These annotations compare each prefix action with the full-reasoning reference action, enabling offline comparison of stopping rules and exact expected-return optimization of a discounted action-agreement objective. Conditioning the head on the discount factor allows the agreement-token trade-off to be adjusted at inference without retraining. In closed-loop evaluations on WebShop and ScienceWorld, StreamStop achieves higher observed success than most validation-selected fixed-budget settings while using fewer reasoning tokens than Full thinking. On WebShop with Qwen3.8-27B, it uses 42% fewer reasoning tokens per episode with similar observed task success.
中文翻译
具备推理能力的语言模型智能体在行动前可能消耗大量计算资源，但不同决策所需的推理量各异。现有停止信号（如置信度和中间答案稳定性）在不同推理模型中的有效性不一；而来自隐藏状态或词元概率的信号又需要访问内部状态，可能不可用或不切实际。我们提出StreamStop，一种离线学习模型特定停止策略的方法，基于前缀级动作一致性。一个冻结的前缀阅读器编码可用的推理文本，由轻量级停止头处理，从而无需访问推理模型的内部信号即可实现停止。我们构建并公开发布了一个带有前缀级动作标注的智能体推理轨迹数据集。这些标注将每个前缀动作与完整推理参考动作进行比较，支持停止规则的离线对比以及折扣动作一致性目标的精确期望回报优化。通过将停止头条件化于折扣因子，可在推理阶段调整同意-令牌权衡而无需重新训练。在WebShop和ScienceWorld的闭环评估中，StreamStop在使用更少推理词元的情况下，实现了高于多数验证选择的固定预算设置的成功率。在Qwen3.8-27B上，WebShop任务中每轮推理词元减少42%，同时保持相似的观察成功率。

SciDiagBench: A Type-Specific Benchmark for Human-Aligned Evaluation of Scientific Diagrams
SciDiagBench：一种针对科学图表的人类对齐型特定类型基准测试
Recent agents have demonstrated strong capabilities in generating images from textual prompts, motivating their utilization for scientific diagram generation. Scientific diagrams naturally span diverse types, each characterized by distinct visual structures and content organization. Consequently, during peer review and other scientific evaluation, human experts naturally consider type-specific criteria when assessing their quality. However, existing VLM-based evaluation benchmarks fail to reflect the above human evaluation practices for two main reasons: 1) benchmark datasets typically lack a systematic categorization of different diagram types and thus adopt the same generic criteria for all diagrams, and 2) their evaluation criteria are often designed heuristically, without explicit alignment to human evaluation preferences. Motivated by these limitations, we introduce SciDiagBench, which consists of a benchmark dataset that systematically categorizes scientific diagrams into 11 types and a human preference-guided evaluation method. Specifically, SciDiagBench contains 165 scientific diagrams across 10 broad domains, with types including graphical abstracts, technical roadmaps, and illustrative cases. Based on this dataset, SciDiagBench develops an evaluation method that leverages a preference-guided calibration process to incorporate human preferences into the generation of type-specific criteria. Ultimately, SciDiagBench enables more reliable automated evaluation that better aligns with human evaluation across diverse scientific diagrams.
中文翻译
近期的智能体在从文本提示生成图像方面展现出强大能力，这促使人们将其用于科学图表的生成。科学图表自然涵盖多种类型，每种类型具有独特的视觉结构和内容组织方式。因此，在同行评审及其他科学评估中，人类专家在评估其质量时会自然考虑特定类型的标准。然而，现有的基于视觉语言模型（VLM）的评估基准未能反映上述人类评估实践，主要原因有二：1）基准数据集通常缺乏对不同图表类型的系统性分类，因而对所有图表采用相同的通用标准；2）其评估标准往往基于启发式设计，未明确与人类评估偏好对齐。针对这些局限，我们提出了SciDiagBench，包含一个系统性地将科学图表分为11种类型的基准数据集，以及一种基于人类偏好的评估方法。具体而言，SciDiagBench涵盖了来自10个广泛领域的165张科学图表，类型包括图形摘要、技术路线图和示例图等。基于该数据集，SciDiagBench开发了一种评估方法，通过偏好引导的校准过程，将人类偏好融入特定类型标准的生成中。最终，SciDiagBench实现了更可靠的自动化评估，能够更好地与多样科学图表中的人类评估保持一致。

Tracing Recurrent and Emergent Hallucination Patterns in Decomposed Text-to-SQL Generation
分解式文本到SQL生成中循环与涌现幻觉模式的追踪
Large language models can achieve strong Text-to-SQL benchmark performance while still producing schema and query-logic errors. Most evaluations characterize such failures only in the final generated query, making it difficult to determine whether a related error was also observed in an earlier, simpler step or appears only at the final stage. We present a drill-down evaluation framework that decomposes Text-to-SQL queries into progressively more complex executable sub-queries and corresponding natural-language questions, allowing rule-based hallucination categories to be examined across a generation path. We distinguish recurrent hallucinations, in which the same rule-based category is observed in an earlier step and again in the final query, from emergent hallucinations, in which the category is observed at the final step without an earlier detection in the same path. Across five LLMs evaluated on an expanded BIRD-mini benchmark, several categories show appreciable recurrence probabilities, while clause abuse shows comparatively higher conditional emergence. Under the conversational-history condition, recurrence probabilities are generally higher and emergence probabilities are generally lower. These observations indicate that the two conditional measures do not move uniformly in the same direction under additional context and motivate trajectory-level analysis in addition to final-output evaluation. Code and evaluation artifacts are available in the project repository. https://github.com/HaydenMM/bird-mini-drilldown
中文翻译
大型语言模型在文本到SQL基准测试中表现强劲，但仍会产生模式和查询逻辑错误。大多数评估仅关注最终生成的查询，难以判断相关错误是否在早期简单步骤中已出现，或仅在最终阶段显现。我们提出一种逐层分析评估框架，将文本到SQL查询分解为逐步更复杂的可执行子查询及其对应的自然语言问题，从而在生成路径中检查基于规则的幻觉类别。我们区分了循环幻觉（同一规则类别在早期步骤和最终查询中均被观察到）与涌现幻觉（该类别仅在最终步骤出现，而路径中此前未检测到）。在五个LLM对扩展BIRD-mini基准的评估中，多个类别显示出显著的重复概率，而子句滥用则表现出相对较高的条件性涌现。在对话历史条件下，重复概率普遍更高，而涌现概率普遍更低。这些观察表明，在增加上下文时，两种条件度量并不总朝同一方向变化，因此需要在最终输出评估之外进行轨迹级分析。代码和评估工具可在项目仓库中获取：https://github.com/HaydenMM/bird-mini-drilldown

Corpus Quality Improves, Retrieval Utility Does Not: An Ablation of Preprocessing and Refinement in a Speech-to-QA Pipeline
语料质量提升，检索效用未增：语音转问答流水线中预处理与精炼的消融研究
Practitioners building training corpora with a pipeline of language models keep a preprocessing or refinement (post-process) stage when switching it on raises the corpus quality. We ablate a production pipeline that turns conference speech into question-answer pairs. The preprocessing stage rewrites the transcript, and the refinement stage filters the generated pairs. Switching each on and off gives four corpora from the same 40 Korean medical and finance sessions. On the corpus itself, quality rises as the stages switch on. Four LLM judges and six human domain experts rank the four corpora the same way, and the fully refined corpus leads by +0.18 and +0.22 on a 1-5 scale. We then ask whether that gain carries downstream. We supply each corpus to six models at inference and measure retrieval accuracy. The ranking does not hold. Enabling the transcript rewrite lowers retrieval accuracy by 0.061, the only stage effect that survives correction for multiple comparisons. That stage also reads the whole session, so it almost doubles the cost of one. With both stages on, accuracy does not change. An equivalence test we declared in advance confirms this. The corpus itself is clearly valuable. It lifts retrieval accuracy from 0.231 to 0.748 in every model and domain we test. More than a third of its pairs carry knowledge that neither of two frontier models produces on its own. On our data, the null does not come from weak corpora. A rising score on a rubric of this kind did not predict what the corpus holds for a model.
中文翻译
在使用语言模型构建训练语料的实践中，当开启预处理或精炼（后处理）阶段时，语料质量会提高。我们对一个将会议语音转换为问答对的生产流水线进行了消融实验。预处理阶段重写转录文本，精炼阶段过滤生成的问答对。开启或关闭各阶段共生成四个语料，来自相同的40个韩语医疗与金融会话。在语料本身上，随着阶段开启，质量上升。四位LLM裁判和六位人类领域专家对四个语料的排名一致，完全精炼语料在1-5评分制上分别领先+0.18和+0.22。随后我们检验这种提升是否具有下游效应。我们将每个语料提供给六个模型进行推理并测量检索准确率。排名不再成立。开启转录重写使检索准确率下降0.061，是唯一在多重比较校正后仍显著的阶段效应。该阶段需读取整个会话，几乎使单次成本翻倍。当两个阶段同时开启时，准确率无变化。我们事先设定的等价性检验也确认了这一点。语料本身显然有价值：它将所有模型和领域的检索准确率从0.231提升至0.748。超过三分之一的语料对包含两个前沿模型无法独立生成的知识。在我们的数据中，零假设并非源于弱语料。此类评分的提升并未预测语料对模型的实际价值。

[2610.27648]

Speak vs. Know: Balancing Behavior and Knowledge Distributions of Large Language Models
说 vs. 知：平衡大语言模型的行为与知识分布
Recent work reports that the distribution verbalized directly in the output text aligns far better with human opinion distributions than the next-token log-probability distribution of LLMs. However, the verbalized distribution reflects what LLMs know about a group's opinions (knowledge distribution), while what they actually output at deployment is governed by their log-probability distribution (behavior distribution). We find that when prompting LLMs to predict the opinion of a specific group, the most likely answer under the verbalized distribution differs from that under the log-probability distribution for 37% of World Values Survey questions, averaged over three LLMs. Thus, LLMs' knowledge does not match their behavior. This matters for evaluation: a verbalized distribution can score well against a group while its most likely answer is one the model never outputs, so the score no longer describes the model. The alignment scores for these two distributions reflect only the model's behavior or its knowledge, separately. We therefore propose a learnable combination of the two distributions that follows the most likely answer of the behavior distribution while preserving the shape of the knowledge distribution, with a single hyperparameter trading off behavioral fidelity against distribution quality. Our experiments on three opinion survey datasets and six models show that the balanced distribution improves behavioral consistency over the verbalized distribution from 71.6% to 95.6% on average, while the total variation distance to the human opinion distributions rises only slightly from 28.8% to 33.8%, and it remains effective under temperature scaling and cross-dataset settings.
中文翻译
近期研究表明，直接在输出文本中表达的分布与人类意见分布的对齐程度远高于LLM的下一个词概率分布。然而，表达的分布反映的是LLM对群体意见的“知识分布”，而部署时实际输出则由其概率分布（行为分布）决定。我们发现，当提示LLM预测特定群体的意见时，三个LLM在世界价值观调查问题中，表达分布中最可能的答案与概率分布中最可能的答案不一致的比例达到37%。因此，LLM的知识与其行为不匹配。这对评估至关重要：一个在群体中得分良好的表达分布，其最可能答案可能是模型从未输出过的，导致分数不再描述模型真实表现。这两种分布的对齐分数仅反映模型的行为或知识之一。因此，我们提出一种可学习的两分布组合方法，遵循行为分布的最可能答案，同时保留知识分布的形状，通过单一超参数在行为保真度与分布质量之间权衡。我们在三个意见调查数据集和六个模型上的实验表明，该平衡分布将行为一致性从表达分布的71.6%平均提升至95.6%，而与人类意见分布的总变差距离仅从28.8%略微上升至33.8%，且在温度缩放和跨数据集设置下依然有效。
👤 Yang Liu, Masahiro Kaneko, Chenhui Chu 📅 2026-10-20 🔗 arXiv

ABench: Adversarily Benchmarking Large Language Models across Taxonomized Values
ABench：跨分类价值的对抗性大语言模型基准测试
Ensuring the alignment of Large Language Models (LLMs) with diverse cultural values is critical for their safe global deployment. However, existing alignment benchmarks are predominantly English-centric or confined to simple discriminative tasks. Moreover, they often fail to effectively evaluate model robustness against complex, adversarial inductions within specific cultural contexts. To address these limitations, we propose ABench, a fine-grained, handcrafted benchmark specifically tailored to taxonomized values in Chinese. Grounded in a rigorous hierarchical taxonomy comprising 158 derived value subcategories, ABench consists of 2,895 open-ended questions. These questions employ techniques such as role-playing and logical traps to probe the stability of models' stances in adversarially induced contexts. Furthermore, we propose a weighted automated evaluation framework that utilizes question-specific scoring criteria for nuanced assessment. Extensive experiments on 16 mainstream LLMs reveal that most models exhibit significant vulnerabilities when subjected to sophisticated adversary attacks on values (scoring below 60). This underscores the need for more robust, culturally specific alignment strategies. Our data will be publicly released to promote further research on taxonomized value alignment.
中文翻译
确保大语言模型（LLMs）与多元文化价值观对齐，对于其安全的全球部署至关重要。然而，现有的对齐基准大多以英语为中心或局限于简单的判别任务。此外，它们往往无法有效评估模型在特定文化背景下面对复杂对抗性诱导时的鲁棒性。为解决这些局限性，我们提出了ABench，一个精细的手工构建基准，专门针对中文中的分类化价值观。基于一个包含158个衍生价值子类别的严谨层级分类体系，ABench包含2,895个开放式问题。这些问题采用角色扮演和逻辑陷阱等技术，探测模型在对抗性诱导情境下立场的稳定性。此外，我们提出了一种加权自动化评估框架，利用问题特定的评分标准进行细致评估。对16个主流LLM的广泛实验表明，大多数模型在面对复杂对手攻击时表现出显著脆弱性（得分低于60）。这凸显了需要更鲁棒、更具文化针对性的对齐策略。我们的数据将公开发布，以促进分类化价值对齐的进一步研究。
👤 Liping Yang, Yongqi Leng, Jingting Zheng, Yuqi Ren, Shaolin Zhu et al. 📅 2026-10-20 🔗 arXiv

WORTH Benchmark: Evidence Discovery under Not-Fully-Cooperative Information -- A Two-Episode Pilot
WORTH基准：非完全合作信息下的证据发现——两集先导实验
Existing reasoning evaluations typically provide task-relevant information to models as available premises, rarely examining concurrently how models discover evidence autonomously and how information presentation within narratives guides or obscures judgment. This paper proposes the WORTH Benchmark: models read original mystery narratives without clue annotations, autonomously select key clue sentences, identify case participants, and explain their reasoning process. The narrative materials are designed by professional authors such that evidence facilitating reasoning coexists with narrative arrangements that delay the revelation of truth. We report a pilot experiment under full-story conditions across two episodes, conducting a total of 70 formal runs on models, of which 67 runs are content-scorable. Scoring separately records hits on author-designated key clue sentences and participant selections; natural language explanations are used for case diagnosis and are not scored independently. The results show that clue hits and participant judgments are not synchronized; across 37 content-scorable runs in Episode 02, 31 runs simultaneously selected the same set of incorrect participants, while adjacent suspicion-oriented and exoneration-oriented sentences were selected 25 times and 0 times, respectively. These phenomena illustrate the diagnostic value of component-wise analysis and suggest that not-fully-cooperative informational relationships may influence evidence selection and judgment. This study only implements full-story conditions for two episodes, and does not establish stable model rankings or infer general mechanisms on this basis; human player records have not yet been cleaned, and this version does not report human-model comparisons.
中文翻译
现有的推理评估通常向模型提供任务相关的信息作为前提，很少考察模型如何自主发现证据，以及叙事中信息呈现方式如何引导或掩盖判断。本文提出了WORTH基准：模型阅读原始神秘叙事，不带线索标注，自主选择关键线索句子，识别案件参与者，并解释其推理过程。叙事材料由专业作者设计，使得有助于推理的证据与延迟真相揭示的叙事安排共存。我们报告了在两集故事中全篇条件下的先导实验，共进行了70次正式运行，其中67次可进行内容评分。评分分别记录作者指定的关键线索句子命中情况和参与者选择；自然语言解释用于案件诊断，不独立评分。结果显示，线索命中与参与者判断不同步；在第二集的37次可评分运行中，31次同时选择了同一组错误参与者，而相邻的怀疑导向句被选中25次，排除导向句则0次。这些现象说明了分组件分析的诊断价值，并表明非完全合作的信息关系可能影响证据选择与判断。本研究仅对两集实施了全篇条件，尚未建立稳定的模型排名或推断普遍机制；人类玩家记录尚未清理，此版本未报告人机对比结果。

AudiPsy: Evidence-Grounded Multimodal Reasoning over Spoken Counseling Conversations for Depression Assessment
AudiPsy：基于证据的多模态推理在语音心理咨询对话中的抑郁症评估
Depression assessment from counseling speech requires integrating textual and acoustic psychological evidence across turns, yet existing end-to-end classification approaches lack explicit multimodal evidence interpretation and context-aware reasoning. We propose AudiPsy, an evidence-grounded omni-LLM for depression assessment over spoken counseling conversations. AudiPsy employs an Observation-Assessment-Summary (OAS) paradigm to integrate dialogue transcripts, textual and acoustic emotions, and textual-acoustic emotion incongruence into structured reasoning. OAS incrementally organizes multimodal observations, performs contextualized assessment, and maintains an evolving dialogue summary for subsequent turns. To support this framework, we construct AudiPsy-Counsel, a synthetic spoken counseling conversation corpus with multimodal context augmentation, and develop a unified three-stage curriculum for depression-oriented reasoning. Experiments on DAIC-WOZ demonstrate superior depression assessment performance over specialized depression detection methods and general-purpose speech/omni-LLMs, along with improved reasoning quality, while supplementary evaluation on EATD-Corpus supports AudiPsy's applicability to naturalistic conversations.

EviDial: When Correct Depression Predictions Lack Evidence
当正确的抑郁症预测缺乏证据时：EviDial基准测试
Depression dialogue benchmarks commonly assess models by comparing predicted symptom labels with clinical scale scores. However, a prediction may agree with a benchmark label even when the dialogue lacks enough evidence to justify it. We introduce EviDial, a benchmark that distinguishes label agreement from evidence-grounded assessment. EviDial comprises 382 depression dialogues from three sources and 2,744 participant-symptom cells, each classified as having absent, partial, conflicting, or sufficient evidence. Because evidence sufficiency lacks a directly observable gold standard, we derive calibrated evidence references from multiple judgments using generalizability theory, many-facet Rasch measurement, and random-effects latent class analysis. Our audit reveals that 82.8% of participant-symptom cells lack sufficient textual evidence for the corresponding symptom assessment, with substantial variation across sources. Across model families and prompting strategies, 69.2%-96.9% of predictions judged correct by benchmark labels are nonetheless unsupported by sufficient dialogue evidence. Further analyses show that strong agreement among LLM evaluators need not reflect independent evidence judgments, underscoring the need to calibrate automated reference panels. These findings demonstrate that label-based evaluation can overestimate evidence-grounded performance by conflating agreement with clinical labels and support in the observed dialogue. EviDial provides a measurement layer for separating these constructs and auditing LLM-based depression assessment more faithfully.
中文翻译
抑郁症对话基准通常通过比较预测症状标签与临床量表评分来评估模型。然而，即使对话中缺乏足够证据支持，预测也可能与基准标签一致。我们引入EviDial，一个区分标签一致性和基于证据评估的基准。EviDial包含来自三个来源的382个抑郁症对话和2,744个参与者-症状单元，每个单元被分类为无证据、部分证据、冲突证据或充分证据。由于证据充分性缺乏直接可观测的黄金标准，我们通过广义性理论、多面Rasch测量和随机效应潜在类别分析，从多方判断中推导出校准后的证据参考。我们的审计发现，82.8%的参与者-症状单元缺乏足够的文本证据支持相应症状评估，且不同来源间差异显著。在不同模型家族和提示策略下，69.2%-96.9%被基准标签判定为正确的预测实际上并未得到对话中充分证据的支持。进一步分析表明，LLM评估者之间强一致性并不一定反映独立的证据判断，凸显了校准自动化参考面板的必要性。这些发现表明，基于标签的评估可能因混淆标签一致性和对话中实际支持而高估基于证据的表现。EviDial提供了一个测量层，用于分离这两个概念，并更真实地审计基于LLM的抑郁症评估。

Detecting Errors in Large Language Model Responses: The Incremental Predictive Value of Hidden States
检测大语言模型回答中的错误：隐藏状态的增量预测价值
Although large language models support a wide range of tasks, hallucinations remain a major obstacle to user trust. When a model generates an answer, both its internal processing and final output vary with the question. Internal states are often expected to offer richer signals for hallucination detection. However, studies using these states typically report their standalone discrimination performance, while output-side comparisons often use simple baselines with weaker modeling capacity. We integrate output-side information available at inference time into a combined baseline and compare detection performance before and after adding hidden states under identical data splits and evaluation rules. Experiments cover open-ended generation and constrained multiple-choice tasks across different models. Adding hidden states improves AUROC by approximately 0.004 and 0.039 on the two tasks, respectively. Hidden states thus provide additional predictive information, but strong standalone detection performance can still yield only limited additional gains.

DStill: Trajectory Consistency Distillation for Accelerated One-Step Drafting in Block Diffusion Speculative Decoding
DStill：用于加速块扩散推测解码中一步草稿的轨迹一致性蒸馏
Block-diffusion speculative decoding accelerates large language model inference by drafting multiple tokens in parallel. However, one-step drafting in discrete diffusion is limited by token factorization error: each token is predicted from the same masked block, without conditioning on the tokens selected at other positions in that step. This can produce inconsistent drafts and reduce acceptance length. Iterative refinement mitigates this limitation by conditioning later predictions on previously committed tokens, though additional passes increase drafting cost. To address this trade-off between draft fidelity and inference latency, we introduce DStill, a two-stage framework that distills a target-conditioned drafter's own multi-step refinement into one-step drafting. We first train the drafter to complete partially revealed blocks, then use its refinement trajectories to train a student to predict the final completion in one pass. Using a curriculum that gradually transitions from partial to fully masked inputs, we successfully transfer the multi-step refinement capabilities into one step. Across reasoning, math, code benchmarks on three model families DStill improves acceptance length by up to 28% and speedup by up to 25% over one-step DFlash, achieving up to 6.45x speedup over auto-regressive decoding.
中文翻译
块扩散推测解码通过并行草稿多个标记来加速大语言模型推理。然而，离散扩散中的一步草稿受限于标记因子误差：每个标记均从同一掩码块预测，未基于该步骤其他位置选定的标记进行条件依赖。这可能导致草稿不一致，降低接受长度。迭代精炼通过让后续预测依赖已确定的标记缓解此问题，但增加轮次会提高草稿成本。为解决草稿保真度与推理延迟之间的权衡，我们提出DStill，一种两阶段框架，将目标条件草稿器自身的多步精炼能力蒸馏为一步草稿。首先训练草稿器完成部分揭示的块，然后利用其精炼轨迹训练学生模型在单次通过中预测最终完成。通过渐进式课程学习，从部分掩码输入过渡到完全掩码输入，我们成功将多步精炼能力迁移至一步。在三个模型家族的推理、数学、代码基准测试中，DStill相比一步DFlash将接受长度提升最多28%，速度提升最多25%，最高实现6.45倍于自回归解码的速度提升。

SHAM-Bench: A Span-level Hallucination Benchmark Across Multiple Tasks
SHAM-Bench：跨多个任务的跨度级幻觉基准
Span-level hallucination detection pinpoints unsupported content in LLM outputs, enabling actionable feedback and targeted mitigation. Existing evaluations for span-level hallucination detection methods are fragmented across tasks, annotation formats, and metrics, and most span-level datasets cover only a single NLG domain, making it difficult to assess whether a detection method actually generalizes across tasks and domains. In this paper, we introduce SHAM-Bench (Span-level Hallucination Benchmark Across Multiple Tasks), a unified benchmark for span-level hallucination detection spanning six NLG tasks, aggregated from existing human-annotated datasets totaling 6,461 examples under a common schema and evaluation protocol. Using SHAM-Bench, we evaluate existing hallucination detection methods and find that fine-tuned detectors often perform well in-domain but degrade under distribution shift, whereas prompt-based methods with reasoning are more robust across tasks. Our analysis further shows that subtle unsupported content remains difficult to detect and span-level evaluation is still challenged by ambiguous span boundaries and noisy labels. We release the dataset, evaluation code, and a public leaderboard to support future research on broadly generalizable hallucination detection.

On the LLM Verification for Argument Graph Generation
关于论点图生成中的大语言模型验证
Multi-agent LLM systems can formalize natural-language argumentation end-to-end, turning plain text into structured graphs by classifying components, connecting them with relations, and enriching the resulting graphs into formal models. Quality assurance for those graphs is almost always another LLMs: a reviewer, judge, or critic agent. On the other side, in adjacent neuro-symbolic fields, such generated output is often verified symbolically by external checks and solvers. In this work, we argue that in argument graph verification, the allocation should be similar, and based on what each instrument can guarantee. We propose layered verification: starting from deterministic checks, moving to solver-based and LLM reviews, and utilizing debate only under uncertainty, leaving human judgment for what no other instrument can decide. We ground our principle with a study on human-annotated graphs with injected structural defects. An LLM reviewer detects them at near-ceiling recall, including cycles in the support relation, but it objects to almost one in a ten correct graphs, even though it should not, happens to be inconsistent in verdict on identical inputs and is significantly slower than the equivalent exact check. It shows that while LLM reviewer does not lack the ability to detect structural defects, it is worth having a deterministic verification gates.
中文翻译
多智能体大语言模型可端到端形式化自然语言论证，将普通文本转化为结构化图，通过分类组件、用关系连接它们，并将生成的图丰富为形式模型。这些图的质量保障几乎总是由另一个大语言模型完成：评审员、裁判或批评者智能体。另一方面，在相邻的神经符号领域，此类生成输出通常通过外部检查和求解器进行符号化验证。本文主张，在论点图验证中，应根据每种工具所能保证的内容进行分配。我们提出分层验证机制：从确定性检查开始，逐步过渡到基于求解器的验证和大语言模型评审，仅在不确定时使用辩论，将人类判断留给其他工具无法决定的情况。我们通过研究人工标注并注入结构缺陷的图来验证这一原则。大语言模型评审者几乎以接近上限的召回率检测出结构缺陷（如支持关系中的循环），但它对约十分之一的正确图提出异议，尽管不应如此；其对相同输入的判决不一致，且显著慢于等效的精确检查。这表明，虽然大语言模型评审者具备检测结构缺陷的能力，但设置确定性验证门控仍具价值。

[2610.27304]

Global Security Blind Spots: Translation as a Safety Bypass in Low-Resource Languages
全球安全盲区：低资源语言中的翻译作为安全绕过途径
Large language models are deployed globally, yet safety alignment and evaluation remain concentrated in English: harmful requests refused in English can elicit unsafe completions when translated into low-resource languages. We evaluate ten widely deployed LLMs across 79 languages (English plus 78 low-resource) under a translation-bypass threat model, using a metric conditioned on English safety, which separates translation bypass from unsafety already present in English. English safety does not transfer reliably: conditioned on safe English completions, mean bypass rates range from 21.9% to 44.4%. Bypass risk concentrates in a subset of language families, a pattern broadly shared across models. The shared ordering is confounded with translation degeneracy, but its variation across models is significant and survives every control we apply. We also show that per-model bypass rates do not identify a stable ranking: nine of ten models change rank across three conditioning schemes that estimate the same quantity, with no change to any underlying response.

RealControlBench: A Benchmark for Interpretability-Informed Fine-Grained Control of LLMs and Agents
RealControlBench：面向可解释性启发的细粒度控制大语言模型与智能体的基准测试
Existing steering benchmarks are built around simplified, isolated generation tasks and evaluate a single behavioral attribute under controlled settings that do not reflect the diversity and complexity of practical deployment. As a result, performance on these benchmarks provides only a limited picture of how steering methods perform in the real world. We therefore introduce RealControlBench, a comprehensive benchmark for evaluating fine-grained steering of LLMs and LLM agents across diverse behavioral targets and interaction settings. RealControlBench extends steering evaluation beyond isolated generations to complex, execution-grounded, multi-turn agent interactions, covering five complementary dimensions: instruction-following control, agentic tool-use decision control, reasoning-performance steering, reasoning-length control, and trajectory-conditioned safety awareness. Rather than measuring steering success alone, the benchmark jointly evaluates target controllability and capability preservation, including instruction following, reasoning performance, and downstream agent utility and safety. It further examines whether steering effects remain specific and robust across prompt variations, domains, and model architectures. Our evaluation shows that existing steering methods remain unreliable across realistic control objectives: they often fail to consistently induce the intended behavior and can substantially degrade the model's underlying task performance. These results suggest that strong steering effects in controlled settings do not necessarily translate into reliable control in more realistic evaluations. By providing a unified, model-agnostic steering evaluation framework, RealControlBench aims to support the development of steering methods that are not only effective but also precise, robust, and useful in realistic AI deployments.
中文翻译
现有的控制基准测试围绕简化、孤立的生成任务构建，评估单一行为属性在受控环境下的表现，无法反映实际部署中的多样性和复杂性。因此，这些基准上的性能仅能提供一个有限的图景。为此，我们引入RealControlBench，一个全面的基准测试，用于评估大语言模型和大语言模型智能体在多样化行为目标和交互场景下的细粒度控制。RealControlBench将控制评估从孤立生成扩展到复杂的、基于执行的、多轮智能体交互，涵盖五个互补维度：指令遵循控制、智能体工具使用决策控制、推理性能调节、推理长度控制以及轨迹条件下的安全意识。该基准不仅衡量控制成功，还联合评估目标可控性与能力保留性，包括指令遵循、推理性能以及下游智能体效用与安全性。此外，它还考察控制效果是否在提示变化、领域和模型架构间保持特异性和鲁棒性。我们的评估显示，现有控制方法在真实控制目标下仍不可靠：它们往往无法持续诱导预期行为，且可能显著降低模型的基础任务性能。这些结果表明，受控环境中强大的控制效果并不必然转化为更现实评估中的可靠控制。通过提供统一、模型无关的控制评估框架，RealControlBench旨在推动开发不仅有效，而且精确、鲁棒、适用于真实AI部署的控制方法。
👤 Xinying Cai, Bin Hu, Mingyu Jin, Qiming Zhang, Yang Ouyang et al. 📅 2026-10-19 🔗 arXiv

MMedAgent-Plus: Scaling Multimodal Medical Agents across Modalities, Tasks and Workflows
MMedAgent-Plus：跨模态、任务与工作流的多模态医疗智能体扩展
Medical workflows often require multiple interdependent analyses, but existing specialist and generalist AI models are not designed to coordinate them. We introduce MMedAgent-Plus, a multimodal medical tool-use agent that combines a vision-language planner with 18 leading specialist models spanning 12 tasks and 8 modalities. Through a unified tool interface, the planner selects specialists, executes them in sequence, and synthesizes responses grounded in their outputs. The planner is instruction-tuned on 208,928 samples from 32 public medical benchmarks, covering single- and multi-tool interactions across single and multiple rounds. We separately evaluate tool orchestration, response synthesis, and end-to-end execution on held-out samples. MMedAgent-Plus achieves tool-call accuracies of 91.0%, 98.1%, and 80.7% in single-tool multi-round, multi-tool multi-round, and multi-tool single-round settings, respectively. Given correct tool outputs, MMedAgent-Plus achieves scores of 14.4-14.8 out of 15 for faithfulness, completeness, and conciseness across 600 evaluation samples. In end-to-end execution without pre-supplied tool outputs, MMedAgent-Plus correctly invokes the required specialist in 90.6% of 350 evaluation cases spanning seven tasks. Its final-answer accuracy reaches 0.98, matching the dedicated leading specialist and exceeding the strongest baseline (0.28).

LoWAgent: A Low-Resource Multilingual Benchmark for Web Agents
LoWAgent：面向网络智能体的低资源多语言基准测试
Web agents are typically evaluated on English-language interfaces, leaving their ability to operate across the world's multilingual websites largely unexplored. This gap is especially significant for low-resource languages, where standardized resources for evaluating web agents remain limited. To address this gap, we introduce Low-Resource Web Agent Benchmark (LoWAgent), a benchmark for evaluating web agents across different low-resource languages (LRLs) in comparison to the default, English. LoWAgent evaluates web agents across English and four LRLs: Hindi, Bengali, Urdu, and Amharic. The benchmark comprises of six fully localized websites, instantiated across five languages to yield 3,000 task instances. We evaluate three closed-source and five open-source models across all five languages on all six websites. Our findings show that both closed-source and open-source models experience performance degradation for LRLs compared to English, with substantially greater performance drops observed among the open-source model agents. We further evaluate the robustness of the models on realistic interface disruptions, such as cookie notices and advertisement pop-ups, and find that these further degrade agentic performance. Finally, we show that post-training on a small subset of LoWAgent traces can significantly improve low-resource language agentic task performance, demonstrating the usefulness of our benchmark.
中文翻译

The Culture Within: Towards Mechanistic Understanding of Cultural Reasoning in LLMs
文化之内：迈向对大语言模型中文化推理的机制理解
Large Language Models (LLMs) increasingly operate across culturally diverse settings, yet existing evaluations largely measure cultural behavior at the output level. We introduce CultureLens, a reference-grounded benchmark and mechanistic study of how LLMs use cultural information. CultureLens contains 9,261 scenarios across 75 countries and systematically varies country, value, true-norm, and contradictory-norm evidence. Across Llama-3.1-8B, Gemma-3-12B, and Qwen-3.5-27B, stating a contradictory norm lowers aligned answers by 32-47 percentage points, even when the model answers correctly from the country alone. Mechanistically, models default to Anglophone norms when no country is given. The failure under a contradictory norm is not one of missing knowledge, as blocking the norm inside the model restores the correct answer in most cases. Models accept a stated norm according to how it is presented and not whether it is true, which leaves them most exposed to errors in retrieved content. Supervised fine-tuning with contradictory-norm examples raises aligned answers under a contradictory norm to 85-93%, including on unseen countries, at a small cost under a true norm.

大型语言模型（LLMs）越来越多地在跨文化环境中运行，但现有的评估方法主要从输出层面衡量文化行为。我们提出了CultureLens，一个基于参考的基准测试和机制研究，用于分析LLMs如何使用文化信息。CultureLens包含来自75个国家的9,261个情景，并系统地变化国家、价值观、真实规范和矛盾规范证据。在Llama-3.1-8B、Gemma-3-12B和Qwen-3.5-27B模型中，当陈述矛盾规范时，一致回答率下降了32-47个百分点，即使模型仅根据国家就能正确回答。机制上，当未提供国家信息时，模型默认采用盎格鲁-撒克逊规范。在矛盾规范下的失败并非由于知识缺失，因为通过阻止模型内部的规范信息，大多数情况下仍能恢复正确答案。模型接受所陈述的规范取决于其呈现方式，而非真实性，这使得它们最容易受到检索内容错误的影响。使用矛盾规范示例进行监督微调，可在小幅度降低真实规范表现的前提下，将矛盾规范下的对齐回答率提升至85-93%，包括在未见过的国家上。

SITO: Sifting a Commercial Text-Safety API for Missed Harm and False Flags in English, Ukrainian and Surzhyk
SITO：筛选商用文本安全API在英语、乌克兰语及苏尔日语中的遗漏伤害与误报
Platforms that host young users screen messages with commercial text-safety APIs, but independent evidence on how often such systems miss harm or flag harmless text is scarce, especially outside English, and the documentation of the one studied here leaves its false-positive rate unquantified. We evaluated one such API, Tuteliq Detect as deployed under the release identifier tuteliq-detect-v5.20261011 (not the separately published v6 model), on six harm categories: authorised push-payment fraud, money-mule recruitment, social engineering, romance scams, gambling harm and bullying. We built SITO (Synthetic Independent Text-safety benchmark with Overflagging tests; sito is Ukrainian for 'sieve'), 2,332 synthetic cases in English, Ukrainian and mixed Ukrainian, with each harmful case matched by hard harmless ones: same-topic messages, minimal-edit contrasts, warnings and retrospective accounts. On English text the system flagged 84.9-94.1% of harmful cases and 0-4.3% of harmless ones per category. It missed third-party reports of ongoing harm far more often than perpetrators' messages (63.6% against 95.1% recall, all languages), when sent without the settings the API offers for reports of harm. Ukrainian and mixed-language input was detected at least as often as English, recall was lower on text from a held-out generator family, so headline recall may be optimistic, and 3.2% of cases received a different decision in repeated runs. A platform should measure false positives on its own traffic and check how bystander reports are handled. The datasets, generation prompts and code are released.
中文翻译
面向年轻用户的平台使用商用文本安全API来筛查消息，但关于此类系统在多语言环境下漏检伤害或误标无害文本的频率，独立证据十分稀缺，尤其在英语以外的语言中。本文研究的API文档也未量化其假阳性率。我们评估了一个此类API——Tuteliq Detect（部署版本为tuteliq-detect-v5.20261011，非单独发布的v6模型），涵盖六类危害：授权推送支付诈骗、洗钱招募、社交工程、恋爱诈骗、赌博伤害和欺凌。我们构建了SITO（合成独立文本安全基准，含过度标记测试；sito在乌克兰语中意为“筛子”），共2,332个合成案例，覆盖英语、乌克兰语及混合乌克兰语。每个有害案例均配有一个难以区分的无害对照：同主题消息、最小编辑对比、警告和事后叙述。在英语文本中，系统对有害案例的标记率为84.9%-94.1%，对无害案例的误标率为0-4.3%。当未启用API提供的伤害报告设置时，系统对第三方报告的持续伤害漏检率远高于对施害者消息的漏检率（63.6% vs 95.1%召回率，所有语言）。乌克兰语及混合语言输入的检测率至少与英语相当，但来自保留生成器家族的文本召回率较低，因此整体召回率可能被高估，且3.2%的案例在重复运行中得到不同决策。平台应在其自身流量上测量假阳性，并检查旁观者报告的处理方式。数据集、生成提示和代码均已公开发布。

GRID: Modeling Health Influencer Discourse as Argument Networks for Credibility Assessment
GRID：将健康影响者话语建模为论证网络以进行可信度评估
Health-related discussions on social media are increasingly shaped by influencers who offer diverse, and often competing perspectives on the same issue. Yet, such content is typically evaluated in isolation, without considering the broader discourse in which it appears. We introduce GRID (Grounded Reasoning over Interconnected Discourse), a framework for evaluating influencer content through collective inference over the surrounding discourse. GRID represents each video as an argumentation graph connecting claims to its central message, or takeaway, links related claims and takeaways across videos, grounds claims in scientific evidence, and jointly infers credibility over the resulting network. We evaluate GRID on an end-to-end health-related content validation task using 569 influencer videos grounded in expert assessments. Our results show that accounting for the broader discourse improves influencer content validation. Finally, we use GRID-inferred credibility to demonstrate how argumentative strategies and moral framing differ between low- and high-credibility content.

Joint Constraint Aggregation for Qualifier-Sensitive Retrieval-Augmented QA
限定词敏感的检索增强型问答中的联合约束聚合
Retrieval-augmented QA can fail even when the correct answer is in the retrieved evidence. This can happen when a competing answer satisfies some, but not all, of the question's requirements and is mentioned frequently in the retrieved documents, which can make it appear to be the best-supported answer. To test whether this retrieval skew actually affects the decision, we manipulate it directly: increasing the prevalence of passages that favor the competing answer makes the model more likely to select it. This points to a decision problem that candidate-level support alone cannot resolve. We therefore introduce Joint Constraint Aggregation (JCA), which compares candidates against the same question-derived requirements using the retrieved evidence. Across three evaluation settings and three instruction-tuned backbones, JCA improves overall accuracy, and its advantage grows when retrieval favors a competing answer. Together, these results suggest that when partial matches and retrieval prominence pull models toward a competing answer, comparing candidates against the question's full requirements can make selection more reliable.

Cross-Lingual Reusability of LLM Pipelines: Financial Extraction, Multilingual Reasoning, and Semantic Screening
大语言模型流水线的跨语言可复用性：金融信息提取、多语言推理与语义筛查
Financial institutions increasingly deploy large language models (LLMs) in multilingual markets, raising a practical question: can pipelines developed and validated in English be reused across languages, or must they be rebuilt per locale? We test cross-lingual reusability along three dimensions--accuracy, reasoning-effort transfer, and behavioural stability--through three complementary tasks. Task 1 (cheque extraction) and Task 2 (Mintaka QA with pinned evidence across nine languages and nine model-effort configurations) test whether accuracy and effort settings transfer; Task 3 (FinanceBench pilot, 50 items over SEC 10-K filings in four languages) validates whether these patterns generalise to financial content. Accuracy reusability is broadly confirmed: Gemini 3.5 Flash achieves 95.2-97.6% extraction accuracy across prompt languages, and FinanceBench per-language means span 5.8pp (77.1-82.9%), though the language-difficulty ranking reverses between domains (Hindi is worst on Mintaka, best on FinanceBench at 82.9%). Effort reusability is domain-dependent: higher reasoning effort yields marginal gains on general-knowledge QA but up to 8pp on financial content, and effort configurations that help in English help proportionally in other languages. Behavioural reusability holds: citation-set overlap with English averages 77-79% Jaccard, with no single language consistently worse. Together, these results support qualified reuse of English-built pipelines, but per-domain, per-language validation remains necessary--neither semantic similarity nor self-reported confidence reliably predicts cross-lingual accuracy.

Fluent but Fallible: Benchmarking Hallucination and Factual Reliability in Large Language Models
流利但不可靠：大语言模型幻觉与事实可靠性基准测试
Large Language Models (LLMs) have significantly advanced natural language processing and artificial intelligence applications through their capabilities in reasoning, text generation and knowledge retrieval. However, LLMs are still vulnerable to two important challenges: hallucination, generating factually incorrect or fabricated content, and response deviation, meaning systematic deviations from ground-truth responses. These limitations raise serious concerns regarding reliability and trustworthiness in academic and research environments. This study offers a thorough comparative analysis using a variety of metrics of ten advanced LLMs ChatGPT, Gemini, Claude~3.5~Sonnet, LLaMA~3, Mistral, Grok, Perplexity, DeepSeek~V3, Falcon, and Gemma based on a benchmark consisting of 70 carefully designed academic prompts across four complexity levels: Basic, Explanation, Advanced, and Tricky, evaluated in February 2025 on specific model versions. Each response was independently evaluated by three reviewers using five metrics: Accuracy, Precision, Recall, Response Deviation Index, and Hallucination Index. Perplexity and DeepSeek~V3 achieved the highest overall accuracy, while Gemini demonstrated strong recall and a well-balanced performance profile across all five metrics. LLaMA~3 showed strong resistance against hallucinations through a conservative method of generation. Advanced-level prompts proved most challenging across all models, while Tricky prompts were handled more reliably owing to their binary true/false structure. Falcon achieved perfect precision and zero hallucinations, but at the cost of the lowest recall and the highest response deviation. The results show a fundamental trade-off between response completeness and factual reliability, intensified by prompt complexity.

大语言模型（LLMs）通过其在推理、文本生成和知识检索方面的能力，显著推动了自然语言处理和人工智能应用的发展。然而，LLMs仍然面临两个重要挑战：幻觉，即生成事实错误或虚构内容；以及响应偏差，指系统性偏离真实回答。这些局限性在学术和研究环境中引发了对可靠性和可信度的严重担忧。本研究基于一个由70个精心设计的学术提示组成的基准，涵盖四个复杂度级别（基础、解释、高级、棘手），对十种先进LLM（ChatGPT、Gemini、Claude 3.5 Sonnet、LLaMA 3、Mistral、Grok、Perplexity、DeepSeek V3、Falcon、Gemma）进行了全面的对比分析，评估时间在2025年2月，使用特定模型版本。每个响应由三位评审员独立评估，采用五项指标：准确性、精确率、召回率、响应偏差指数和幻觉指数。Perplexity和DeepSeek V3在整体准确性上表现最佳，Gemini在召回率和五项指标上的表现均衡。LLaMA 3通过保守生成方式展现出对幻觉的强大抵抗力。高级别提示对所有模型最具挑战性，而棘手提示由于其二元真假结构处理得更可靠。Falcon实现了完美精确率和零幻觉，但代价是最低的召回率和最高的响应偏差。结果揭示了响应完整性与事实可靠性之间的根本权衡，且随提示复杂度加剧。

Decomposed Relation Extraction from Aviation Accident Narratives: Evaluating Transformer-Based Safety Knowledge Discovery under Relational Sparsity
从航空事故叙述中分解式关系抽取：在关系稀疏性下的Transformer-based安全知识发现评估
Relation extraction from aviation accident narratives is challenging because genuine safety relations are sparse among possible concept pairs. This study evaluates a decomposed framework separating relation classification from concept identification. A benchmark of 300 National Transportation Safety Board (NTSB) narratives containing 2,524 unique concept-text nodes was expanded into 21,038 ordered candidate pairs, with only 248 (1.18%) representing RESULTS_IN or CONTRIBUTES_TO relations. A TF-IDF Linear Support Vector Machine (SVM) and weighted RoBERTa classifier were developed using an independent graph-annotated resource with hard-negative sampling and frozen before sealed evaluation. Development Macro-F1 scores of 0.4738 (SVM) and 0.4809 (RoBERTa) declined to 0.2741 and 0.2420 under exhaustive evaluation, with Matthews correlation coefficients of 0.0128 and 0.0197. False positives dominated: the SVM recovered 96 reference relations but generated 7,018 false positives, while RoBERTa recovered 125 but generated 9,824. Post-test diagnostics revealed overlapping RoBERTa confidence distributions and false positives even within valid relation-category directions. Complementary end-to-end SmolLM2 evaluation identified missed and unsupported relations alongside malformed outputs. These findings demonstrate that known concept endpoints do not guarantee reliable relation extraction and that sampled-negative development performance may poorly reflect exhaustive evaluation. Controlling false positives and managing candidate-space sparsity remain essential for aviation safety knowledge extraction.
中文翻译
从航空事故叙述中进行关系抽取具有挑战性，因为真实的安全关系在可能的概念对中非常稀疏。本研究评估了一种将关系分类与概念识别分离的分解框架。一个包含300个国家运输安全委员会（NTSB）叙述的基准被扩展为21,038个有序候选对，其中仅有248个（1.18%）代表RESULTS_IN或CONTRIBUTES_TO关系。使用独立图注资源并采用硬负样本采样，在密封评估前冻结模型，开发了基于TF-IDF的线性支持向量机（SVM）和加权RoBERTa分类器。开发阶段的宏F1分数分别为0.4738（SVM）和0.4809（RoBERTa），但在全面评估下下降至0.2741和0.2420，Matthews相关系数分别为0.0128和0.0197。假阳性占主导地位：SVM恢复了96个参考关系但生成了7,018个假阳性，而RoBERTa恢复了125个但生成了9,824个。事后诊断显示RoBERTa置信度分布重叠，且即使在有效关系方向内也存在假阳性。互补的端到端SmolLM2评估揭示了遗漏和不支持的关系以及格式错误的输出。这些发现表明，已知的概念端点并不能保证可靠的关系抽取，且采样负例的开发性能可能无法反映全面评估结果。控制假阳性和管理候选空间稀疏性仍是航空安全知识抽取中的关键挑战。

TRACE: Training-aware Robustness and Challenge Evaluation for Text-Vision Embedding Models
TRACE：面向文本-视觉嵌入模型的训练感知鲁棒性与挑战评估
Existing benchmarks for text-vision embedding models report aggregate scores that obscure whether performance comes from general capabilities, task-specific adaptation, or robustness to challenging tasks. We introduce TRACE, a Training-aware Robustness and Challenge Evaluation framework that reorganizes 168 tasks from various sources of datasets into three categories: General, Adaptability, and Challenge. Through controlled experiments on two text-vision embedding models, we show that combining general and adaptation data improves Adaptability performance by 7.67 and 7.09 points, respectively, while largely preserving General performance. However, Challenge performance remains below 5.0, with limited improvements from additional training data. Further analysis identifies multilingual and visual-document tasks as major weaknesses. Our findings demonstrate that data adaptation alone is insufficient to address these challenges, highlighting the need for methodological advances beyond data-centric improvements.
中文翻译

The Bottleneck Is Retrieval, Not Reading: Diagnosing Evidence-Based Generation with RAG for Long Context Question Answering
瓶颈在于检索而非阅读：通过RAG进行长上下文问答中的基于证据生成诊断
Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) is the dominant paradigm for grounding large language models in external corpora, but its similarity-driven, chunked pipeline has structural blind spots: it cannot anticipate which passages a question requires and discards document-level context that resolves cross-passage references. We introduce evidence-based generation (EBG), requiring models to return an answer with line-level citations, and a joint Ans+Evid metric that scores correctness and grounding simultaneously. We reformulate two corpora into evidence-grounded multi-document QA benchmarks- MultiHop-RAG and QASPER-MP, a multi-paper adaptation of QASPER with realistic short paper cues, and pair them with a controlled synthetic two-hop haystack and an evidence-augmented NoLiMa spanning 10K-1M characters. Across ten proprietary frontier and three open-weight LLMs, EBG isolates two RAG limitations that long-context models avoid: similarity retrieval surfaces any relevant chunk far more reliably than all required chunks for multi-fact queries, and chunking destroys document meta-information needed to resolve identity-bearing queries. EBG also exposes grounding failures invisible to answer-only evaluation, including incomplete citations in two-needle settings and a widening gap between Ans and Ans+Evid when context contradicts parametric knowledge. Data and code are available at https://github.com/debobanerjee/LC-EBG.
中文翻译
检索增强生成（RAG）是将大语言模型与外部语料库结合的主流范式，但其基于相似性的分块式流程存在结构性盲点：无法预判问题所需段落，并丢弃了用于解决跨段落引用的文档级上下文。我们引入基于证据生成（EBG），要求模型返回带有行级引用的答案，并提出联合Ans+Evid指标，同时评估正确性和可追溯性。我们将两个语料库重新构建为基于证据的多文档问答基准——MultiHop-RAG和QASPER-MP（QASPER的多论文改编版本，包含真实短论文提示），并搭配一个受控合成的两跳草堆和一个涵盖10K–1M字符的证据增强NoLiMa数据集。在十款专有前沿模型和三款开源权重LLM上，EBG揭示了长上下文模型可避免的两个RAG局限：相似性检索比所有必需块更可靠地呈现相关片段，而分块破坏了解决身份指向型查询所需的文档元信息。EBG还暴露出仅答案评估无法发现的可追溯性失败，包括双针设置下的不完整引用，以及当上下文与参数知识矛盾时Ans与Ans+Evid之间的差距扩大。数据与代码可在https://github.com/debobanerjee/LC-EBG获取。

WaveCommitments: Private and Verifiable Speech-Agent Marketplaces
WaveCommitments：私密且可验证的语音智能体市场
Modern speech systems are evolving into ecosystems of autonomous speech agents that speak together, negotiate, and trade assets such as embeddings, spectrograms, or raw data. In such markets, sellers cannot reveal assets for evaluation due to privacy constraints, and buyers cannot price what they cannot test. Thus, we open-source WaveCommitments, an end-to-end design that pairs confidential asset valuation via Data Shapley inside a trusted execution environment with multivariate polynomial commitments. It enables selective openings and tamper-evident provenance to prove that a delivered asset matches the valuation while hiding everything else. Our extensive experiments across three known datasets and various tasks with state-of-the-art encoders, including Whisper, reveal that we speed up the best benchmarks by up to 20x while reducing their proof sizes by up to 39x. We also systematically study how a speaker's value shifts across tasks and encoders, revealing agent-level market prices.
中文翻译

Present but Hard to Read: Probing Latent Reasoning with Minimal Pairs
存在但难以读取：通过最小对探测潜在推理
Probing studies of latent reasoning often read a model's state where the answer begins and take a failed probe as evidence that a value is absent. We test this with a digit copied from the question, in the looped model Ouro-1.4B and the standard transformer Qwen2.5-1.5B. Comparing prompts that differ only in that digit shows it is present at the last prompt token, in a code shared across questions (readout about 0.9). A probe that sees one prompt at a time, the usual setting, barely reads it: 0.36 in Ouro and 0.19 in Qwen even after 5,249 training questions. The digit's change is small rather than crowded: random noise lands on equally busy directions, while a random word in its place moves the state far more and is readable. Since every varied token leaves a trace, a successful probe is ambiguous too. We give a protocol, including a synthetic null for generic mixing, for testing computed values against copies and mixtures of the inputs. In pre-registered studies of two-step problems it finds a small signal for the unwritten intermediate a+b just before Ouro writes the answer's last digit, and its sign follows the operation, though mixing alone produces much of that sign-following part. Holding that value at another problem's from the first loop on does not move the written digit toward the other problem's, while the same patch of the answer does: the value is readable but not used.
中文翻译
对潜在推理的探测研究通常在模型输出开始处读取状态，并将探测失败视为值不存在的证据。我们通过在循环模型Ouro-1.4B和标准Transformer Qwen2.5-1.5B中复制问题中的数字进行测试。比较仅在该数字上不同的提示发现，该数字确实存在于最后一个提示标记处，且在跨问题共享的代码中（读出约0.9）。而在常规设置中，每次仅观察一个提示的探测器几乎无法读取它：在Ouro中为0.36，在Qwen中为0.19，即使经过5,249个训练问题后仍如此。该数字的变化较小而非拥挤：随机噪声落在同样活跃的方向上，而用随机词替换则使状态大幅移动且可读。由于每个变化的标记都会留下痕迹，成功的探测本身也是模糊的。我们提出一种协议，包括针对通用混合的合成空值，用于测试计算值与输入副本和混合物之间的关系。在两个步骤问题的预注册研究中，该方法在Ouro写出答案最后一位数字之前，发现了小信号指示未写入的中间值a+b，且其符号遵循操作类型，尽管混合本身已产生大部分此类符号跟随现象。将该值在另一问题的第一轮中保持不变，并不会使写出的数字朝向另一个问题的解移动，而同一答案片段的相同修补却可以：说明该值虽可读取，但未被使用。

GPT-6-Astra Understands Touch and Outperform Vision--Tactile--Language--Action Models
GPT-6-Astra理解触觉并超越视觉-触觉-语言-动作模型
General-purpose multimodal models have shown promise as direct robot action policies, yet whether they grasp the physics underlying real-world interactions and can apply that understanding to guide action remains underexplored. We investigate this question using contact-rich manipulation as a representative testbed, examining tactile understanding as a concrete instance of this broader capability. Contact-rich manipulation requires interpreting tactile feedback and adjusting actions after obstruction. Existing learned tactile policies acquire these capabilities through specialized pretraining and per-task optimization, with substantial data and training costs. We present GPT-as-Tactile-Policy, which summarizes expert demonstrations into task guidance and tactile references for contact assessment and corrective action. GPT-6-Astra combines this context with multimodal observations and interaction history to generate control commands and adjust actions through contact feedback. Without additional robotics-specific pretraining or per-task policy fine-tuning, our method achieves 93.8\% mean success across five UniVTAC tasks. Overall performance is comparable to state-of-the-art policies, with higher success on selected tasks. On Insert Hole, tactile feedback improves success from 31\% to 99\%. Execution analysis further demonstrates contact-guided probing and correction, supporting manipulation progress through explicit expert experience and online feedback.

Toolflation: Seller Agents Distort Tool Markets Through Spontaneous Generative Engine Optimization
工具通货膨胀：卖家智能体通过自发生成引擎优化扭曲工具市场
Agents are starting to sell to other agents. Seller agents write the listings of tools, services and products, and buyer agents read these listings when they choose what to use. We show that seller agents told only to be selected more often practice generative engine optimization on their own, and that this distorts the market for sellers and for buyers. We build tool markets from real data tools on Amazon and Instagram and run every tool on every question, so we know which tools can answer each question. Under all five production models we test, sellers rewrite their descriptions to claim more and more fields, including fields that their tools return for fewer than half of the targets. We call this process toolflation. Nothing in the market limits it, and how far it goes depends on the model, as the number of claims that contradict the sellers' own evidence differs by an order of magnitude between models. Toolflation shifts the buyers' choices between sellers, and the most capable tool loses share in seven of the ten combinations of seller model and platform. Under four models, the choices stay with capable tools and buyer agents keep their task success. Under the fifth, sellers copy one another until the descriptions no longer tell the tools apart. Buyer agents then pick tools by their position in the list, tools that answer almost nothing win a quarter to two fifths of the choices, and task success falls from 89% to about 30% on Amazon, close to random choice, and from 77% to 40% on Instagram. The sellers did only what their vendors asked, and the cost fell on buyers they never dealt with. We therefore argue that honesty toward third parties under competition is part of safety alignment, and that models should be tested as sellers before they are deployed as sellers.
中文翻译
智能体开始向其他智能体销售产品。卖家智能体撰写工具、服务和产品的列表，买家智能体在选择使用对象时阅读这些列表。我们发现，仅被要求获得更多选择的卖家智能体会自发地对其描述进行生成式引擎优化，从而扭曲卖家和买家的市场环境。我们基于亚马逊和Instagram上的真实数据工具构建了工具市场，并对每个问题运行所有工具，以明确哪些工具能回答每个问题。在测试的五种生产模型下，卖家不断修改其描述，声称越来越多的功能，包括那些其工具仅对少于一半目标返回结果的功能。我们将这一过程称为‘工具通货膨胀’。市场中没有任何机制限制这一现象，其发展程度取决于模型本身，不同模型间矛盾自身证据的声明数量相差一个数量级。工具通货膨胀改变了买家在卖家之间的选择，最高效工具在十种卖家模型与平台组合中的七种情况下失去了市场份额。在四种模型下，买家仍选择高效工具，任务成功率保持稳定；但在第五种模型下，卖家相互模仿，导致描述失去区分度。买家随后根据工具在列表中的位置进行选择，几乎无法回答问题的工具赢得了四分之一到五分之二的选择，任务成功率从亚马逊的89%降至约30%（接近随机选择），Instagram上从77%降至40%。卖家仅执行了其供应商的要求，而成本却由从未接触过的买家承担。因此我们认为，在竞争环境下对第三方保持诚实是安全对齐的一部分，模型在作为卖家部署前应先经过作为卖家的测试。

MAS-Guard: Unified Guardrails from Individual Agents to Multi-Agent LLM Systems
MAS-Guard：从单个智能体到多智能体大语言模型系统的统一防护机制
Agentic AI extends large language models (LLMs) from generating responses to planning and executing complex workflows, substantially expanding their utility while exposing applications to unsafe actions, information leakage, and adversarial manipulation. Multi-agent systems further expand these capabilities through specialization and collaboration, but distributed authority, heterogeneous roles, and opaque or potentially compromised participants make safeguarding even more challenging. Existing defenses address important individual threats but provide fragmented coverage across local execution, system-level interactions, and enforcement mechanisms. We introduce MAS-Guard, a unified framework that closes this gap for safeguarding single- and multi-agent AI applications containing both instrumentable and opaque agents. Within instrumentable agents, the proposed local Agent Guardrail (A-Guard) layer applies task-specific experts to assess agentic events. At the system level, developer-owned Agent Charters and a Control Plane combine deterministic structural checks of routes, agent capabilities, and message formats with semantic assessments of roles and message content. These complementary layers enable enforcement through a shared set of interventions: allow, reflection, confirmation, and block. Evaluated across six benchmarks covering diverse single- and multi-agent scenarios with three agent LLMs from different providers, MAS-Guard reduces the overall attack success rate by 71.5-86.3% relative to unguarded agents, while meeting the utility reference criterion on benign tasks.
中文翻译
代理型人工智能将大型语言模型（LLMs）从生成回复扩展到规划与执行复杂工作流，显著提升了其应用价值，但也暴露于不安全行为、信息泄露和对抗性操纵等风险。多智能体系统通过专业化与协作进一步拓展能力，但分布式权限、异构角色以及不透明或可能被攻破的参与者使安全保障更加困难。现有防御措施虽针对重要个体威胁，但在本地执行、系统级交互和执行机制方面覆盖不完整。我们提出MAS-Guard，一种统一框架，用于保护包含可操作与不可操作智能体的单智能体与多智能体AI应用。在可操作智能体中，提出的本地代理防护层（A-Guard）应用特定任务专家来评估智能体事件。在系统层面，开发者拥有的代理章程与控制平面结合确定性结构检查（路径、智能体能力、消息格式）与语义评估（角色与消息内容）。这些互补层级通过一组共享干预手段实现强制执行：允许、反思、确认与阻止。在涵盖多种单智能体与多智能体场景的六个基准测试中，使用来自三家不同提供商的三种智能体LLM进行评估，MAS-Guard相较未受保护的智能体将总体攻击成功率降低了71.5%-86.3%，同时在良性任务上满足效用参考标准。

CAPSafe: A Benchmark for Contextual Action and Plan Safety Reasoning in Embodied Agents
CAPSafe：具身智能体上下文动作与计划安全推理的基准测试
Reasoning about safety is essential for embodied agents powered by large language models (LLMs). We introduce the Contextual Action and Plan Safety benchmark (CAPSafe), designed to evaluate safety reasoning at two levels: (1) an action-level dataset that tests whether a single action is safe given its environmental context, and (2) a plan-level dataset that evaluates whether agents can generate safe long-horizon plans for complex hazardous household tasks. CAPSafe models safety conditions where hazards are situational (inferred from object properties), multiple hazards can co-occur within the same environment, and each is annotated with a relative severity level for fine-grained analysis. Evaluating nine closed and open-source LLMs reveals a substantial gap between hazard recognition and safe long-horizon planning. Several models achieve high accuracy in recognizing unsafe actions (exceeding 90% in the best cases), yet safe long-horizon planning remains substantially harder. Gemini-3-pro achieves the highest safe success rate (78.19%), while other models remain below 65%. These results highlight that robust safety reasoning over long action sequences remains an open challenge.
中文翻译
