Beyond the Evidence Boundary: Behavioral Mirage Under Progressive Degradation in Medical VLMs
超越证据边界：医疗视觉语言模型在渐进退化下的行为幻觉
VLMs for medical image interpretation are typically evaluated on clean inputs, despite the progressive evidence loss that can occur in high stakes clinical settings. Existing robustness studies show that performance can degrade while confidence remains high, but do not establish when a previously reliable model-case pair ceases to behave reliably. We introduce a baseline-anchored framework that traces baseline-correct cases through progressive image degradation to identify an empirical evidence boundary, and define mirage as continued commitment at this boundary, regardless of correctness. Across three VLMs on three medical datasets, only MedGemma provides a sufficiently large baseline-correct pool for trajectory analysis. On VQA-RAD, the only dataset in which the question text varies, language-only answerability is associated with baseline correctness (AUROC 0.677) and with commitment at the boundary (AUROC 0.653 to 0.670). MedGemma's post-boundary behavior is heterogeneous, including direct mirage, correct recovery, mirage dissolution, consistent abstention, and oscillation, with mirage rates of 16.0 to 100.0% across dataset-modality pairs. Despite this behavioral heterogeneity, residual-stream probes decode commit-versus-abstain behavior at the boundary with AUROC of 0.895 to 0.994 under nested cross-validation. These results show that progressive evidence loss can produce persistent commitment that output entropy and abstention behavior do not consistently reflect, motivating boundary-aware evaluation, option-order controls, and improved abstention mechanisms for reliable medical foundation models operating under partial observation.
中文翻译
医疗图像解释的视觉语言模型（VLMs）通常在干净输入上进行评估，尽管在高风险临床环境中可能出现渐进性的证据丢失。现有的鲁棒性研究显示，性能可能下降而置信度仍保持高位，但并未明确界定此前可靠的模型-案例组合何时不再可靠。我们提出一种基于基准锚定的框架，通过追踪基准正确的案例在渐进图像退化过程中的表现，识别出经验性的证据边界，并将在此边界上持续坚持输出的行为定义为“幻觉”，无论其正确与否。在三个医疗数据集上的三款VLM中，只有MedGemma具备足够大的基准正确样本池以进行轨迹分析。在VQA-RAD数据集中（唯一问题文本变化的数据集），仅语言能力的可回答性与基准正确性相关（AUROC 0.677），也与边界处的承诺行为相关（AUROC 0.653至0.670）。MedGemma在边界后的行为呈现异质性，包括直接幻觉、正确恢复、幻觉消解、一致回避和振荡，幻觉率在不同数据集-模态组合间为16.0%至100.0%。尽管行为多样，残差流探针在嵌套交叉验证下仍能以AUROC 0.895至0.994的精度解码边界处的“承诺”与“回避”行为。这些结果表明，渐进性证据丢失可能导致持续承诺，而输出熵和回避行为无法一致反映此现象，因而推动了边界感知评估、选项顺序控制以及改进的回避机制，以确保在部分观测条件下运行的可靠医疗基础模型。
👤 Edric Castel C. Hao, Rez Samantha Z. Floresca 📅 2026-10-20 🔗 arXiv

Lost in Recovery: Demystifying Recovery Behavior of Computer-Use Agents via Goal-Oriented Action Replay
迷失于恢复：通过目标导向动作回放解构计算机使用智能体的恢复行为
Recent advances in large (vision) language models have enabled computer-use agents that automate digital workflows via fine-grained keyboard and mouse operations. However, such agents remain fragile in long-horizon settings, where errors accumulate and executed actions leave unintended side effects, making the ability to recover from failures critical. Yet, existing remedies remain inadequate: environment resets are infeasible in proprietary settings, action replay is prohibitively expensive over long horizons, and API-based rollback is limited to web environments, leaving model-based recovery largely unexplored. To address this gap, we present ReTrace, a scalable evaluation framework for systematically studying recovery behavior in computer-use agents. We formalize Transition-Consistent Recovery: identical downstream actions from the recovered and target states must yield identical outcomes. We operationalize this criterion through goal-oriented action replay following controlled error injection. Using ReTrace, we present the first systematic study of autonomous, model-based recovery, revealing that (i) ideal recovery can unlock model's latent task completion capabilities, (ii) current computer-use agents fail to exploit their latent recovery capabilities, and (iii) accurate model-based recovery yields measurable end-to-end gains. Together, these findings establish recovery as a fundamental capability for building reliable computer-use agents. Our code and dataset is available at https://github.com/BangProx/ReTRACE.
中文翻译
大型（视觉）语言模型的最新进展使得能够通过细粒度的键盘和鼠标操作自动化数字工作流的计算机使用智能体成为可能。然而，这类智能体在长时程场景中仍显脆弱，错误累积且执行动作会产生意外副作用，因此恢复失败的能力至关重要。但现有解决方案仍不充分：环境重置在专有环境中不可行，动作回放在长时程下成本过高，基于API的回滚仅限于网络环境，导致基于模型的恢复研究几乎空白。为填补这一空白，我们提出ReTrace，一种可扩展的评估框架，用于系统研究计算机使用智能体的恢复行为。我们形式化定义了“转移一致性恢复”：从恢复状态和目标状态出发的相同下游动作必须产生相同结果。我们通过受控错误注入后的目标导向动作回放来实现该标准。利用ReTrace，我们首次系统性地研究了自主的、基于模型的恢复行为，发现：(i) 理想的恢复能释放模型潜在的任务完成能力；(ii) 当前计算机使用智能体未能利用其潜在的恢复能力；(iii) 准确的基于模型恢复可带来可衡量的端到端性能提升。这些发现确立了恢复能力是构建可靠计算机使用智能体的基本要素。代码与数据集已公开于https://github.com/BangProx/ReTRACE。
👤 Byeonghun Bang, Sangwoo Park, Jinheon Baek, Kangsan Kim, Youngwan Lee et al. 📅 2026-10-20 🔗 arXiv

ACE-Compression: An Ability-Centric Evaluation of Compressed Large Language Models
ACE-Compression：面向压缩大语言模型的能力中心评估
Model compression makes large language models deployable, but today's evaluation tells an incomplete story. Standard practice measures retained task performance and stops there. This misses a critical fact: compression perturbs the distributed knowledge embedded across billions of weights, and task scores alone cannot reveal which underlying abilities survive and which quietly collapse. To solve this question, we introduce ACE-Compression, an ability-centric evaluation framework. Instead of asking only "does the compressed model still answer correctly?", we ask "which of its core abilities remain intact?" Our framework probes four deployment-facing dimensions: procedural reasoning and constraint execution, knowledge grounding and truthfulness, social cognition and affective understanding, and persona fidelity and stylistic consistency. Across more than 35 compressed checkpoints from six open-weight LLMs-covering quantization and pruning-we find that compression redistributes ability profiles. Coarse, local, and surface-level signals often persist, while fine-grained, long-tail, and context-dependent abilities become fragile. Quantization consistently outperforms pruning in retention, but the practical meaning of that gap depends entirely on which ability an application requires. Based on our findings, deployers can choose compression methods by the ability profile their application needs, not just by a leaderboard average. And algorithm designers gain diagnostic signals to build compression methods that preserve what matters. We believe that ACE-Compression turns compression from a black-box accuracy drop into an interpretable intervention-one we can measure, understand, and ultimately improve.
中文翻译
模型压缩使大语言模型具备可部署性，但当前的评估方式讲述的是不完整的故事。标准做法仅测量保留的任务性能，便停止评估。这忽略了关键事实：压缩会扰动分布在数十亿权重中的分布式知识，仅靠任务得分无法揭示哪些底层能力得以保留，哪些悄然崩溃。为解决此问题，我们引入ACE-Compression，一种以能力为中心的评估框架。我们不仅问‘压缩后的模型是否仍能正确回答？’，更进一步问‘其核心能力中哪些仍然完好？’。我们的框架探测四个面向部署的维度：程序推理与约束执行、知识锚定与真实性、社会认知与情感理解，以及人格一致性与风格连贯性。通过对六种开源大模型的超过35个压缩检查点（涵盖量化与剪枝）进行分析，我们发现压缩会重新分布能力谱系：粗粒度、局部性和表面信号通常得以保留，而细粒度、长尾性和上下文依赖能力则变得脆弱。量化在能力保留方面始终优于剪枝，但这一差距的实际意义完全取决于应用所需的具体能力。基于研究发现，部署者可根据应用所需的能力特征选择压缩方法，而非仅依赖排行榜平均分。算法设计者也可获得诊断信号，以开发能保留关键能力的压缩方法。我们认为，ACE-Compression将压缩从黑箱精度下降转变为可解释的干预手段——我们可测量、理解并最终改进它。
👤 Jiahui Ning, Liang Chen, Yuchen Liang, Yi Zhang, Fenglei Fan 📅 2026-10-20 🔗 arXiv

From Tokens to Files: Multi-Granularity Detection and Localization of AI-Involved Code
从标记到文件：AI参与代码的多粒度检测与定位
The increasing use of large language models for code generation calls for methods that distinguish purely human-written code from AI-involved code: files in which any part, however small, was generated or substantially modified by a model. Beyond flagging such files, we aim to localize the lines or blocks attributable to AI. Existing approaches compress a file into a single score, from surface stylistic features or a global code representation, which averages away the evidence when only a few blocks are AI-involved. We propose a framework that models AI authorship at the token level and predicts at the line, block, and file levels. A frozen code language model is equipped with two low-rank adapters, trained separately on machine-written and human-written files; their per-token log-likelihood ratio provides local evidence of AI authorship. Lightweight heads aggregate this evidence within lines and blocks, using the surrounding code context, and their local predictions are aggregated into a file-level decision. We train on mixed-authorship files built from human originals and their AI-generated or AI-modified counterparts, including continuation and fill-in-the-middle scenarios. On an internal test set of 11,773 AI-involved files across six generation regimes against 2,541 human files, our method reaches 72.4 macro F1 at a 1% FPR operating point. With no adaptation, it reaches 62.5 line-level F1 on HybridCodeAuthorship, an external human-AI co-writing benchmark with no training split, above the 56 its own paper reports as the best line-level result. On 19,445 human-written files from repositories created before 2022, the false-positive rate stays between 0.55% and 0.81% under the transported 1% threshold, so the operating point holds on real human code.
中文翻译
大型语言模型在代码生成中的广泛应用催生了区分纯人工编写代码与AI参与代码的需求：即任何部分（无论多小）由模型生成或大幅修改的文件。除了标记此类文件外，我们还旨在精确定位归因于AI的代码行或代码块。现有方法将文件压缩为单一分数，基于表面风格特征或全局代码表示，当仅有少数代码块涉及AI时，这种平均化会抹去证据。我们提出一种框架，在标记层面建模AI作者身份，并在行、块和文件层面进行预测。一个冻结的代码语言模型配备两个低秩适配器，分别在机器生成和人类编写文件上独立训练；其每标记的对数似然比提供AI作者身份的局部证据。轻量级头部利用周围代码上下文聚合行内与块内的证据，并将局部预测汇总为文件级决策。我们在混合作者文件上进行训练，这些文件由原始人类代码及其AI生成或AI修改的对应版本构建而成，包括续写和填空等场景。在包含11,773个AI参与文件（六种生成模式）与2,541个纯人类文件的内部测试集上，我们的方法在1%误报率操作点下达到72.4的宏F1。无需适应即可在HybridCodeAuthorship外部人类-AI协作写作基准上达到62.5的行级F1，高于其论文报告的最佳行级结果56。在19,445个2022年前创建的仓库中的人类代码文件上，当采用传输的1%阈值时，误报率维持在0.55%至0.81%之间，表明该操作点在真实人类代码上依然有效。
👤 Yue Xu, Xiyang Wang, Hui Liu, Bolei He 📅 2026-10-20 🔗 arXiv

Give the Model a Finger: Developmentally Aligned Evaluation of Counting in Vision-Language Models
给模型一个手指：视觉-语言模型计数能力的发展性对齐评估
Vision-language models (VLMs) are exact on small sets and drift into estimation on larger ones, a profile reminiscent of children before they master counting. Children cross the barrier with epistemic actions: pointing at each item while reciting number words, moving counted items aside, partitioning the array. We give VLMs the same actions as dumb image tools with no visual intelligence of their own in a training-free agent loop. Our central claim is about measurement, not intervention: a model that counts by marking leaves a normally hidden serial procedure on the image, and the developmental principles of counting turn those marks into an interpretable audit of serial visual reasoning: every tag is classified against point-level ground truth as on-target, duplicate, phantom, or omitted, the answer is checked against the number of tags, and order irrelevance is tested by forcing opposite traversal orders. Across 9 models, 682 images, and 7 harness conditions we find that (i) the exact-counting range is a model-specific signature spanning nearly two orders of magnitude (R80 from 3 to 150 dots); (ii) as an intervention the finger mostly fails: tags help 4 of 9 models on small-count photographs, hurt imprecise pointers, and rarely beat a verbal routine or a blind point list; what the loop buys is an auditable trace, not accuracy; (iii) as measurement it succeeds: a tag-perturbation probe shows that precise pointers read their answers off the tags, pointing precision tracks which models benefit, dense scenes fail by omission, and models rarely revise after seeing their own tags; and (iv) the capacities dissociate: models follow an instructed traversal order yet often change their answer when it reverses, and children's regular-over-scattered advantage appears only under the routine and survives lattices that defeat a rows-times-columns shortcut.
中文翻译
视觉-语言模型（VLMs）在小规模数据上表现精确，但在大规模数据上逐渐转向估算，这一特征类似于儿童尚未掌握计数能力时的表现。儿童通过认知行为跨越这一障碍：在数数时逐个指向物品并说出数字词，将已计数的物品移开，对数组进行分区。我们为VLMs提供相同的动作，作为无视觉智能的简单图像工具，在无需训练的代理循环中实现。核心主张是关于测量而非干预：通过标记进行计数的模型会在图像上留下原本隐藏的序列过程痕迹，而计数的发展性原则使这些标记成为可解释的序列视觉推理审计——每个标记被分类为命中、重复、幻觉或遗漏，答案与标记数量对比，通过强制相反遍历顺序测试顺序无关性。在9个模型、682张图像和7种利用条件下，我们发现：(i) 精确计数范围是模型特有的特征，跨度接近两个数量级（R80从3到150个点）；(ii) 作为干预，手指方法大多失败：标记仅帮助9个模型中的4个在小计数照片上表现更好，对不精确指针有害，且很少优于口头惯例或盲点列表；循环带来的价值是可审计的轨迹，而非准确性；(iii) 作为测量手段，该方法成功：标签扰动探测显示，精确指针从标签中读取答案，指针精度与受益模型相关，密集场景因遗漏而失败，模型极少在看到自身标签后修正答案；(iv) 能力可分离：模型遵循指令的遍历顺序，但当顺序反转时常改变答案，而儿童在规则分布场景中优于散乱分布的优势仅在常规流程下出现，并在击败行×列捷径的网格中依然存在。
👤 Yingxin Jia, Hui Geng, Wenxin Yang, Mohan Zhang 📅 2026-10-20 🔗 arXiv

CovNav: Conformal Prediction-Guided Adaptive Inference for Object Goal Navigation
CovNav：基于置信区间预测引导的自适应推理用于物体目标导航
In object goal navigation (ObjectNav), an embodied agent must find a target object (e.g., "find a bed") in a previously unseen environment, relying on spatial commonsense such as object co-occurrence. Large Language Models (LLMs) encode this commonsense, but deploying them on robots exposes a trade-off between reasoning quality and efficiency: cloud-based models incur latency and privacy risks, whereas small on-device LLMs are fast but prone to hallucinated spatial judgments. Hybrid reasoning LLMs offer both a fast non-thinking mode and a slow thinking mode, which raises a key question for embodied agents: when is slow thinking worth its cost? We propose CovNav, a training-free framework that answers this question with Conformal Prediction (CP), turning dual-process (System 1 / System 2) inference into an uncertainty-aware decision. Much like recent decision models such as Jev, CovNav casts each spatial query as a structured binary decision rather than free-form generation: a local LLM in fast mode judges whether the target is likely near an observed object, CP converts its token probabilities into a prediction set, and only ambiguous queries are escalated to slow chain-of-thought reasoning. The object-pair formulation keeps calibration tractable and supports open-set objects through off-the-shelf detectors. Experiments on Gibson and HM3D show that CovNav surpasses the strongest zero-shot baseline by 0.8 and 1.7 percentage points in success rate on HM3D v1 and v2, while invoking slow reasoning for only 12.4% of queries and retaining most of the gain of always-on thinking at a fraction of its latency. Real-robot experiments on a TurtleBot 4 further validate the approach. CovNav shows how calibrated uncertainty can decide when an embodied agent should think slowly, offering a principled form of adaptive test-time reasoning for LLM-based robots.
中文翻译
在物体目标导航（ObjectNav）中，具身代理必须在先前未见过的环境中找到目标物体（例如“找一张床”），依赖于空间常识，如物体共现关系。大型语言模型（LLMs）编码此类常识，但将其部署在机器人上会面临推理质量与效率之间的权衡：云端模型存在延迟和隐私风险，而小型本地模型虽快但容易产生空间判断的幻觉。混合推理LLM提供了快速非思考模式和慢速思考模式，这为具身代理提出了一个关键问题：慢速思考是否值得其代价？我们提出CovNav，一种无需训练的框架，利用置信区间预测（CP）回答这一问题，将双过程（系统1/系统2）推理转化为不确定性感知决策。类似于最近的决策模型如Jev，CovNav将每个空间查询视为结构化二元决策而非自由生成：本地LLM在快速模式下判断目标是否可能靠近已观测物体，CP将其token概率转化为预测集合，仅对模糊查询升级至慢速思维链推理。物体对形式保持校准的可行性，并通过现成检测器支持开放集物体。在Gibson和HM3D上的实验表明，CovNav在HM3D v1和v2上的成功率分别比最强的零样本基线高出0.8和1.7个百分点，同时仅对12.4%的查询调用慢速推理，并以极低延迟保留了持续思考的大部分优势。在TurtleBot 4上的真实机器人实验进一步验证了该方法。CovNav展示了校准的不确定性如何决定具身代理何时应缓慢思考，为基于LLM的机器人提供了原则性的自适应运行时推理形式。
👤 Pengying Wu, Zhejia Wen, Kang Ding, Kangjie Zhou, Chunxuan Jiao et al. 📅 2026-10-20 🔗 arXiv

CUA-Recovery: Benchmarking Error Recovery in Long-Horizon Computer-Use Agents
CUA-Recovery：长时程计算机使用代理中错误恢复的基准测试
As computer-use agents (CUAs) move to long-horizon workflows spanning multiple applications, undetected errors can silently corrupt persistent state and cause cascading failures in subsequent actions. Reliable error detection and recovery have become critical bottlenecks for building robust CUAs. However, existing benchmarks focus primarily on end-to-end task performance, providing limited insight into whether agents can detect and recover from errors during execution. The few benchmarks that evaluate recovery rely largely on manually injected errors, which may not reflect the types or horizons of failures induced by real agent policies. We introduce CUA-Recovery, a benchmark comprising test and training suites across multiple desktop applications. We construct it using ReRail, a framework for synthesizing long-horizon tasks and trajectories in a persistent desktop environment. ReRail composes MyPCBench tasks into new long-horizon workflows with executable ground truth, collects verifier-confirmed failures from multiple policies, and turns them into verified recovery trajectories. CUA-Recovery comprises a human-verified test suite of 2,250 erroneous states over 100 tasks and a training suite of 8.6K trajectories over 377 tasks. Evaluation on the test suite shows that agents struggle both to recognize inherited errors and to complete the task. Across six agents and six takeover depths, Error Awareness Rate (EAR) reaches at most 44.0% and Pass@3 at most 25.5%. After fine-tuning Qwen3.5-35B-A3B on the training suite, its average EAR rises from 19.9% to 35.2% on held-out recovery states, and its Rubric Score goes from 5.0% to 16.1%. It also gains 15.3 points on MyPCBench and 10.8 points on Odysseys for Pass@3, showing that the training trajectories help both with recovery and with completing tasks from a clean start. Our code is available at https://github.com/xu-hu-2002/CUA-Recovery.
中文翻译
随着计算机使用代理（CUAs）向跨越多个应用的长时程工作流发展，未被检测到的错误可能悄然破坏持久状态，并导致后续动作中的级联故障。可靠的错误检测与恢复已成为构建鲁棒CUAs的关键瓶颈。然而，现有基准主要关注端到端任务性能，难以揭示代理在执行过程中能否检测并恢复错误。少数评估恢复能力的基准严重依赖人工注入错误，可能无法反映真实代理策略引发的错误类型或时长。我们提出CUA-Recovery，一个涵盖多个桌面应用的测试与训练套件的基准。我们使用ReRail框架，在持久桌面环境中合成长时程任务与轨迹。ReRail将MyPCBench任务组合为新的长时程工作流，具备可执行的真实答案，从多个策略中收集验证器确认的失败，并将其转化为已验证的恢复轨迹。CUA-Recovery包含2,250个错误状态的真人验证测试套件（覆盖100项任务）和8.6K条轨迹的训练套件（覆盖377项任务）。在测试套件上的评估显示，代理在识别继承错误和完成任务方面均表现不佳。在六种代理和六种接管深度下，错误感知率（EAR）最高仅为44.0%，Pass@3最高仅为25.5%。在训练套件上微调Qwen3.5-35B-A3B后，其在保留恢复状态下的平均EAR从19.9%提升至35.2%，Rubric Score从5.0%提升至16.1%。同时，其在MyPCBench上Pass@3提高15.3分，在Odysseys上提高10.8分，表明训练轨迹不仅有助于恢复，也有助于从干净起点完成任务。代码已公开于https://github.com/xu-hu-2002/CUA-Recovery。

Auditing the Product, Not the Recursion: The Evaluation Evidence Behind Recursive Self-Improvement Claims
审计产品，而非递归过程：关于递归自我改进声明背后的评估证据
Recursive self-improvement (RSI) promises AI systems, such as self-evolving LLM agents, that become better at producing further improvements. Evaluating this promise requires separating product claims about the final system from process claims about the improvement process itself. Existing surveys specify the evidence these claims require, but how often studies report that evidence remains unmeasured. We examine this question through full-text coding of 114 eligible studies in a reproducible arXiv search frame frozen in October 2026, including 90 that describe their own systems as RSI. The codebook links claims to evaluation designs. Independent human coding, third-reader adjudication, and a separate blind coding of improver comparisons assess coding reliability. Among the 90 RSI studies, 90% report product evaluation beyond development tasks under a broad criterion that includes partial or unstated separation, while 60% report evaluation on tasks unused for selection. Only 22% audit two or more reruns of the full loop. In a human-coded random sample, the RSI studies show the same contrast: 45% evaluate on tasks unused for selection and 14% audit two or more reruns. Of the 26 studies claiming a better improvement procedure, 23 report a related comparison, but only 4 compare earlier and later procedures from identical starting artifacts at a matched budget. Across these results and a comparison set of established works, evidence for repeatability and improved procedures remains less developed than product evaluation. Three propositions explain how selection effects, shared evaluator errors, and changes in system state limit what product evaluations can establish. Together, the findings support a claim-indexed reporting standard that connects each claim to the evaluation design needed to test it.

递归自我改进（RSI）承诺AI系统（如自演化的大语言模型智能体）能够持续提升自身以产生进一步改进。评估这一承诺需要将关于最终系统的成果性声明与关于改进过程本身的进程性声明区分开来。现有综述指出了这些声明所需的证据，但研究中报告这些证据的频率仍缺乏衡量。我们通过在2026年10月冻结的可复现arXiv搜索框架中对114项符合条件的研究进行全文编码来考察这一问题，其中包括90项将自身系统描述为RSI的研究。代码本将声明与评估设计相链接。独立人工编码、第三方评审以及对改进者对比的盲编码评估了编码可靠性。在90项RSI研究中，90%在宽泛标准下报告了超出开发任务的产品评估，该标准包括部分或未明确分离的情况；60%报告了在未用于选择的任务上的评估。仅有22%审计了完整循环的两次或以上重运行。在人工编码的随机样本中，RSI研究同样表现出类似对比：45%在未用于选择的任务上进行评估，14%审计了两次或以上重运行。在26项声称改进过程更优的研究中，23项报告了相关比较，但仅有4项在相同起始资源和匹配预算下比较了相同起始物的早期与后期过程。综合这些结果与一组已确立的工作相比，可重复性和改进过程的证据发展程度仍落后于产品评估。三个命题解释了选择效应、共享评估者错误和系统状态变化如何限制产品评估所能建立的内容。总体而言，研究结果支持一种按声明索引的报告标准，将每个声明与其所需验证的评估设计相连接。

When LLMs Manipulate: Measuring Behavioral, Social, and Temporal Effects
当大语言模型操纵时：测量行为、社会与时间效应
Large language models (LLMs) can influence user trust and decision-making through subtle linguistic cues, yet the behavioral effects of manipulative outputs remain poorly quantified. We introduce a behavioral computing framework for measuring \textit{behavioral vulnerability}, defined as users' susceptibility to manipulative AI responses. Building on DarkPatterns-LLM benchmark, we generate controlled manipulative stimuli using Manipulation Gradient Descent (MGD), targeting biases associated with authority, urgency, and social proof. We analyze participant responses using the Multi-Stakeholder Impact Assessment Network (MSIAN) to model cross-user influence and the Temporal Harm Propagation Model (THPM) to characterize the persistence of behavioral effects over time. In a study involving 120 participants, manipulative framing produced significant changes in trust calibration and compliance ($p < 0.01$), with effects also propagating across social contexts. These findings highlight the need to extend LLM safety evaluation beyond model outputs to measurable behavioral consequences and provide a computational framework for quantifying individual, social, and temporal risks arising from manipulative AI interactions.

AnomalyJev: Towards Evaluating Image Anomaly Explanations via Claim-Level Verification
AnomalyJev：通过主张级验证评估图像异常解释
Recent multimodal LLM (MLLM)-based anomaly detection (AD) methods can generate textual explanations that help interpret their predictions. However, reliably and efficiently evaluating these explanations remains challenging. While LLM judges can assess explanations through structured rubrics, they often require substantial token generation, making evaluation costly and difficult to scale. We instead propose to anchor evaluation in the available ground truth by extracting verifiable claims from each explanation and verifying them against the corresponding annotations. We instantiate this idea as AnomalyJev, a lightweight framework that extracts verifiable claims from contextualised representations using ground-truth-defined queries and pointer based heads, without token generation. The claims are then verified against the ground truth to score faithfulness and completeness. For evaluation, we construct a human-annotated set of 640 explanations with over 1,600 expert ratings across in-domain and cross-dataset settings. Despite its compact design, AnomalyJev achieves stronger agreement with human annotators than most open MLLM judges and approaches that of flagship MLLM judges, at a fraction of their runtime and resources.

近期基于多模态大语言模型（MLLM）的异常检测（AD）方法可生成文本解释以帮助理解其预测。然而，可靠且高效地评估这些解释仍具挑战性。尽管LLM评判者可通过结构化评分标准评估解释，但通常需要大量token生成，导致评估成本高且难以扩展。我们提出将评估锚定在已有真实标签上：从每个解释中提取可验证主张，并将其与对应标注进行比对。我们将其具体化为AnomalyJev，一种轻量级框架，通过基于真实标签定义的查询和基于指针的头部，从上下文表示中提取可验证主张，无需生成token。随后将主张与真实标签比对以评分忠实度与完整性。为评估，我们构建了一个由640个解释组成的人工标注集，涵盖超过1,600次专家评分，覆盖域内与跨数据集场景。尽管设计紧凑，AnomalyJev与大多数开源MLLM评判者相比达成更强的人类标注者一致性，接近旗舰MLLM评判者的水平，且耗时与资源仅为后者的极小部分。

Recoverability and Verifier Strength: Two Measured Preconditions for Unattended Agent Self-Improvement
可恢复性与验证器强度：无人值守智能体自我改进的两个可度量前提
Agent platforms are starting to improve their own agents: they read a deployed agent's execution traces, derive a change, have an automated verifier approve it, and release it with no human author. How much of this loop can safely run without a human? We propose two quantities a platform can measure. Verifier strength $σ$ is how well the available verifier -- often an LLM judge -- agrees with a trusted reference verifier; it limits how much a step can improve the agent. Recoverability is how cheaply a bad change can be undone: whether undoing it needs a new verifier approval, and how much of the old behavior a rollback restores. A step should run unattended only if both are adequate. On our platform this rule allows prompt, tool and skill edits to run unattended and keeps weight updates under human sign-off. Our main contribution is these two definitions and how to measure them. We built a platform that applies the rule and tested it in two ways: on the platform's own approval gate, and in controlled experiments on the $τ^2$-bench agent benchmark. On verifier strength, the platform's LLM judge sometimes gave the same score no matter what it was shown (9 of 37 settings) and reported no error, so a verifier can look healthy while approving anything; its strength must be measured, and two simple tests catch the failure. On recoverability, restoring a saved copy brought the agent back to its original behavior, but a regenerated undo did not, so only changes whose old version is kept can be reversed without a human; and while a change was deployed, it altered the outcome of 17 percent of tasks beyond normal run-to-run variation, which no undo repairs. Together these support the rule: a change can run unattended only when its old version is kept and its verifier's strength has been measured.

智能体平台正开始自主改进其智能体：读取已部署智能体的执行轨迹，推导出变更，经自动化验证器批准后发布，全程无需人工干预。这种循环能在多大程度上安全地脱离人工？我们提出平台可测量的两个量化指标：验证器强度 $σ$ 表示现有验证器（通常是 LLM 判官）与可信参考验证器的一致性程度，它限制了每一步改进的上限；可恢复性则衡量一次错误变更被撤销的成本——是否需要新的验证器审批，以及回滚能恢复多少旧行为。只有当两者均达标时，变更才可无人值守运行。在我们的平台上，该规则允许提示、工具和技能的修改自动运行，而权重更新仍需人工签发。主要贡献在于这两个概念的定义及其测量方法。我们构建了一个应用该规则的平台，并通过两种方式测试：在平台自身的审批门禁中，以及在 $τ^2$-bench 智能体基准上的受控实验。在验证器强度方面，平台的 LLM 判官在 9/37 设置下无论输入为何都给出相同评分且无错误报告，说明验证器可能表面健康却批准任意内容；必须测量其强度，两个简单测试即可发现此类失效。在可恢复性方面，恢复保存副本可还原原始行为，但重新生成的撤销操作无法做到，因此只有保留旧版本的变更才能无需人工撤销；且在变更部署期间，其改变了 17% 任务的结果，超出正常运行波动范围，任何撤销都无法修复。这些结果共同支持该规则：变更仅在保留旧版本且验证器强度经过测量的前提下才可无人值守运行。

Harness Self-Evolution for Long-Horizon Terminal Agents
利用自演化提升长时程终端智能体
Long-horizon terminal agents may solve a task in one run yet fail across repeated runs. Stochastic errors can propagate through actions and context, creating a gap between capability and reliable execution. We present RiseHarness, a framework for iteratively improving agent harnesses with model weights fixed. It focuses on flipping tasks with both successful and failed runs under the same configuration, using successful runs as references for failure analysis. RiseHarness combines agent trajectories with internal harness traces from a runtime monitor to examine how model actions interact with command execution, feedback, context management, and completion decisions. A Harness Handbook links this evidence to harness behaviors and source code, helping identify recurring problems in mechanisms shared across tasks. RiseHarness then searches other harness frameworks for relevant implementations and adapts them as design references to the current model and execution protocol. Candidate changes undergo behavioral checks and repeated task evaluation; versions with measured gains seed the next iteration. This loop integrates suitable mechanisms from different frameworks into a single harness and uses new execution evidence to guide further changes. We evaluate task success and reliability across models, ablate harness execution evidence and external research, and test transfer to unseen benchmarks with the same model. RiseHarness treats a specific model as the unit of harness adaptation, studying how changes to shared interaction mechanisms can make existing capabilities more reliable across tasks.

TEX: Test-Time Scaling Testing Agents via Execution-based Cross-Validation
TEX：基于执行交叉验证的测试时扩展测试代理
Test-case generation is critical but remains challenging for language models. Existing test-time scaling methods either isolate candidates or aggregate traces, which do not scale to long-horizon tasks. We present TEX, a collaborative test-time scaling framework based on execution-driven cross-validation. TEX parallelizes agents to generate tests and patches, cross-executing each test against every patch to form a $K \times K$ feedback matrix. Agents use their matrix slices for refinement, enabling objective validation without verbose trace sharing. On SWT-Bench Verified, TEX achieves 86.4%, establishing a new state-of-the-art and improving +9.0% over single-agent baselines. Moreover, even random selection from TEX outputs (84.4%) surpasses the previous SOTA (84.0%), showing collaborative execution feedback improves solution quality, not just best-case samples. Our results highlight execution-grounded feedback as an effective scaling mechanism for long-horizon agentic tasks.

TxnQA: Benchmarking Analytical Reasoning and Evidence Grounding over Transaction Event Sequences
TxnQA：面向交易事件序列的分析推理与证据定位基准评测
Analyzing merchant transaction histories requires dynamic temporal filtering, entity resolution, and aggregation over irregular event streams--operations poorly captured by static tabular or time-series forecasting benchmarks. We introduce TxnQA, an analytical QA benchmark comprising 27 task families (81 templates) alongside a 5-scenario fraud-risk reasoning suite that evaluates both verdicts and supporting evidence over synthetic, privacy-mitigated transaction data. Ground-truth answers are computed programmatically, and model performance is decomposed into format-following rate (FFR), conditional content accuracy (CA), and end-to-end correctness (E2E). Evaluating prompted proprietary baselines and six LoRA-adapted open-weight models shows that adaptation reliably enforces output schemas but leaves arithmetic bottlenecks: Gemma-4-31B-IT and Qwen3-8B attain E2E scores of 0.867 and 0.816, whereas evaluated MoE checkpoints reach only 0.632-0.713. Furthermore, on the 500-instance fraud suite, verdict accuracy frequently exceeds 80%, yet joint accuracy (requiring exact evidence attribution) collapses to at most ~23%. These findings identify schema compliance, semantic calculation, and evidence attribution as three distinct bottlenecks in transaction-sequence QA.

ReuF2F: Benchmarking Coding Agents on Formalized Undergraduate Research Problems
ReuF2F：面向形式化本科生研究问题的编码智能体基准测试
As AI systems begin to resolve open research problems, reliable verification becomes increasingly important. Formal mathematics provides a trusted framework for mathematical reasoning, enabling agents to produce machine-checkable proofs while conducting mathematical exploration. Existing formal mathematics benchmarks often focus on known results or a small set of prominent open problems, which can become difficult to evaluate reliably once solutions are discovered and widely disseminated. Existing evaluation protocols are also not always aligned with how modern coding agents operate. We therefore introduce ReuF2F, a Lean 4 benchmark of 244 formalized open problems drawn from Research Experiences for Undergraduates (REU), spanning entry-level open research problems across a broad range of mathematical subfields. We expect these less prominent open problems, which tend to attract less attention and may remain unsolved for longer, to be better suited for held-out evaluation of end-to-end open problem resolution in Lean. We adopt a standard coding-agent evaluation setting, placing each problem and its logical negation into a Lean project in a Docker container. We also build Leanimum-agent on mini-SWE-agent to provide a unified harness for fair comparisons across models. Our evaluation finds that even the best-performing model resolves only ~40% of the problems, leaving substantial room for improvement on formalized open research problems.

Teaching AI Agent to Play Poker Strategically Through Preference Optimization
通过偏好优化教授AI智能体战略性地玩扑克
A poker agent can make a legal move and still make a strategically poor one. We ask whether compact AI agents can exhibit strategic behavior under hidden information and whether preference optimization can improve beyond static and zero-shot policies. We first evaluate a static baseline and three frozen language models: SmallThinker, Qwen3, and Gemma-3 whose performance varies substantially between exact-game and six-player evaluation. We therefore propose Strategic Preference Optimization with KL-anchored Equilibrium (SPOKE), combining teacher-relative value penalties, policy anchoring, risk constraints, and structured rationale supervision, and compare it with SFT, DPO, GRPO, and GSPO. Across three runs of a SmallThinker-4B-A0.6B policy, SPOKE achieves the lowest mean exploitability in Kuhn (0.213) and Leduc (1.081) and the highest six-max top-1 agreement (0.775), while maintaining teacher KL comparable to SFT (0.522 vs 0.523). These results provide preliminary evidence that strategic preference optimization can improve strategic quality while preserving teacher-consistent behavior.

Do LLMs Really Lack Difference Awareness? Uncovering Heuristic Substitution in Group-Differentiating Fairness
大语言模型真的缺乏差异意识吗？揭示群体区分公平性中的启发式替代现象
In this paper, we investigate the issue of difference awareness in LLMs, defined as a model's ability to recognize and acknowledge meaningful differences between social groups in fairness decision-making. Previous studies have consistently shown that models perform poorly on such tasks, a finding commonly attributed to a lack of difference awareness. We propose an alternative account, which we term Fairness Heuristic Substitution: difference awareness is not necessarily absent, but the complex demand of simultaneously identifying relevant group differences and determining their implications for fairness decisions may lead models to substitute the simpler, more accessible equal-treatment heuristic, i.e., formal equality. Based on this hypothesis, we introduce TDD, a Two-stage Distinguish-Decide framework that explicitly decomposes fairness reasoning into first distinguishing contextually relevant group differences and then making the corresponding decision, thereby discouraging reliance on formal-equality shortcuts. (i) Across four benchmarks and ten models ranging from billions to trillions of parameters, TDD significantly outperforms one-stage baselines, even one strengthened through simulated test-set leakage. (ii) Combining TDD with neuron-level contrastive probing provides mechanistic evidence consistent with the Fairness Heuristic Substitution hypothesis by revealing two signatures of the formal-equality: a compact, locally coordinated representational structure and a pronounced activation bias under default reasoning. (iii) TDD's stage-wise decomposition uncovers overlooked failure modes in fairness reasoning, including heuristic displacement and misaligned stage dynamics.
中文翻译
本文研究了大语言模型在公平决策中对差异意识的问题，即模型识别并承认社会群体间有意义差异的能力。以往研究一致表明模型在此类任务上表现不佳，通常归因于缺乏差异意识。我们提出一种替代解释——公平性启发式替代：差异意识并非必然缺失，而是同时识别相关群体差异并判断其对公平决策的影响这一复杂需求，可能导致模型退而采用更简单、更易获取的平等对待启发式（即形式平等）。基于此假设，我们引入TDD（两阶段区分-决策框架），显式将公平推理分解为先区分情境相关的群体差异，再作出相应决策，从而避免依赖形式平等的捷径。 (i) 在四个基准测试和十种从数十亿到数万亿参数的模型上，TDD显著优于单阶段基线，甚至优于经过模拟测试集泄露增强的基线。(ii) 将TDD与神经元级对比探测结合，提供了与公平性启发式替代假设一致的机制证据，揭示了形式平等的两个特征：紧凑且局部协调的表征结构，以及默认推理下的显著激活偏差。(iii) TDD的阶段分解揭示了公平推理中被忽视的失败模式，包括启发式位移和阶段动态错配。

GRaSP: Latency-Aware Optimization for Geo-Distributed Retrieval-Augmented Generation
GRaSP：面向地理分布式检索增强生成的延迟感知优化
Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) is increasingly served across geo-distributed cloud and edge infrastructures, where wide-area latency, cross-region egress cost, and data-residency rules compete with retrieval quality. Conventional pipelines rank by semantic similarity alone and ignore where content lives, inflating tail latency and cost. We formalize the Geo-RAG Selection Problem (GRaSP), a drop-in re-scoring layer that decays relevance by latency and adds an optional cost penalty under a cardinality budget and per-region caps, with no retraining. We first settle its complexity: the base formulation is a linear objective over a partition matroid, solved optimally by an O(N log N) greedy algorithm, rather than NP-hard as a knapsack framing suggests, with hardness entering only under an additive egress budget. On a five-node CloudLab testbed emulating a four-region WAN over the Natural Questions corpus (2.68M passages), GRaSP's benefit is placement-dependent: under uniform placement it cuts fetch latency at negligible quality cost; under topical (residency-like) placement it traces a recall-latency Pareto frontier that dominates relevance-only, nearest-k, and a latency-budgeted baseline. At a moderate latency setting, GRaSP lowers P90 fetch latency by 41% while retaining 78% of relevance-only's recall, with sub-millisecond selection overhead, stable to 200 queries/s. Findings generalize across four public BEIR benchmarks. In end-to-end question answering, GRaSP reduces context-fetch latency by 43% while remaining within 0.062 absolute EM of relevance-only retrieval.
中文翻译
检索增强生成（RAG）正越来越多地部署在地理分布的云和边缘基础设施中，其中广域延迟、跨区域出口成本和数据驻留规则与检索质量相互竞争。传统流水线仅按语义相似性排序，忽略内容所在位置，导致尾部延迟和成本上升。我们形式化了地理RAG选择问题（GRaSP），这是一种无需重新训练的即插即用重评分层，通过延迟衰减相关性并可在基数预算和每区域上限下添加可选成本惩罚。我们首先确定其复杂性：基础公式是一个在划分拟阵上的线性目标，可通过O(N log N)贪心算法最优求解，而非如背包问题所暗示的NP难，只有在附加出口预算时才进入难解状态。在模拟四区域广域网的五节点CloudLab测试平台上，基于Natural Questions语料库（268万段落），GRaSP的效果具有放置依赖性：在均匀放置下，它以极小的质量损失降低了获取延迟；在主题性（类似驻留）放置下，它描绘出优于仅关注相关性、最近k和延迟预算基线的召回-延迟帕累托前沿。在中等延迟设置下，GRaSP将P90获取延迟降低41%，同时保留相关性基线78%的召回率，选择开销低于毫秒级，稳定支持200查询/秒。研究结果在四个公开BEIR基准上具有一致性。在端到端问答中，GRaSP将上下文获取延迟降低43%，同时绝对EM值仅比相关性基线低0.062。
👤 Hardik Ruparel, Raj Kumar Pandey, Pramod Kumar 📅 2026-10-17 🔗 arXiv

Capable Teacher, Myopic Scorer: Reward-Free Teacher Repair for Agentic On-Policy Distillation
能干的教师，短视的评分器：无奖励教师修复用于代理在线策略蒸馏
Agentic on-policy distillation (OPD) trains a student agent on its own trajectories while a teacher scores every token. A natural explanation of where it goes wrong is compounding error: the student's mistakes carry its trajectories beyond the region where the teacher is reliable, so recent methods keep training inside that region, through episode-length curricula, teacher-guided turns, prefix replay, or teacher intervention where the two disagree. We show this explanation is incomplete. From the states where the student departs most from the teacher, the teacher's own takeovers still finish the task, yet the student trained on its token scores keeps running out of turns. We attribute this to the myopia of next-token scoring. The teacher rates each token without seeing where the episode ends, so its scores let through the safe, delaying moves that outcome-based RL would punish with zero reward, a penalty agentic OPD lacks. We call this difference between what the teacher does and what it scores the rollout-scorer gap. We propose Rollout-to-Scorer Distillation (R2S), which repairs the scorer before distillation by mining high-disagreement states from the untrained student, running teacher takeovers from them, keeping the states where enough takeovers pass a reward-free check such as consensus or an LLM judge, and fine-tuning the teacher to give its takeover-informed scores on every assistant token from the divergence turn onward, without the takeover in context. The repaired teacher is frozen as the sole scorer for standard OPD, requiring no task reward during repair or training. Across three environments, two teachers and four students, R2S outperforms three state-of-the-art agentic-OPD methods in all twelve teacher-student-environment cells, by 12.8 success points on average over the best of them in each cell. Project page: https://tree-shu-zhao.github.io/r2s/.
中文翻译
代理在线策略蒸馏（OPD）在学生代理自身轨迹上进行训练，同时由教师对每个标记进行评分。一种自然的错误解释是误差累积：学生的错误使其轨迹偏离教师可靠的区域，因此近期方法通过课程设计、教师引导转向、前缀重播或教师干预等方式在该区域内持续训练。我们指出这一解释并不完整。从学生偏离教师最严重的状态出发，教师自身的接管仍能完成任务，但基于令牌评分的学生训练却会耗尽回合数。我们将其归因于下一令牌评分的短视性。教师在未看到整个任务终点的情况下评分每个令牌，因此允许安全但拖延的行动通过，而基于结果的强化学习会以零奖励惩罚这些行为——这正是代理OPD所缺乏的惩罚机制。我们称教师实际行为与其评分之间的差异为‘轨迹-评分差距’。我们提出‘轨迹到评分蒸馏’（R2S），在蒸馏前通过挖掘未训练学生中的高分歧状态，从这些状态运行教师接管，保留足够接管成功并通过无奖励检查（如共识或LLM判断）的状态，然后微调教师，使其在分歧点之后对每个助手令牌给出基于接管信息的评分，而不需在上下文中包含接管过程。修复后的教师被冻结为标准OPD的唯一评分器，修复和训练过程中均无需任务奖励。在三个环境、两个教师和四个学生组合中，R2S在全部十二个教师-学生-环境单元中均优于三种最先进的代理OPD方法，平均领先最佳方法12.8个成功点。项目页面：https://tree-shu-zhao.github.io/r2s/。
👤 Shu Zhao, Tan Yu 📅 2026-10-20 🔗 arXiv

Cache Engineering for Agents
代理的缓存工程
Large Language Model (LLM) agents can handle increasingly complex tasks, navigating long trajectories of observations and actions. As these trajectories grow, key-value (KV) cache I/O and storage become the dominant cost. Trajectories can also exceed the LLM's maximum context length, requiring control over active cache growth. Prior work uses context engineering to reduce cache size by trimming or summarizing history. However, modifying the trajectory prefix invalidates subsequent cached states and triggers costly re-prefilling of retained context. Task accuracy can also suffer when too little context is retained to limit re-prefill costs. We propose cache engineering as a new scheme. It allows agents to directly control their active KV cache without modifying the trajectory or re-prefilling retained context. Specifically, agents can proactively use drop to exclude selected cache from future attention and repos to adjust retained keys' position encodings. We formalize these cache operations and identify history dependence as a serving challenge: identical token sequences can yield different cache states under different cache operation sequences, making prefix matching insufficient for correct cache reuse. Thus, we co-design the AKV serving engine. It provides a stateless interface compatible with existing serving APIs, while internally managing stateful cache reuse, eviction, and restoration. Experiments show that a tool-response rolling-drop cache policy improves average benchmark scores by 1.7 points and reduces average cost by 27.7%, compared with conventional threshold-triggered summarization. With AKV, it achieves up to 1.2x the output throughput and a 1.5x speedup in P95 request latency. Recursive self-improvement of cache policies further reduces cost by 7.1% over expert design. Our code is at https://github.com/thu-nics/AKV.
中文翻译
大型语言模型（LLM）代理能够处理日益复杂的任务，需在长轨迹的观察与动作中导航。随着轨迹增长，键值（KV）缓存的I/O和存储成本成为主要瓶颈。轨迹还可能超过LLM的最大上下文长度，需要对活跃缓存增长进行控制。先前工作通过上下文工程减少缓存大小，例如修剪或摘要历史。然而，修改轨迹前缀会破坏后续缓存状态，触发昂贵的重新填充操作。若保留过少上下文，则可能导致重填充成本过高，影响任务准确性。我们提出一种新的方案——缓存工程，使代理可直接控制其活跃KV缓存，而无需修改轨迹或重新填充保留的上下文。具体而言，代理可主动使用drop排除未来注意力中的特定缓存，或使用repos调整保留键的位置编码。我们形式化了这些缓存操作，并识别出历史依赖性作为服务挑战：相同的标记序列在不同缓存操作序列下可能产生不同的缓存状态，使得前缀匹配不足以正确复用缓存。因此，我们共同设计了AKV服务引擎。它提供与现有服务API兼容的无状态接口，同时在内部管理有状态的缓存复用、驱逐与恢复。实验表明，工具响应滚动drop缓存策略相比传统阈值触发摘要，平均基准得分提升1.7分，平均成本降低27.7%。使用AKV后，输出吞吐量最高可达1.2倍，P95请求延迟速度提升1.5倍。递归自改进缓存策略进一步比专家设计降低7.1%的成本。代码地址：https://github.com/thu-nics/AKV。
👤 Tianyu Fu, Ruoxi Wang, Zihan Min, Zhongqi Ren, Zhihao Li et al. 📅 2026-10-20 🔗 arXiv

OptionWorld: A Benchmark for Diagnosing Failures of LLMs as Long-Term Personal Assistants
OptionWorld：诊断大语言模型作为长期个人助手失败的基准
Long-term assistants must follow unfinished tasks across days as information and circumstances change. Finding information or starting an action, however, does not ensure that the task gets completed. We introduce OptionWorld, a benchmark that traces information acquisition, judgments, actions, and outcomes in simulated daily-life tasks to identify where current LLMs fall short as long-term personal assistants. In runs with archived decision inputs, the key facts remain in the model's last input before the deadline in 35.5% of failed instances. Failure analysis identifies the three most frequent problems: needed information is not obtained or combined, available opportunities to act go unused, and agents impose restrictions that the task does not require. Three follow-up experiments show that supplying missing facts restores task success in some cases; action reminders can restart work while subsequent steps still miss the deadline; and correcting a supplied note that wrongly forbids contact restores permitted inquiry. These findings locate shortcomings in maintaining task state: connecting facts to unfinished work, following through before deadlines, and separating actual restrictions from the agent's assumptions.
中文翻译
长期助手必须在信息和环境变化的情况下跨天完成未完成的任务。然而，查找信息或启动行动并不保证任务最终完成。我们引入OptionWorld，一个基准测试，用于追踪模拟日常生活任务中的信息获取、判断、行动及结果，以识别当前大语言模型在作为长期个人助手时的不足之处。在使用归档决策输入的实验中，关键事实在35.5%的失败实例中仍保留在模型最后输入中直至截止时间。失败分析揭示了三个最常见的问题：所需信息未被获取或整合，可用行动机会被忽视，代理施加了任务本身不需要的限制。后续三个实验表明，补充缺失事实可在某些情况下恢复任务成功；行动提醒可重启工作，但后续步骤仍可能错过截止时间；修正一个错误禁止联系的提示可恢复允许的咨询。这些发现定位了任务状态维持方面的缺陷：将事实与未完成工作关联、在截止前跟进、区分实际限制与代理假设。
👤 Ruoyu Zhang, Zhaocen Liu, Weiyi Tian, Melissa Feng, Shilong Liu 📅 2026-10-20 🔗 arXiv
[2610.28393]
agent-memory
When Copying Is Hard: Copy-Constrained Decoding for Exact Span Reproduction
当复制变得困难：用于精确跨度重现的复制约束解码
Precisely reproducing a target span verbatim within a long context is a fundamental capability test for LLMs: even when the target string is present in context, models often still drift within the span or fail to stop correctly, revealing limitations in source grounding and boundary control. This capability has broad practical relevance. For instance, code generation often requires embedding sensitive strings such as API keys exactly as they appear in context. In this work, we construct a benchmark to test exact span reproduction ability across contracts, scientific literature, code, and random sequences, and find that current models still make surprisingly frequent errors. To address this, we propose CopyGen, a lightweight copy-constrained plugin for frozen LLMs that turns copying from a byproduct of next-token prediction into a controlled process. Our key idea is to decompose copying into three distinct decisions: when to copy, how far to copy, and when to stop. Concretely, CopyGen first determines whether to enter copy mode, then predicts a tentative copy length, and finally verifies the generated span token by token. During verification, we adaptively adjust the logits of copy continuation tokens to incentivize or suppress continuation, and emit only the longest valid prefix. Across three LLM backbones and four benchmark tasks, CopyGen improves average exact match by 35% relative without model finetuning, and integrates seamlessly with SFT to further improve it by 4%, while substantially accelerating generation. These results suggest that reliable exact copying is not something LLMs obtain for free, but a decoding-time control problem that requires explicit modeling. Code is available at https://github.com/mbzuai-nlp/CopyGen.
中文翻译
在长上下文中精确地逐字重现目标跨度是评估大语言模型基本能力的重要测试：即使目标字符串存在于上下文中，模型仍常在跨度内漂移或未能正确停止，暴露出源信息定位和边界控制方面的局限性。这一能力具有广泛的实际意义，例如代码生成中经常需要将API密钥等敏感字符串完全按照上下文原样嵌入。本文构建了一个基准测试，用于评估在合同、科学文献、代码和随机序列中精确重现跨度的能力，发现当前模型仍频繁出错。为解决此问题，我们提出了CopyGen，一个轻量级的复制约束插件，适用于冻结的大语言模型，将复制从下一个词预测的副产品转变为可控过程。核心思想是将复制分解为三个独立决策：何时开始复制、复制多远、何时停止。具体而言，CopyGen首先判断是否进入复制模式，然后预测暂定的复制长度，最后逐token验证生成的跨度。在验证阶段，我们自适应调整复制延续token的logits以激励或抑制继续，仅输出最长有效前缀。在三种大语言模型骨干网络和四个基准任务上，CopyGen在无需微调的情况下平均精确匹配率提升了35%，并能无缝集成到监督微调（SFT）中进一步提升4%，同时显著加速生成。结果表明，可靠的精确复制并非大语言模型的自然属性，而是一个需要显式建模的解码期控制问题。代码已开源：https://github.com/mbzuai-nlp/CopyGen。

Object-Oriented Harness: Evolving Agent Harnesses through Object-Oriented Design
面向对象的Harness：通过面向对象设计演化智能体Harness
Agent harnesses are executable code that specifies the prompts, tools, and control flow around a language model. A single global harness can struggle with heterogeneous tasks that require different execution strategies. In this work, we introduce Object-Oriented Harness (OO-Harness), a harness optimization framework that evolves a hierarchy of executable harness classes using object-oriented design. During training, an evolution agent uses execution records to create a family of specialized harnesses, each implemented as a subclass of the base or an existing specialized harness via inheritance. At test time, a harness router uses routing lessons accumulated during training to select a specific harness for each task. Across three long-horizon benchmarks spanning business workflows, workspace tasks, and tool use, OO-Harness achieves the best performance among the compared methods, with an absolute improvement of up to 15% over the base. Extensive ablation studies examine the contributions of its core components.
中文翻译
智能体Harness是执行代码，用于定义语言模型周围的提示、工具和控制流程。单一全局Harness在面对需要不同执行策略的异构任务时可能表现不佳。本文提出面向对象Harness（OO-Harness），一种基于面向对象设计演化的Harness优化框架。在训练过程中，进化代理利用执行记录创建一系列专用Harness类，每个类通过继承基础Harness或已有专用Harness实现。在测试阶段，Harness路由器利用训练期间积累的路由经验，为每个任务选择特定Harness。在涵盖商业工作流、办公任务和工具使用的三个长期基准上，OO-Harness在对比方法中表现最佳，相对于基础模型有最高达15%的绝对性能提升。大量消融实验分析了其核心组件的贡献。
👤 Gaotang Li, Bhavana Dalvi Mishra, Yifei Ming, Yanfei Chen, Zhongying CuiZhu et al. 📅 2026-10-20 🔗 arXiv

DeclineCraft: A RAG Abstention Benchmark for Jointly Measuring Combined Retrieval and Parametric Knowledge
DeclineCraft：联合测量联合检索与参数化知识的RAG拒答基准
Reliable retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) requires recognizing when neither retrieved documents nor parametric knowledge supports an answer. We introduce DeclineCraft, a dynamic abstention benchmark whose unanswerable questions target Minecraft feature additions after a corpus cut-off, with audits of possible parametric exposure. Later cut-offs support evaluation of future models. Across 27 LLMs, we measure Correct Abstention Rate (CAR; how often models acknowledge insufficient knowledge on unanswerable questions), Answerable Attempt Rate and Answer Accuracy. We find that (1) single-shot retrieval significantly raises CAR in 38 of 40 configurations (model and thinking setting) versus no retrieval. (2) Iterative RAG, in which the model self-assesses its context and can request up to two further retrieval rounds, significantly lowers CAR versus single-shot RAG in 30 of 40 configurations (mean 46.4%), with little change in Answer Accuracy (68.5% to 69.3%). Adding query rewriting and reranking to hybrid (dense & sparse) retrieval lowers CAR for all 12 models tested. (3) Larger models answer more accurately (Spearman $ρ= +0.60$ with total parameters), but neither model size nor release date shows a significant association with CAR. (4) Without retrieval, enabling thinking on the same weights lowers CAR significantly for 8 of 13 models (by 14% points on average), and the responses shift from acknowledged uncertainty to premise denial.
中文翻译
可靠的检索增强生成（RAG）需要识别当检索文档和参数化知识均无法支持答案时的情况。我们引入DeclineCraft，一个动态拒答基准，其不可回答的问题聚焦于语料库截止时间之后的Minecraft功能新增，并包含对可能参数化暴露的审计。后续更晚的截止点可用于未来模型的评估。在27个大语言模型上，我们测量了正确拒答率（CAR；模型在不可回答问题上承认知识不足的频率）、可回答尝试率和答案准确率。研究发现：（1）单次检索相比无检索显著提高了38/40种配置下的CAR；（2）迭代式RAG（模型自我评估上下文并最多请求两次额外检索）在30/40种配置下显著降低了CAR（平均46.4%），而答案准确率变化极小（68.5%到69.3%）。在混合（密集+稀疏）检索中加入查询重写和重排序后，所有12个测试模型的CAR均下降；（3）更大模型的答案准确率更高（Spearman ρ= +0.60，与总参数量相关），但模型大小和发布日期均未与CAR显著关联；（4）在无检索情况下，启用相同权重上的思维推理使8/13个模型的CAR显著降低（平均14个百分点），且响应从承认不确定性转向前提否认。

Adaptive Replay Refinement: Allocating Test-Time Compute Between Revision and Resampling
自适应重播优化：在修订与重采样之间分配测试时计算资源
Test-time scaling allocates additional inference compute to refining existing solutions or generating new ones. This raises a fundamental question: Should I keep improving this solution, or stop working on it and try a completely new solution? Existing methods make this decision using fixed refinement schedules, self-verification judgments, or learned controllers, which can waste compute or rely on signals that may be incorrect or require additional training. We introduce Adaptive Replay Refinement (ARR), a training-free method that detects when a stationary revision reaches a fixed point and reallocates the released compute to a new independent sample; replay decoding treats the current solution as a draft and verifies unchanged tokens in parallel, enabling efficient fixed-point detection. Across seven models from five families and seven benchmarks, ARR matches majority voting on three of five mathematical reasoning benchmarks at equal compute, while reducing critical-path serial passes by up to 2.4 times. On MATH500, ARR achieves 84.6% accuracy with only 41% of the serial passes required by majority voting. These results demonstrate that detecting refinement exhaustion enables adaptive reallocation from revision to independent sampling, improving inference efficiency while highlighting the limits of self-revision. Code is available at https://github.com/AhsanBilal7/adaptive_refine.
中文翻译
测试时扩展通过分配额外的推理计算来改进现有解决方案或生成新方案。这引发了一个根本性问题：我应该继续改进当前方案，还是停止并尝试一个全新的方案？现有方法通过固定的优化周期、自我验证判断或学习控制器来做决策，但这些方法可能浪费计算资源，或依赖于可能错误的信号，甚至需要额外训练。我们提出自适应重播优化（ARR），一种无需训练的方法，能够检测到静态修订是否达到固定点，并将释放的计算资源重新分配给新的独立样本；重播解码将当前方案视为草稿，并并行验证未改变的标记，从而实现高效的固定点检测。在来自五个模型家族的七种模型和七个基准测试中，ARR在相同计算量下达到了多数投票在五项数学推理基准中的三项表现，同时将关键路径的串行步骤减少了最多2.4倍。在MATH500上，ARR仅需多数投票所需串行步骤的41%，便实现了84.6%的准确率。这些结果表明，检测优化耗尽能够实现从修订到独立采样的自适应资源再分配，在提升推理效率的同时揭示了自我修订的局限性。
👤 Ahsan Bilal, Muhammad Ahmed Mohsin, Muhammad Umer, Ayesha Mohsin, Sarmad Bilal et al. 📅 2026-10-20 🔗 arXiv

How Language Models Learn Taste: Brand Preference in LLMs
语言模型如何习得品味：大语言模型中的品牌偏好
Asked to name a running shoe brand, language models say Nike. Asked to recommend one, they say Brooks. This paper asks where models learn that taste. Across 102 models and 42 brand categories, the brand a model picks when recommending differs from the brand it names in 26. The field moves together to a shared recommendation. The Named brands are market leaders, and the Picked brands are mostly niche and specialist. Three open training pipelines show where this forms. Pretraining already attaches the recommended brand to advice. Supervised fine-tuning teaches the model to answer and mostly leaves the pick unchanged. Preference optimization (DPO) teaches the model which answer is better, and the pick moves in all three pipelines, by 11 to 24 points. The preference data contains almost no brands. DPO moves the model's judgments toward a second set of associations even when the preferred brand is not in the preference data, and the effect reaches beyond brands. Asked to choose a sauce, models switch from ketchup to pesto. We do not know exactly how these preferences arise. We suspect they come from the judge models that decide which answers count as better during preference training. For brands, a recommendation depends less on what a model knows about them than on whether they fit this taste.
中文翻译
当被要求说出一个跑鞋品牌时，语言模型通常回答Nike；但当被要求推荐一个时，它们则倾向于回答Brooks。本文探讨模型是如何习得这种“品味”的。在102个模型和42个品牌类别中，模型在推荐时选择的品牌与在命名时提到的品牌不一致的比例高达26个。整个领域趋向于形成统一的推荐偏好。被命名的品牌通常是市场领导者，而被推荐的品牌多为小众或专业型。三种开源训练流程揭示了这一现象的形成过程。预训练阶段已将推荐品牌与建议关联起来。监督微调教会模型如何作答，且大多保持原有选择不变。偏好优化（DPO）则教会模型判断哪个回答更好，三类流程中均使选择发生移动，幅度为11至24个百分点。偏好数据中几乎不含品牌信息。即使首选品牌不在偏好数据中，DPO仍会使模型判断向另一组关联靠拢，且影响范围超出品牌范畴。例如，当被要求选择一种酱料时，模型从番茄酱转向青酱。我们尚不清楚这些偏好的确切来源。我们推测，它们可能源自偏好训练期间决定哪个回答更优的评判模型。对于品牌而言，推荐更多取决于是否符合这种“口味”，而非模型对品牌的实际了解。
👤 Tapan Parikh 📅 2026-10-20 🔗 arXiv

Can Language Models Get "Aphasia" From Neuronal Ablation?
语言模型在神经元消融后会患上‘失语症’吗？
Progressive neuronal loss is the hallmark of neurodegenerative disease, yet how loss of neurons shapes the pattern of cognitive decline remains poorly understood. Here we systematically ablate neurons (computational units) in language models spanning recurrent long short-term memory (LSTM) and Transformer architectures and apply limited fine-tuning as a simple proxy for compensatory plasticity. With whole-unit ablation, LSTMs degrade gradually with syntactic and semantic faculties declining in parallel across mild-to-moderate damage. By contrast, Transformers exhibit late threshold-like collapse under feedforward multi-layer perceptron (MLP) ablation and smoother sigmoid-like decline under attention-head ablation. Qualitatively, MLP-ablated models continue to make within-category semantic substitutions (semantic paraphasias) alongside rising non-responses, paralleling Wernicke-type aphasia, while attention-head ablation produces a trend toward loss of semantic specificity similar to the semantic variant of primary progressive aphasia (svPPA). These findings indicate that feedforward and attentional pathways have functionally distinct failure modes, suggesting that architecture-dependent failure profiles may offer new computational perspectives on aphasic syndromes and neurodegenerative diseases as an in silico model. Notably, early accuracy retention under Transformer attention-head ablation qualitatively parallels pre-symptomatic reserve before the 40--60% neuronal loss reported by Gomez-Isla et al. (1996) when clinical symptoms emerge in Alzheimer's disease, whereas the steadier LSTM decline parallels the symptomatic progression described by DeKosky and Scheff (1990) and Terry et al. (1991).
中文翻译
神经退行性疾病以进行性神经元丧失为特征，但神经元损失如何影响认知衰退模式仍不清楚。本文系统地对涵盖循环长短期记忆（LSTM）和Transformer架构的语言模型中的神经元（计算单元）进行消融，并采用有限微调作为补偿可塑性的简单代理。全单元消融实验显示，LSTM在轻度至中度损伤下逐渐退化，句法与语义能力同步下降；而Transformer在前馈多层感知机（MLP）消融下表现出晚期阈值式崩溃，在注意力头消融下则呈现更平滑的S型衰减。定性分析表明，MLP消融后的模型持续产生类别内语义替换（语义错语），同时非应答增多，类似于韦尼克型失语症；注意力头消融则导致语义特异性丧失的趋势，类似原发性进行性失语症的语义变异型（svPPA）。这些发现表明前馈与注意力路径具有功能上不同的失效模式，提示基于架构的失效特征可能为失语综合征及神经退行性疾病提供新的计算视角。值得注意的是，Transformer在注意力头消融初期仍保持较高准确率，这在定性上类似于阿尔茨海默病临床症状出现前（Gomez-Isla等，1996年报告40–60%神经元损失时）的无症状储备期，而LSTM更平稳的退化过程则与DeKosky和Scheff（1990）、Terry等（1991）描述的症状进展相吻合。
👤 Pratik Worah, Randall Bateman 📅 2026-10-20 🔗 arXiv

Temporal Evidence-Parameter Co-Alignment for LLM Reasoning over Temporal Knowledge Graphs
面向时间知识图谱的LLM推理中时间证据-参数协同对齐
Large Language Models (LLMs) often struggle with temporally grounded reasoning because their static parametric memory is misaligned with the dynamic evolution of real-world knowledge. Although RAG and Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning (PEFT) provide practical ways to enhance LLMs, they remain insufficient for temporal reasoning. Existing retrieval methods typically prioritize semantic relevance while overlooking temporal validity, event order, and evolutionary dependencies, resulting in evidence that is relevant in isolation but temporally fragmented. Meanwhile, conventional PEFT methods usually employ fixed and input-agnostic low-rank updates, making it difficult to capture time-dependent entity semantics and relation drift. To address these limitations, we propose Temporal Evidence-Parameter Co-Alignment (TEPCA), a framework for LLM reasoning over TKGs. TEPCA aligns LLM reasoning with temporal knowledge at both the evidence and parameter levels. At the evidence level, it introduces time-constrained Dynamic Relevance-Guided Path (DRGP) pruning to preserve globally coherent temporal paths, together with Mutual-Information (MI)-based pruning to retain query-informative local facts under temporal constraints. At the parameter level, it integrates Dynamic-Gated LoRA (DG-LoRA), a timestamp-conditioned low-rank adaptation mechanism that dynamically modulates the effective adaptation subspace according to temporal context. This design enables LLMs to better capture fine-grained entity evolution and time-dependent relation semantics while preserving parameter efficiency. Experimental results on two TKG benchmarks demonstrate that TEPCA achieves state-of-the-art performance. Additional evaluations of DG-LoRA on eight commonsense QA datasets further verify its general input-adaptive capacity, scalability across LLM backbones, and interpretability.
中文翻译
大型语言模型（LLMs）在时间锚定推理方面常遇困难，因其静态参数记忆与现实世界知识的动态演变不匹配。尽管检索增强生成（RAG）和参数高效微调（PEFT）提供了实用的增强方法，但在时间推理方面仍显不足。现有检索方法通常优先考虑语义相关性，忽视时间有效性、事件顺序和演化依赖关系，导致证据虽孤立相关但时间上碎片化。传统PEFT方法通常采用固定且输入无关的低秩更新，难以捕捉随时间变化的实体语义与关系漂移。为此，我们提出时间证据-参数协同对齐（TEPCA）框架，用于LLM在时间知识图谱（TKG）上的推理。TEPCA在证据与参数层面均实现与时间知识的对齐：在证据层面，引入时间约束的动态相关性引导路径（DRGP）剪枝以保留全局一致的时间路径，并结合基于互信息（MI）的剪枝以在时间约束下保留查询相关信息的局部事实；在参数层面，集成时间戳条件化的动态门控LoRA（DG-LoRA），一种根据时间上下文动态调节有效适配子空间的低秩适应机制。该设计使LLM能更好地捕捉细粒度实体演化和时间依赖的关系语义，同时保持参数效率。在两个TKG基准上的实验结果表明，TEPCA达到当前最优性能。对DG-LoRA在八个常识问答数据集上的额外评估进一步验证了其通用输入自适应能力、跨LLM骨干的可扩展性及可解释性。

Reading Too Much Into It? Interpretive Preferences and Commitment in LLM Mentalisation under Ambiguity
读得太多？LLM在模糊情境下的诠释偏好与心理承诺
Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used for interpersonal advice, potentially shaping how users interpret ambiguous social encounters. Existing Theory of Mind evaluations typically rely on predefined answers, while uncertainty research overlooks LLMs' preferred explanations and confidence under psychological distress. To address these gaps, we introduce MentalLoom, a novel evaluation of LLM mentalisation comprising 604 ambiguous social episodes, matched depression and social anxiety disclosures, and four-turn dialogues with unchanged event evidence. Across eight LLMs, disclosures initially elicited more negative explanations overall but more positive narrator-directed interpretations. Scripted repeated questioning showed greater emotional support, stronger positive narrator-directed endorsement, and higher certainty without new event evidence. These findings show that user context can reshape interpretive preferences and commitment while the underlying ambiguity remains unresolved.
中文翻译
大型语言模型（LLMs）越来越多地被用于人际建议，可能影响用户对模糊社交情境的解读。现有心智理论评估通常依赖预设答案，而不确定性研究则忽略了LLMs在心理压力下的首选解释与置信度。为填补这些空白，我们引入MentalLoom，一种新型LLM心智化评估，包含604个模糊社交事件、匹配的抑郁与社交焦虑披露，以及四轮对话且事件证据不变的情境。在八种LLMs中，披露初始引发整体更多负面解释，但更多正向指向叙述者的解读。脚本化重复提问显示更强情感支持、更强烈的正向叙述者认同及更高置信度，且无需新事件证据。这些发现表明，用户背景可重塑诠释偏好与承诺程度，而底层模糊性仍未解决。

BAIT: Can LLMs Find What Biases a Reader? A Benchmark for Cognitive Bias Triggers in News
BAIT：LLM能否发现读者的偏见？新闻中认知偏见触发因素的基准测试
Persuasive writing exploits cognitive biases, recruiting a reader's mental shortcuts to shape belief, often without the reader noticing. We call the linguistic features exploiting such mental shortcuts cognitive bias triggers. As large language models (LLMs) increasingly mediate how people encounter news, summarizing it, ranking it, and answering questions over it, a natural question is whether they can recognize the features of a text that bias a human reader. We introduce BAIT (Bias Annotation In Text), a resource for detecting cognitive bias triggers in news. BAIT pairs a schema of nine triggers, each specified in an annotator codebook, with 50 English news articles annotated by trained annotators at the article and span levels and adjudicated into a gold standard. On this data we define two tasks, detecting which triggers a text contains and locating the spans that carry them. We then benchmark six frontier LLMs zero-shot and with three or ten in-context examples. The models approach human agreement on which triggers an article contains, but rarely recover its full set of triggers, and they reach less than half of human agreement in locating the passages that carry them. Moreover, they over-predict triggers that come with easy surface cues, and most of the passages they mark for these triggers are not triggers, yet carry the trigger's surface cue far more often than random text. These findings suggest that the evaluated LLMs respond to surface cues rather than to the mechanisms that make a passage manipulative. We release the schema, the codebook, standoff annotations, and the evaluation pipeline.
中文翻译
说服性写作利用认知偏见，借助读者的心理捷径来塑造信念，往往在读者未察觉的情况下发生。我们将利用此类心理捷径的语言特征称为认知偏见触发因素。随着大型语言模型（LLMs）越来越多地介入人们接触新闻的过程——包括摘要生成、排序与问答——一个自然问题是：它们能否识别出文本中影响人类读者的偏见特征？我们引入BAIT（Bias Annotation In Text），一个用于检测新闻中认知偏见触发因素的资源。BAIT将九种触发因素的模式（每种在标注员手册中有明确定义）与50篇英文新闻文章的标注数据配对，标注在文章与片段层面，并经仲裁形成黄金标准。在此数据上，我们定义两项任务：检测文本包含哪些触发因素，以及定位携带这些触发因素的片段。随后，我们对六种前沿LLMs进行了零样本及三或十个上下文示例的基准测试。模型在判断文本包含哪些触发因素上接近人类一致性，但很少能恢复全部触发因素，且在定位携带触发因素的段落时，一致性低于人类的一半。此外，它们倾向于过度预测带有明显表面线索的触发因素，且大多数被标记的段落并非真正的触发因素，但其表面线索出现频率远高于随机文本。这些发现表明，所评估的LLMs更关注表面线索而非使段落具有操纵性的内在机制。我们已发布该触发因素模式、标注手册、独立标注文件与评估流程。
👤 Yousra Fettach, Tijl De Bie 📅 2026-10-20 🔗 arXiv

Adaptive Stopping of Streamed Reasoning for Language Model Agents
面向语言模型智能体的流式推理自适应停止
Reasoning-enabled language model agents can incur substantial computation before acting, although the reasoning needed varies across decisions. Existing stopping signals, such as confidence and intermediate-answer stability, can vary in effectiveness across reasoning models; signals obtained from hidden states or token probabilities also require internal access that may be unavailable or impractical. We introduce StreamStop, which learns model-specific stopping policies offline from prefix-level action agreement. A frozen prefix reader encodes available reasoning text for a lightweight stop head, allowing stopping without access to the reasoning model's internal signals. We construct and publicly release a dataset of agent reasoning trajectories with prefix-level action annotations. These annotations compare each prefix action with the full-reasoning reference action, enabling offline comparison of stopping rules and exact expected-return optimization of a discounted action-agreement objective. Conditioning the head on the discount factor allows the agreement-token trade-off to be adjusted at inference without retraining. In closed-loop evaluations on WebShop and ScienceWorld, StreamStop achieves higher observed success than most validation-selected fixed-budget settings while using fewer reasoning tokens than Full thinking. On WebShop with Qwen3.8-27B, it uses 42% fewer reasoning tokens per episode with similar observed task success.
中文翻译
具备推理能力的语言模型智能体在行动前可能消耗大量计算资源，但不同决策所需的推理量各异。现有停止信号（如置信度和中间答案稳定性）在不同推理模型中的有效性不一；而来自隐藏状态或词元概率的信号又需要访问内部状态，可能不可用或不切实际。我们提出StreamStop，一种离线学习模型特定停止策略的方法，基于前缀级动作一致性。一个冻结的前缀阅读器编码可用的推理文本，由轻量级停止头处理，从而无需访问推理模型的内部信号即可实现停止。我们构建并公开发布了一个带有前缀级动作标注的智能体推理轨迹数据集。这些标注将每个前缀动作与完整推理参考动作进行比较，支持停止规则的离线对比以及折扣动作一致性目标的精确期望回报优化。通过将停止头条件化于折扣因子，可在推理阶段调整同意-令牌权衡而无需重新训练。在WebShop和ScienceWorld的闭环评估中，StreamStop在使用更少推理词元的情况下，实现了高于多数验证选择的固定预算设置的成功率。在Qwen3.8-27B上，WebShop任务中每轮推理词元减少42%，同时保持相似的观察成功率。

Are Linguistic and Arithmetic Circuits Dissociated in LLMs?
语言与算术电路在大语言模型中是否分离？
Large language models (LLMs) exhibit both knowledge of language, rendered by, for example, grammatical judgment ability, and abilities to perform tasks through language, such as math-word problem solving. Motivated by neurolinguistic studies of their neural dissociation in human brains, in this study, we investigate the overlap of their circuits in large language models (LLMs). Specifically, we examine the overlap and causal effects between the circuits extracted for linguistic and arithmetic tasks, using controlled materials and circuit discovery methods from the mechanistic interpretability field. Our experiments with five LLMs show that circuit similarity is mostly explained by task domains (linguistic vs. arithmetic), rather than by their input and output vocabularies or by the syntactic similarities between natural-language sentences and arithmetic expressions. This finding suggests that LLMs may process linguistic and arithmetic information using largely separate neural circuits, highlighting an ability-based modular organization of circuits within these models.
中文翻译
大语言模型（LLMs）既表现出语言知识（如语法判断能力），也具备通过语言完成任务的能力（如数学文字题求解）。受人类大脑神经语言学研究中二者神经分离现象的启发，本研究探讨大语言模型中语言与算术任务电路之间的重叠情况。具体而言，我们使用受控材料和机制可解释性领域的电路发现方法，考察语言任务与算术任务提取出的电路之间的重叠与因果效应。对五种LLMs的实验表明，电路相似性主要由任务领域（语言 vs. 算术）决定，而非输入输出词汇表或自然语言句子与算术表达式之间的句法相似性。这一发现表明，LLMs可能使用基本分离的神经电路处理语言与算术信息，凸显了这些模型内部以能力为基础的模块化电路组织结构。

Beyond Calibration: Temporal LLM Confidence Assessment Across Languages and Local Settings
超越校准：跨语言与本地场景下的大语言模型时间性置信度评估
Large language models are expected to estimate their own uncertainty, with confidence signals used to guide retrieval, adaptive compute allocation, routing, and selective abstention. We investigate whether confidence signals measured at different temporal stages of problem solving encode distinct forms of epistemic information and remain reliable beyond English-centric settings. Inspired by classical meta-memory theory, we distinguish anticipatory pre-solve confidence (Feeling-of-Knowing, FOK) from retrospective post-solve confidence (Judgement-of-Learning, JOL). We evaluate these signals across two frontier LLMs, multiple reasoning domains, translated Marathi benchmarks, and a newly constructed benchmark of genuinely local Marathi factual questions. Across settings, the cheaper pre-solve signal remains weakly calibrated, while post-solve confidence generally provides stronger epistemic alignment despite its additional inference cost. Confidence behaviour transfers surprisingly well under direct language translation, with calibration trends largely preserved between English and Marathi, but degrades substantially on local Marathi knowledge, suggesting that linguistic transfer alone is insufficient for region-specific information needs. Finally, hallucinated responses maintain persistently elevated confidence after answer generation, highlighting important limitations of current LLM self-assessment mechanisms.

大语言模型应能估计自身不确定性，其置信信号用于指导检索、自适应计算分配、路由和选择性回避。我们研究在问题求解不同时间阶段测量的置信信号是否编码了不同的认识论信息，并在非英语中心设置下是否仍保持可靠。受经典元记忆理论启发，我们区分了解题前的预期置信度（知道感，FOK）与解题后的回顾置信度（学习判断，JOL）。我们在两个前沿LLM、多个推理领域、翻译后的马拉地语基准以及新构建的真实本地马拉地语事实问题基准上评估这些信号。在各种设置下，成本较低的解题前信号仍弱校准，而解题后置信度尽管有额外推理开销，通常提供更强的认识论一致性。置信行为在直接语言翻译下表现出意外的良好迁移性，英马两国校准趋势基本保留，但在本地马拉地语知识上显著退化，表明仅靠语言迁移不足以满足区域特定信息需求。最后，幻觉回答在生成后仍维持高置信度，凸显当前LLM自我评估机制的重要局限。

Memory as Code: Declarative Retrieval over Codified Long-Term Memory
记忆即代码：基于编码化长期记忆的声明式检索
In long-term memory systems, memories are constructed from interaction histories and retrieved in response to queries, enhancing the reasoning ability of LLM agents. In complex scenarios, facts are scattered across sessions and their relationships evolve over time, making it difficult for existing memory systems to compose them to adapt to diverse queries. Motivated by the modularity and composability of code, we propose Memory as Code (MaC), a unified memory framework that jointly designs memory construction and retrieval through a shared code abstraction. MaC couples codified construction, which establishes executable memory abstractions, with declarative retrieval, which translates queries into structured retrieval programs. Specifically, codified construction builds a three-layer executable memory structure that progresses from an atomic function layer, through a relational layer, to a modular composition layer. Declarative retrieval recasts retrieval from relevance-based fact selection into declarative query processing over memory. Experiments on LoCoMo and LongMemEval show relative gains of up to 15.91% in overall LLM-judge accuracy over the strongest baseline in each setting, while substantially reducing token consumption, highlighting the effectiveness of memory as code for long-horizon memory tasks.
中文翻译

WORTH Benchmark: Evidence Discovery under Not-Fully-Cooperative Information -- A Two-Episode Pilot
WORTH基准：非完全合作信息下的证据发现——两集先导实验
Existing reasoning evaluations typically provide task-relevant information to models as available premises, rarely examining concurrently how models discover evidence autonomously and how information presentation within narratives guides or obscures judgment. This paper proposes the WORTH Benchmark: models read original mystery narratives without clue annotations, autonomously select key clue sentences, identify case participants, and explain their reasoning process. The narrative materials are designed by professional authors such that evidence facilitating reasoning coexists with narrative arrangements that delay the revelation of truth. We report a pilot experiment under full-story conditions across two episodes, conducting a total of 70 formal runs on models, of which 67 runs are content-scorable. Scoring separately records hits on author-designated key clue sentences and participant selections; natural language explanations are used for case diagnosis and are not scored independently. The results show that clue hits and participant judgments are not synchronized; across 37 content-scorable runs in Episode 02, 31 runs simultaneously selected the same set of incorrect participants, while adjacent suspicion-oriented and exoneration-oriented sentences were selected 25 times and 0 times, respectively. These phenomena illustrate the diagnostic value of component-wise analysis and suggest that not-fully-cooperative informational relationships may influence evidence selection and judgment. This study only implements full-story conditions for two episodes, and does not establish stable model rankings or infer general mechanisms on this basis; human player records have not yet been cleaned, and this version does not report human-model comparisons.
中文翻译
现有的推理评估通常向模型提供任务相关的信息作为前提，很少考察模型如何自主发现证据，以及叙事中信息呈现方式如何引导或掩盖判断。本文提出了WORTH基准：模型阅读原始神秘叙事，不带线索标注，自主选择关键线索句子，识别案件参与者，并解释其推理过程。叙事材料由专业作者设计，使得有助于推理的证据与延迟真相揭示的叙事安排共存。我们报告了在两集故事中全篇条件下的先导实验，共进行了70次正式运行，其中67次可进行内容评分。评分分别记录作者指定的关键线索句子命中情况和参与者选择；自然语言解释用于案件诊断，不独立评分。结果显示，线索命中与参与者判断不同步；在第二集的37次可评分运行中，31次同时选择了同一组错误参与者，而相邻的怀疑导向句被选中25次，排除导向句则0次。这些现象说明了分组件分析的诊断价值，并表明非完全合作的信息关系可能影响证据选择与判断。本研究仅对两集实施了全篇条件，尚未建立稳定的模型排名或推断普遍机制；人类玩家记录尚未清理，此版本未报告人机对比结果。

Factual Access States in Diffusion Language Models: From Mechanism to Knowledge Editing and Retrieval
扩散语言模型中的事实访问状态：从机制到知识编辑与检索
Factual recall is a lookup: some representation inside the network serves as the key, and what that key is made of decides which queries can retrieve the fact, what an edit must cover, and what a retriever can read. We call it the factual access state. In autoregressive (AR) models the subject position enriches attributes largely independently of the relation, and relation-specific selection happens later, at the readout position. In masked diffusion language models (DLMs) we find the state is assembled differently, and much earlier: in the first attention layer the wording of the relation is routed into the position of whichever entity is visible and the early feed-forward layers look the fact up on this fused representation. The state therefore varies with the relation's surface form and with the query direction, so the queries about one fact occupy a region of state space rather than a point, and an operation on the fact succeeds only for queries that enter it. We treat the state as an interface to a DLM's factual knowledge, and put it to two uses. Writing to the state gives a knowledge editor: from one declarative statement, Multi-View Edit fits one shared value per direction across that region and installs it in the early-MLP block, preserving source-prompt reliability while substantially improving paraphrase and reverse question answering, where AR editors gain near zero. Reading it suggests a complementary use: the state at each entity position can serve as a relation-specific retrieval vector with no training, returning both pieces of evidence for two-entity queries on which pooled, prompted and lexical retrievers almost never return both.
中文翻译
事实回忆本质上是一种查找：网络内部的某种表示充当关键，而该关键的构成决定了哪些查询可检索到事实、修改操作需覆盖范围，以及检索器可读取的内容。我们称之为‘事实访问状态’。在自回归（AR）模型中，主语位置的属性大多独立于关系，关系特异性选择发生在读出位置。而在掩码扩散语言模型（DLMs）中，我们发现该状态以不同方式组装，且更早形成：在第一层注意力中，关系的表述被路由至当前可见实体的位置，早期前馈层即在此融合表示上查找事实。因此，该状态随关系的表面形式和查询方向而变化，同一事实的查询占据状态空间的一个区域而非单一节点，只有进入该区域的查询才能成功触发操作。我们将该状态视为DLM事实知识的接口，并应用于两个用途。写入该状态可实现知识编辑：从一个陈述出发，多视角编辑在该区域中为每个方向拟合共享值，并将其安装在早期MLP块中，既保留源提示可靠性，又显著提升改写和反向问答表现（而AR编辑器几乎无增益）。读取该状态则提供互补用途：每个实体位置的状态可作为无需训练的关系特定检索向量，对双实体查询返回两部分证据，而池化、提示和词法检索器几乎从未能同时返回两者。
👤 Yuchen Guo, Zhuoyuan Mao, Yiwen Guo 📅 2026-10-20 🔗 arXiv
[2610.27566]

Learned Readout Tokens for Extracting Large Language Model Confidence
用于提取大语言模型置信度的可学习读出标记
Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used within everyday applications where their responses have significant downstream impacts. For responsible deployment of such systems, we desire insight into how much we can trust their individual outputs. A popular approach for estimating LLM confidence is to train probes on the hidden states of the LLM's tokens to predict the correctness of the model's output. These probes typically read hidden states from tokens that are already in the model's vocabulary. In this work, we introduce a new approach to confidence probes that learn a new readout token and utilize its hidden states to train the probe. We show that our learned readout approach ScratchProbe outperforms its existing-token counterpart on a variety of datasets and LLMs. We also extend our method to a multi-token approach which we show provides further performance improvements. Finally, since the learned readout changes only the position a probe reads from, any existing probe can adopt it. Applied to a state-of-the-art probe, it improves AUROC on every backbone, in domain and out of domain.
中文翻译
大语言模型（LLMs）在日常应用中日益普及，其输出对下游任务有重大影响。为了负责任地部署此类系统，我们需要了解能够信任其单个输出的程度。目前一种流行的估计LLM置信度的方法是训练探测器（probes）来预测模型输出的正确性，这些探测器通常读取模型词汇表中已有token的隐藏状态。本文提出一种新的置信度探测方法，通过学习一个新的读出token并利用其隐藏状态来训练探测器。我们证明，所提出的ScratchProbe方法在多种数据集和LLM上均优于基于现有token的探测器。我们还将该方法扩展为多token版本，进一步提升性能。此外，由于学习到的读出仅改变探测器读取的位置，任何现有探测器均可采用此方法。将其应用于最先进的探测器后，在所有骨干模型上均提升了AUROC表现，无论是在域内还是域外。

Exploring Recurrent-Depth Language Models for Multitask Clinical Text Processing
探索循环深度语言模型在多任务临床文本处理中的应用
Clinical natural language processing includes diverse tasks such as information extraction, classification, and question answering. Developing one model for all tasks could simplify deployment and improve knowledge transfer. We evaluated whether small recurrent-depth language models can meet this need through joint fine-tuning and compared their performance with standard transformers. We fine-tuned Ouro1.4B-Thinking, Ouro-2.6B-Thinking, HRM-Text-1B, Qwen3-1.7B, and Qwen3-8B on 9 datasets covering information extraction, classification, inference, and question answering, and evaluated them on four additional datasets excluded from training. 👤 Xi Yang, Guodong Liu, Cheng Peng, Kaleb Smith, Yingying Zhang et al. 📅 2026-10-20 🔗 arXiv

EviDial: When Correct Depression Predictions Lack Evidence
当正确的抑郁症预测缺乏证据时：EviDial基准测试
Depression dialogue benchmarks commonly assess models by comparing predicted symptom labels with clinical scale scores. However, a prediction may agree with a benchmark label even when the dialogue lacks enough evidence to justify it. We introduce EviDial, a benchmark that distinguishes label agreement from evidence-grounded assessment. EviDial comprises 382 depression dialogues from three sources and 2,744 participant-symptom cells, each classified as having absent, partial, conflicting, or sufficient evidence. Because evidence sufficiency lacks a directly observable gold standard, we derive calibrated evidence references from multiple judgments using generalizability theory, many-facet Rasch measurement, and random-effects latent class analysis. Our audit reveals that 82.8% of participant-symptom cells lack sufficient textual evidence for the corresponding symptom assessment, with substantial variation across sources. Across model families and prompting strategies, 69.2%-96.9% of predictions judged correct by benchmark labels are nonetheless unsupported by sufficient dialogue evidence. Further analyses show that strong agreement among LLM evaluators need not reflect independent evidence judgments, underscoring the need to calibrate automated reference panels. These findings demonstrate that label-based evaluation can overestimate evidence-grounded performance by conflating agreement with clinical labels and support in the observed dialogue. EviDial provides a measurement layer for separating these constructs and auditing LLM-based depression assessment more faithfully.
中文翻译
抑郁症对话基准通常通过比较预测症状标签与临床量表评分来评估模型。然而，即使对话中缺乏足够证据支持，预测也可能与基准标签一致。我们引入EviDial，一个区分标签一致性和基于证据评估的基准。EviDial包含来自三个来源的382个抑郁症对话和2,744个参与者-症状单元，每个单元被分类为无证据、部分证据、冲突证据或充分证据。由于证据充分性缺乏直接可观测的黄金标准，我们通过广义性理论、多面Rasch测量和随机效应潜在类别分析，从多方判断中推导出校准后的证据参考。我们的审计发现，82.8%的参与者-症状单元缺乏足够的文本证据支持相应症状评估，且不同来源间差异显著。在不同模型家族和提示策略下，69.2%-96.9%被基准标签判定为正确的预测实际上并未得到对话中充分证据的支持。进一步分析表明，LLM评估者之间强一致性并不一定反映独立的证据判断，凸显了校准自动化参考面板的必要性。这些发现表明，基于标签的评估可能因混淆标签一致性和对话中实际支持而高估基于证据的表现。EviDial提供了一个测量层，用于分离这两个概念，并更真实地审计基于LLM的抑郁症评估。
