Judging the Justification: Decision Certificate Supervision for Generative Reward Models
判断理由：生成奖励模型的决策证书监督
Generative reward models (GenRMs) generate natural language rationales to justify preference judgments, yet a correct verdict does not guarantee a sound justification. Existing approaches often rely on reference explanations to supervise rationales, but obtaining such references can be costly and does not necessarily establish whether generated rationales support the resulting verdict. To address these limitations, we propose DeCert, a framework that evaluates the validity and verdict consistency of generated rationales without requiring reference explanations. Crucially, DeCert translates these assessments into preference supervision that accounts for both verdict correctness and justification quality. In particular, when multiple sampled judgments share the same correct verdict, differences in certificate quality enable DeCert to construct rationale preference pairs that distinguish stronger from weaker justifications. Extensive empirical studies across two model families and multiple in- and out-of-distribution benchmarks demonstrate that DeCert consistently outperforms strong generative judge baselines while improving rationale quality. Moreover, DeCert effectively mitigates positional bias, improving position-consistent accuracy by up to 14.8 points. These results highlight the importance of supervising not only which verdict is correct, but also how that verdict is justified.

生成式奖励模型（GenRMs）生成自然语言理由以解释偏好判断，但正确的结论并不保证理由合理。现有方法通常依赖参考解释来监督理由生成，但获取这些参考成本高昂，且不一定能确认生成的理由是否真正支持最终结论。为解决这些问题，我们提出DeCert框架，无需参考解释即可评估生成理由的有效性和结论一致性。关键在于，DeCert将这些评估转化为偏好监督信号，同时考虑结论正确性和理由质量。具体而言，当多个采样判断具有相同正确结论时，证书质量的差异使DeCert能够构建区分更强与较弱理由的理由偏好对。在两个模型家族及多个分布内和分布外基准上的大量实证研究表明，DeCert始终优于强大的生成裁判基线，同时提升了理由质量。此外，DeCert有效缓解了位置偏差，使位置一致性准确率最高提升14.8个百分点。这些结果强调了不仅要监督结论是否正确，更要监督结论是如何被合理论证的。

The Router Already Knows
路由器早已知晓
Mixture-of-experts (MoE) models already make a sparse choice for every token: which experts should process it. We ask whether a sparse dictionary should reuse that choice. NR-SAE gives each routed expert a small dictionary and opens only those the frozen router selects. At block 15 of Gemma 4 26B-A4B, after ten million C4 training tokens and across three seeds, the expert-aligned autoencoder explains 0.518 of the variance in router-weighted expert outputs on a fixed evaluation capture. A rank-addressed control with the same parameters but sixteen times as many candidates reaches 0.481, a candidate-matched control 0.458, and an architecture-matched control with randomized expert identity 0.387. A transcoder variant also leads all three controls: 0.432, against 0.347, 0.371, and 0.118. Part of this advantage is architectural: each expert writes into a subspace of at most a quarter of the model width, so naming the expert names the subspace. How much it explains, we have not measured. At this layer and budget, the router already knows something a dictionary would otherwise have to learn. Our implementation did not train faster, and semantic interpretability, downstream loss, and causal faithfulness remain untested.

混合专家（MoE）模型已在每个标记上做出稀疏选择：由哪些专家处理该标记。我们探讨是否应复用这一选择建立稀疏字典。NR-SAE为每个路由专家分配一个小字典，并仅开启冻结路由器所选的那些。在Gemma 4 26B-A4B模型第15层，经过千万级C4训练样本及三次随机种子测试后，专家对齐的自编码器在固定评估采样中解释了路由器加权专家输出方差的0.518。具有相同参数但候选数为十六倍的秩地址控制组解释率为0.481，候选匹配控制组为0.458，架构匹配控制组（专家身份随机化）为0.387。一种变体译码器也优于三者：0.432 vs. 0.347、0.371 和 0.118。部分优势源于架构设计：每个专家仅写入至多四分之一模型宽度的子空间，因此命名专家即等价于命名子空间。其解释程度尚未测量。在此层与预算下，路由器已掌握原本需字典学习的信息。我们的实现未提升训练速度，语义可解释性、下游损失及因果忠实性仍待验证。

SHAM-Bench: A Span-level Hallucination Benchmark Across Multiple Tasks
SHAM-Bench：跨多个任务的跨度级幻觉基准
Span-level hallucination detection pinpoints unsupported content in LLM outputs, enabling actionable feedback and targeted mitigation. Existing evaluations for span-level hallucination detection methods are fragmented across tasks, annotation formats, and metrics, and most span-level datasets cover only a single NLG domain, making it difficult to assess whether a detection method actually generalizes across tasks and domains. In this paper, we introduce SHAM-Bench (Span-level Hallucination Benchmark Across Multiple Tasks), a unified benchmark for span-level hallucination detection spanning six NLG tasks, aggregated from existing human-annotated datasets totaling 6,461 examples under a common schema and evaluation protocol. Using SHAM-Bench, we evaluate existing hallucination detection methods and find that fine-tuned detectors often perform well in-domain but degrade under distribution shift, whereas prompt-based methods with reasoning are more robust across tasks. Our analysis further shows that subtle unsupported content remains difficult to detect and span-level evaluation is still challenged by ambiguous span boundaries and noisy labels. We release the dataset, evaluation code, and a public leaderboard to support future research on broadly generalizable hallucination detection.

On the LLM Verification for Argument Graph Generation
关于论点图生成中的大语言模型验证
Multi-agent LLM systems can formalize natural-language argumentation end-to-end, turning plain text into structured graphs by classifying components, connecting them with relations, and enriching the resulting graphs into formal models. Quality assurance for those graphs is almost always another LLMs: a reviewer, judge, or critic agent. On the other side, in adjacent neuro-symbolic fields, such generated output is often verified symbolically by external checks and solvers. In this work, we argue that in argument graph verification, the allocation should be similar, and based on what each instrument can guarantee. We propose layered verification: starting from deterministic checks, moving to solver-based and LLM reviews, and utilizing debate only under uncertainty, leaving human judgment for what no other instrument can decide. We ground our principle with a study on human-annotated graphs with injected structural defects. An LLM reviewer detects them at near-ceiling recall, including cycles in the support relation, but it objects to almost one in a ten correct graphs, even though it should not, happens to be inconsistent in verdict on identical inputs and is significantly slower than the equivalent exact check. It shows that while LLM reviewer does not lack the ability to detect structural defects, it is worth having a deterministic verification gates.
中文翻译
多智能体大语言模型可端到端形式化自然语言论证，将普通文本转化为结构化图，通过分类组件、用关系连接它们，并将生成的图丰富为形式模型。这些图的质量保障几乎总是由另一个大语言模型完成：评审员、裁判或批评者智能体。另一方面，在相邻的神经符号领域，此类生成输出通常通过外部检查和求解器进行符号化验证。本文主张，在论点图验证中，应根据每种工具所能保证的内容进行分配。我们提出分层验证机制：从确定性检查开始，逐步过渡到基于求解器的验证和大语言模型评审，仅在不确定时使用辩论，将人类判断留给其他工具无法决定的情况。我们通过研究人工标注并注入结构缺陷的图来验证这一原则。大语言模型评审者几乎以接近上限的召回率检测出结构缺陷（如支持关系中的循环），但它对约十分之一的正确图提出异议，尽管不应如此；其对相同输入的判决不一致，且显著慢于等效的精确检查。这表明，虽然大语言模型评审者具备检测结构缺陷的能力，但设置确定性验证门控仍具价值。

Evaluating Context Compaction on the Questions That Come Later
评估上下文压缩对后续问题的影响
Language-model agents compact their context for the question at hand, but the compacted context must also serve questions that come later. We evaluate compaction on these later questions, using four corpora that annotate the evidence of several questions per text and recorded agent trajectories, and compare every method with random deletion at the same budget. On MultiRC, keeping the sentences that gpt-6-luna (through the OpenAI Decisions API) judges necessary for the current question raises its accuracy on that question by 11.4 points at 25% of the tokens. On the later questions, however, no method that selects sentences for the current question does detectably better than deleting sentences at random. Deleting words instead of sentences does better: LLMLingua-2, which ignores the question, beats random sentence deletion at the same token count by 12.5 points on the later MultiRC questions. It keeps about twice random deletion's share of the evidence's content words, and at equal content-word retention its gain shrinks to 7.3 points (exploratory). On FEVER, label accuracy can overstate what the kept text supports: the gain of sentence selection is no longer detected when answers are scored against the kept evidence, and the 21.0-point gain of LLMLingua-2 is not shown to rest on the kept text. The ranking also depends on the setting: on Evidence Inference sentence selection beats LLMLingua-2 at a nominal 25% budget, and on $τ$-bench trajectories, whose later tool calls need exact identifiers, it beats random deletion while LLMLingua-2 shows no detected gain. Scores on the question a compaction was made for do not reliably show how it serves later ones.
中文翻译
语言模型智能体会为其当前问题压缩上下文，但压缩后的上下文也需服务于后续问题。我们评估了压缩对后续问题的表现，使用四个语料库，这些语料库为每篇文本标注了多个问题的证据并记录了智能体轨迹，并将每种方法与相同预算下的随机删除进行比较。在MultiRC上，保留gpt-6-luna（通过OpenAI Decisions API）判定为当前问题必要句子，使该问题准确率提升11.4个百分点，仅使用25%的词元。然而，在后续问题上，任何为当前问题选择句子的方法均未显著优于随机删除句子。相比之下，删除词语而非句子表现更好：LLMLingua-2（忽略问题）在相同词元数下比随机句子删除在后续MultiRC问题上高出12.5分。它保留了随机删除约两倍的内容词，而在内容词保留相同时，其增益缩小至7.3分（探索性分析）。在FEVER上，标签准确率可能夸大保留文本的支持能力：当答案基于保留证据评分时，句子选择带来的增益不再显著，LLMLingua-2的21.0分增益也无法证明源于保留文本。排名还依赖于设置：在Evidence Inference中，句子选择在名义25%预算下优于LLMLingua-2；在$τ$-bench轨迹中，后续工具调用需要精确标识符，句子选择优于随机删除，而LLMLingua-2未表现出可检测的增益。针对压缩所针对问题的得分并不能可靠反映其对后续问题的服务效果。

RealControlBench: A Benchmark for Interpretability-Informed Fine-Grained Control of LLMs and Agents
RealControlBench：面向可解释性启发的细粒度控制大语言模型与智能体的基准测试
Existing steering benchmarks are built around simplified, isolated generation tasks and evaluate a single behavioral attribute under controlled settings that do not reflect the diversity and complexity of practical deployment. As a result, performance on these benchmarks provides only a limited picture of how steering methods perform in the real world. We therefore introduce RealControlBench, a comprehensive benchmark for evaluating fine-grained steering of LLMs and LLM agents across diverse behavioral targets and interaction settings. RealControlBench extends steering evaluation beyond isolated generations to complex, execution-grounded, multi-turn agent interactions, covering five complementary dimensions: instruction-following control, agentic tool-use decision control, reasoning-performance steering, reasoning-length control, and trajectory-conditioned safety awareness. Rather than measuring steering success alone, the benchmark jointly evaluates target controllability and capability preservation, including instruction following, reasoning performance, and downstream agent utility and safety. It further examines whether steering effects remain specific and robust across prompt variations, domains, and model architectures. Our evaluation shows that existing steering methods remain unreliable across realistic control objectives: they often fail to consistently induce the intended behavior and can substantially degrade the model's underlying task performance. These results suggest that strong steering effects in controlled settings do not necessarily translate into reliable control in more realistic evaluations. By providing a unified, model-agnostic steering evaluation framework, RealControlBench aims to support the development of steering methods that are not only effective but also precise, robust, and useful in realistic AI deployments.
中文翻译
现有的控制基准测试围绕简化、孤立的生成任务构建，评估单一行为属性在受控环境下的表现，无法反映实际部署中的多样性和复杂性。因此，这些基准上的性能仅能提供一个有限的图景。为此，我们引入RealControlBench，一个全面的基准测试，用于评估大语言模型和大语言模型智能体在多样化行为目标和交互场景下的细粒度控制。RealControlBench将控制评估从孤立生成扩展到复杂的、基于执行的、多轮智能体交互，涵盖五个互补维度：指令遵循控制、智能体工具使用决策控制、推理性能调节、推理长度控制以及轨迹条件下的安全意识。该基准不仅衡量控制成功，还联合评估目标可控性与能力保留性，包括指令遵循、推理性能以及下游智能体效用与安全性。此外，它还考察控制效果是否在提示变化、领域和模型架构间保持特异性和鲁棒性。我们的评估显示，现有控制方法在真实控制目标下仍不可靠：它们往往无法持续诱导预期行为，且可能显著降低模型的基础任务性能。这些结果表明，受控环境中强大的控制效果并不必然转化为更现实评估中的可靠控制。通过提供统一、模型无关的控制评估框架，RealControlBench旨在推动开发不仅有效，而且精确、鲁棒、适用于真实AI部署的控制方法。

MMedAgent-Plus: Scaling Multimodal Medical Agents across Modalities, Tasks and Workflows

Medical workflows often require multiple interdependent analyses, but existing specialist and generalist AI models are not designed to coordinate them. We introduce MMedAgent-Plus, a multimodal medical tool-use agent that combines a vision-language planner with 18 leading specialist models spanning 12 tasks and 8 modalities. Its final-answer accuracy reaches 0.98, matching the dedicated leading specialist and exceeding the strongest baseline (0.28).

On the Fragility of Tasks under Composition: Implicit Budgeting in Language Models
任务组合下的脆弱性：语言模型中的隐式思维预算
Modern language models are increasingly asked to handle several tasks within a single prompt. Doing so requires solving each task correctly while integrating the resulting answers into a coherent response. In this paper, we identify a fundamental limitation in this process, where models make substantially more errors when tasks are composed, even when they can solve every constituent task reliably in isolation. This degradation cannot be fully explained by the accumulation of independent errors. Across both open-weight models and the frontier model Claude Opus 5, our findings instead point to an implicit thinking budget underlying this effect. In particular, under composition, models use substantially fewer tokens per task than in isolation, even when the full sequence remains well within the context window. Tasks that require longer solution traces in isolation are consequently more $\textit{fragile}$ under composition, i.e., models make more errors on prompts containing these tasks. In isolation, non-fragile tasks receive more reasoning than they need and waste compute. However, by grouping tasks according to their fragility, the implicit budgeting can be manipulated to reduce token usage by $43$\% across a range of math and linguistic tasks while also preserving accuracy.
中文翻译
现代语言模型越来越被要求在一个提示中处理多个任务。这需要正确解决每个任务，并将结果整合成连贯的回答。本文揭示了这一过程中的一个根本性局限：当任务组合时，模型犯错显著增多，即使它们在孤立状态下能可靠地解决每个子任务。这种性能下降无法完全由独立错误累积解释。在开放权重模型和前沿模型Claude Opus 5中，我们的发现指向一种隐式的思维预算机制。具体而言，在任务组合时，模型每任务使用的token数量显著减少，即使整个序列仍在上下文窗口范围内。那些在孤立状态下需要更长推理路径的任务在组合下更为“脆弱”，即在包含这些任务的提示中出错更多。在孤立状态下，非脆弱任务会使用超出所需量的推理资源，造成计算浪费。然而，通过按脆弱性分组任务，可以操纵这种隐式预算，在一系列数学和语言任务中将token使用量降低43%，同时保持准确率。
👤 Chenpan Li, Thies Brockmoeller, Stefanie Jegelka, Sharut Gupta, Nikolaos Karalias 📅 2026-10-19 🔗 arXiv
[2610.27149]

GRID: Modeling Health Influencer Discourse as Argument Networks for Credibility Assessment
GRID：将健康影响者话语建模为论证网络以进行可信度评估
Health-related discussions on social media are increasingly shaped by influencers who offer diverse, and often competing perspectives on the same issue. Yet, such content is typically evaluated in isolation, without considering the broader discourse in which it appears. We introduce GRID (Grounded Reasoning over Interconnected Discourse), a framework for evaluating influencer content through collective inference over the surrounding discourse. GRID represents each video as an argumentation graph connecting claims to its central message, or takeaway, links related claims and takeaways across videos, grounds claims in scientific evidence, and jointly infers credibility over the resulting network. We evaluate GRID on an end-to-end health-related content validation task using 569 influencer videos grounded in expert assessments. Our results show that accounting for the broader discourse improves influencer content validation. Finally, we use GRID-inferred credibility to demonstrate how argumentative strategies and moral framing differ between low- and high-credibility content.

Joint Constraint Aggregation for Qualifier-Sensitive Retrieval-Augmented QA
限定词敏感的检索增强型问答中的联合约束聚合
Retrieval-augmented QA can fail even when the correct answer is in the retrieved evidence. This can happen when a competing answer satisfies some, but not all, of the question's requirements and is mentioned frequently in the retrieved documents, which can make it appear to be the best-supported answer. To test whether this retrieval skew actually affects the decision, we manipulate it directly: increasing the prevalence of passages that favor the competing answer makes the model more likely to select it. This points to a decision problem that candidate-level support alone cannot resolve. We therefore introduce Joint Constraint Aggregation (JCA), which compares candidates against the same question-derived requirements using the retrieved evidence. Across three evaluation settings and three instruction-tuned backbones, JCA improves overall accuracy, and its advantage grows when retrieval favors a competing answer. Together, these results suggest that when partial matches and retrieval prominence pull models toward a competing answer, comparing candidates against the question's full requirements can make selection more reliable.
中文翻译
即使正确答案存在于检索到的证据中，检索增强型问答仍可能失败。这种情况可能发生在竞争性答案满足部分但非全部问题要求，并且在检索文档中频繁出现时，使其看起来像是最有力支持的答案。为了测试这种检索偏差是否确实影响决策，我们直接操纵该偏差：增加有利于竞争性答案的段落的出现频率，会使模型更倾向于选择该答案。这表明仅依靠候选答案级别的支持无法解决这一决策问题。因此，我们提出联合约束聚合（JCA），通过检索到的证据将候选答案与同一问题导出的要求进行比较。在三个评估设置和三种指令调优的骨干模型上，JCA均提升了整体准确率，且当检索偏向于竞争性答案时，其优势更加明显。这些结果表明，当部分匹配和检索显著性使模型倾向竞争性答案时，将候选答案与问题的完整要求进行比较可使选择更可靠。

Discrete Neural Interlingua for Ontology-Agnostic EHR Modeling
用于本体无关电子健康记录建模的离散神经中间语
EHR foundation models promise scalable clinical prediction across health systems, yet their deployment is constrained by a tokenization bottleneck. The same clinical concept can appear as different ontology codes, local identifiers, synonyms, or free-text descriptions, which standard EHR tokenizers treat as unrelated symbols. A model trained on MIMIC-III with a closed-vocabulary tokenizer can use only 13% of eICU events. We introduce MEDIATOR, a discrete ontology-agnostic tokenizer that maps any clinical concept expressed as text into a shared vector-quantized codebook, turning EHR harmonization into open-vocabulary tokenization without manual mappings or a fixed vocabulary. Trained with synonym supervision and guided by enriched concept descriptions, code co-occurrence, and relation graphs, MEDIATOR collapses equivalent expressions while organizing distinct concepts in a medically meaningful discrete space. Used as the token interface of an EHR Transformer, MEDIATOR improves mean AUPRC over the strongest prior tokenizer by 4.0 points (8% relative) on 20 MIMIC-III tasks and by 8.6 points (29% relative) in zero-shot transfer to 14 eICU tasks, where it uses all events. The transfer gain combines coverage and representation, as MEDIATOR remains the best tokenizer under identical inputs (+1.0 and +1.9 points on operational and subcohort-mortality tasks). Same-encoder controls show that the text encoder drives in-domain accuracy, while the learned objectives of MEDIATOR drive transfer robustness and entity-linking quality. MEDIATOR also enables transferable attribution across hospitals.

电子健康记录（EHR）基础模型有望实现跨医疗系统的可扩展临床预测，但其部署受限于分词瓶颈。同一临床概念可能表现为不同的本体编码、本地标识符、同义词或自由文本描述，标准EHR分词器将其视为无关符号。在一个封闭词汇表分词器上训练的MIMIC-III模型只能使用13%的eICU事件。我们提出MEDIATOR，一种离散的本体无关分词器，能将任何以文本表达的临床概念映射到共享的向量量化代码簿中，将EHR标准化转化为无需手动映射或固定词汇表的开放词汇分词。通过同义词监督、丰富概念描述、代码共现和关系图引导训练，MEDIATOR压缩等价表达并组织不同概念于具有医学意义的离散空间中。作为EHR Transformer的分词接口，MEDIATOR在20个MIMIC-III任务上比最强的先前分词器提升平均AUPRC 4.0点（相对提升8%），在14个eICU任务的零样本迁移中提升8.6点（相对提升29%），并使用了所有事件。迁移增益结合了覆盖率和表示能力，即使在相同输入下，MEDIATOR仍是最佳分词器（在操作和亚队列死亡率任务上分别提升+1.0和+1.9点）。相同编码器对照实验表明，文本编码器驱动领域内准确性，而MEDIATOR的学习目标驱动迁移鲁棒性和实体链接质量。MEDIATOR还实现了跨医院的可转移归因。

Fluent but Fallible: Benchmarking Hallucination and Factual Reliability in Large Language Models
流利但不可靠：大语言模型幻觉与事实可靠性基准测试
Large Language Models (LLMs) have significantly advanced natural language processing and artificial intelligence applications through their capabilities in reasoning, text generation and knowledge retrieval. However, LLMs are still vulnerable to two important challenges: hallucination, generating factually incorrect or fabricated content, and response deviation, meaning systematic deviations from ground-truth responses. These limitations raise serious concerns regarding reliability and trustworthiness in academic and research environments. This study offers a thorough comparative analysis using a variety of metrics of ten advanced LLMs ChatGPT, Gemini, Claude~3.5~Sonnet, LLaMA~3, Mistral, Grok, Perplexity, DeepSeek~V3, Falcon, and Gemma based on a benchmark consisting of 70 carefully designed academic prompts across four complexity levels: Basic, Explanation, Advanced, and Tricky, evaluated in February 2025 on specific model versions. Each response was independently evaluated by three reviewers using five metrics: Accuracy, Precision, Recall, Response Deviation Index, and Hallucination Index. Perplexity and DeepSeek~V3 achieved the highest overall accuracy, while Gemini demonstrated strong recall and a well-balanced performance profile across all five metrics. LLaMA~3 showed strong resistance against hallucinations through a conservative method of generation. Advanced-level prompts proved most challenging across all models, while Tricky prompts were handled more reliably owing to their binary true/false structure. Falcon achieved perfect precision and zero hallucinations, but at the cost of the lowest recall and the highest response deviation. The results show a fundamental trade-off between response completeness and factual reliability, intensified by prompt complexity.
中文翻译
大语言模型（LLMs）通过其在推理、文本生成和知识检索方面的能力，显著推动了自然语言处理和人工智能应用的发展。然而，LLMs仍然面临两个重要挑战：幻觉，即生成事实错误或虚构内容；以及响应偏差，指系统性偏离真实回答。这些局限性在学术和研究环境中引发了对可靠性和可信度的严重担忧。本研究基于一个由70个精心设计的学术提示组成的基准，涵盖四个复杂度级别（基础、解释、高级、棘手），对十种先进LLM（ChatGPT、Gemini、Claude 3.5 Sonnet、LLaMA 3、Mistral、Grok、Perplexity、DeepSeek V3、Falcon、Gemma）进行了全面的对比分析，评估时间在2025年2月，使用特定模型版本。每个响应由三位评审员独立评估，采用五项指标：准确性、精确率、召回率、响应偏差指数和幻觉指数。Perplexity和DeepSeek V3在整体准确性上表现最佳，Gemini在召回率和五项指标上的表现均衡。LLaMA 3通过保守生成方式展现出对幻觉的强大抵抗力。高级别提示对所有模型最具挑战性，而棘手提示由于其二元真假结构处理得更可靠。Falcon实现了完美精确率和零幻觉，但代价是最低的召回率和最高的响应偏差。结果揭示了响应完整性与事实可靠性之间的根本权衡，且随提示复杂度加剧。

How to get the best and fairest performance out of your ASR system using activation steering
利用激活引导实现ASR系统最佳且公平性能的方法
Activation steering for ASR systems is an underexplored technique in improving both ASR performance and demographic fairness. We investigate two forms of activation steering: paired activation subtraction, and supervised optimization. We find the former to be ineffective at ameliorating ASR performance whatsoever, while the latter is quite effective at deriving steering vectors (SVs) that improve at once overall ASR performance and demographic fairness along such diverse axes as gender, age, and dialect. We also explore what types of data---from the most to the least personal---are necessary for calculating or training SVs. First, we show significant performance in performance from training SVs using an eclectic group of speakers from the same dataset. Furthermore, we find that vectors trained using acoustically similar speakers leads to ASR performances that are competitive or superior to vectors trained on the same speaker, or speakers from the same labeled demographic group. This finding shows that we can harness activation steering for few-shot adaptation given access to a speaker's demography, or zero-shot adaptation to unseen speakers without the need for any labels at all.
中文翻译

TRACE: Training-aware Robustness and Challenge Evaluation for Text-Vision Embedding Models
TRACE：面向文本-视觉嵌入模型的训练感知鲁棒性与挑战评估
Existing benchmarks for text-vision embedding models report aggregate scores that obscure whether performance comes from general capabilities, task-specific adaptation, or robustness to challenging tasks. We introduce TRACE, a Training-aware Robustness and Challenge Evaluation framework that reorganizes 168 tasks from various sources of datasets into three categories: General, Adaptability, and Challenge. Through controlled experiments on two text-vision embedding models, we show that combining general and adaptation data improves Adaptability performance by 7.67 and 7.09 points, respectively, while largely preserving General performance. However, Challenge performance remains below 5.0, with limited improvements from additional training data. Further analysis identifies multilingual and visual-document tasks as major weaknesses. Our findings demonstrate that data adaptation alone is insufficient to address these challenges, highlighting the need for methodological advances beyond data-centric improvements.

Think Before You Transcribe: Context-Steered Reasoning for Hotword-Aware Speech Recognition
先思考再转录：面向热词感知语音识别的上下文引导推理
Contextual automatic speech recognition (ASR) improves rare-entity recognition by supplying candidate hotwords, but retrieved candidates may be incomplete or incorrect. A reliable system must therefore exploit a relevant candidate without allowing unsupported alternatives to override the acoustic evidence. We propose a context-steered reasoning framework that generates a short, audio-grounded caption before transcription. This task-specific representation lets the model assess candidate relevance before making a lexical decision. Supervised fine-tuning establishes the structured caption-to-transcription behavior, while targeted trajectory injection strengthens group relative policy optimization when difficult utterances yield only unsuccessful rollouts. Experiments across diverse context conditions show that the proposed method consistently improves both transcription quality and hotword recognition while remaining robust to irrelevant candidates.

LAMB: Latent Multi-Hypothesis Beliefs for Long-Horizon Large Language Agents
LAMB：面向长时程大语言智能体的潜在多假设信念
Long-horizon language agents require compact memories that retain interaction evidence while preserving competing hypotheses about unobserved states. Although effective, many belief-state methods encode memory in discrete natural language, where repeated compression can omit evidence or prematurely discard plausible alternatives. We introduce LAMB, a recurrent framework for latent multi-hypothesis beliefs. A Common--Delta factorization combines a shared Common state for interaction evidence with weighted Delta components for competing hypotheses. Relative evidence from action-outcome predictions guides recurrent hypothesis updates without generating textual memory. A context-conditioned readout exposes hypotheses separately to the policy through a fixed-token belief prefix in the default configuration. The belief modules and policy are jointly trained using action imitation and auxiliary state, outcome, and observation-discrimination objectives. Across ALFWorld, BabyAI, ScienceWorld, and Crafter, LAMB matches or outperforms PABU, improving ALFWorld SR@30 and ScienceWorld success by 6.0 and 22.0 percentage points, respectively, and Crafter achievement progress by 26.37 points. On ALFWorld, it also reduces the redundant-action rate by 17.70 percentage points. These results support latent multi-hypothesis beliefs as compact memory for effective long-horizon decision making

The Bottleneck Is Retrieval, Not Reading: Diagnosing Evidence-Based Generation with RAG for Long Context Question Answering
瓶颈在于检索而非阅读：通过RAG进行长上下文问答中的基于证据生成诊断
Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) is the dominant paradigm for grounding large language models in external corpora, but its similarity-driven, chunked pipeline has structural blind spots: it cannot anticipate which passages a question requires and discards document-level context that resolves cross-passage references. We introduce evidence-based generation (EBG), requiring models to return an answer with line-level citations, and a joint Ans+Evid metric that scores correctness and grounding simultaneously. We reformulate two corpora into evidence-grounded multi-document QA benchmarks- MultiHop-RAG and QASPER-MP, a multi-paper adaptation of QASPER with realistic short paper cues, and pair them with a controlled synthetic two-hop haystack and an evidence-augmented NoLiMa spanning 10K-1M characters. Across ten proprietary frontier and three open-weight LLMs, EBG isolates two RAG limitations that long-context models avoid: similarity retrieval surfaces any relevant chunk far more reliably than all required chunks for multi-fact queries, and chunking destroys document meta-information needed to resolve identity-bearing queries. EBG also exposes grounding failures invisible to answer-only evaluation, including incomplete citations in two-needle settings and a widening gap between Ans and Ans+Evid when context contradicts parametric knowledge. Data and code are available at https://github.com/debobanerjee/LC-EBG.

检索增强生成（RAG）是将大语言模型与外部语料库结合的主流范式，但其基于相似性的分块式流程存在结构性盲点：无法预判问题所需段落，并丢弃了用于解决跨段落引用的文档级上下文。我们引入基于证据生成（EBG），要求模型返回带有行级引用的答案，并提出联合Ans+Evid指标，同时评估正确性和可追溯性。我们将两个语料库重新构建为基于证据的多文档问答基准——MultiHop-RAG和QASPER-MP（QASPER的多论文改编版本，包含真实短论文提示），并搭配一个受控合成的两跳草堆和一个涵盖10K–1M字符的证据增强NoLiMa数据集。在十款专有前沿模型和三款开源权重LLM上，EBG揭示了长上下文模型可避免的两个RAG局限：相似性检索比所有必需块更可靠地呈现相关片段，而分块破坏了解决身份指向型查询所需的文档元信息。EBG还暴露出仅答案评估无法发现的可追溯性失败，包括双针设置下的不完整引用，以及当上下文与参数知识矛盾时Ans与Ans+Evid之间的差距扩大。数据与代码可在https://github.com/debobanerjee/LC-EBG获取。

AtomSD: Procedural Atom-Guided Self-Distillation for LLM Agents
AtomSD：基于过程原子的自蒸馏用于大语言模型智能体
Large language models (LLMs) possess broad factual knowledge and strong reasoning capabilities, but solving complex tasks also requires procedural knowledge about how to act. Existing approaches often represent procedural knowledge through trajectories or bundled skills, limiting flexible reuse across interaction histories. This paper introduces Procedural Atoms, fine-grained and independently applicable units that represent Intent, When, Do, and Avoid guidance. Building on this representation, we propose AtomSD, an Atom-guided Self-Distillation framework in which the learner continually extracts Procedural Atoms from its own successful trajectories and distills them into the evolving policy through Atom-conditioned supervision and task-reward optimization. Comprehensive experiments show that AtomSD consistently outperforms strong reward-based and distillation methods, exceeding the state-of-the-art baseline by up to 9.31 points in average performance. Further analyses demonstrate that the proposed Procedural Atoms provide effective guidance, highlighting the value of atom-level procedural knowledge for policy learning.
中文翻译
大型语言模型（LLMs）具备广泛的事实知识和强大的推理能力，但解决复杂任务还需要关于如何行动的过程性知识。现有方法通常通过轨迹或捆绑技能来表示过程性知识，限制了在不同交互历史中的灵活复用。本文引入了过程原子（Procedural Atoms），即细粒度且可独立应用的单元，代表意图、何时、做什么和避免什么的指导。基于这一表示，我们提出了AtomSD——一种原子引导的自蒸馏框架，学习者持续从自身成功轨迹中提取过程原子，并通过原子条件监督和任务奖励优化将它们蒸馏到不断演进的策略中。全面实验表明，AtomSD始终优于强奖励驱动和蒸馏方法，在平均性能上超过最先进基线高达9.31分。进一步分析表明，所提出的流程原子提供了有效指导，凸显了原子级别过程性知识对策略学习的价值。
👤 Bohan Yu, Pengfei Cao, Kai Tian, Chen Han, Shi-Yang Li et al. 📅 2026-10-17 🔗 arXiv

AutoTerm-SST: Adaptive Terminology Memory with Zero Session-Time Setup for Streaming Speech Translation
AutoTerm-SST：面向流式语音翻译的自适应术语记忆，无需会话初始化
Retrieval-augmented speech translation improves rare terminology, but systems such as RASST still assume that users provide a glossary before each session. That assumption is inconvenient for live talks, while merging every domain into one growing glossary eventually places too many irrelevant terms in the same retrieval and prompt competition. We present AutoTerm-SST, an interactive system that removes session-time glossary upload and domain selection. It organizes a large offline terminology catalog into topic slices, matches recent streaming translation context to bilingual slice prototypes, and activates a compact multi-slice working set under fixed retrieval and prompt budgets. On five ACL and five ESO oncology talks, AutoTerm matches a known-domain reference in terminology accuracy (88.3% vs. 88.1%) and mWER-resegmented BLEU (45.09 vs. 45.19), while requiring no domain input. Compared with a one-million-entry merged glossary, it improves terminology accuracy by 3.9 points and BLEU by 2.30. Routing plus four-slice retrieval remains lightweight: the complete system runs at 0.406 RTF, and the vLLM host sustains 32 real-time sessions. The live demo exposes active slices and retrieved terminology as the topic changes, providing zero-setup terminology-aware streaming translation.
中文翻译
检索增强的语音翻译能够提升罕见术语的表现，但现有系统如RASST仍假设用户在每次会话前提供术语表。这一假设在实时演讲中极为不便，而将所有领域合并到一个不断增长的术语表中，最终会导致大量无关术语在同一检索和提示竞争中出现。我们提出AutoTerm-SST，一种交互式系统，可消除会话期间的术语表上传和领域选择需求。该系统将大型离线术语目录组织为话题片段，将最近的流式翻译上下文与双语片段原型匹配，并在固定检索和提示预算下激活一个紧凑的多片段工作集。在五场ACL会议和五场ESO肿瘤学讲座上，AutoTerm在术语准确率（88.3% vs. 88.1%）和mWER重分段BLEU（45.09 vs. 45.19）方面达到已知领域参考水平，且无需任何领域输入。相比包含一百万条目的合并术语表，其术语准确率提升3.9个百分点，BLEU提升2.30。路由加四片段检索保持轻量级：整个系统运行速度为0.406 RTF，vLLM主机可维持32个实时会话。实时演示展示了随着主题变化而动态激活的片段和检索到的术语，实现了零配置的术语感知流式翻译。

H2DF: Calibrated Target Bands for Controllable Language-Model Unlearning
H2DF：用于可控语言模型遗忘的校准目标带
Gradient ascent (GA) is a simple language-model unlearning baseline, but it continues increasing forget loss after useful forgetting has occurred. This unbounded pressure can damage retained utility and make forgotten examples abnormally high-loss. We introduce H2DF, a calibrated target-band objective that applies GA-like pressure below a data-dependent lower target and suppresses, or reverses, that pressure after the target region is reached. The targets are estimated from matched non-member examples before training, so the base objective requires one forget-batch forward/backward pass per optimization step; our reported TOFU variant adds sparse retain replay every tenth step. We instantiate a one-sided objective for TOFU question answering and a two-sided objective for MUSE-News language modeling. Across three TOFU seeds, H2DF obtains a forget NLL of 1.528 with margin 0.049 and retain NLL of 1.165 with margin 0.047, matching GradDiff while using retain replay only every tenth step and showing substantially lower variability than GA. On MUSE-News, H2DF improves retention over GA and NPO and is competitive with SimNPO, but does not dominate it. Ablations show that the upper target reduces overshoot and that validation-based checkpoint selection is important. These results support calibrated target bands as a practical mechanism for controlling forgetting strength, while also exposing sensitivity to random seed and calibration quality.
中文翻译
梯度上升（GA）是一种简单的语言模型遗忘基线方法，但在有效遗忘完成后仍持续增加遗忘损失。这种无界压力可能损害保留能力，并使被遗忘样本表现出异常高的损失。我们提出H2DF，一种校准的目标带目标函数，它在数据依赖的下界目标以下施加类似GA的压力，并在达到目标区域后抑制或反转该压力。目标值在训练前通过匹配的非成员样本估计，因此基础目标每优化步骤仅需一次遗忘批次的前向/反向传播；我们报告的TOFU变体每十步添加一次稀疏保留重播。我们为TOFU问答任务实例化了一侧目标，为MUSE-News语言建模任务实例化了两侧目标。在三个TOFU种子下，H2DF获得遗忘NLL为1.528（边际0.049），保留NLL为1.165（边际0.047），与GradDiff相当，但仅每十步使用一次保留重播，且方差显著低于GA。在MUSE-News上，H2DF的保留性能优于GA和NPO，与SimNPO相当，但未完全超越。消融实验表明上界目标能减少过冲，验证基于检查点选择至关重要。这些结果支持校准目标带作为控制遗忘强度的实用机制，同时也揭示了对随机种子和校准质量的敏感性。

Binary Discrete Diffusion: Candidate-Set Diffusion for Logic and Planning Tasks
二进制离散扩散：用于逻辑与规划任务的候选集扩散
Discrete diffusion models iteratively denoise a noisy sequence of categorical data such as text, enabling any-order generation with self-correction and in-filling capabilities. This makes them promising candidates for reasoning, planning, and constrained-generation tasks like coding and math. Yet masked and uniform diffusion erase a token's identity in a single corruption event, so their noisy states cannot explicitly represent partially resolved uncertainty in which several alternatives remain plausible. We close this gap with Binary Discrete Diffusion, a family of continuous-time discrete-diffusion processes whose noisy state is a set of candidate tokens per position, represented as a binary membership vector. Binary diffusion changes candidate sets through additions and removals, allowing information to be lost and recovered gradually while the diffusion state itself represents partial uncertainty. On Sudoku-Extreme, Boxoban, and graph coloring, the symmetric formulation of binary diffusion consistently outperforms strong, well-tuned masked and uniform baselines under matched architectures and controlled training, sampling, and tuning budgets, with its largest gains on the hardest instances, while remaining competitive on language modeling (Text8, TinyStories, OpenWebText). These results support candidate sets as an effective intermediate representation for iterative reasoning and show that the corruption kernel can materially change the computation learned by the reverse model.
中文翻译
离散扩散模型通过迭代去噪类别数据序列（如文本），实现任意顺序生成并具备自我修正和补全能力，使其成为推理、规划及编码、数学等受限生成任务的有力候选。然而，掩码和均匀扩散在单次污染事件中抹除标记的身份，导致其噪声状态无法显式表示多个备选方案仍合理的部分不确定性。我们通过引入二进制离散扩散（Binary Discrete Diffusion）填补这一空白，这是一种连续时间离散扩散过程家族，其噪声状态为每个位置的一组候选标记，以二进制成员向量表示。二进制扩散通过增删候选集来改变状态，允许信息渐进地丢失与恢复，同时扩散状态本身可表示部分不确定性。在Sudoku-Extreme、Boxoban和图着色任务上，二进制扩散的对称形式在匹配架构、受控训练、采样和调优预算下，始终优于强而调优良好的掩码和均匀基线，尤其在最难实例上取得最大提升；在语言建模任务（Text8、TinyStories、OpenWebText）上也保持竞争力。这些结果支持候选集作为迭代推理的有效中间表示，并表明扰动核可显著影响反向模型所学习的计算过程。
👤 Rodrigo Casado Noguerales, Dimitri von Rütte, Bernhard Schölkopf, Thomas Hofmann, Aran Raoufi 📅 2026-10-20 🔗 arXiv

Present but Hard to Read: Probing Latent Reasoning with Minimal Pairs
存在但难以读取：通过最小对探测潜在推理
Probing studies of latent reasoning often read a model's state where the answer begins and take a failed probe as evidence that a value is absent. We test this with a digit copied from the question, in the looped model Ouro-1.4B and the standard transformer Qwen2.5-1.5B. Comparing prompts that differ only in that digit shows it is present at the last prompt token, in a code shared across questions (readout about 0.9). A probe that sees one prompt at a time, the usual setting, barely reads it: 0.36 in Ouro and 0.19 in Qwen even after 5,249 training questions. The digit's change is small rather than crowded: random noise lands on equally busy directions, while a random word in its place moves the state far more and is readable. Since every varied token leaves a trace, a successful probe is ambiguous too. We give a protocol, including a synthetic null for generic mixing, for testing computed values against copies and mixtures of the inputs. In pre-registered studies of two-step problems it finds a small signal for the unwritten intermediate a+b just before Ouro writes the answer's last digit, and its sign follows the operation, though mixing alone produces much of that sign-following part. Holding that value at another problem's from the first loop on does not move the written digit toward the other problem's, while the same patch of the answer does: the value is readable but not used.
中文翻译
对潜在推理的探测研究通常在模型输出开始处读取状态，并将探测失败视为值不存在的证据。我们通过在循环模型Ouro-1.4B和标准Transformer Qwen2.5-1.5B中复制问题中的数字进行测试。比较仅在该数字上不同的提示发现，该数字确实存在于最后一个提示标记处，且在跨问题共享的代码中（读出约0.9）。而在常规设置中，每次仅观察一个提示的探测器几乎无法读取它：在Ouro中为0.36，在Qwen中为0.19，即使经过5,249个训练问题后仍如此。该数字的变化较小而非拥挤：随机噪声落在同样活跃的方向上，而用随机词替换则使状态大幅移动且可读。由于每个变化的标记都会留下痕迹，成功的探测本身也是模糊的。我们提出一种协议，包括针对通用混合的合成空值，用于测试计算值与输入副本和混合物之间的关系。在两个步骤问题的预注册研究中，该方法在Ouro写出答案最后一位数字之前，发现了小信号指示未写入的中间值a+b，且其符号遵循操作类型，尽管混合本身已产生大部分此类符号跟随现象。将该值在另一问题的第一轮中保持不变，并不会使写出的数字朝向另一个问题的解移动，而同一答案片段的相同修补却可以：说明该值虽可读取，但未被使用。
👤 Aur Shalev Merin 📅 2026-10-20 🔗 arXiv

MAS-Guard: Unified Guardrails from Individual Agents to Multi-Agent LLM Systems
MAS-Guard：从单个智能体到多智能体大语言模型系统的统一防护机制
Agentic AI extends large language models (LLMs) from generating responses to planning and executing complex workflows, substantially expanding their utility while exposing applications to unsafe actions, information leakage, and adversarial manipulation. Multi-agent systems further expand these capabilities through specialization and collaboration, but distributed authority, heterogeneous roles, and opaque or potentially compromised participants make safeguarding even more challenging. Existing defenses address important individual threats but provide fragmented coverage across local execution, system-level interactions, and enforcement mechanisms. We introduce MAS-Guard, a unified framework that closes this gap for safeguarding single- and multi-agent AI applications containing both instrumentable and opaque agents. Within instrumentable agents, the proposed local Agent Guardrail (A-Guard) layer applies task-specific experts to assess agentic events. At the system level, developer-owned Agent Charters and a Control Plane combine deterministic structural checks of routes, agent capabilities, and message formats with semantic assessments of roles and message content. These complementary layers enable enforcement through a shared set of interventions: allow, reflection, confirmation, and block. Evaluated across six benchmarks covering diverse single- and multi-agent scenarios with three agent LLMs from different providers, MAS-Guard reduces the overall attack success rate by 71.5-86.3% relative to unguarded agents, while meeting the utility reference criterion on benign tasks.
中文翻译
代理型人工智能将大型语言模型（LLMs）从生成回复扩展到规划与执行复杂工作流，显著提升了其应用价值，但也暴露于不安全行为、信息泄露和对抗性操纵等风险。多智能体系统通过专业化与协作进一步拓展能力，但分布式权限、异构角色以及不透明或可能被攻破的参与者使安全保障更加困难。现有防御措施虽针对重要个体威胁，但在本地执行、系统级交互和执行机制方面覆盖不完整。我们提出MAS-Guard，一种统一框架，用于保护包含可操作与不可操作智能体的单智能体与多智能体AI应用。在可操作智能体中，提出的本地代理防护层（A-Guard）应用特定任务专家来评估智能体事件。在系统层面，开发者拥有的代理章程与控制平面结合确定性结构检查（路径、智能体能力、消息格式）与语义评估（角色与消息内容）。这些互补层级通过一组共享干预手段实现强制执行：允许、反思、确认与阻止。在涵盖多种单智能体与多智能体场景的六个基准测试中，使用来自三家不同提供商的三种智能体LLM进行评估，MAS-Guard相较未受保护的智能体将总体攻击成功率降低了71.5%-86.3%，同时在良性任务上满足效用参考标准。
👤 Mohammad Kachuee, Phu Mon Htut, Sheng Li, Nikolaos Pappas, Miguel Ballesteros Martinez et al. 📅 2026-10-20 🔗 arXiv

CAPSafe: A Benchmark for Contextual Action and Plan Safety Reasoning in Embodied Agents
CAPSafe：具身智能体上下文动作与计划安全推理的基准测试
Reasoning about safety is essential for embodied agents powered by large language models (LLMs). We introduce the Contextual Action and Plan Safety benchmark (CAPSafe), designed to evaluate safety reasoning at two levels: (1) an action-level dataset that tests whether a single action is safe given its environmental context, and (2) a plan-level dataset that evaluates whether agents can generate safe long-horizon plans for complex hazardous household tasks. CAPSafe models safety conditions where hazards are situational (inferred from object properties), multiple hazards can co-occur within the same environment, and each is annotated with a relative severity level for fine-grained analysis. Evaluating nine closed and open-source LLMs reveals a substantial gap between hazard recognition and safe long-horizon planning. Several models achieve high accuracy in recognizing unsafe actions (exceeding 90% in the best cases), yet safe long-horizon planning remains substantially harder. Gemini-3-pro achieves the highest safe success rate (78.19%), while other models remain below 65%. These results highlight that robust safety reasoning over long action sequences remains an open challenge.
中文翻译
对于由大型语言模型（LLMs）驱动的具身智能体而言，安全推理至关重要。我们提出了上下文动作与计划安全基准测试（CAPSafe），旨在评估安全推理在两个层面的表现：(1) 动作级数据集，测试在特定环境背景下单一动作是否安全；(2) 计划级数据集，评估智能体能否为复杂的危险家庭任务生成安全的长期计划。CAPSafe建模了情境性安全条件（由物体属性推断），允许多个危险在同一环境中共存，并为每个危险标注相对严重程度以支持细粒度分析。对九个闭源与开源LLMs的评估揭示了危险识别与安全长期计划之间存在显著差距。多个模型在识别不安全动作方面表现优异（最佳情况超过90%准确率），但安全长期计划仍极为困难。Gemini-3-pro达到最高安全成功率（78.19%），而其他模型均低于65%。这些结果表明，对长序列动作的鲁棒安全推理仍是开放挑战。
👤 Youmna Farag, Svetlana Stoyanchev, Simon Keizer, Mohan Li, Rama Doddipatla 📅 2026-10-20 🔗 arXiv

Disclosure Begets Disclosure: A Self-Reinforcing Privacy Loop in LLM Agents with Persistent Memory
披露引发更多披露：具有持久记忆的大语言模型智能体中的自我强化隐私循环
Large language model (LLM) agents increasingly use persistent memory to personalize responses, and personal attributes in this memory pose a privacy risk if exposed. We ask how consulting memory during dialogue changes what users go on to say, and thus what the memory comes to reveal. In multi-session simulations, a simulated user with 24 personal attributes talks with a support agent that does or does not consult memory, and in both settings attributes are inferred from the full accumulated record. Across four support models, memory raises response quality but also empirical leakage, the share of attribute values correctly inferred from the record, by a margin that widens over sessions. The cause is a loop in ordinary, non-adversarial assistance. Memory-conditioned responses carry earlier values forward, often as presuppositions, and users affirm them or leave them uncorrected, so that cues and the agent's own guesses become recorded values. The loop reinforces itself, and removing the agent's mentions of an attribute lowers its later disclosure about twice as much as single-turn effects predict. Users cannot leave it on their own, since correcting a value discloses the attribute, declining to discuss it shifts later predictions toward sensitive values, and incorrect values left in place become entrenched. The loop is best weakened at the agent's response. Instructing the agent not to state or presuppose attributes brings leakage to about the no-memory level while keeping most of the quality gains, whereas filtering or anonymizing what users say or what is stored gives up much of them.
中文翻译
大型语言模型（LLM）智能体越来越多地使用持久记忆来个性化响应，而记忆中的个人属性若被暴露则构成隐私风险。我们探讨在对话中查阅记忆如何影响用户后续所说的内容，从而导致记忆逐渐揭示更多信息。在多轮会话模拟中，一个拥有24个个人属性的模拟用户与一个查阅或不查阅记忆的支持型智能体进行交互，两种情况下均从累积记录中推断属性。在四种支持模型中，使用记忆提升了响应质量，但也增加了实际信息泄露——即从记录中正确推断出属性值的比例，且该差距随会话轮次增加而扩大。其根源在于普通非对抗性协助中的反馈循环：记忆条件化响应会携带早期值延续下去，常作为预设前提，用户予以确认或未纠正，使得线索与智能体自身的猜测成为记录值。该循环自我强化，移除智能体提及某属性后，其后续披露减少量约为单轮效应预测值的两倍。用户无法自行摆脱该循环，因为纠正一个值会暴露该属性，拒绝讨论会使后续预测偏向敏感值，而错误值若长期保留则会固化。该循环的最佳缓解方式在于智能体的回应环节。指示智能体不陈述或预设属性可将泄露降至接近无记忆水平，同时保留大部分质量提升；而过滤或匿名化用户发言或存储内容则会牺牲大量质量收益。
👤 Masahiro Kaneko, Hiromi Arai, Timothy Baldwin 📅 2026-10-20 🔗 arXiv
[2610.27993]
agentic-rlagent-memory
Building Telecom Language Models with RSOD: Domain Data, Specialist Post-Training, Memory-Conscious Distillation, and Networking Evaluation
基于RSOD构建电信语言模型：领域数据、专家后训练、记忆感知蒸馏与网络评估
Telecom language models must combine broad networking knowledge with specialist behavior across routing, broadband services, access technologies, and operational diagnostics while remaining practical to deploy as a single model. We present an end-to-end framework that builds source-grounded synthetic training data from structured networking taxonomies and expert graphs, materializes 212,559 routed training records across fourteen networking routes, trains six frozen specialist LoRA teachers over one shared continued-pretrained backbone, and consolidates their behavior into one deployable student adapter using Routed Specialist On-Policy Distillation (RSOD). In RSOD, the committed student generates each trajectory, the route-assigned specialist teacher-forces the exact native token sequence, compact teacher hidden states are reconstructed through the shared frozen output head, and only the student LoRA is updated. A controlled reconstruction validation shows mean absolute logit error of 7.26 x 10^-6 with 100% top-1 agreement between native and reconstructed teacher logits. On four RTX 6000 Ada GPUs, the completed 2,000-step run produced 16,000 trajectories with zero observed student-version or token- ID mismatches. Under the primary route-conditioned evaluation over 2,850 held-out questions, the raw Meta-Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct model scores 12.8661/25 micro (13.0172/25 macro), the combined routed six-teacher system scores 21.7641/25 micro, and the consolidated RSOD student, released as Llama-CalixCLM v1, reaches 21.7112/25 micro (21.8006/25 macro) on the same five dimension LLM-judge rubric. Under a separate neutral deployment-like prompt, the same student reaches 20.0028/25 micro (20.0778/25 macro). The consolidated student remains within 0.0529 micro-score points of the combined routed-teacher system while retaining a numerically validated and auditable train-serve execution path.
中文翻译
电信语言模型需要在路由、宽带服务、接入技术及运维诊断等多个专业领域具备专业知识，同时保持作为单一模型的可部署性。我们提出一个端到端框架，从结构化网络分类体系和专家图中构建源基合成训练数据，生成跨十四种网络路径的212,559条有路由记录的训练样本，使用一个共享的持续预训练主干网络对六个冻结的专家LoRA教师进行训练，并通过路由专家在线策略蒸馏（RSOD）将它们的行为整合为一个可部署的学生适配器。在RSOD中，学生生成每条轨迹，分配路由的专家教师强制输出原始token序列，通过共享的冻结输出头重建紧凑的教师隐藏状态，仅更新学生LoRA。受控重建验证显示，原始与重构教师logit之间的平均绝对logit误差为7.26×10^-6，top-1一致率达到100%。在四块RTX 6000 Ada GPU上完成2000步运行后，生成了16,000条轨迹且未观察到学生版本或token-ID不匹配。在2,850个保留问题上的主要路由条件评估中，原始Meta-Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct模型得分为12.8661/25微平均（13.0172/25宏平均），六教师组合系统得分为21.7641/25微平均，而整合后的RSOD学生模型（发布为Llama-CalixCLM v1）在相同五维LLM裁判评分标准下达到21.7112/25微平均（21.8006/25宏平均）。在独立的中立部署类提示下，同一学生模型得分20.0028/25微平均（20.0778/25宏平均）。该整合学生模型在微分值上仅落后于组合教师系统0.0529分，同时保持了数值验证且可审计的训练-服务执行路径。

Lost in Recovery: Demystifying Recovery Behavior of Computer-Use Agents via Goal-Oriented Action Replay
迷失于恢复：通过目标导向动作回放解构计算机使用智能体的恢复行为
Recent advances in large (vision) language models have enabled computer-use agents that automate digital workflows via fine-grained keyboard and mouse operations. However, such agents remain fragile in long-horizon settings, where errors accumulate and executed actions leave unintended side effects, making the ability to recover from failures critical. Yet, existing remedies remain inadequate: environment resets are infeasible in proprietary settings, action replay is prohibitively expensive over long horizons, and API-based rollback is limited to web environments, leaving model-based recovery largely unexplored. To address this gap, we present ReTrace, a scalable evaluation framework for systematically studying recovery behavior in computer-use agents. We formalize Transition-Consistent Recovery: identical downstream actions from the recovered and target states must yield identical outcomes. We operationalize this criterion through goal-oriented action replay following controlled error injection. Using ReTrace, we present the first systematic study of autonomous, model-based recovery, revealing that (i) ideal recovery can unlock model's latent task completion capabilities, (ii) current computer-use agents fail to exploit their latent recovery capabilities, and (iii) accurate model-based recovery yields measurable end-to-end gains. Together, these findings establish recovery as a fundamental capability for building reliable computer-use agents. Our code and dataset is available at https://github.com/BangProx/ReTRACE.
中文翻译
大型（视觉）语言模型的最新进展使得能够通过细粒度的键盘和鼠标操作自动化数字工作流的计算机使用智能体成为可能。然而，这类智能体在长时程场景中仍显脆弱，错误累积且执行动作会产生意外副作用，因此恢复失败的能力至关重要。但现有解决方案仍不充分：环境重置在专有环境中不可行，动作回放在长时程下成本过高，基于API的回滚仅限于网络环境，导致基于模型的恢复研究几乎空白。为填补这一空白，我们提出ReTrace，一种可扩展的评估框架，用于系统研究计算机使用智能体的恢复行为。我们形式化定义了“转移一致性恢复”：从恢复状态和目标状态出发的相同下游动作必须产生相同结果。我们通过受控错误注入后的目标导向动作回放来实现该标准。利用ReTrace，我们首次系统性地研究了自主的、基于模型的恢复行为，发现：(i) 理想的恢复能释放模型潜在的任务完成能力；(ii) 当前计算机使用智能体未能利用其潜在的恢复能力；(iii) 准确的基于模型恢复可带来可衡量的端到端性能提升。这些发现确立了恢复能力是构建可靠计算机使用智能体的基本要素。代码与数据集已公开于https://github.com/BangProx/ReTRACE。

ACE-Compression: An Ability-Centric Evaluation of Compressed Large Language Models
ACE-Compression：面向压缩大语言模型的能力中心评估
Model compression makes large language models deployable, but today's evaluation tells an incomplete story. Standard practice measures retained task performance and stops there. This misses a critical fact: compression perturbs the distributed knowledge embedded across billions of weights, and task scores alone cannot reveal which underlying abilities survive and which quietly collapse. To solve this question, we introduce ACE-Compression, an ability-centric evaluation framework. Instead of asking only "does the compressed model still answer correctly?", we ask "which of its core abilities remain intact?" Our framework probes four deployment-facing dimensions: procedural reasoning and constraint execution, knowledge grounding and truthfulness, social cognition and affective understanding, and persona fidelity and stylistic consistency. Across more than 35 compressed checkpoints from six open-weight LLMs-covering quantization and pruning-we find that compression redistributes ability profiles. Coarse, local, and surface-level signals often persist, while fine-grained, long-tail, and context-dependent abilities become fragile. Quantization consistently outperforms pruning in retention, but the practical meaning of that gap depends entirely on which ability an application requires. Based on our findings, deployers can choose compression methods by the ability profile their application needs, not just by a leaderboard average. And algorithm designers gain diagnostic signals to build compression methods that preserve what matters. We believe that ACE-Compression turns compression from a black-box accuracy drop into an interpretable intervention-one we can measure, understand, and ultimately improve.
中文翻译
模型压缩使大语言模型具备可部署性，但当前的评估方式讲述的是不完整的故事。标准做法仅测量保留的任务性能，便停止评估。这忽略了关键事实：压缩会扰动分布在数十亿权重中的分布式知识，仅靠任务得分无法揭示哪些底层能力得以保留，哪些悄然崩溃。为解决此问题，我们引入ACE-Compression，一种以能力为中心的评估框架。我们不仅问‘压缩后的模型是否仍能正确回答？’，更进一步问‘其核心能力中哪些仍然完好？’。我们的框架探测四个面向部署的维度：程序推理与约束执行、知识锚定与真实性、社会认知与情感理解，以及人格一致性与风格连贯性。通过对六种开源大模型的超过35个压缩检查点（涵盖量化与剪枝）进行分析，我们发现压缩会重新分布能力谱系：粗粒度、局部性和表面信号通常得以保留，而细粒度、长尾性和上下文依赖能力则变得脆弱。量化在能力保留方面始终优于剪枝，但这一差距的实际意义完全取决于应用所需的具体能力。基于研究发现，部署者可根据应用所需的能力特征选择压缩方法，而非仅依赖排行榜平均分。算法设计者也可获得诊断信号，以开发能保留关键能力的压缩方法。我们认为，ACE-Compression将压缩从黑箱精度下降转变为可解释的干预手段——我们可测量、理解并最终改进它。

Staged Continual Adaptation of Multimodal Foundation Models for Japanese Financial Documents
多模态基础模型在日本金融文档上的分阶段持续适应
Staged post-training of multimodal foundation models chains vision-language alignment, reasoning distillation, and domain tuning, yet how each phase trades capabilities against the others remains under-characterised. Tracking an 8.4B-parameter model from an un-fine-tuned baseline through three training phases on Japanese financial documents (evaluated on five text-input benchmarks), we find the phases interact non-additively: math-only distillation overwrites the financial knowledge acquired earlier even as procedural math skill survives, and the domain phase repairs more than it installs. A skip-phase ablation is best for domain knowledge but forfeits numerical-reasoning gains, so per-phase contributions are not decomposable and the best checkpoint is task-dependent, with implications for checkpoint retention and scheduling rather than added stages.

From Tokens to Files: Multi-Granularity Detection and Localization of AI-Involved Code
从标记到文件：AI参与代码的多粒度检测与定位
The increasing use of large language models for code generation calls for methods that distinguish purely human-written code from AI-involved code: files in which any part, however small, was generated or substantially modified by a model. Beyond flagging such files, we aim to localize the lines or blocks attributable to AI. Existing approaches compress a file into a single score, from surface stylistic features or a global code representation, which averages away the evidence when only a few blocks are AI-involved. We propose a framework that models AI authorship at the token level and predicts at the line, block, and file levels. A frozen code language model is equipped with two low-rank adapters, trained separately on machine-written and human-written files; their per-token log-likelihood ratio provides local evidence of AI authorship. Lightweight heads aggregate this evidence within lines and blocks, using the surrounding code context, and their local predictions are aggregated into a file-level decision. We train on mixed-authorship files built from human originals and their AI-generated or AI-modified counterparts, including continuation and fill-in-the-middle scenarios. On an internal test set of 11,773 AI-involved files across six generation regimes against 2,541 human files, our method reaches 72.4 macro F1 at a 1% FPR operating point. With no adaptation, it reaches 62.5 line-level F1 on HybridCodeAuthorship, an external human-AI co-writing benchmark with no training split, above the 56 its own paper reports as the best line-level result. On 19,445 human-written files from repositories created before 2022, the false-positive rate stays between 0.55% and 0.81% under the transported 1% threshold, so the operating point holds on real human code.
中文翻译
大型语言模型在代码生成中的广泛应用催生了区分纯人工编写代码与AI参与代码的需求：即任何部分（无论多小）由模型生成或大幅修改的文件。除了标记此类文件外，我们还旨在精确定位归因于AI的代码行或代码块。现有方法将文件压缩为单一分数，基于表面风格特征或全局代码表示，当仅有少数代码块涉及AI时，这种平均化会抹去证据。我们提出一种框架，在标记层面建模AI作者身份，并在行、块和文件层面进行预测。一个冻结的代码语言模型配备两个低秩适配器，分别在机器生成和人类编写文件上独立训练；其每标记的对数似然比提供AI作者身份的局部证据。轻量级头部利用周围代码上下文聚合行内与块内的证据，并将局部预测汇总为文件级决策。我们在混合作者文件上进行训练，这些文件由原始人类代码及其AI生成或AI修改的对应版本构建而成，包括续写和填空等场景。在包含11,773个AI参与文件（六种生成模式）与2,541个纯人类文件的内部测试集上，我们的方法在1%误报率操作点下达到72.4的宏F1。无需适应即可在HybridCodeAuthorship外部人类-AI协作写作基准上达到62.5的行级F1，高于其论文报告的最佳行级结果56。在19,445个2022年前创建的仓库中的人类代码文件上，当采用传输的1%阈值时，误报率维持在0.55%至0.81%之间，表明该操作点在真实人类代码上依然有效。

Give the Model a Finger: Developmentally Aligned Evaluation of Counting in Vision-Language Models
给模型一个手指：视觉-语言模型计数能力的发展性对齐评估
Vision-language models (VLMs) are exact on small sets and drift into estimation on larger ones, a profile reminiscent of children before they master counting. Children cross the barrier with epistemic actions: pointing at each item while reciting number words, moving counted items aside, partitioning the array. We give VLMs the same actions as dumb image tools with no visual intelligence of their own in a training-free agent loop. Our central claim is about measurement, not intervention: a model that counts by marking leaves a normally hidden serial procedure on the image, and the developmental principles of counting turn those marks into an interpretable audit of serial visual reasoning: every tag is classified against point-level ground truth as on-target, duplicate, phantom, or omitted, the answer is checked against the number of tags, and order irrelevance is tested by forcing opposite traversal orders. Across 9 models, 682 images, and 7 harness conditions we find that (i) the exact-counting range is a model-specific signature spanning nearly two orders of magnitude (R80 from 3 to 150 dots); (ii) as an intervention the finger mostly fails: tags help 4 of 9 models on small-count photographs, hurt imprecise pointers, and rarely beat a verbal routine or a blind point list; what the loop buys is an auditable trace, not accuracy; (iii) as measurement it succeeds: a tag-perturbation probe shows that precise pointers read their answers off the tags, pointing precision tracks which models benefit, dense scenes fail by omission, and models rarely revise after seeing their own tags; and (iv) the capacities dissociate: models follow an instructed traversal order yet often change their answer when it reverses, and children's regular-over-scattered advantage appears only under the routine and survives lattices that defeat a rows-times-columns shortcut.
中文翻译
视觉-语言模型（VLMs）在小规模数据上表现精确，但在大规模数据上逐渐转向估算，这一特征类似于儿童尚未掌握计数能力时的表现。儿童通过认知行为跨越这一障碍：在数数时逐个指向物品并说出数字词，将已计数的物品移开，对数组进行分区。我们为VLMs提供相同的动作，作为无视觉智能的简单图像工具，在无需训练的代理循环中实现。核心主张是关于测量而非干预：通过标记进行计数的模型会在图像上留下原本隐藏的序列过程痕迹，而计数的发展性原则使这些标记成为可解释的序列视觉推理审计——每个标记被分类为命中、重复、幻觉或遗漏，答案与标记数量对比，通过强制相反遍历顺序测试顺序无关性。在9个模型、682张图像和7种利用条件下，我们发现：(i) 精确计数范围是模型特有的特征，跨度接近两个数量级（R80从3到150个点）；(ii) 作为干预，手指方法大多失败：标记仅帮助9个模型中的4个在小计数照片上表现更好，对不精确指针有害，且很少优于口头惯例或盲点列表；循环带来的价值是可审计的轨迹，而非准确性；(iii) 作为测量手段，该方法成功：标签扰动探测显示，精确指针从标签中读取答案，指针精度与受益模型相关，密集场景因遗漏而失败，模型极少在看到自身标签后修正答案；(iv) 能力可分离：模型遵循指令的遍历顺序，但当顺序反转时常改变答案，而儿童在规则分布场景中优于散乱分布的优势仅在常规流程下出现，并在击败行×列捷径的网格中依然存在。

When Does Experience Memory Help Closed-Loop LLM Driving Agents? A Controlled Evaluation with Resampled Warmup Pools, Token-Matched Fillers, and Audited Baselines
经验记忆何时有助于闭环大语言模型驾驶代理？基于重采样预热池、令牌匹配填充物和审计基线的受控评估
When does experience memory help a closed-loop LLM driving agent? Published gains cannot say, because the two factors we find to matter most, which warmup experiences the pool contains and what any injected tokens do to the prompt, are controlled in none of them. We answer with a controlled evaluation on one audited HighwayEnv testbed (1Hz, gpt-4o-mini for the decisive contrasts). We treat the warmup pool as a resampled random factor (ten disjoint pools) and add token-matched irrelevant-filler controls, oracle-certified diagnostics, caution-pricing metrics, and quantified audits of the baseline ports. The probe suite spans flat-text retrieval (a DiLu port), a single-frame grid port (RESPOND-inspired), our episodic temporal-graph memory, a retrieval-free consolidated rulebook, and a flat K-step observation-history baseline. Four findings follow, each a property of this testbed. (1) Headline memory gains belong to the warmup draw. The reported pool is the best of ten for both retrieval memories, which beat No-Memory in only one pool of ten (sign test p=0.011), and even the rulebook's apparent pool-immunity collapsed on five fresh draws. (2) No retrieval design tested exceeds token-matched filler. The collision-only merge ceiling is reached by a single 15-token caution instruction, and metrics that price caution erase the rulebook's lead. (3) Representational sophistication is nowhere robustly operative. (4) Baseline port quality silently inverts conclusions. On this testbed, a single-pool comparison without filler controls would have reported memory gains that these controls reverse. We release the testbed, audited ports, seeds, and caches.
中文翻译
经验记忆何时有助于闭环大语言模型（LLM）驾驶代理？现有研究无法回答此问题，因为影响最大的两个因素——预热池中包含哪些经验，以及注入的任何令牌如何影响提示——在所有已有研究中均未得到控制。我们通过在一个经审计的HighwayEnv测试平台（1Hz，使用gpt-4o-mini进行关键对比）上的受控评估来解答。我们将预热池视为重采样的随机因素（十个互不重叠的池），并引入令牌匹配的无关填充控制、Oracle认证诊断、谨慎定价度量以及基线端口的量化审计。探测套件涵盖平面文本检索（DiLu端口）、单帧网格端口（RESPOND启发式）、我们的情景时间图记忆、无检索的整合规则手册，以及平面K步观察历史基线。四个发现随之而来，均为该测试平台的特性：(1) 头部记忆收益来自预热抽取。报告的池在两种检索记忆中均为十池中最佳，仅在一个池中优于无记忆（符号检验p=0.011），即使规则手册看似不受池影响，在五个新抽取样本中也崩溃了；(2) 所测试的任一检索设计均未超过令牌匹配填充物。仅需一条15令牌的谨慎指令即可达到仅碰撞合并的上限，而对谨慎进行定价的度量抹平了规则手册的优势；(3) 表示复杂性在任何情况下均未表现出稳健性；(4) 基线端口质量无声地反转结论。在此测试平台上，若无填充物控制的单池比较会报告记忆收益，但这些控制却使其逆转。我们公开测试平台、审计端口、种子和缓存。
👤 Yingxin Jia, Xuanyi Li 📅 2026-10-20 🔗 arXiv

CUA-Recovery: Benchmarking Error Recovery in Long-Horizon Computer-Use Agents
CUA-Recovery：长时程计算机使用代理中错误恢复的基准测试
As computer-use agents (CUAs) move to long-horizon workflows spanning multiple applications, undetected errors can silently corrupt persistent state and cause cascading failures in subsequent actions. Reliable error detection and recovery have become critical bottlenecks for building robust CUAs. However, existing benchmarks focus primarily on end-to-end task performance, providing limited insight into whether agents can detect and recover from errors during execution. The few benchmarks that evaluate recovery rely largely on manually injected errors, which may not reflect the types or horizons of failures induced by real agent policies. We introduce CUA-Recovery, a benchmark comprising test and training suites across multiple desktop applications. We construct it using ReRail, a framework for synthesizing long-horizon tasks and trajectories in a persistent desktop environment. ReRail composes MyPCBench tasks into new long-horizon workflows with executable ground truth, collects verifier-confirmed failures from multiple policies, and turns them into verified recovery trajectories. CUA-Recovery comprises a human-verified test suite of 2,250 erroneous states over 100 tasks and a training suite of 8.6K trajectories over 377 tasks. Evaluation on the test suite shows that agents struggle both to recognize inherited errors and to complete the task. Across six agents and six takeover depths, Error Awareness Rate (EAR) reaches at most 44.0% and Pass@3 at most 25.5%. After fine-tuning Qwen3.5-35B-A3B on the training suite, its average EAR rises from 19.9% to 35.2% on held-out recovery states, and its Rubric Score goes from 5.0% to 16.1%. It also gains 15.3 points on MyPCBench and 10.8 points on Odysseys for Pass@3, showing that the training trajectories help both with recovery and with completing tasks from a clean start. Our code is available at https://github.com/xu-hu-2002/CUA-Recovery.
中文翻译
随着计算机使用代理（CUAs）向跨越多个应用的长时程工作流发展，未被检测到的错误可能悄然破坏持久状态，并导致后续动作中的级联故障。可靠的错误检测与恢复已成为构建鲁棒CUAs的关键瓶颈。然而，现有基准主要关注端到端任务性能，难以揭示代理在执行过程中能否检测并恢复错误。少数评估恢复能力的基准严重依赖人工注入错误，可能无法反映真实代理策略引发的错误类型或时长。我们提出CUA-Recovery，一个涵盖多个桌面应用的测试与训练套件的基准。我们使用ReRail框架，在持久桌面环境中合成长时程任务与轨迹。ReRail将MyPCBench任务组合为新的长时程工作流，具备可执行的真实答案，从多个策略中收集验证器确认的失败，并将其转化为已验证的恢复轨迹。CUA-Recovery包含2,250个错误状态的真人验证测试套件（覆盖100项任务）和8.6K条轨迹的训练套件（覆盖377项任务）。在测试套件上的评估显示，代理在识别继承错误和完成任务方面均表现不佳。在六种代理和六种接管深度下，错误感知率（EAR）最高仅为44.0%，Pass@3最高仅为25.5%。在训练套件上微调Qwen3.5-35B-A3B后，其在保留恢复状态下的平均EAR从19.9%提升至35.2%，Rubric Score从5.0%提升至16.1%。同时，其在MyPCBench上Pass@3提高15.3分，在Odysseys上提高10.8分，表明训练轨迹不仅有助于恢复，也有助于从干净起点完成任务。代码已公开于https://github.com/xu-hu-2002/CUA-Recovery。

Auditing the Product, Not the Recursion: The Evaluation Evidence Behind Recursive Self-Improvement Claims
审计产品，而非递归过程：关于递归自我改进声明背后的评估证据
Recursive self-improvement (RSI) promises AI systems, such as self-evolving LLM agents, that become better at producing further improvements. Evaluating this promise requires separating product claims about the final system from process claims about the improvement process itself. Existing surveys specify the evidence these claims require, but how often studies report that evidence remains unmeasured. We examine this question through full-text coding of 114 eligible studies in a reproducible arXiv search frame frozen in October 2026, including 90 that describe their own systems as RSI. The codebook links claims to evaluation designs. Independent human coding, third-reader adjudication, and a separate blind coding of improver comparisons assess coding reliability. Among the 90 RSI studies, 90% report product evaluation beyond development tasks under a broad criterion that includes partial or unstated separation, while 60% report evaluation on tasks unused for selection. Only 22% audit two or more reruns of the full loop. In a human-coded random sample, the RSI studies show the same contrast: 45% evaluate on tasks unused for selection and 14% audit two or more reruns. Of the 26 studies claiming a better improvement procedure, 23 report a related comparison, but only 4 compare earlier and later procedures from identical starting artifacts at a matched budget. Across these results and a comparison set of established works, evidence for repeatability and improved procedures remains less developed than product evaluation. Three propositions explain how selection effects, shared evaluator errors, and changes in system state limit what product evaluations can establish. Together, the findings support a claim-indexed reporting standard that connects each claim to the evaluation design needed to test it.

递归自我改进（RSI）承诺AI系统（如自演化的大语言模型智能体）能够持续提升自身以产生进一步改进。评估这一承诺需要将关于最终系统的成果性声明与关于改进过程本身的进程性声明区分开来。现有综述指出了这些声明所需的证据，但研究中报告这些证据的频率仍缺乏衡量。我们通过在2026年10月冻结的可复现arXiv搜索框架中对114项符合条件的研究进行全文编码来考察这一问题，其中包括90项将自身系统描述为RSI的研究。代码本将声明与评估设计相链接。独立人工编码、第三方评审以及对改进者对比的盲编码评估了编码可靠性。在90项RSI研究中，90%在宽泛标准下报告了超出开发任务的产品评估，该标准包括部分或未明确分离的情况；60%报告了在未用于选择的任务上的评估。仅有22%审计了完整循环的两次或以上重运行。在人工编码的随机样本中，RSI研究同样表现出类似对比：45%在未用于选择的任务上进行评估，14%审计了两次或以上重运行。在26项声称改进过程更优的研究中，23项报告了相关比较，但仅有4项在相同起始资源和匹配预算下比较了相同起始物的早期与后期过程。综合这些结果与一组已确立的工作相比，可重复性和改进过程的证据发展程度仍落后于产品评估。三个命题解释了选择效应、共享评估者错误和系统状态变化如何限制产品评估所能建立的内容。总体而言，研究结果支持一种按声明索引的报告标准，将每个声明与其所需验证的评估设计相连接。

When LLMs Manipulate: Measuring Behavioral, Social, and Temporal Effects
当大语言模型操纵时：测量行为、社会与时间效应
Large language models (LLMs) can influence user trust and decision-making through subtle linguistic cues, yet the behavioral effects of manipulative outputs remain poorly quantified. We introduce a behavioral computing framework for measuring \textit{behavioral vulnerability}, defined as users' susceptibility to manipulative AI responses. Building on DarkPatterns-LLM benchmark, we generate controlled manipulative stimuli using Manipulation Gradient Descent (MGD), targeting biases associated with authority, urgency, and social proof. We analyze participant responses using the Multi-Stakeholder Impact Assessment Network (MSIAN) to model cross-user influence and the Temporal Harm Propagation Model (THPM) to characterize the persistence of behavioral effects over time. In a study involving 120 participants, manipulative framing produced significant changes in trust calibration and compliance ($p < 0.01$), with effects also propagating across social contexts. These findings highlight the need to extend LLM safety evaluation beyond model outputs to measurable behavioral consequences and provide a computational framework for quantifying individual, social, and temporal risks arising from manipulative AI interactions.

Can Native Safety Travel? Reusing Safety Pretraining Across Models via On-Policy Distillation
原生安全能否迁移？通过在线策略蒸馏实现跨模型的安全预训练复用
Safety pretraining has emerged as a promising paradigm for embedding safety deeply into a model during pretraining, yielding robustness to subsequent fine-tuning that conventional safety post-training often lacks. We refer to this safety acquired during model pretraining as native safety. Yet native safety is expensive to acquire, confined to the model in which it was pretrained, and often accompanied by lower general utility. In this work, we rethink the practical value of safety-pretrained models and introduce, for the first time, the native safety reuse problem: Can native safety be transferred from a specialized, potentially smaller, safety-pretrained "teacher" to standard-pretrained "students," enabling them to inherit its robustness while preserving their general utility? We show that native safety can "travel" through on-policy distillation. A standard-pretrained student generates responses to safety-pretraining prompts, while a small safety-pretrained teacher supervises the student's token-level predictions along its own generated trajectories; cross-tokenizer alignment further enables transfer across model families. The resulting students exhibit substantially greater safety robustness than their safety-post-trained counterparts while retaining higher general utility than the teacher: subsequent fine-tuning raises the average attack success rate (ASR) of the 1.7B student by only 5.00%, compared with 11.58% for safety post-training, and its average utility of 48.20% far exceeds the aligned teacher's 27.42%. Unlike conventional large-to-small knowledge distillation, our framework enables selective, small-to-large safety transfer: it reuses the robust safety expertise of a smaller pretrained teacher without inheriting its utility limitations. These findings establish native safety as a reusable model property rather than one permanently tied to the originally pretrained model.

安全预训练已成为一种有前景的方法，在预训练阶段深度嵌入安全性，从而在后续微调中展现出优于传统安全后训练的鲁棒性。我们将这种在模型预训练期间获得的安全性称为原生安全。然而，原生安全获取成本高，局限于其预训练的模型，且常伴随较低的通用实用性。本文重新思考安全预训练模型的实际价值，首次提出原生安全复用问题：能否将专门的、可能更小的、经过安全预训练的“教师”模型的原生安全，迁移到标准预训练的“学生”模型中，使其继承其鲁棒性的同时保留通用实用性？我们证明原生安全可通过在线策略蒸馏实现“迁移”。标准预训练的学生模型生成对安全预训练提示的响应，而小型安全预训练的教师模型则在其自身生成轨迹上监督学生的逐标记预测；跨分词器对齐进一步实现跨模型家族的迁移。由此产生的学生模型在安全性鲁棒性上显著优于其安全后训练的同类，同时保留了更高的通用实用性：后续微调使1.7B学生模型的平均攻击成功率（ASR）仅上升5.00%，而安全后训练模型为11.58%；其平均实用性48.20%远超对齐教师的27.42%。与传统的大型到小型知识蒸馏不同，我们的框架实现了选择性的、小型到大型的安全迁移：它复用了小型预训练教师的鲁棒安全专长，而不继承其实用性局限。这些发现确立原生安全为可复用的模型属性，而非永久绑定于最初预训练模型的特性。

Self-Evolving Agents through Privileged Retrospective Distillation
通过特权回溯蒸馏实现自演化智能体
Self-evolving agents aim to improve from their own experience rather than remain fixed after deployment, but naturally collected trajectories often lack ground-truth solutions or verifier-derived rewards. We introduce Retrospective Self-Distillation (RSD), a multi-round framework that turns such experience into dense supervision using privileged retrospective context. For each on-policy trajectory, the same underlying model acts as both student and teacher: the teacher re-scores the student's realized actions while conditioning on cross-task precedents and lessons, together with turn-level reflection and future hindsight from the completed trajectory. The resulting teacher distribution is distilled back into the policy through on-policy self-distillation. Across rounds, the experience bank grows and the retrospective context is refreshed, allowing the model, its data, and its supervision to co-evolve. We further introduce frontier-guided context-policy evolution, where a stronger frontier model proposes constrained edits to how privileged context is constructed. Across ALFWorld, ScienceWorld, and SearchQA with Qwen3-8B and Qwen3-14B, RSD improves performance by up to 59.7% relative to the base model, while frontier-guided context-policy evolution adds up to a further 44.7% relative improvement over a fixed context policy. These results show that naturally accumulated experience can be converted into persistent model capability without ground-truth trajectories or verifier-derived rewards for the core policy update.
中文翻译
自演化智能体旨在从自身经验中持续改进，而非部署后保持固定。然而，自然收集的轨迹通常缺乏真实解或验证器生成的奖励信号。我们提出回溯自蒸馏（RSD），一种多轮框架，利用特权回溯上下文将此类经验转化为密集监督。在每条在线轨迹中，同一基础模型同时充当学生和教师：教师在考虑跨任务先例、经验教训以及完成轨迹中的逐轮反思与未来 hindsight 的基础上，重新评估学生的实际行为。由此产生的教师分布通过在线自蒸馏回传至策略中。随着轮次推进，经验库不断增长，回溯上下文得以刷新，使模型、其数据及其监督能够共同演化。我们进一步引入前沿引导的上下文-策略演化机制，由更强的前沿模型提出对特权上下文构建方式的约束性修改。在 ALFWorld、ScienceWorld 和 SearchQA 上使用 Qwen3-8B 和 Qwen3-14B 进行实验，RSD 相比基线模型性能提升最高达 59.7%，而前沿引导的上下文-策略演化在此基础上再带来最高 44.7% 的相对提升。结果表明，无需真实轨迹或验证器奖励即可将自然积累的经验转化为持久的模型能力。

Recoverability and Verifier Strength: Two Measured Preconditions for Unattended Agent Self-Improvement
可恢复性与验证器强度：无人值守智能体自我改进的两个可度量前提
Agent platforms are starting to improve their own agents: they read a deployed agent's execution traces, derive a change, have an automated verifier approve it, and release it with no human author. How much of this loop can safely run without a human? We propose two quantities a platform can measure. Verifier strength $σ$ is how well the available verifier -- often an LLM judge -- agrees with a trusted reference verifier; it limits how much a step can improve the agent. Recoverability is how cheaply a bad change can be undone: whether undoing it needs a new verifier approval, and how much of the old behavior a rollback restores. A step should run unattended only if both are adequate. On our platform this rule allows prompt, tool and skill edits to run unattended and keeps weight updates under human sign-off. Our main contribution is these two definitions and how to measure them. We built a platform that applies the rule and tested it in two ways: on the platform's own approval gate, and in controlled experiments on the $τ^2$-bench agent benchmark. On verifier strength, the platform's LLM judge sometimes gave the same score no matter what it was shown (9 of 37 settings) and reported no error, so a verifier can look healthy while approving anything; its strength must be measured, and two simple tests catch the failure. On recoverability, restoring a saved copy brought the agent back to its original behavior, but a regenerated undo did not, so only changes whose old version is kept can be reversed without a human; and while a change was deployed, it altered the outcome of 17 percent of tasks beyond normal run-to-run variation, which no undo repairs. Together these support the rule: a change can run unattended only when its old version is kept and its verifier's strength has been measured.

智能体平台正开始自主改进其智能体：读取已部署智能体的执行轨迹，推导出变更，经自动化验证器批准后发布，全程无需人工干预。这种循环能在多大程度上安全地脱离人工？我们提出平台可测量的两个量化指标：验证器强度 $σ$ 表示现有验证器（通常是 LLM 判官）与可信参考验证器的一致性程度，它限制了每一步改进的上限；可恢复性则衡量一次错误变更被撤销的成本——是否需要新的验证器审批，以及回滚能恢复多少旧行为。只有当两者均达标时，变更才可无人值守运行。在我们的平台上，该规则允许提示、工具和技能的修改自动运行，而权重更新仍需人工签发。主要贡献在于这两个概念的定义及其测量方法。我们构建了一个应用该规则的平台，并通过两种方式测试：在平台自身的审批门禁中，以及在 $τ^2$-bench 智能体基准上的受控实验。在验证器强度方面，平台的 LLM 判官在 9/37 设置下无论输入为何都给出相同评分且无错误报告，说明验证器可能表面健康却批准任意内容；必须测量其强度，两个简单测试即可发现此类失效。在可恢复性方面，恢复保存副本可还原原始行为，但重新生成的撤销操作无法做到，因此只有保留旧版本的变更才能无需人工撤销；且在变更部署期间，其改变了 17% 任务的结果，超出正常运行波动范围，任何撤销都无法修复。这些结果共同支持该规则：变更仅在保留旧版本且验证器强度经过测量的前提下才可无人值守运行。

Ask to Distill: Learning to Synthesize Data for On-Policy Distillation
提问以蒸馏：学习合成用于在线蒸馏的数据
On-policy distillation (OPD) can succeed with surprisingly few training questions, motivating a view of questions as carriers of distributional knowledge transfer. How can we assess whether a newly generated question is valuable for distillation, and learn to generate better questions---without access to its correct answer? We introduce Ask-to-Distill (\method), a framework that treats synthetic questions as distribution elicitors and optimizes the teacher supervision they expose along student-generated trajectories. A student-initialized synthesizer is optimized using a distributional surrogate for question value, computed from a frozen student--teacher pair without reference answers, correctness rewards, or per-candidate student updates. The reward combines teacher--student disagreement with teacher probability mass on the student's candidate support, together with length and diversity controls. The generated questions are filtered and deduplicated into a prompt-only bank for standard OPD. Across mathematical reasoning, code generation, and agentic tasks, our method improves average performance and teacher-gap recovery over unoptimized student-generated prompts and KL-filtered real questions, while matching or exceeding real-bank OPD in the evaluated settings. Signal analyses further show that optimized questions can elicit denser supervision and more total signal from shorter student responses, supporting elicited distributional supervision as an objective for synthetic distillation data. Our code, data and models are publicly available at https://github.com/GasolSun36/Ask_to_Distill.

在线蒸馏（OPD）可在极少训练问题下取得成功，这促使我们将问题视为分布知识迁移的载体。如何评估新生成的问题对蒸馏的价值，并学习生成更优问题——且无需访问正确答案？我们提出 Ask-to-Distill（ extit{method}）框架，将合成问题视为分布诱导器，并优化其在学生生成轨迹中暴露的教师监督信号。一个由学生初始化的合成器通过分布代理指标进行优化，该指标基于冻结的学生-教师对计算，无需参考答案、正确性奖励或候选学生更新。奖励结合教师与学生之间的分歧、教师对学生产生候选支持的概率质量，以及长度和多样性控制。生成的问题经筛选与去重后形成仅含提示的银行，用于标准 OPD。在数学推理、代码生成和智能体任务中，我们的方法在平均性能和教师差距恢复方面优于未优化的学生生成提示及 KL 过滤的真实问题，在所评估场景中达到或超过真实银行 OPD 的表现。信号分析进一步表明，优化后的问题能激发更密集的监督信号并从更短的学生响应中提取更多总信号，支持通过诱导分布监督作为合成蒸馏数据的目标。代码、数据和模型已在 https://github.com/GasolSun36/Ask_to_Distill 公开。

Harness Self-Evolution for Long-Horizon Terminal Agents
利用自演化提升长时程终端智能体
Long-horizon terminal agents may solve a task in one run yet fail across repeated runs. Stochastic errors can propagate through actions and context, creating a gap between capability and reliable execution. We present RiseHarness, a framework for iteratively improving agent harnesses with model weights fixed. It focuses on flipping tasks with both successful and failed runs under the same configuration, using successful runs as references for failure analysis. RiseHarness combines agent trajectories with internal harness traces from a runtime monitor to examine how model actions interact with command execution, feedback, context management, and completion decisions. A Harness Handbook links this evidence to harness behaviors and source code, helping identify recurring problems in mechanisms shared across tasks. RiseHarness then searches other harness frameworks for relevant implementations and adapts them as design references to the current model and execution protocol. Candidate changes undergo behavioral checks and repeated task evaluation; versions with measured gains seed the next iteration. This loop integrates suitable mechanisms from different frameworks into a single harness and uses new execution evidence to guide further changes. We evaluate task success and reliability across models, ablate harness execution evidence and external research, and test transfer to unseen benchmarks with the same model. RiseHarness treats a specific model as the unit of harness adaptation, studying how changes to shared interaction mechanisms can make existing capabilities more reliable across tasks.
中文翻译
长时程终端智能体可能在单次运行中解决问题，但在多次重复运行中失败。随机误差可在动作和上下文间传播，导致能力与可靠执行之间存在差距。我们提出 RiseHarness 框架，通过固定模型权重迭代改进智能体的执行框架。该框架聚焦于在相同配置下既有成功也有失败的运行任务，以成功运行作为失败分析的参照。RiseHarness 将智能体轨迹与运行时监控器生成的内部框架追踪相结合，分析模型动作如何与命令执行、反馈、上下文管理及完成决策相互作用。一个框架手册将这些证据与框架行为和源码关联，帮助识别跨任务共享机制中的重复问题。随后，RiseHarness 在其他框架中搜索相关实现，并将其作为设计参考适配当前模型与执行协议。候选变更经过行为检查与重复任务评估；具有可测量收益的版本将作为下一轮迭代的种子。该循环将不同框架中的合适机制整合到单一框架中，并利用新的执行证据指导进一步改进。我们在多种模型上评估任务成功率与可靠性，分析框架执行证据与外部研究的影响，并测试在未见基准上的迁移能力。RiseHarness 将特定模型作为框架适应的单位，研究对共享交互机制的调整如何使现有能力在各类任务中更具可靠性。
👤 Ruhan Wang, Yucheng Shi, Zongxia Li, Junyao Yang, Zhongzhi Li et al. 📅 2026-10-20 🔗 arXiv
