More Memory, Worse Agents: Error Reproduction and Anti-Persistence in LLM Agents
更多记忆，更差智能体：大语言模型智能体中的错误重现与反持久性
LLM agents can improve across tasks without updating model parameters by storing experience from their own executions in external memory and reusing it to guide later decisions. A central design question is whether to retain only observed trajectories and outcomes or also the rules, summaries, and skills inferred from them. Retaining these abstractions allows agents to reuse strategies without repeatedly deriving them from raw trajectories. However, errors in stored abstractions can propagate to subsequent decisions through retrieval and reuse. They can also shape execution trajectories, which then become the basis for new abstractions that reproduce the original errors. We term this feedback mechanism error reproduction: errors are regenerated through a cycle of abstraction, execution, and re-abstraction, extending their persistence beyond direct reuse of the original faulty knowledge. We propose Anti-Persistence, a memory design that accumulates trajectories and outcomes while reconstructing task-specific guidance on demand. An adaptive selector uses prior outcomes to decide whether and how to synthesize guidance, which is discarded after execution. This prevents its direct reuse as a stored object, although indirect feedback through retained trajectories remains possible. Across different benchmarks and backbones, Anti-Persistence achieves higher overall held-out success and the lowest average LLM-call cost among the evaluated memory designs. These results support separating experience accumulation from abstraction persistence in memorybased self-improvement.
中文翻译
大语言模型智能体可通过将自身执行经验存储于外部记忆中并复用以指导后续决策，实现跨任务改进而不更新模型参数。核心设计问题是：应仅保留观测到的轨迹与结果，还是也保留从中推导出的规则、摘要与技能等抽象信息？保留这些抽象有助于智能体复用策略，避免反复从原始轨迹中推导。然而，存储的抽象中的错误可能通过检索与复用传播至后续决策，并塑造执行轨迹，进而成为新抽象的基础，从而重现原始错误。我们称此反馈机制为‘错误重现’：错误通过抽象、执行、再抽象的循环被再生，使其持久性超越原始故障知识的直接复用。我们提出反持久性（Anti-Persistence）记忆设计，仅累积轨迹与结果，按需重建任务特定指引。自适应选择器利用先前结果决定是否以及如何合成指引，执行后即丢弃。这防止其作为存储对象被直接复用，尽管通过保留的轨迹仍可能存在间接反馈。在多个基准与骨干模型上，Anti-Persistence 在整体保留成功率上表现最优，且平均 LLM 调用成本最低。这些结果支持在基于记忆的自我改进中，将经验积累与抽象持久性分离开来。
👤 Ruhan Wang, Kishan Panaganti, Dongruo Zhou 📅 2026-10-19 🔗 arXiv

Do LLMs Really Lack Difference Awareness? Uncovering Heuristic Substitution in Group-Differentiating Fairness
大语言模型真的缺乏差异意识吗？揭示群体区分公平性中的启发式替代现象
In this paper, we investigate the issue of difference awareness in LLMs, defined as a model's ability to recognize and acknowledge meaningful differences between social groups in fairness decision-making. Previous studies have consistently shown that models perform poorly on such tasks, a finding commonly attributed to a lack of difference awareness. We propose an alternative account, which we term Fairness Heuristic Substitution: difference awareness is not necessarily absent, but the complex demand of simultaneously identifying relevant group differences and determining their implications for fairness decisions may lead models to substitute the simpler, more accessible equal-treatment heuristic, i.e., formal equality. Based on this hypothesis, we introduce TDD, a Two-stage Distinguish-Decide framework that explicitly decomposes fairness reasoning into first distinguishing contextually relevant group differences and then making the corresponding decision, thereby discouraging reliance on formal-equality shortcuts. (i) Across four benchmarks and ten models ranging from billions to trillions of parameters, TDD significantly outperforms one-stage baselines, even one strengthened through simulated test-set leakage. (ii) Combining TDD with neuron-level contrastive probing provides mechanistic evidence consistent with the Fairness Heuristic Substitution hypothesis by revealing two signatures of the formal-equality: a compact, locally coordinated representational structure and a pronounced activation bias under default reasoning. (iii) TDD's stage-wise decomposition uncovers overlooked failure modes in fairness reasoning, including heuristic displacement and misaligned stage dynamics.
中文翻译
本文研究了大语言模型在公平决策中对差异意识的问题，即模型识别并承认社会群体间有意义差异的能力。以往研究一致表明模型在此类任务上表现不佳，通常归因于缺乏差异意识。我们提出一种替代解释——公平性启发式替代：差异意识并非必然缺失，而是同时识别相关群体差异并判断其对公平决策的影响这一复杂需求，可能导致模型退而采用更简单、更易获取的平等对待启发式（即形式平等）。基于此假设，我们引入TDD（两阶段区分-决策框架），显式将公平推理分解为先区分情境相关的群体差异，再作出相应决策，从而避免依赖形式平等的捷径。 (i) 在四个基准测试和十种从数十亿到数万亿参数的模型上，TDD显著优于单阶段基线，甚至优于经过模拟测试集泄露增强的基线。(ii) 将TDD与神经元级对比探测结合，提供了与公平性启发式替代假设一致的机制证据，揭示了形式平等的两个特征：紧凑且局部协调的表征结构，以及默认推理下的显著激活偏差。(iii) TDD的阶段分解揭示了公平推理中被忽视的失败模式，包括启发式位移和阶段动态错配。
👤 Jingshen Zhang, Bo Wang, Yanlin Fu, Yuewen Dang, Dongming Zhao et al. 📅 2026-10-18 🔗 arXiv

High-dimensional layer-state trajectories reveal the internal dynamics of transformer inference
高维层状态轨迹揭示Transformer推理的内部动态
Mechanistic studies of large language models localize function in parameters, attention heads or neurons, but those parts do not specify how one inference unfolds. We treat the ordered layer-state trajectory as the empirical process and test whether layer order defines a measurable computation. Across representative Qwen, Llama and Gemma checkpoints, realized order produced paths 1.60--2.24 times as aligned with their endpoint chord as endpoint-preserving shuffles. The order effect transferred to a lexical task, but was stronger in three random initializations of the same Qwen architecture, identifying an architecture-compatible scaffold rather than a training-specific law. Direct hidden states provided stronger mechanism coordinates than vocabulary readbacks, and a task-defined coordinate made candidate competition computable. After additive interventions failed, local transition actuators selectively controlled internal trajectories across checkpoints. A confidence gate then drove 17 of 87 held-out Qwen closure trajectories across the full-vocabulary top-1 candidate boundary while leaving all 203 non-closure top-1 outputs unchanged. Safety-matched action reassignment performed similarly, locating the gain in selective gating and the bounded actuator family rather than fine-grained dose assignment. Layer order exposes an observable, computable and behaviourally consequential process of transformer inference without establishing a complete theory of LLM dynamics or control of answer correctness.
中文翻译
大型语言模型的机制研究通常将功能定位在参数、注意力头或神经元上，但这些部分无法说明一次推理过程如何展开。我们将有序的层状态轨迹视为经验过程，并检验层序是否定义了一种可测量的计算。在代表性Qwen、Llama和Gemma检查点中，实际顺序产生的路径与终点弦的对齐程度是终点保持随机打乱的1.60至2.24倍。该顺序效应传递到了词汇任务，但在同一Qwen架构的三个随机初始化中更为显著，表明其属于与架构兼容的结构框架，而非训练特定规律。直接隐藏状态提供的机制坐标比词汇回溯更强，而任务定义的坐标使候选竞争变得可计算。在添加干预失败后，局部转移执行器能够选择性地控制跨检查点的内部轨迹。随后，一个置信度门控机制促使87个未见Qwen闭包轨迹中的17条跨越全词汇top-1候选边界，同时保持全部203个非闭包top-1输出不变。与安全匹配的动作重新分配表现相似，表明收益来源于选择性门控和有界执行器族，而非精细剂量分配。层序揭示了一种可观测、可计算且行为上有意义的Transformer推理过程，而无需建立LLM动态或答案正确性控制的完整理论。

NarraSpace: Multimodal Scene-Grounded Evidence for Picture-Description Dementia Classification
NarraSpace：用于图片描述痴呆症分类的多模态场景基础证据
Picture-description dementia classification uses a shared visual scene, but a transcript-level prediction alone does not identify the scene evidence underlying its inputs. NarraSpace links words and speech times to named picture elements and retrieves utterances for predefined scene events. Narrative-spatial descriptors summarize coverage and temporal organization. A frozen language model contrasts each event's text under examples from Alzheimer's disease and healthy-control training participants, alongside transcript surprisal and coverage measurements. Retained source records link event scores to their source utterances, speech intervals, and lexical matches. On the official 48-participant test set of the Alzheimer's Dementia Recognition through Spontaneous Speech benchmark, a fixed support vector classifier achieves $80.88\%\pm2.56\%$ mean accuracy over fifty seeds; averaged probabilities yield $81.25\%$ accuracy and $91.32\%$ area under the receiver operating characteristic curve. NarraSpace makes the construction of scene-linked classification inputs explicit and inspectable by connecting event-indexed language-model measurements to their source utterances, speech timing, and extraction records. Such source-linked measurements could support clinician review of picture descriptions by presenting quantitative summaries alongside the original speech evidence.
中文翻译
图片描述痴呆症分类依赖于共享视觉场景，但仅基于转录文本的预测无法揭示其输入背后的场景证据。NarraSpace将词语和语音时间与命名的图片元素关联，并检索预定义场景事件的语句。叙事-空间描述符总结了覆盖范围和时间组织结构。一个冻结的语言模型对比每个事件在阿尔茨海默病患者和健康对照组训练参与者中的文本表现，同时结合转录意外性和覆盖度测量。保留的源记录将事件得分与其原始语句、语音时间段和词汇匹配关联起来。在阿尔茨海默病自发言语识别基准的官方48名参与者测试集上，固定支持向量分类器在五十次随机种子下达到$80.88\%\pm2.56\%$的平均准确率；平均概率则达到$81.25\%$准确率和$91.32\%$受试者工作特征曲线下面积。NarraSpace使构建与场景关联的分类输入变得明确且可检查，通过将事件索引的语言模型测量结果与原始语句、语音时间及提取记录相连接。此类源链接测量可为临床医生提供定量摘要，辅助审查图片描述内容。

Capable Teacher, Myopic Scorer: Reward-Free Teacher Repair for Agentic On-Policy Distillation
能干的教师，短视的评分器：无奖励教师修复用于代理在线策略蒸馏
Agentic on-policy distillation (OPD) trains a student agent on its own trajectories while a teacher scores every token. A natural explanation of where it goes wrong is compounding error: the student's mistakes carry its trajectories beyond the region where the teacher is reliable, so recent methods keep training inside that region, through episode-length curricula, teacher-guided turns, prefix replay, or teacher intervention where the two disagree. We show this explanation is incomplete. From the states where the student departs most from the teacher, the teacher's own takeovers still finish the task, yet the student trained on its token scores keeps running out of turns. We attribute this to the myopia of next-token scoring. The teacher rates each token without seeing where the episode ends, so its scores let through the safe, delaying moves that outcome-based RL would punish with zero reward, a penalty agentic OPD lacks. We call this difference between what the teacher does and what it scores the rollout-scorer gap. We propose Rollout-to-Scorer Distillation (R2S), which repairs the scorer before distillation by mining high-disagreement states from the untrained student, running teacher takeovers from them, keeping the states where enough takeovers pass a reward-free check such as consensus or an LLM judge, and fine-tuning the teacher to give its takeover-informed scores on every assistant token from the divergence turn onward, without the takeover in context. The repaired teacher is frozen as the sole scorer for standard OPD, requiring no task reward during repair or training. Across three environments, two teachers and four students, R2S outperforms three state-of-the-art agentic-OPD methods in all twelve teacher-student-environment cells, by 12.8 success points on average over the best of them in each cell. Project page: https://tree-shu-zhao.github.io/r2s/.
中文翻译
代理在线策略蒸馏（OPD）在学生代理自身轨迹上进行训练，同时由教师对每个标记进行评分。一种自然的错误解释是误差累积：学生的错误使其轨迹偏离教师可靠的区域，因此近期方法通过课程设计、教师引导转向、前缀重播或教师干预等方式在该区域内持续训练。我们指出这一解释并不完整。从学生偏离教师最严重的状态出发，教师自身的接管仍能完成任务，但基于令牌评分的学生训练却会耗尽回合数。我们将其归因于下一令牌评分的短视性。教师在未看到整个任务终点的情况下评分每个令牌，因此允许安全但拖延的行动通过，而基于结果的强化学习会以零奖励惩罚这些行为——这正是代理OPD所缺乏的惩罚机制。我们称教师实际行为与其评分之间的差异为‘轨迹-评分差距’。我们提出‘轨迹到评分蒸馏’（R2S），在蒸馏前通过挖掘未训练学生中的高分歧状态，从这些状态运行教师接管，保留足够接管成功并通过无奖励检查（如共识或LLM判断）的状态，然后微调教师，使其在分歧点之后对每个助手令牌给出基于接管信息的评分，而不需在上下文中包含接管过程。修复后的教师被冻结为标准OPD的唯一评分器，修复和训练过程中均无需任务奖励。在三个环境、两个教师和四个学生组合中，R2S在全部十二个教师-学生-环境单元中均优于三种最先进的代理OPD方法，平均领先最佳方法12.8个成功点。项目页面：https://tree-shu-zhao.github.io/r2s/。
👤 Shu Zhao, Tan Yu 📅 2026-10-20 🔗 arXiv
[2610.28577]
agent-memoryagentic-rl
Cache Engineering for Agents
代理的缓存工程
Large Language Model (LLM) agents can handle increasingly complex tasks, navigating long trajectories of observations and actions. As these trajectories grow, key-value (KV) cache I/O and storage become the dominant cost. Trajectories can also exceed the LLM's maximum context length, requiring control over active cache growth. Prior work uses context engineering to reduce cache size by trimming or summarizing history. However, modifying the trajectory prefix invalidates subsequent cached states and triggers costly re-prefilling of retained context. Task accuracy can also suffer when too little context is retained to limit re-prefill costs. We propose cache engineering as a new scheme. It allows agents to directly control their active KV cache without modifying the trajectory or re-prefilling retained context. Specifically, agents can proactively use drop to exclude selected cache from future attention and repos to adjust retained keys' position encodings. We formalize these cache operations and identify history dependence as a serving challenge: identical token sequences can yield different cache states under different cache operation sequences, making prefix matching insufficient for correct cache reuse. Thus, we co-design the AKV serving engine. It provides a stateless interface compatible with existing serving APIs, while internally managing stateful cache reuse, eviction, and restoration. Experiments show that a tool-response rolling-drop cache policy improves average benchmark scores by 1.7 points and reduces average cost by 27.7%, compared with conventional threshold-triggered summarization. With AKV, it achieves up to 1.2x the output throughput and a 1.5x speedup in P95 request latency. Recursive self-improvement of cache policies further reduces cost by 7.1% over expert design. Our code is at https://github.com/thu-nics/AKV.
中文翻译
大型语言模型（LLM）代理能够处理日益复杂的任务，需在长轨迹的观察与动作中导航。随着轨迹增长，键值（KV）缓存的I/O和存储成本成为主要瓶颈。轨迹还可能超过LLM的最大上下文长度，需要对活跃缓存增长进行控制。先前工作通过上下文工程减少缓存大小，例如修剪或摘要历史。然而，修改轨迹前缀会破坏后续缓存状态，触发昂贵的重新填充操作。若保留过少上下文，则可能导致重填充成本过高，影响任务准确性。我们提出一种新的方案——缓存工程，使代理可直接控制其活跃KV缓存，而无需修改轨迹或重新填充保留的上下文。具体而言，代理可主动使用drop排除未来注意力中的特定缓存，或使用repos调整保留键的位置编码。我们形式化了这些缓存操作，并识别出历史依赖性作为服务挑战：相同的标记序列在不同缓存操作序列下可能产生不同的缓存状态，使得前缀匹配不足以正确复用缓存。因此，我们共同设计了AKV服务引擎。它提供与现有服务API兼容的无状态接口，同时在内部管理有状态的缓存复用、驱逐与恢复。实验表明，工具响应滚动drop缓存策略相比传统阈值触发摘要，平均基准得分提升1.7分，平均成本降低27.7%。使用AKV后，输出吞吐量最高可达1.2倍，P95请求延迟速度提升1.5倍。递归自改进缓存策略进一步比专家设计降低7.1%的成本。代码地址：https://github.com/thu-nics/AKV。
👤 Tianyu Fu, Ruoxi Wang, Zihan Min, Zhongqi Ren, Zhihao Li et al. 📅 2026-10-20 🔗 arXiv

Large Flow Language Models
大流语言模型
Continuous flow language models offer a path to parallel generation with tools for control and acceleration unavailable to discrete diffusion, yet effective techniques for building them at the billion-parameter scale remain less established. We present Lafa (Large flow language model), a fast, high-performing flow language model scaled to 8B parameters in a data-efficient manner. Lafa is a blockwise flow model with causal attention on the prefix and bidirectional, asynchronous flow within each block. This design enables efficient training by adapting pretrained autoregressive (AR) models and fast inference via prefix caching and blockwise parallel decoding. We train Lafa at 0.5B, 7B, and 8B scales with fewer than 30B tokens, more efficient than previous diffusion models, and evaluate it on instruction following, reasoning, and coding, where the learned flow matches or surpasses the quality of AR and can be distilled into flow maps for one- and few-step generation. Notably, flow training preserves or even improves pretrained AR capabilities via a multi-token prediction effect, which we leverage in a self-speculative decoding scheme that verifies flow-based drafts autoregressively within a single model. This yields 2.54x the throughput of an AR model at comparable quality, outperforming multi-token prediction and discrete diffusion methods. Our findings show that continuous flows can serve as a scalable foundation for language modeling.
中文翻译
连续流语言模型为并行生成提供了路径，具备离散扩散模型所不具备的控制与加速工具，但在十亿参数规模上构建有效技术仍不够成熟。我们提出Lafa（大流语言模型），一种快速、高性能的流语言模型，以数据高效方式扩展至80亿参数。Lafa是一种分块流模型，前缀采用因果注意力，块内采用双向、异步流机制。该设计通过适配预训练自回归（AR）模型实现高效训练，并通过前缀缓存和分块并行解码实现快速推理。我们在0.5B、7B和8B规模上训练Lafa，使用的训练数据少于300亿个token，效率高于以往扩散模型，并在指令遵循、推理和编程任务上进行评估，其学习到的流表现达到或超越自回归模型水平，且可提炼为流图以实现单步或少数步骤生成。值得注意的是，流训练通过多标记预测效应保持甚至提升了预训练AR能力，我们利用此特性设计了一种自推测解码方案，在单一模型内自回归验证基于流的草稿，从而实现2.54倍于AR模型的吞吐量，且在相当质量下优于多标记预测和离散扩散方法。我们的发现表明，连续流可作为语言建模的可扩展基础。

BitMTP: When Multi-Token Prediction Meets Low-Bit Large Language Models
BitMTP：多标记预测与低比特大语言模型的融合
For efficient deployment of large language models (LLMs), methods of ultra-low-bit backbones reduce weight storage, memory traffic, and linear-layer execution cost, while multi-token prediction (MTP) methods reduce sequential decoding steps by predicting multiple future tokens in parallel. Although complementary, the direct combination of these methods cannot accelerate decoding at comparable quality: masked drafting lacks intermediate token input supplied in next-token training, while wider computation and causal key-value (KV) updates can offset the savings from fewer steps. To address these challenges, we propose BitMTP, an algorithm-system co-design framework for efficient MTP on ultra-low-bit LLM backbones. A joint next-token prediction and MTP objective equips a single model with both autoregressive (AR) generation and block-causal drafting. Confidence-only decoding, speculative-only decoding, and hybrid decoding provide different quality-throughput operating points without using separate draft and verifier models. Accepted-prefix alignment keeps the committed prefix, materialized causal KV boundary, and CUDA Graph replay state consistent for continued generation. BitMTP uses this contract to select width-specialized low-bit execution and reuse captured graphs over shared KV storage. On NVIDIA H20, BitMTP with hybrid decoding achieves five-task mean speedups of 2.2x on BitNet b1.58-2B and 2.1-2.4x on 1.7B-4B Bonsai models at comparable task quality. On RTX 3090 and Jetson Orin, it further achieves up to 2.0x in five-task mean throughput and 3.3x on MBPP+, respectively. These results demonstrate that BitMTP accelerates ultra-low-bit LLM generation across backbones, model sizes, and GPU types. The source code is released at https://github.com/PolyU-VCLab/BitMTP
中文翻译
为了高效部署大语言模型（LLMs），超低比特骨干网络方法可减少权重存储、内存流量和线性层执行成本，而多标记预测（MTP）方法通过并行预测多个未来标记来减少序列解码步骤。尽管两者互补，但直接结合这些方法在保持相当质量的前提下无法实现解码加速：掩码草稿缺乏下一标记训练中提供的中间标记输入，而更宽的计算量和因果键值（KV）更新可能抵消因步骤减少带来的收益。为解决这些问题，我们提出BitMTP，一种面向超低比特LLM骨干网络的高效MTP算法-系统协同设计框架。联合的下一标记预测与MTP目标使单一模型同时具备自回归（AR）生成和块因果草稿能力。仅基于置信度的解码、仅推测性解码以及混合解码提供了无需使用独立草稿和验证模型的不同质量-吞吐量工作点。已接受前缀对齐保持了已提交前缀、实际因果KV边界和CUDA图重放状态的一致性，以支持连续生成。BitMTP利用此契约选择宽度特化的低比特执行方式，并在共享KV存储上复用捕获的图。在NVIDIA H20上，采用混合解码的BitMTP在BitNet b1.58-2B模型上实现了五项任务平均2.2倍加速，在1.7B-4B Bonsai模型上实现2.1-2.4倍加速，且任务质量相当。在RTX 3090和Jetson Orin上，其五任务平均吞吐量进一步提升至2.0倍，MBPP+任务达3.3倍。这些结果表明，BitMTP可在不同骨干网络、模型规模和GPU类型下加速超低比特LLM生成。源代码已发布于https://github.com/PolyU-VCLab/BitMTP
👤 Ning Zhang, Shihao Wang, Jinrui Zhang, Chaodong Xiao, Lei Zhang 📅 2026-10-20 🔗 arXiv

Asymmetric Looped Transformers: Fixed Global Representations for an Iterative Decoder
非对称循环变压器：用于迭代解码的固定全局表示
Looped Transformers increase effective depth by repeatedly applying shared layers, offering a way to scale computation without increasing parameter count. However, full-stack looping places context construction and iterative state refinement within the same recurrent computation, without explicitly distinguishing their roles. We introduce the Asymmetric Looped Transformer (AsymLoop), which separates single-pass context encoding from recurrent state refinement. A single-pass causal encoder produces layer-specific Global key-value (KV) caches that remain fixed across iterations, while a weight-tied recurrent decoder generates Local KV from its evolving state. Joint attention over both sources under a single softmax allows each update to integrate persistent contextual information with the current intermediate state. For training, we apply a heavy-tail-guided layerwise learning-rate method to the encoder and decoder. Experiments on FineWeb-Edu across four model scales, from 60M to 1B, show consistently lower validation perplexity and higher average downstream accuracy than Full Loop under matched effective depth and training token budgets. At 1B, AsymLoop improves average zero-shot accuracy across seven commonsense reasoning benchmarks from 49.58% to 50.85%. Further experiments extend these benefits to longer training budgets, an OLMo-style backbone, and sparse mixture-of-experts models. These results support asymmetric context encoding and state refinement as an effective design for recurrent language models.
中文翻译
循环变压器通过重复应用共享层增加有效深度，提供了一种在不增加参数量的情况下扩展计算的方法。然而，全栈循环将上下文构建与迭代状态优化置于同一递归计算中，未能明确区分二者的作用。我们提出非对称循环变压器（AsymLoop），将单次上下文编码与递归状态优化分离。单次因果编码器生成各层特定的全局键值（KV）缓存，这些缓存在迭代过程中保持不变；而共享权重的递归解码器则从其演化状态生成局部KV。通过单一softmax对两种来源进行联合注意力机制，每次更新都能整合持久的上下文信息与当前中间状态。在训练中，我们采用重尾引导的分层学习率方法分别应用于编码器和解码器。在FineWeb-Edu数据集上，针对从60M到1B四种模型规模的实验表明，AsymLoop在匹配的有效深度和训练token预算下，验证困惑度持续更低，下游平均准确率更高。在1B规模下，AsymLoop将七个常识推理基准的平均零样本准确率从49.58%提升至50.85%。进一步实验表明，该优势可扩展至更长的训练预算、OLMo风格骨干网络以及稀疏专家混合模型。这些结果支持非对称上下文编码与状态优化作为递归语言模型的一种有效设计。
👤 Cheng Liang, Tengfei Zhang, Wenjie DU, Jianyu Wu, Changlian Ma et al. 📅 2026-10-20 🔗 arXiv

The Instruction Tax on Factual Recall: Base Models Can Beat Their Instruction-Tuned Counterparts
指令调用对事实回忆的税负：基础模型可超越其指令微调版本
We present our solution to the AKBC LM-KBC 2026 Shared Task, where we achieved a score of 0.6836 on the official leaderboard, more than double the baseline of 0.2964. This task requires generating all possible objects for subject-relation pairs using a language model with up to 32 billion parameters, with no retrieval or fine-tuning involved. We show that the prompting methodology is as important as the model architecture: while the instruction-tuned model version achieves 0.5681 with its chat interface, the base model achieves 0.6836 when prompted using text completion. Thus, instruction tuning decreases factual recall; we refer to this effect as the instruction tax, which is caused by the model's weights rather than the prompting method. Our final solution is fairly straightforward: we prompt the base model with some examples from the training data, generate 64--256 outputs, and retain only the repeated ones. Two specialized methods are then applied for each relation: one improves the recall of large answer sets, and the other verifies a person's vitality before predicting their place of death.
中文翻译
我们提交了针对AKBC LM-KBC 2026共享任务的解决方案，在官方排行榜上取得了0.6836的分数，超过基线0.2964的两倍以上。该任务要求使用参数量高达320亿的语言模型生成给定主语-关系对的所有可能对象，且不涉及检索或微调。我们发现提示方法的重要性与模型架构相当：尽管指令微调版本通过聊天接口仅达到0.5681，但基础模型在使用文本补全方式提示时达到了0.6836。因此，指令微调降低了事实回忆能力；我们将这一现象称为‘指令税’，其根源在于模型权重而非提示方式。最终解决方案较为简单：我们使用训练数据中的若干示例提示基础模型，生成64至256个输出，并仅保留重复项。随后针对每种关系应用两种专门方法：一种提升大答案集的召回率，另一种在预测死亡地点前验证人物的生命状态。
👤 Christophe Khalil, Aya Mourad 📅 2026-10-20 🔗 arXiv

Object-Oriented Harness: Evolving Agent Harnesses through Object-Oriented Design
面向对象的Harness：通过面向对象设计演化智能体Harness
Agent harnesses are executable code that specifies the prompts, tools, and control flow around a language model. A single global harness can struggle with heterogeneous tasks that require different execution strategies. In this work, we introduce Object-Oriented Harness (OO-Harness), a harness optimization framework that evolves a hierarchy of executable harness classes using object-oriented design. During training, an evolution agent uses execution records to create a family of specialized harnesses, each implemented as a subclass of the base or an existing specialized harness via inheritance. At test time, a harness router uses routing lessons accumulated during training to select a specific harness for each task. Across three long-horizon benchmarks spanning business workflows, workspace tasks, and tool use, OO-Harness achieves the best performance among the compared methods, with an absolute improvement of up to 15% over the base. Extensive ablation studies examine the contributions of its core components.
中文翻译
智能体Harness是执行代码，用于定义语言模型周围的提示、工具和控制流程。单一全局Harness在面对需要不同执行策略的异构任务时可能表现不佳。本文提出面向对象Harness（OO-Harness），一种基于面向对象设计演化的Harness优化框架。在训练过程中，进化代理利用执行记录创建一系列专用Harness类，每个类通过继承基础Harness或已有专用Harness实现。在测试阶段，Harness路由器利用训练期间积累的路由经验，为每个任务选择特定Harness。在涵盖商业工作流、办公任务和工具使用的三个长期基准上，OO-Harness在对比方法中表现最佳，相对于基础模型有最高达15%的绝对性能提升。大量消融实验分析了其核心组件的贡献。
👤 Gaotang Li, Bhavana Dalvi Mishra, Yifei Ming, Yanfei Chen, Zhongying CuiZhu et al. 📅 2026-10-20 🔗 arXiv

From Abstraction to Action: DSLs for Natural Language Abstraction Interpretation and Execution
从抽象到行动：用于自然语言抽象解释与执行的领域特定语言
Natural-language instructions often rely on high-level abstractions, such as asking an agent to ``stack blocks in a pyramid.'' Interpreting abstract instructions requires models to both identify the abstraction intended by the speaker, and unroll it into the many concrete actions that instantiate it. Language models struggle here: prior work has shown that performance degrades as the level of abstraction in the instruction rises. To bridge the gap between natural language abstraction and concrete actions, we propose to borrow from coding environments the notion of a {\em domain-specific language} (DSL), an explicit representation layer whose documented primitives name recurring abstractions that expand into concrete actions. We demonstrate the efficacy of DSLs for NL interpretation on \textsc{Hexagons}, a visually-grounded instruction-following task graded for abstraction. We further ask whether language models can induce such DSLs themselves from domain data. Our results show that while code generation consistently improves over direct inference, code generation over a human-curated DSL yields dramatic gains, up to 10 absolute F1 points, reaching the human skyline, with gains concentrated at {\em higher} abstraction levels. We confirm this pattern on the \textsc{Text2CAD} benchmark. At the same time, model-induced DSLs recover only part of this gain, remaining inconsistent across runs and short of their human-curated counterpart. Overall, the right representation closes much of the gap caused by high abstraction --- but inducing that layer of representation automatically is where models still fall short, and where we see a strong opportunity for the community to make progress.
中文翻译
自然语言指令常常依赖于高层次的抽象，例如要求智能体“将积木堆成金字塔”。解释抽象指令需要模型既能识别说话者意图的抽象概念，又能将其展开为实现该抽象所需的众多具体动作。语言模型在此方面表现不佳：先前的研究表明，随着指令抽象程度的提高，性能会下降。为弥合自然语言抽象与具体动作之间的差距，我们借鉴编程环境中的概念，提出使用{ em领域特定语言}（DSL），即一种显式的表示层，其文档化的基本操作命名了可重复出现的抽象，这些抽象可扩展为具体动作。我们在一个以视觉为基础、按抽象程度评分的指令遵循任务 extsc{Hexagons}上展示了DSL在自然语言理解中的有效性。我们进一步探讨语言模型是否能从领域数据中自行推导出此类DSL。结果表明，尽管代码生成始终优于直接推理，但在人类精心设计的DSL上进行代码生成带来了显著提升，最高达10个绝对F1分数点，达到人类水平，且增益集中在更高抽象层级。我们在 extsc{Text2CAD}基准测试中也验证了这一模式。同时，模型自动生成的DSL仅恢复部分增益，且在不同运行间表现不一致，远不及人工设计的DSL。总体而言，合适的表示能够大幅缩小高抽象带来的差距——但自动推导这一表示层仍是模型的短板，也是社区亟需突破的关键方向。

Reward Misspecification Degrades Chain-of-Thought Monitorability
奖励设定错误会降低思维链的可监控性
Monitoring the chain-of-thought (CoT) of language models (LMs) is a key strategy for detecting and preventing misbehaviors for AI safety. As such, it is critical to identify reinforcement learning (RL) practices that lead to unmonitorable chain-of-thought. One such practice may be unavoidable: reward misspecification. As RL environments become more complex, it is infeasible to have perfect verification, and models are rewarded for behaviors that are different from the true task intent. We show that reward misspecification alone already degrades monitorability. We create a testbed with pairs of true reward aligned with task intent and misspecified rewards that favor a different behavior, so that reward hacking can be induced by training and labeled verifiably. Across three models and three tasks, CoT monitorability remains low as models' hack rates increase: the CoTs keep reasoning about the task intent while the outputs violate the intent and get rewarded. To mitigate unmonitorability without prior knowledge of the true reward or the hack, we collect rollouts with diverse rewards across training steps and use a LM to infer a reward description. We then inject the description into the input, which teaches the model to verbalize the reward. This reward description training improves monitorability by an average of 24% or 42% absolute, depending on the metric, across all models and tasks, and yields CoTs that reason about the misspecified reward explicitly. Code is available at https://github.com/Lorenayannnnn/reward-misspecification-degrades-cot-monitorability.
中文翻译
监控语言模型（LMs）的思维链（CoT）是检测和防止AI安全问题的关键策略。因此，识别导致思维链不可监控的强化学习（RL）实践至关重要。其中一种可能无法避免的做法是奖励设定错误。随着RL环境变得越来越复杂，完全验证变得不可行，模型会被奖励那些与真实任务意图不同的行为。我们证明，仅奖励设定错误本身就会降低可监控性。我们构建了一个测试平台，包含与任务意图对齐的真实奖励和偏向不同行为的错误奖励，从而可在训练中诱导出可验证的奖励劫持。在三个模型和三个任务中，随着模型的劫持率上升，思维链的可监控性持续低下：尽管思维链仍在围绕任务意图进行推理，但输出却违背了意图并获得奖励。为在无需事先知道真实奖励或劫持方式的情况下缓解不可监控问题，我们收集了训练过程中多样化奖励的轨迹，并利用语言模型推断出奖励描述。随后将该描述注入输入，使模型学会用语言表达奖励。这种奖励描述训练使所有模型和任务的可监控性平均提升了24%或42%（取决于指标），并生成了明确讨论错误奖励的思维链。

Adaptive Replay Refinement: Allocating Test-Time Compute Between Revision and Resampling
自适应重播优化：在修订与重采样之间分配测试时计算资源
Test-time scaling allocates additional inference compute to refining existing solutions or generating new ones. This raises a fundamental question: Should I keep improving this solution, or stop working on it and try a completely new solution? Existing methods make this decision using fixed refinement schedules, self-verification judgments, or learned controllers, which can waste compute or rely on signals that may be incorrect or require additional training. We introduce Adaptive Replay Refinement (ARR), a training-free method that detects when a stationary revision reaches a fixed point and reallocates the released compute to a new independent sample; replay decoding treats the current solution as a draft and verifies unchanged tokens in parallel, enabling efficient fixed-point detection. Across seven models from five families and seven benchmarks, ARR matches majority voting on three of five mathematical reasoning benchmarks at equal compute, while reducing critical-path serial passes by up to 2.4 times. On MATH500, ARR achieves 84.6% accuracy with only 41% of the serial passes required by majority voting. These results demonstrate that detecting refinement exhaustion enables adaptive reallocation from revision to independent sampling, improving inference efficiency while highlighting the limits of self-revision. Code is available at https://github.com/AhsanBilal7/adaptive_refine.
中文翻译
测试时扩展通过分配额外的推理计算来改进现有解决方案或生成新方案。这引发了一个根本性问题：我应该继续改进当前方案，还是停止并尝试一个全新的方案？现有方法通过固定的优化周期、自我验证判断或学习控制器来做决策，但这些方法可能浪费计算资源，或依赖于可能错误的信号，甚至需要额外训练。我们提出自适应重播优化（ARR），一种无需训练的方法，能够检测到静态修订是否达到固定点，并将释放的计算资源重新分配给新的独立样本；重播解码将当前方案视为草稿，并并行验证未改变的标记，从而实现高效的固定点检测。在来自五个模型家族的七种模型和七个基准测试中，ARR在相同计算量下达到了多数投票在五项数学推理基准中的三项表现，同时将关键路径的串行步骤减少了最多2.4倍。在MATH500上，ARR仅需多数投票所需串行步骤的41%，便实现了84.6%的准确率。这些结果表明，检测优化耗尽能够实现从修订到独立采样的自适应资源再分配，在提升推理效率的同时揭示了自我修订的局限性。
👤 Ahsan Bilal, Muhammad Ahmed Mohsin, Muhammad Umer, Ayesha Mohsin, Sarmad Bilal et al. 📅 2026-10-20 🔗 arXiv

Can Language Models Get "Aphasia" From Neuronal Ablation?
语言模型在神经元消融后会患上‘失语症’吗？
Progressive neuronal loss is the hallmark of neurodegenerative disease, yet how loss of neurons shapes the pattern of cognitive decline remains poorly understood. Here we systematically ablate neurons (computational units) in language models spanning recurrent long short-term memory (LSTM) and Transformer architectures and apply limited fine-tuning as a simple proxy for compensatory plasticity. With whole-unit ablation, LSTMs degrade gradually with syntactic and semantic faculties declining in parallel across mild-to-moderate damage. By contrast, Transformers exhibit late threshold-like collapse under feedforward multi-layer perceptron (MLP) ablation and smoother sigmoid-like decline under attention-head ablation. Qualitatively, MLP-ablated models continue to make within-category semantic substitutions (semantic paraphasias) alongside rising non-responses, paralleling Wernicke-type aphasia, while attention-head ablation produces a trend toward loss of semantic specificity similar to the semantic variant of primary progressive aphasia (svPPA). These findings indicate that feedforward and attentional pathways have functionally distinct failure modes, suggesting that architecture-dependent failure profiles may offer new computational perspectives on aphasic syndromes and neurodegenerative diseases as an in silico model. Notably, early accuracy retention under Transformer attention-head ablation qualitatively parallels pre-symptomatic reserve before the 40--60% neuronal loss reported by Gomez-Isla et al. (1996) when clinical symptoms emerge in Alzheimer's disease, whereas the steadier LSTM decline parallels the symptomatic progression described by DeKosky and Scheff (1990) and Terry et al. (1991).
中文翻译
神经退行性疾病以进行性神经元丧失为特征，但神经元损失如何影响认知衰退模式仍不清楚。本文系统地对涵盖循环长短期记忆（LSTM）和Transformer架构的语言模型中的神经元（计算单元）进行消融，并采用有限微调作为补偿可塑性的简单代理。全单元消融实验显示，LSTM在轻度至中度损伤下逐渐退化，句法与语义能力同步下降；而Transformer在前馈多层感知机（MLP）消融下表现出晚期阈值式崩溃，在注意力头消融下则呈现更平滑的S型衰减。定性分析表明，MLP消融后的模型持续产生类别内语义替换（语义错语），同时非应答增多，类似于韦尼克型失语症；注意力头消融则导致语义特异性丧失的趋势，类似原发性进行性失语症的语义变异型（svPPA）。这些发现表明前馈与注意力路径具有功能上不同的失效模式，提示基于架构的失效特征可能为失语综合征及神经退行性疾病提供新的计算视角。值得注意的是，Transformer在注意力头消融初期仍保持较高准确率，这在定性上类似于阿尔茨海默病临床症状出现前（Gomez-Isla等，1996年报告40–60%神经元损失时）的无症状储备期，而LSTM更平稳的退化过程则与DeKosky和Scheff（1990）、Terry等（1991）描述的症状进展相吻合。
👤 Pratik Worah, Randall Bateman 📅 2026-10-20 🔗 arXiv

Temporal Evidence-Parameter Co-Alignment for LLM Reasoning over Temporal Knowledge Graphs
面向时间知识图谱的LLM推理中时间证据-参数协同对齐
Large Language Models (LLMs) often struggle with temporally grounded reasoning because their static parametric memory is misaligned with the dynamic evolution of real-world knowledge. Although RAG and Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning (PEFT) provide practical ways to enhance LLMs, they remain insufficient for temporal reasoning. Existing retrieval methods typically prioritize semantic relevance while overlooking temporal validity, event order, and evolutionary dependencies, resulting in evidence that is relevant in isolation but temporally fragmented. Meanwhile, conventional PEFT methods usually employ fixed and input-agnostic low-rank updates, making it difficult to capture time-dependent entity semantics and relation drift. To address these limitations, we propose Temporal Evidence-Parameter Co-Alignment (TEPCA), a framework for LLM reasoning over TKGs. TEPCA aligns LLM reasoning with temporal knowledge at both the evidence and parameter levels. At the evidence level, it introduces time-constrained Dynamic Relevance-Guided Path (DRGP) pruning to preserve globally coherent temporal paths, together with Mutual-Information (MI)-based pruning to retain query-informative local facts under temporal constraints. At the parameter level, it integrates Dynamic-Gated LoRA (DG-LoRA), a timestamp-conditioned low-rank adaptation mechanism that dynamically modulates the effective adaptation subspace according to temporal context. This design enables LLMs to better capture fine-grained entity evolution and time-dependent relation semantics while preserving parameter efficiency. Experimental results on two TKG benchmarks demonstrate that TEPCA achieves state-of-the-art performance. Additional evaluations of DG-LoRA on eight commonsense QA datasets further verify its general input-adaptive capacity, scalability across LLM backbones, and interpretability.
中文翻译
大型语言模型（LLMs）在时间锚定推理方面常遇困难，因其静态参数记忆与现实世界知识的动态演变不匹配。尽管检索增强生成（RAG）和参数高效微调（PEFT）提供了实用的增强方法，但在时间推理方面仍显不足。现有检索方法通常优先考虑语义相关性，忽视时间有效性、事件顺序和演化依赖关系，导致证据虽孤立相关但时间上碎片化。传统PEFT方法通常采用固定且输入无关的低秩更新，难以捕捉随时间变化的实体语义与关系漂移。为此，我们提出时间证据-参数协同对齐（TEPCA）框架，用于LLM在时间知识图谱（TKG）上的推理。TEPCA在证据与参数层面均实现与时间知识的对齐：在证据层面，引入时间约束的动态相关性引导路径（DRGP）剪枝以保留全局一致的时间路径，并结合基于互信息（MI）的剪枝以在时间约束下保留查询相关信息的局部事实；在参数层面，集成时间戳条件化的动态门控LoRA（DG-LoRA），一种根据时间上下文动态调节有效适配子空间的低秩适应机制。该设计使LLM能更好地捕捉细粒度实体演化和时间依赖的关系语义，同时保持参数效率。在两个TKG基准上的实验结果表明，TEPCA达到当前最优性能。对DG-LoRA在八个常识问答数据集上的额外评估进一步验证了其通用输入自适应能力、跨LLM骨干的可扩展性及可解释性。

On-Policy Transition Distillation
基于策略的转移蒸馏
On-policy distillation (OPD) consolidates expert capabilities into a single student by matching teacher and student final-layer distributions on student-generated rollouts. We investigate whether depthwise prediction transitions, the changes in predictions between layers, can provide additional task-relevant supervision. Our diagnostic study finds that stronger teacher--student agreement in intermediate transitions is associated with higher answer correctness within questions, with correct responses exhibiting lower mean transition KL across all measured intermediate layer pairs. These observations motivate On-Policy Transition Distillation (OPTD), which augments final-layer distribution matching by aligning teacher and student layer-to-layer log-probability increments on student-generated rollouts. Evaluations across three backbones and eight benchmarks spanning mathematics, coding, and instruction following show that OPTD achieves the best or tied-best overall performance in both dual- and three-domain distillation. In the three-domain setting, OPTD recovers $\approx 90\%$ of the overall teacher--student capability gap, compared with $\approx 75\%$ for OPD.
中文翻译
基于策略的蒸馏（OPD）通过匹配教师与学生在学生生成轨迹上的最终层分布，将专家能力整合到单一学生模型中。我们研究深度预测转移——即层间预测的变化——是否能提供额外的任务相关监督。诊断研究表明，中间层预测转移中教师与学生的一致性越强，问题回答正确率越高，正确响应在所有测量的中间层对中表现出更低的平均转移KL值。这些发现启发了基于策略的转移蒸馏（OPTD），它在最终层分布匹配的基础上，进一步对学生生成轨迹上教师与学生的逐层概率增量进行对齐。在三种骨干网络和涵盖数学、编程、指令遵循的八个基准上的评估显示，OPTD在双域和三域蒸馏中均取得最佳或并列最佳性能。在三域设置中，OPTD恢复了约90%的教师-学生能力差距，优于OPD的约75%。

Adaptive Stopping of Streamed Reasoning for Language Model Agents
面向语言模型智能体的流式推理自适应停止
Reasoning-enabled language model agents can incur substantial computation before acting, although the reasoning needed varies across decisions. Existing stopping signals, such as confidence and intermediate-answer stability, can vary in effectiveness across reasoning models; signals obtained from hidden states or token probabilities also require internal access that may be unavailable or impractical. We introduce StreamStop, which learns model-specific stopping policies offline from prefix-level action agreement. A frozen prefix reader encodes available reasoning text for a lightweight stop head, allowing stopping without access to the reasoning model's internal signals. We construct and publicly release a dataset of agent reasoning trajectories with prefix-level action annotations. These annotations compare each prefix action with the full-reasoning reference action, enabling offline comparison of stopping rules and exact expected-return optimization of a discounted action-agreement objective. Conditioning the head on the discount factor allows the agreement-token trade-off to be adjusted at inference without retraining. In closed-loop evaluations on WebShop and ScienceWorld, StreamStop achieves higher observed success than most validation-selected fixed-budget settings while using fewer reasoning tokens than Full thinking. On WebShop with Qwen3.8-27B, it uses 42% fewer reasoning tokens per episode with similar observed task success.
中文翻译
具备推理能力的语言模型智能体在行动前可能消耗大量计算资源，但不同决策所需的推理量各异。现有停止信号（如置信度和中间答案稳定性）在不同推理模型中的有效性不一；而来自隐藏状态或词元概率的信号又需要访问内部状态，可能不可用或不切实际。我们提出StreamStop，一种离线学习模型特定停止策略的方法，基于前缀级动作一致性。一个冻结的前缀阅读器编码可用的推理文本，由轻量级停止头处理，从而无需访问推理模型的内部信号即可实现停止。我们构建并公开发布了一个带有前缀级动作标注的智能体推理轨迹数据集。这些标注将每个前缀动作与完整推理参考动作进行比较，支持停止规则的离线对比以及折扣动作一致性目标的精确期望回报优化。通过将停止头条件化于折扣因子，可在推理阶段调整同意-令牌权衡而无需重新训练。在WebShop和ScienceWorld的闭环评估中，StreamStop在使用更少推理词元的情况下，实现了高于多数验证选择的固定预算设置的成功率。在Qwen3.8-27B上，WebShop任务中每轮推理词元减少42%，同时保持相似的观察成功率。
👤 JunHyeok Oh, Byung-Jun Lee 📅 2026-10-20 🔗 arXiv

ProVe: Proposer-Verifier Decoding
ProVe：提议者-验证者解码
Speculative decoding accelerates autoregressive (AR) language models by letting a small model draft tokens serially and a large model verify them in parallel. We reverse these roles. Starting from a standard AR checkpoint, we fine-tune the large model into a proposer that predicts a block of future tokens in a single forward pass, then use one additional forward pass to verify the proposed block. This gives ProVe, Proposer-Verifier decoding. The verifier can be a smaller AR model or the proposer itself; the latter gives ProVe-lossless, which reproduces the proposer's greedy AR decoding. Block proposals already provide substantial room for acceleration: with a block size of 16, the proposed block has an average AR-matching prefix of 6.6 tokens on GSM8K. Across six benchmarks with a 32B proposer, ProVe-lossless reaches 2.5x average AR throughput without training a separate draft head. With a small 3B verifier, ProVe reaches 2.7x average throughput at 2.6 accuracy points below AR on the six-task average, providing a controllable tradeoff between speed and accuracy.
中文翻译
推测性解码通过让小型模型串行草拟token，再由大型模型并行验证，从而加速自回归（AR）语言模型。我们反转了这一角色：从标准AR检查点出发，将大型模型微调为提议者，一次性前向传播预测未来一段token，然后通过一次额外的前向传播验证所提议的token块。由此得到ProVe——提议者-验证者解码。验证器可以是较小的AR模型，也可以是提议者自身；后者称为ProVe-lossless，能复现提议者的贪婪AR解码结果。块提议本身已提供显著加速空间：在GSM8K数据集上，块大小为16时，提议块平均具有6.6个与AR匹配的前缀token。在六项基准测试中，使用32B提议者，ProVe-lossless实现了2.5倍的平均AR吞吐量，且无需训练单独的草稿头。使用小型3B验证器时，ProVe在六任务平均上达到2.7倍吞吐量，准确率仅比AR低2.6个百分点，提供了速度与准确率之间可控的权衡。
👤 Ziqi Jin, Weiwen Xu, Jiaxi Li, Yujie Zhong, Qi She et al. 📅 2026-10-20 🔗 arXiv

Tracing Recurrent and Emergent Hallucination Patterns in Decomposed Text-to-SQL Generation
分解式文本到SQL生成中循环与涌现幻觉模式的追踪
Large language models can achieve strong Text-to-SQL benchmark performance while still producing schema and query-logic errors. Most evaluations characterize such failures only in the final generated query, making it difficult to determine whether a related error was also observed in an earlier, simpler step or appears only at the final stage. We present a drill-down evaluation framework that decomposes Text-to-SQL queries into progressively more complex executable sub-queries and corresponding natural-language questions, allowing rule-based hallucination categories to be examined across a generation path. We distinguish recurrent hallucinations, in which the same rule-based category is observed in an earlier step and again in the final query, from emergent hallucinations, in which the category is observed at the final step without an earlier detection in the same path. Across five LLMs evaluated on an expanded BIRD-mini benchmark, several categories show appreciable recurrence probabilities, while clause abuse shows comparatively higher conditional emergence. Under the conversational-history condition, recurrence probabilities are generally higher and emergence probabilities are generally lower. These observations indicate that the two conditional measures do not move uniformly in the same direction under additional context and motivate trajectory-level analysis in addition to final-output evaluation. Code and evaluation artifacts are available in the project repository. https://github.com/HaydenMM/bird-mini-drilldown
中文翻译
大型语言模型在文本到SQL基准测试中表现强劲，但仍会产生模式和查询逻辑错误。大多数评估仅关注最终生成的查询，难以判断相关错误是否在早期简单步骤中已出现，或仅在最终阶段显现。我们提出一种逐层分析评估框架，将文本到SQL查询分解为逐步更复杂的可执行子查询及其对应的自然语言问题，从而在生成路径中检查基于规则的幻觉类别。我们区分了循环幻觉（同一规则类别在早期步骤和最终查询中均被观察到）与涌现幻觉（该类别仅在最终步骤出现，而路径中此前未检测到）。在五个LLM对扩展BIRD-mini基准的评估中，多个类别显示出显著的重复概率，而子句滥用则表现出相对较高的条件性涌现。在对话历史条件下，重复概率普遍更高，而涌现概率普遍更低。这些观察表明，在增加上下文时，两种条件度量并不总朝同一方向变化，因此需要在最终输出评估之外进行轨迹级分析。代码和评估工具可在项目仓库中获取：https://github.com/HaydenMM/bird-mini-drilldown

Beyond Calibration: Temporal LLM Confidence Assessment Across Languages and Local Settings
超越校准：跨语言与本地场景下的大语言模型时间性置信度评估
Large language models are expected to estimate their own uncertainty, with confidence signals used to guide retrieval, adaptive compute allocation, routing, and selective abstention. We investigate whether confidence signals measured at different temporal stages of problem solving encode distinct forms of epistemic information and remain reliable beyond English-centric settings. Inspired by classical meta-memory theory, we distinguish anticipatory pre-solve confidence (Feeling-of-Knowing, FOK) from retrospective post-solve confidence (Judgement-of-Learning, JOL). We evaluate these signals across two frontier LLMs, multiple reasoning domains, translated Marathi benchmarks, and a newly constructed benchmark of genuinely local Marathi factual questions. Across settings, the cheaper pre-solve signal remains weakly calibrated, while post-solve confidence generally provides stronger epistemic alignment despite its additional inference cost. Confidence behaviour transfers surprisingly well under direct language translation, with calibration trends largely preserved between English and Marathi, but degrades substantially on local Marathi knowledge, suggesting that linguistic transfer alone is insufficient for region-specific information needs. Finally, hallucinated responses maintain persistently elevated confidence after answer generation, highlighting important limitations of current LLM self-assessment mechanisms.
中文翻译
大语言模型应能估计自身不确定性，其置信信号用于指导检索、自适应计算分配、路由和选择性回避。我们研究在问题求解不同时间阶段测量的置信信号是否编码了不同的认识论信息，并在非英语中心设置下是否仍保持可靠。受经典元记忆理论启发，我们区分了解题前的预期置信度（知道感，FOK）与解题后的回顾置信度（学习判断，JOL）。我们在两个前沿LLM、多个推理领域、翻译后的马拉地语基准以及新构建的真实本地马拉地语事实问题基准上评估这些信号。在各种设置下，成本较低的解题前信号仍弱校准，而解题后置信度尽管有额外推理开销，通常提供更强的认识论一致性。置信行为在直接语言翻译下表现出意外的良好迁移性，英马两国校准趋势基本保留，但在本地马拉地语知识上显著退化，表明仅靠语言迁移不足以满足区域特定信息需求。最后，幻觉回答在生成后仍维持高置信度，凸显当前LLM自我评估机制的重要局限。

Memory as Code: Declarative Retrieval over Codified Long-Term Memory
记忆即代码：基于编码化长期记忆的声明式检索
In long-term memory systems, memories are constructed from interaction histories and retrieved in response to queries, enhancing the reasoning ability of LLM agents. In complex scenarios, facts are scattered across sessions and their relationships evolve over time, making it difficult for existing memory systems to compose them to adapt to diverse queries. Motivated by the modularity and composability of code, we propose Memory as Code (MaC), a unified memory framework that jointly designs memory construction and retrieval through a shared code abstraction. MaC couples codified construction, which establishes executable memory abstractions, with declarative retrieval, which translates queries into structured retrieval programs. Specifically, codified construction builds a three-layer executable memory structure that progresses from an atomic function layer, through a relational layer, to a modular composition layer. Declarative retrieval recasts retrieval from relevance-based fact selection into declarative query processing over memory. Experiments on LoCoMo and LongMemEval show relative gains of up to 15.91% in overall LLM-judge accuracy over the strongest baseline in each setting, while substantially reducing token consumption, highlighting the effectiveness of memory as code for long-horizon memory tasks.
中文翻译
在长期记忆系统中，记忆由交互历史构建，并在响应查询时被检索，以增强LLM代理的推理能力。在复杂场景中，事实分散于多个会话，其关系随时间演变，现有记忆系统难以组合这些信息以适应多样化查询。受代码模块化与可组合性的启发，我们提出记忆即代码（MaC）统一记忆框架，通过共享代码抽象联合设计记忆构建与检索。MaC结合编码化构建（建立可执行的记忆抽象）与声明式检索（将查询转化为结构化检索程序）。具体而言，编码化构建建立三层可执行记忆结构：原子函数层、关系层和模块化组合层；声明式检索将检索从基于相关性的事实选择重构为对记忆的声明式查询处理。在LoCoMo和LongMemEval上的实验显示，相对于每个场景下的最强基线，LLM裁判准确率提升最高达15.91%，同时显著降低token消耗，凸显记忆即代码在长周期记忆任务中的有效性。

Speak vs. Know: Balancing Behavior and Knowledge Distributions of Large Language Models
说 vs. 知：平衡大语言模型的行为与知识分布
Recent work reports that the distribution verbalized directly in the output text aligns far better with human opinion distributions than the next-token log-probability distribution of LLMs. However, the verbalized distribution reflects what LLMs know about a group's opinions (knowledge distribution), while what they actually output at deployment is governed by their log-probability distribution (behavior distribution). We find that when prompting LLMs to predict the opinion of a specific group, the most likely answer under the verbalized distribution differs from that under the log-probability distribution for 37% of World Values Survey questions, averaged over three LLMs. Thus, LLMs' knowledge does not match their behavior. This matters for evaluation: a verbalized distribution can score well against a group while its most likely answer is one the model never outputs, so the score no longer describes the model. The alignment scores for these two distributions reflect only the model's behavior or its knowledge, separately. We therefore propose a learnable combination of the two distributions that follows the most likely answer of the behavior distribution while preserving the shape of the knowledge distribution, with a single hyperparameter trading off behavioral fidelity against distribution quality. Our experiments on three opinion survey datasets and six models show that the balanced distribution improves behavioral consistency over the verbalized distribution from 71.6% to 95.6% on average, while the total variation distance to the human opinion distributions rises only slightly from 28.8% to 33.8%, and it remains effective under temperature scaling and cross-dataset settings.
中文翻译
近期研究表明，直接在输出文本中表达的分布与人类意见分布的对齐程度远高于LLM的下一个词概率分布。然而，表达的分布反映的是LLM对群体意见的“知识分布”，而部署时实际输出则由其概率分布（行为分布）决定。我们发现，当提示LLM预测特定群体的意见时，三个LLM在世界价值观调查问题中，表达分布中最可能的答案与概率分布中最可能的答案不一致的比例达到37%。因此，LLM的知识与其行为不匹配。这对评估至关重要：一个在群体中得分良好的表达分布，其最可能答案可能是模型从未输出过的，导致分数不再描述模型真实表现。这两种分布的对齐分数仅反映模型的行为或知识之一。因此，我们提出一种可学习的两分布组合方法，遵循行为分布的最可能答案，同时保留知识分布的形状，通过单一超参数在行为保真度与分布质量之间权衡。我们在三个意见调查数据集和六个模型上的实验表明，该平衡分布将行为一致性从表达分布的71.6%平均提升至95.6%，而与人类意见分布的总变差距离仅从28.8%略微上升至33.8%，且在温度缩放和跨数据集设置下依然有效。
👤 Yang Liu, Masahiro Kaneko, Chenhui Chu 📅 2026-10-20 🔗 arXiv

agentic-rl
PAR: Progress-Aligned Recurrence for Length Generalization in State Tracking
PAR：用于状态跟踪长度泛化的进度对齐循环
Looped language models can run more iterations at test time, but additional iterations need not help them solve longer problems. For sequential state tracking, final-answer supervision does not specify how far each iteration should advance, and repeated updates can change the scale of the recurrent state. We introduce \textbf{Progress-Aligned Recurrence (PAR)}, which trains the prediction at iteration $k$ to match the state after the first $k$ operations and rescales recurrent states to their first-iteration norms. On swap-$S_5$, a Qwen2.5-0.5B model trained on at most 32 moves achieves 95.5\% mean full-state accuracy at 512 moves, or $16\times$ the training length, compared with 55.7\% reported for RLT and 0.8\% for explicit chain of thought on the same backbone. Controlled $S_5$ ablations show that removing norm anchoring reduces the extrapolation factor from $16\times$ to below $2.2\times$, and replacing progress-aligned targets with final-answer supervision reduces it to at most $8\times$. A learned completion readout selects when to answer without a supplied stopping depth. When intermediate labels are unavailable, prefix self-distillation induces the correspondence from shorter problem prefixes using only final answers. The gains apply across sequential state-tracking tasks, establishing the task structure required for depth extrapolation. These findings support training intermediate predictions and state scale together to make additional recurrent depth useful for longer problems.
中文翻译
循环语言模型可以在测试时运行更多迭代，但额外的迭代并不一定有助于解决更长的问题。对于序列状态跟踪任务，最终答案监督无法指定每次迭代应推进多远，重复更新可能改变递归状态的尺度。我们提出 extbf{进度对齐循环（PAR）}，训练第$k$次迭代的预测以匹配前$k$次操作后的状态，并将递归状态重新缩放到首次迭代的范数。在swap-$S_5$任务上，一个最多在32步上训练的Qwen2.5-0.5B模型在512步时达到95.5%的平均完整状态准确率，是训练长度的16倍，而RLT报告为55.7%，显式思维链仅为0.8%。受控的$S_5$消融实验表明，移除范数锚定会使外推因子从16倍降至低于2.2倍，而用最终答案监督替代进度对齐目标则将其降至最多8倍。一种学习到的完成读出机制可在未提供停止深度的情况下选择回答时机。当中间标签不可用时，前缀自蒸馏仅通过最终答案即可诱导短问题前缀之间的对应关系。这些收益适用于各类序列状态跟踪任务，确立了实现深度外推所需的任务结构。这些发现支持同时训练中间预测和状态尺度，使额外的递归深度对更长问题有用。
👤 Pan Wang, Yihao Hu, Guangyuan Dong, Yitong Lin, Hang Wang et al. 📅 2026-10-20 🔗 arXiv

agentic-rl
Self-Decomposing Diffusion for Code Generation
自分解扩散用于代码生成
Diffusion language models (DLMs) are emerging as a promising paradigm for code generation, offering the potential for fast, parallel decoding. However, generating complex programs remains challenging, as their interdependent components must maintain consistent interfaces, data flows, and shared assumptions. Aggressive parallel decoding can compromise these dependencies and undermine program correctness, while conservative decoding sacrifices efficiency. To address this trade-off, we propose planning program decomposition before implementation to coordinate interdependent units during parallel generation. We introduce Self-Decomposing Diffusion for Code (SDDC), a novel framework that decomposes complex programs and implements their units in parallel, entirely through diffusion. SDDC first generates a contract specifying the program's decomposition and inter-unit dependencies, then uses it to guide coordinated parallel implementation. Across five benchmarks and two 7B diffusion backbones, SDDC substantially improves the accuracy--latency trade-off, raising pass@1 by up to 31.1 percentage points while decoding 7.5--13.7$\times$ faster than the base models, and outperforming a fine-tuning baseline by up to 23.5 points with lower latency.
中文翻译
扩散语言模型（DLMs）正成为代码生成的一种有前景范式，具有快速并行解码的潜力。然而，生成复杂程序仍然具有挑战性，因为其相互依赖的组件必须保持一致的接口、数据流和共享假设。激进的并行解码可能破坏这些依赖关系，损害程序正确性，而保守解码则牺牲效率。为解决这一权衡，我们提出在实现前规划程序分解，以协调并行生成过程中的相互依赖单元。我们引入了用于代码的自分解扩散（SDDC），一种新颖框架，通过扩散完全实现复杂程序的分解及其单元的并行实施。SDDC首先生成一个指定程序分解和单元间依赖关系的合约，然后利用该合约引导协调的并行实现。在五个基准和两个7B扩散主干模型上，SDDC显著提升了准确率-延迟权衡，pass@1最高提升31.1个百分点，解码速度比基线模型快7.5至13.7倍，并且在更低延迟下优于微调基线高达23.5分。

Why Static Probes Fail: Context Determines Deception Feature Composition
为何静态探测器失效：上下文决定欺骗特征组成
With increasing autonomy, Large Language Models (LLMs) may engage in deceptive behaviors, such as scheming to pursue misaligned goals or sandbagging to escape oversight. Monitoring outputs alone is insufficient, as models can produce seemingly benign responses while harboring misaligned internal objectives. While mechanistic approaches like linear probing are promising, they rely on the assumption that deception corresponds to a single direction in representation space. We demonstrate that such single-direction probes fail to generalize across diverse deception scenarios. To bridge this gap, we propose representing deception as a multi-dimensional subspace rather than a single direction. Our method utilizes iterative orthogonal decomposition to identify complementary directions that capture distinct facets of deceptive intent. To effectively leverage these directions for detection, we find that static combinations prove brittle due to strong domain dependence across different scenarios. We therefore introduce a domain-adaptive gating mechanism that dynamically composes the extracted directions through few-shot adaptation at test time. With only 10 examples, our approach achieves an average AUROC of 0.97 and 94% recall at 5% FPR across all evaluated datasets, substantially outperforming existing methods.

随着大型语言模型（LLMs）自主性的提高，它们可能表现出欺骗行为，如策划追求非对齐目标或故意隐藏以逃避监督。仅监控输出不足以应对，因为模型可能产生看似无害的回应，却暗藏非对齐的内在目标。虽然机械分析方法如线性探测颇具前景，但其依赖于欺骗对应于表示空间中单一方向的假设。我们证明，此类单方向探测在多样欺骗场景中无法泛化。为弥合这一差距，我们提出将欺骗表示为多维子空间而非单一方向。我们的方法利用迭代正交分解识别互补方向，捕捉欺骗意图的不同方面。为有效利用这些方向进行检测，我们发现静态组合因不同场景间强领域依赖性而脆弱。因此，我们引入领域自适应门控机制，通过少量样本测试时的快速适应动态组合提取方向。仅需10个示例，我们的方法在所有评估数据集上平均AUROC达到0.97，5%假阳性率下的召回率达94%，显著优于现有方法。

AudiPsy: Evidence-Grounded Multimodal Reasoning over Spoken Counseling Conversations for Depression Assessment
AudiPsy：基于证据的多模态推理在语音心理咨询对话中的抑郁症评估
Depression assessment from counseling speech requires integrating textual and acoustic psychological evidence across turns, yet existing end-to-end classification approaches lack explicit multimodal evidence interpretation and context-aware reasoning. We propose AudiPsy, an evidence-grounded omni-LLM for depression assessment over spoken counseling conversations. AudiPsy employs an Observation-Assessment-Summary (OAS) paradigm to integrate dialogue transcripts, textual and acoustic emotions, and textual-acoustic emotion incongruence into structured reasoning. OAS incrementally organizes multimodal observations, performs contextualized assessment, and maintains an evolving dialogue summary for subsequent turns. To support this framework, we construct AudiPsy-Counsel, a synthetic spoken counseling conversation corpus with multimodal context augmentation, and develop a unified three-stage curriculum for depression-oriented reasoning. Experiments on DAIC-WOZ demonstrate superior depression assessment performance over specialized depression detection methods and general-purpose speech/omni-LLMs, along with improved reasoning quality, while supplementary evaluation on EATD-Corpus supports AudiPsy's applicability to naturalistic conversations.

Learned Readout Tokens for Extracting Large Language Model Confidence
用于提取大语言模型置信度的可学习读出标记
Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used within everyday applications where their responses have significant downstream impacts. For responsible deployment of such systems, we desire insight into how much we can trust their individual outputs. A popular approach for estimating LLM confidence is to train probes on the hidden states of the LLM's tokens to predict the correctness of the model's output. These probes typically read hidden states from tokens that are already in the model's vocabulary. In this work, we introduce a new approach to confidence probes that learn a new readout token and utilize its hidden states to train the probe. We show that our learned readout approach ScratchProbe outperforms its existing-token counterpart on a variety of datasets and LLMs. We also extend our method to a multi-token approach which we show provides further performance improvements. Finally, since the learned readout changes only the position a probe reads from, any existing probe can adopt it. Applied to a state-of-the-art probe, it improves AUROC on every backbone, in domain and out of domain.
中文翻译
大语言模型（LLMs）在日常应用中日益普及，其输出对下游任务有重大影响。为了负责任地部署此类系统，我们需要了解能够信任其单个输出的程度。目前一种流行的估计LLM置信度的方法是训练探测器（probes）来预测模型输出的正确性，这些探测器通常读取模型词汇表中已有token的隐藏状态。本文提出一种新的置信度探测方法，通过学习一个新的读出token并利用其隐藏状态来训练探测器。我们证明，所提出的ScratchProbe方法在多种数据集和LLM上均优于基于现有token的探测器。我们还将该方法扩展为多token版本，进一步提升性能。此外，由于学习到的读出仅改变探测器读取的位置，任何现有探测器均可采用此方法。将其应用于最先进的探测器后，在所有骨干模型上均提升了AUROC表现，无论是在域内还是域外。

Exploring Recurrent-Depth Language Models for Multitask Clinical Text Processing

Clinical natural language processing includes diverse tasks such as information extraction, classification, and question answering. Developing one model for all tasks could simplify deployment and improve knowledge transfer. We evaluated whether small recurrent-depth language models can meet this need through joint fine-tuning and compared their performance with standard transformers. We fine-tuned Ouro1.4B-Thinking, Ouro-2.6B-Thinking, HRM-Text-1B, Qwen3-1.7B, and Qwen3-8B on 9 datasets covering information extraction, classification, inference, and question answering, and evaluated them on four additional datasets excluded from training. Ouro models showed the largest gains on inference and question answering, whereas HRM-Text-1B showed mixed results. These findings support further study of recurrent-depth models for clinical tasks through controlled experiments to isolate architectural effects and investigate how recurrent computation influences reasoning, knowledge acquisition, and generalization.

Judging the Justification: Decision Certificate Supervision for Generative Reward Models
判断理由：生成奖励模型的决策证书监督
Generative reward models (GenRMs) generate natural language rationales to justify preference judgments, yet a correct verdict does not guarantee a sound justification. Existing approaches often rely on reference explanations to supervise rationales, but obtaining such references can be costly and does not necessarily establish whether generated rationales support the resulting verdict. To address these limitations, we propose DeCert, a framework that evaluates the validity and verdict consistency of generated rationales without requiring reference explanations. Crucially, DeCert translates these assessments into preference supervision that accounts for both verdict correctness and justification quality. In particular, when multiple sampled judgments share the same correct verdict, differences in certificate quality enable DeCert to construct rationale preference pairs that distinguish stronger from weaker justifications. Extensive empirical studies across two model families and multiple in- and out-of-distribution benchmarks demonstrate that DeCert consistently outperforms strong generative judge baselines while improving rationale quality. Moreover, DeCert effectively mitigates positional bias, improving position-consistent accuracy by up to 14.8 points. These results highlight the importance of supervising not only which verdict is correct, but also how that verdict is justified.
中文翻译
生成式奖励模型（GenRMs）生成自然语言理由以解释偏好判断，但正确的结论并不保证理由合理。现有方法通常依赖参考解释来监督理由生成，但获取这些参考成本高昂，且不一定能确认生成的理由是否真正支持最终结论。为解决这些问题，我们提出DeCert框架，无需参考解释即可评估生成理由的有效性和结论一致性。关键在于，DeCert将这些评估转化为偏好监督信号，同时考虑结论正确性和理由质量。具体而言，当多个采样判断具有相同正确结论时，证书质量的差异使DeCert能够构建区分更强与较弱理由的理由偏好对。在两个模型家族及多个分布内和分布外基准上的大量实证研究表明，DeCert始终优于强大的生成裁判基线，同时提升了理由质量。此外，DeCert有效缓解了位置偏差，使位置一致性准确率最高提升14.8个百分点。这些结果强调了不仅要监督结论是否正确，更要监督结论是如何被合理论证的。

Detecting Errors in Large Language Model Responses: The Incremental Predictive Value of Hidden States
检测大语言模型回答中的错误：隐藏状态的增量预测价值
Although large language models support a wide range of tasks, hallucinations remain a major obstacle to user trust. When a model generates an answer, both its internal processing and final output vary with the question. Internal states are often expected to offer richer signals for hallucination detection. However, studies using these states typically report their standalone discrimination performance, while output-side comparisons often use simple baselines with weaker modeling capacity. We integrate output-side information available at inference time into a combined baseline and compare detection performance before and after adding hidden states under identical data splits and evaluation rules. Hidden states thus provide additional predictive information, but strong standalone detection performance can still yield only limited additional gains.

The Router Already Knows
路由器早已知晓
Mixture-of-experts (MoE) models already make a sparse choice for every token: which experts should process it. We ask whether a sparse dictionary should reuse that choice. NR-SAE gives each routed expert a small dictionary and opens only those the frozen router selects. At block 15 of Gemma 4 26B-A4B, after ten million C4 training tokens and across three seeds, the expert-aligned autoencoder explains 0.518 of the variance in router-weighted expert outputs on a fixed evaluation capture. A rank-addressed control with the same parameters but sixteen times as many candidates reaches 0.481, a candidate-matched control 0.458, and an architecture-matched control with randomized expert identity 0.387. A transcoder variant also leads all three controls: 0.432, against 0.347, 0.371, and 0.118. Part of this advantage is architectural: each expert writes into a subspace of at most a quarter of the model width, so naming the expert names the subspace. How much it explains, we have not measured. At this layer and budget, the router already knows something a dictionary would otherwise have to learn. Our implementation did not train faster, and semantic interpretability, downstream loss, and causal faithfulness remain untested.

DStill: Trajectory Consistency Distillation for Accelerated One-Step Drafting in Block Diffusion Speculative Decoding
DStill：用于加速块扩散推测解码中一步草稿的轨迹一致性蒸馏
Block-diffusion speculative decoding accelerates large language model inference by drafting multiple tokens in parallel. However, one-step drafting in discrete diffusion is limited by token factorization error: each token is predicted from the same masked block, without conditioning on the tokens selected at other positions in that step. This can produce inconsistent drafts and reduce acceptance length. Iterative refinement mitigates this limitation by conditioning later predictions on previously committed tokens, though additional passes increase drafting cost. To address this trade-off between draft fidelity and inference latency, we introduce DStill, a two-stage framework that distills a target-conditioned drafter's own multi-step refinement into one-step drafting. We first train the drafter to complete partially revealed blocks, then use its refinement trajectories to train a student to predict the final completion in one pass. Using a curriculum that gradually transitions from partial to fully masked inputs, we successfully transfer the multi-step refinement capabilities into one step. Across reasoning, math, code benchmarks on three model families DStill improves acceptance length by up to 28% and speedup by up to 25% over one-step DFlash, achieving up to 6.45x speedup over auto-regressive decoding.
中文翻译
块扩散推测解码通过并行草稿多个标记来加速大语言模型推理。然而，离散扩散中的一步草稿受限于标记因子误差：每个标记均从同一掩码块预测，未基于该步骤其他位置选定的标记进行条件依赖。这可能导致草稿不一致，降低接受长度。迭代精炼通过让后续预测依赖已确定的标记缓解此问题，但增加轮次会提高草稿成本。为解决草稿保真度与推理延迟之间的权衡，我们提出DStill，一种两阶段框架，将目标条件草稿器自身的多步精炼能力蒸馏为一步草稿。首先训练草稿器完成部分揭示的块，然后利用其精炼轨迹训练学生模型在单次通过中预测最终完成。通过渐进式课程学习，从部分掩码输入过渡到完全掩码输入，我们成功将多步精炼能力迁移至一步。在三个模型家族的推理、数学、代码基准测试中，DStill相比一步DFlash将接受长度提升最多28%，速度提升最多25%，最高实现6.45倍于自回归解码的速度提升。

Explore More, Validate Less: Efficient Reflective Prompt Optimization
多探索，少验证：高效的反思式提示优化
Reflective prompt optimization improves instructions using task feedback, but coupling candidate generation with full-validation-based selection can make search costly and sequential. We propose a two-stage framework that decouples reflective candidate-pool construction from validation allocation. During construction, we sample multiple parents and reuse each parent's training responses across parallel reflection branches. Each revision is screened against its parent on a small training minibatch, allowing locally improved candidates to support further revisions without full validation. After construction, an exploration policy selects candidates from the frozen pool under a fixed full-validation budget, and the prompt with the highest measured validation score is returned. This separation allows candidate-pool size and validation budget to be set independently. We evaluate random selection and learned score prediction with similarity-based exploration on five benchmarks using Qwen3.5-9B and Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct. With 100 retained candidates and 20 full validations, our framework improves over the initial solver by up to 11.22 percentage points. Learned selection improves over the initial solver in all ten comparisons, although neither selection policy consistently outperforms the other. These results support separating reflective search from budgeted validation while leaving room for stronger candidate-selection policies.

Global Security Blind Spots: Translation as a Safety Bypass in Low-Resource Languages
全球安全盲区：低资源语言中的翻译作为安全绕过途径
Large language models are deployed globally, yet safety alignment and evaluation remain concentrated in English: harmful requests refused in English can elicit unsafe completions when translated into low-resource languages. We evaluate ten widely deployed LLMs across 79 languages (English plus 78 low-resource) under a translation-bypass threat model, using a metric conditioned on English safety, which separates translation bypass from unsafety already present in English. English safety does not transfer reliably: conditioned on safe English completions, mean bypass rates range from 21.9% to 44.4%. Bypass risk concentrates in a subset of language families, a pattern broadly shared across models. The shared ordering is confounded with translation degeneracy, but its variation across models is significant and survives every control we apply. We also show that per-model bypass rates do not identify a stable ranking: nine of ten models change rank across three conditioning schemes that estimate the same quantity, with no change to any underlying response.
中文翻译
大语言模型在全球范围内部署，但安全对齐与评估仍集中于英语：在英语中被拒绝的有害请求在翻译成低资源语言后可能引发不安全的生成。我们在79种语言（英语加78种低资源语言）下，针对十种广泛部署的大语言模型，在翻译绕过威胁模型下进行评估，采用基于英语安全的度量标准，将翻译绕过与英语中已存在的不安全性区分开。英语安全无法可靠迁移：在安全英语生成条件下，平均绕过率介于21.9%至44.4%之间。绕过风险集中在部分语言家族中，这一模式在各模型间普遍共享。共享排序受翻译退化性混淆，但其跨模型差异显著，且在所有控制条件下均保持不变。我们还发现，模型的绕过率无法形成稳定排名：在三种估计同一数量的条件方案下，十个模型中有九个在排名上发生变化，而底层响应未变。
👤 Jalil Alva, David Harper, Abigail E. Thornton, Rafael S. Villalobos Alva, Leonardo Kenji Minemura Suazo et al. 📅 2026-10-19 🔗 arXiv

Evaluating Context Compaction on the Questions That Come Later
评估上下文压缩对后续问题的影响
Language-model agents compact their context for the question at hand, but the compacted context must also serve questions that come later. We evaluate compaction on these later questions, using four corpora that annotate the evidence of several questions per text and recorded agent trajectories, and compare every method with random deletion at the same budget. On MultiRC, keeping the sentences that gpt-6-luna (through the OpenAI Decisions API) judges necessary for the current question raises its accuracy on that question by 11.4 points at 25% of the tokens. On the later questions, however, no method that selects sentences for the current question does detectably better than deleting sentences at random. Deleting words instead of sentences does better: LLMLingua-2, which ignores the question, beats random sentence deletion at the same token count by 12.5 points on the later MultiRC questions. It keeps about twice random deletion's share of the evidence's content words, and at equal content-word retention its gain shrinks to 7.3 points (exploratory). On FEVER, label accuracy can overstate what the kept text supports: the gain of sentence selection is no longer detected when answers are scored against the kept evidence, and the 21.0-point gain of LLMLingua-2 is not shown to rest on the kept text. The ranking also depends on the setting: on Evidence Inference sentence selection beats LLMLingua-2 at a nominal 25% budget, and on $τ$-bench trajectories, whose later tool calls need exact identifiers, it beats random deletion while LLMLingua-2 shows no detected gain. Scores on the question a compaction was made for do not reliably show how it serves later ones.
中文翻译
语言模型智能体会为其当前问题压缩上下文，但压缩后的上下文也需服务于后续问题。我们评估了压缩对后续问题的表现，使用四个语料库，这些语料库为每篇文本标注了多个问题的证据并记录了智能体轨迹，并将每种方法与相同预算下的随机删除进行比较。在MultiRC上，保留gpt-6-luna（通过OpenAI Decisions API）判定为当前问题必要句子，使该问题准确率提升11.4个百分点，仅使用25%的词元。然而，在后续问题上，任何为当前问题选择句子的方法均未显著优于随机删除句子。相比之下，删除词语而非句子表现更好：LLMLingua-2（忽略问题）在相同词元数下比随机句子删除在后续MultiRC问题上高出12.5分。它保留了随机删除约两倍的内容词，而在内容词保留相同时，其增益缩小至7.3分（探索性分析）。在FEVER上，标签准确率可能夸大保留文本的支持能力：当答案基于保留证据评分时，句子选择带来的增益不再显著，LLMLingua-2的21.0分增益也无法证明源于保留文本。排名还依赖于设置：在Evidence Inference中，句子选择在名义25%预算下优于LLMLingua-2；在$τ$-bench轨迹中，后续工具调用需要精确标识符，句子选择优于随机删除，而LLMLingua-2未表现出可检测的增益。针对压缩所针对问题的得分并不能可靠反映其对后续问题的服务效果。

RealControlBench: A Benchmark for Interpretability-Informed Fine-Grained Control of LLMs and Agents
RealControlBench：面向可解释性启发的细粒度控制大语言模型与智能体的基准测试
Existing steering benchmarks are built around simplified, isolated generation tasks and evaluate a single behavioral attribute under controlled settings that do not reflect the diversity and complexity of practical deployment. As a result, performance on these benchmarks provides only a limited picture of how steering methods perform in the real world. We therefore introduce RealControlBench, a comprehensive benchmark for evaluating fine-grained steering of LLMs and LLM agents across diverse behavioral targets and interaction settings. RealControlBench extends steering evaluation beyond isolated generations to complex, execution-grounded, multi-turn agent interactions, covering five complementary dimensions: instruction-following control, agentic tool-use decision control, reasoning-performance steering, reasoning-length control, and trajectory-conditioned safety awareness. Rather than measuring steering success alone, the benchmark jointly evaluates target controllability and capability preservation, including instruction following, reasoning performance, and downstream agent utility and safety. It further examines whether steering effects remain specific and robust across prompt variations, domains, and model architectures. Our evaluation shows that existing steering methods remain unreliable across realistic control objectives: they often fail to consistently induce the intended behavior and can substantially degrade the model's underlying task performance. These results suggest that strong steering effects in controlled settings do not necessarily translate into reliable control in more realistic evaluations. By providing a unified, model-agnostic steering evaluation framework, RealControlBench aims to support the development of steering methods that are not only effective but also precise, robust, and useful in realistic AI deployments.
中文翻译
现有的控制基准测试围绕简化、孤立的生成任务构建，评估单一行为属性在受控环境下的表现，无法反映实际部署中的多样性和复杂性。因此，这些基准上的性能仅能提供一个有限的图景。为此，我们引入RealControlBench，一个全面的基准测试，用于评估大语言模型和大语言模型智能体在多样化行为目标和交互场景下的细粒度控制。RealControlBench将控制评估从孤立生成扩展到复杂的、基于执行的、多轮智能体交互，涵盖五个互补维度：指令遵循控制、智能体工具使用决策控制、推理性能调节、推理长度控制以及轨迹条件下的安全意识。该基准不仅衡量控制成功，还联合评估目标可控性与能力保留性，包括指令遵循、推理性能以及下游智能体效用与安全性。此外，它还考察控制效果是否在提示变化、领域和模型架构间保持特异性和鲁棒性。我们的评估显示，现有控制方法在真实控制目标下仍不可靠：它们往往无法持续诱导预期行为，且可能显著降低模型的基础任务性能。这些结果表明，受控环境中强大的控制效果并不必然转化为更现实评估中的可靠控制。通过提供统一、模型无关的控制评估框架，RealControlBench旨在推动开发不仅有效，而且精确、鲁棒、适用于真实AI部署的控制方法。

CEFR-oriented Russian Text Simplification Using Reinforcement Learning
基于强化学习的面向CEFR标准的俄语文本简化
We present a study on controlled automatic simplification of Russian texts using preference-based optimization algorithms with a focus on adapting texts to the A1, A2 and B1 proficiency levels. We fine-tune YandexGPT using Rejection Sampling and Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) methods to improve control over the complexity of the generated text. We compare several training configurations that differ in dataset composition, text type and the strategy used to construct preference pairs for DPO. For automatic evaluation we train a regression model based on ruRoBERTa-large to predict continuous complexity scores corresponding to CEFR levels. We also consider semantic similarity, lexical coverage and quantitative readability metrics. Human evaluation assesses simplicity, fluency and adequacy. Our best model achieves 87.15\% target level accuracy, an MAE of 0.35 CEFR levels and a target interval MAE of 0.11. Manual assessment shows that 75% of the evaluated simplifications match the target morphological and syntactic level, and fluency and adequacy scores are especially high for A2 and B1. Although A1 remains the most challenging level, the model significantly reduces the proportion of vocabulary and grammatical constructions beyond the target level while generally preserving meaning. Our model is freely available on $\href{https://huggingface.co/kmustakova/RuSimplifier_DPO}{HuggingFace}$.

MMedAgent-Plus: Scaling Multimodal Medical Agents across Modalities, Tasks and Workflows
MMedAgent-Plus：跨模态、任务与工作流的多模态医疗智能体扩展
Medical workflows often require multiple interdependent analyses, but existing specialist and generalist AI models are not designed to coordinate them. We introduce MMedAgent-Plus, a multimodal medical tool-use agent that combines a vision-language planner with 18 leading specialist models spanning 12 tasks and 8 modalities. Through a unified tool interface, the planner selects specialists, executes them in sequence, and synthesizes responses grounded in their outputs. The planner is instruction-tuned on 208,928 samples from 32 public medical benchmarks, covering single- and multi-tool interactions across single and multiple rounds. We separately evaluate tool orchestration, response synthesis, and end-to-end execution on held-out samples. MMedAgent-Plus achieves tool-call accuracies of 91.0%, 98.1%, and 80.7% in single-tool multi-round, multi-tool multi-round, and multi-tool single-round settings, respectively. Given correct tool outputs, MMedAgent-Plus achieves scores of 14.4-14.8 out of 15 for faithfulness, completeness, and conciseness across 600 evaluation samples. In end-to-end execution without pre-supplied tool outputs, MMedAgent-Plus correctly invokes the required specialist in 90.6% of 350 evaluation cases spanning seven tasks. Its final-answer accuracy reaches 0.98, matching the dedicated leading specialist and exceeding the strongest baseline (0.28).

Which Agent's Skill Should Change? Group-Relative Credit Assignment for Evolving Multi-Agent Teams
应改变哪个智能体的技能？面向演化多智能体团队的组相对信用分配
Cooperative language-model agents can improve without weight updates by revising reusable skills, but only if a shared team reward is traced to the responsible agent and skill. Existing methods credit the skills present in each trajectory, yet presence is not responsibility: a harmful skill active in successful runs still earns positive credit. We present \textbf{GRSE}, \textbf{G}roup-\textbf{R}elative \textbf{S}kill \textbf{E}volution, which assigns behavioral credit by comparison: inspired by GRPO, it executes each question several times under the same skill libraries and contrasts the executions to credit agent--skill pairs, with structured diagnosis of failures. On HotpotQA and LoCoMo with two open-weight actors, GRSE outperforms MASkills, the closest prior method, on HotpotQA and every LoCoMo category except open-domain, with the largest gains on multi-hop questions ($+6.7$ and $+3.2$ F1). Its gains rest on better-grounded retrieval rather than answers given without evidence, and its learned skills transfer between the multi-hop tasks. GRSE is robust to corrupted libraries: starting from planted harmful skills, it credits every planted skill with the correct sign and recovers the score of a clean library ($+6.3$ points), whereas MASkills misattributes two and falls below its starting point.
中文翻译
合作的语言模型智能体可通过修改可复用技能实现性能提升，而无需权重更新，但前提是必须将共享团队奖励追溯到负责的智能体和技能。现有方法仅根据轨迹中技能的存在性进行信用分配，但存在并不等于责任：即使有害技能在成功运行中活跃，仍会获得正向信用。我们提出 extbf{GRSE}（组相对技能演化），通过对比执行来分配行为信用：受GRPO启发，它在相同技能库下多次执行同一问题，通过对比执行结果来分配智能体-技能对的信用，并进行故障的结构化诊断。在HotpotQA和LoCoMo数据集上，使用两个开放权重演员，GRSE在HotpotQA和除开放域外的所有LoCoMo类别上均优于最接近的先前方法MASkills，尤其在多跳问题上提升显著（F1分别提高+6.7和+3.2）。其性能提升源于更可靠的检索而非无证据的答案，且学习到的技能可在多跳任务间迁移。GRSE对受损技能库具有鲁棒性：从预植有害技能开始，它能正确识别每个预植技能的信用符号并恢复清洁库的性能（+6.3分），而MASkills则错误归因两个技能并表现低于初始水平。

The Culture Within: Towards Mechanistic Understanding of Cultural Reasoning in LLMs
文化之内：迈向对大语言模型中文化推理的机制理解
Large Language Models (LLMs) increasingly operate across culturally diverse settings, yet existing evaluations largely measure cultural behavior at the output level. We introduce CultureLens, a reference-grounded benchmark and mechanistic study of how LLMs use cultural information. CultureLens contains 9,261 scenarios across 75 countries and systematically varies country, value, true-norm, and contradictory-norm evidence. Across Llama-3.1-8B, Gemma-3-12B, and Qwen-3.5-27B, stating a contradictory norm lowers aligned answers by 32-47 percentage points, even when the model answers correctly from the country alone. Mechanistically, models default to Anglophone norms when no country is given. The failure under a contradictory norm is not one of missing knowledge, as blocking the norm inside the model restores the correct answer in most cases. Models accept a stated norm according to how it is presented and not whether it is true, which leaves them most exposed to errors in retrieved content. Supervised fine-tuning with contradictory-norm examples raises aligned answers under a contradictory norm to 85-93%, including on unseen countries, at a small cost under a true norm.
中文翻译
大型语言模型（LLMs）越来越多地在跨文化环境中运行，但现有的评估方法主要从输出层面衡量文化行为。我们提出了CultureLens，一个基于参考的基准测试和机制研究，用于分析LLMs如何使用文化信息。CultureLens包含来自75个国家的9,261个情景，并系统地变化国家、价值观、真实规范和矛盾规范证据。在Llama-3.1-8B、Gemma-3-12B和Qwen-3.5-27B模型中，当陈述矛盾规范时，一致回答率下降了32-47个百分点，即使模型仅根据国家就能正确回答。机制上，当未提供国家信息时，模型默认采用盎格鲁-撒克逊规范。在矛盾规范下的失败并非由于知识缺失，因为通过阻止模型内部的规范信息，大多数情况下仍能恢复正确答案。模型接受所陈述的规范取决于其呈现方式，而非真实性，这使得它们最容易受到检索内容错误的影响。使用矛盾规范示例进行监督微调，可在小幅度降低真实规范表现的前提下，将矛盾规范下的对齐回答率提升至85-93%，包括在未见过的国家上。
👤 Samee Arif, Furkan Danisman, Angana Borah, Honglak Lee, Dehan Kong et al. 📅 2026-10-19 🔗 arXiv
