Preference-Aware Pareto Steering for Multi-Task Speech LLMs
面向多任务语音大模型的偏好感知帕累托引导
Speech large language models (LLMs) perform multiple tasks, including automatic speech recognition (ASR), timestamp prediction (TS), and speech translation (ST). Joint training of speech LLMs creates task trade-offs, yet standard training with fixed loss weights does not generally control the resulting trade-off according to deployment preferences. To address this, we propose Endpoint-Anchored Preference-Aware Sampling Uniformly along the Pareto Front (EA-SURF), an a priori method that adapts scalarization weights so that the resulting task trade-off reflects the desired preference. EA-SURF retains the standard weighted-loss training pipeline, tracking the current model's performance relative to each task-specific reference and adapting the scalarization weights as training proceeds. We theoretically show that EA-SURF can match a desired preference, with the update becoming stationary at the target under checkable regularity conditions. Across six three-objective speech LLM requests, EA-SURF empirically converges toward the requested targets and increases endpoint-inclusive normalized hypervolume by 17.8% over fixed loss weighting.
中文翻译
语音大语言模型（LLMs）执行多项任务，包括自动语音识别（ASR）、时间戳预测（TS）和语音翻译（ST）。语音LLMs的联合训练会产生任务之间的权衡，但使用固定损失权重的标准训练通常无法根据部署偏好控制这种权衡。为解决此问题，我们提出了一种先验方法——端点锚定的偏好感知均匀沿帕累托前沿采样（EA-SURF），该方法自适应调整标量权重，使最终的任务权衡反映期望的偏好。EA-SURF保留了标准加权损失训练流程，通过跟踪当前模型相对于每个任务特定参考的表现，并在训练过程中动态调整标量权重。我们从理论上证明了EA-SURF能够匹配期望偏好，且在可验证的正则条件下更新趋于稳定。在六项三目标语音LLM请求中，EA-SURF在经验上收敛到请求目标，并使包含端点的归一化超体积提升了17.8%（相比固定损失权重）。
👤 Liuyuan Jiang, Vishal Sunder, Samuel Thomas, Brian Kingsbury, Jeff Kuo et al. 📅 2026-10-19 🔗 arXiv

On the Fragility of Tasks under Composition: Implicit Budgeting in Language Models
任务组合下的脆弱性：语言模型中的隐式思维预算
Modern language models are increasingly asked to handle several tasks within a single prompt. Doing so requires solving each task correctly while integrating the resulting answers into a coherent response. In this paper, we identify a fundamental limitation in this process, where models make substantially more errors when tasks are composed, even when they can solve every constituent task reliably in isolation. This degradation cannot be fully explained by the accumulation of independent errors. Across both open-weight models and the frontier model Claude Opus 5, our findings instead point to an implicit thinking budget underlying this effect. In particular, under composition, models use substantially fewer tokens per task than in isolation, even when the full sequence remains well within the context window. Tasks that require longer solution traces in isolation are consequently more $\textit{fragile}$ under composition, i.e., models make more errors on prompts containing these tasks. In isolation, non-fragile tasks receive more reasoning than they need and waste compute. However, by grouping tasks according to their fragility, the implicit budgeting can be manipulated to reduce token usage by $43$\% across a range of math and linguistic tasks while also preserving accuracy.
中文翻译
现代语言模型越来越被要求在一个提示中处理多个任务。这需要正确解决每个任务，并将结果整合成连贯的回答。本文揭示了这一过程中的一个根本性局限：当任务组合时，模型犯错显著增多，即使它们在孤立状态下能可靠地解决每个子任务。这种性能下降无法完全由独立错误累积解释。在开放权重模型和前沿模型Claude Opus 5中，我们的发现指向一种隐式的思维预算机制。具体而言，在任务组合时，模型每任务使用的token数量显著减少，即使整个序列仍在上下文窗口范围内。那些在孤立状态下需要更长推理路径的任务在组合下更为“脆弱”，即在包含这些任务的提示中出错更多。在孤立状态下，非脆弱任务会使用超出所需量的推理资源，造成计算浪费。然而，通过按脆弱性分组任务，可以操纵这种隐式预算，在一系列数学和语言任务中将token使用量降低43%，同时保持准确率。

ICLHerd: Herding-Based Data Selection for LLMs with In-Context Learning as a Proxy
ICLHerd：基于群体行为的数据选择方法，以上下文学习作为代理
Data selection plays a central role in modern large language model training, as the quality of training data strongly influences downstream performance. A common approach is to curate a development set that encodes the desired skills (e.g., reasoning or multilinguality) and select data that are similar to them. Existing methods compute similarity using cosine distance over either the model's hidden states or gradients. However, we observe that this max-similarity strategy induces a mode-seeking bias: the selected data clusters tightly around the development samples. To address this, we propose ICLHerd, a data selection framework that leverages the von Mises-Fisher kernel to balance mode-seeking with mode-averaging. We further introduce in-context learning as a validation proxy, enabling efficient hyperparameter search over the balance trade-off. Results show that ICLHerd outperforms existing LLM data selection algorithms, highlighting the importance of balancing mode-seeking and mode-averaging.
中文翻译
数据选择在现代大语言模型训练中起着核心作用，因为训练数据的质量强烈影响下游性能。一种常见方法是构建一个包含期望技能（如推理或多语言能力）的开发集，并选择与之相似的数据。现有方法通常使用模型隐藏状态或梯度的余弦距离计算相似性。然而，我们观察到这种最大相似性策略会引入模式寻找偏差：所选数据会紧密聚集在开发样本周围。为解决此问题，我们提出ICLHerd，一种利用冯·米塞斯-费舍尔核的数据选择框架，能够在模式寻找与模式平均之间取得平衡。我们进一步引入上下文学习作为验证代理，实现对平衡权衡的高效超参数搜索。实验结果表明，ICLHerd优于现有的大语言模型数据选择算法，凸显了在模式寻找与模式平均之间保持平衡的重要性。

Discrete Neural Interlingua for Ontology-Agnostic EHR Modeling
用于本体无关电子健康记录建模的离散神经中间语
EHR foundation models promise scalable clinical prediction across health systems, yet their deployment is constrained by a tokenization bottleneck. The same clinical concept can appear as different ontology codes, local identifiers, synonyms, or free-text descriptions, which standard EHR tokenizers treat as unrelated symbols. A model trained on MIMIC-III with a closed-vocabulary tokenizer can use only 13% of eICU events. We introduce MEDIATOR, a discrete ontology-agnostic tokenizer that maps any clinical concept expressed as text into a shared vector-quantized codebook, turning EHR harmonization into open-vocabulary tokenization without manual mappings or a fixed vocabulary. Trained with synonym supervision and guided by enriched concept descriptions, code co-occurrence, and relation graphs, MEDIATOR collapses equivalent expressions while organizing distinct concepts in a medically meaningful discrete space. Used as the token interface of an EHR Transformer, MEDIATOR improves mean AUPRC over the strongest prior tokenizer by 4.0 points (8% relative) on 20 MIMIC-III tasks and by 8.6 points (29% relative) in zero-shot transfer to 14 eICU tasks, where it uses all events. The transfer gain combines coverage and representation, as MEDIATOR remains the best tokenizer under identical inputs (+1.0 and +1.9 points on operational and subcohort-mortality tasks). Same-encoder controls show that the text encoder drives in-domain accuracy, while the learned objectives of MEDIATOR drive transfer robustness and entity-linking quality. MEDIATOR also enables transferable attribution across hospitals.

Cross-Lingual Reusability of LLM Pipelines: Financial Extraction, Multilingual Reasoning, and Semantic Screening
大语言模型流水线的跨语言可复用性：金融信息提取、多语言推理与语义筛查
Financial institutions increasingly deploy large language models (LLMs) in multilingual markets, raising a practical question: can pipelines developed and validated in English be reused across languages, or must they be rebuilt per locale? We test cross-lingual reusability along three dimensions--accuracy, reasoning-effort transfer, and behavioural stability--through three complementary tasks. Task 1 (cheque extraction) and Task 2 (Mintaka QA with pinned evidence across nine languages and nine model-effort configurations) test whether accuracy and effort settings transfer; Task 3 (FinanceBench pilot, 50 items over SEC 10-K filings in four languages) validates whether these patterns generalise to financial content. Accuracy reusability is broadly confirmed: Gemini 3.5 Flash achieves 95.2-97.6% extraction accuracy across prompt languages, and FinanceBench per-language means span 5.8pp (77.1-82.9%), though the language-difficulty ranking reverses between domains (Hindi is worst on Mintaka, best on FinanceBench at 82.9%). Effort reusability is domain-dependent: higher reasoning effort yields marginal gains on general-knowledge QA but up to 8pp on financial content, and effort configurations that help in English help proportionally in other languages. Behavioural reusability holds: citation-set overlap with English averages 77-79% Jaccard, with no single language consistently worse. Together, these results support qualified reuse of English-built pipelines, but per-domain, per-language validation remains necessary--neither semantic similarity nor self-reported confidence reliably predicts cross-lingual accuracy.

金融机构越来越多地在多语言市场部署大型语言模型（LLMs），引发一个实际问题：在英语中开发并验证的流水线能否跨语言复用，还是必须针对每个地区重新构建？我们通过三个互补任务，在三个维度——准确性、推理努力传递性和行为稳定性——测试跨语言可复用性。任务1（支票提取）和任务2（Mintaka QA，涵盖九种语言和九种模型努力配置，附带固定证据）测试准确性与努力设置的传递性；任务3（FinanceBench试点，4种语言的50个SEC 10-K文件条目）验证这些模式是否可推广至金融内容。准确性可复用性总体得到证实：Gemini 3.5 Flash在不同提示语言下的提取准确率达到95.2%-97.6%，FinanceBench各语言平均值跨度为5.8个百分点（77.1%-82.9%），但语言难度排名在不同领域反转（印地语在Mintaka中表现最差，在FinanceBench中表现最好，达82.9%）。努力可复用性具有领域依赖性：更高的推理努力在通用知识QA中带来微小收益，但在金融内容中最高可达8个百分点，且在英语中有效的努力配置在其他语言中也按比例有效。行为可复用性成立：与英语的引用集重叠度平均为77-79% Jaccard，无单一语言始终表现更差。综合来看，这些结果支持对英语构建流水线的有条件复用，但每领域、每语言的验证仍必不可少——语义相似性或自我报告置信度无法可靠预测跨语言准确性。

Decomposed Relation Extraction from Aviation Accident Narratives: Evaluating Transformer-Based Safety Knowledge Discovery under Relational Sparsity
从航空事故叙述中分解式关系抽取：在关系稀疏性下的Transformer-based安全知识发现评估
Relation extraction from aviation accident narratives is challenging because genuine safety relations are sparse among possible concept pairs. This study evaluates a decomposed framework separating relation classification from concept identification. A benchmark of 300 National Transportation Safety Board (NTSB) narratives containing 2,524 unique concept-text nodes was expanded into 21,038 ordered candidate pairs, with only 248 (1.18%) representing RESULTS_IN or CONTRIBUTES_TO relations. A TF-IDF Linear Support Vector Machine (SVM) and weighted RoBERTa classifier were developed using an independent graph-annotated resource with hard-negative sampling and frozen before sealed evaluation. Development Macro-F1 scores of 0.4738 (SVM) and 0.4809 (RoBERTa) declined to 0.2741 and 0.2420 under exhaustive evaluation, with Matthews correlation coefficients of 0.0128 and 0.0197. False positives dominated: the SVM recovered 96 reference relations but generated 7,018 false positives, while RoBERTa recovered 125 but generated 9,824. Post-test diagnostics revealed overlapping RoBERTa confidence distributions and false positives even within valid relation-category directions. Complementary end-to-end SmolLM2 evaluation identified missed and unsupported relations alongside malformed outputs. These findings demonstrate that known concept endpoints do not guarantee reliable relation extraction and that sampled-negative development performance may poorly reflect exhaustive evaluation. Controlling false positives and managing candidate-space sparsity remain essential for aviation safety knowledge extraction.

How to get the best and fairest performance out of your ASR system using activation steering
利用激活引导实现ASR系统最佳且公平性能的方法
Activation steering for ASR systems is an underexplored technique in improving both ASR performance and demographic fairness. We investigate two forms of activation steering: paired activation subtraction, and supervised optimization. We find the former to be ineffective at ameliorating ASR performance whatsoever, while the latter is quite effective at deriving steering vectors (SVs) that improve at once overall ASR performance and demographic fairness along such diverse axes as gender, age, and dialect. We also explore what types of data---from the most to the least personal---are necessary for calculating or training SVs. First, we show significant performance in performance from training SVs using an eclectic group of speakers from the same dataset. Furthermore, we find that vectors trained using acoustically similar speakers leads to ASR performances that are competitive or superior to vectors trained on the same speaker, or speakers from the same labeled demographic group. This finding shows that we can harness activation steering for few-shot adaptation given access to a speaker's demography, or zero-shot adaptation to unseen speakers without the need for any labels at all.

Think Before You Transcribe: Context-Steered Reasoning for Hotword-Aware Speech Recognition

Contextual automatic speech recognition (ASR) improves rare-entity recognition by supplying candidate hotwords, but retrieved candidates may be incomplete or incorrect. A reliable system must therefore exploit a relevant candidate without allowing unsupported alternatives to override the acoustic evidence. We propose a context-steered reasoning framework that generates a short, audio-grounded caption before transcription. This task-specific representation lets the model assess candidate relevance before making a lexical decision. Supervised fine-tuning establishes the structured caption-to-transcription behavior, while targeted trajectory injection strengthens group relative policy optimization when difficult utterances yield only unsuccessful rollouts. Experiments across diverse context conditions show that the proposed method consistently improves both transcription quality and hotword recognition while remaining robust to irrelevant candidates.

LAMB: Latent Multi-Hypothesis Beliefs for Long-Horizon Large Language Agents
LAMB：面向长时程大语言智能体的潜在多假设信念
Long-horizon language agents require compact memories that retain interaction evidence while preserving competing hypotheses about unobserved states. Although effective, many belief-state methods encode memory in discrete natural language, where repeated compression can omit evidence or prematurely discard plausible alternatives. We introduce LAMB, a recurrent framework for latent multi-hypothesis beliefs. A Common--Delta factorization combines a shared Common state for interaction evidence with weighted Delta components for competing hypotheses. Relative evidence from action-outcome predictions guides recurrent hypothesis updates without generating textual memory. A context-conditioned readout exposes hypotheses separately to the policy through a fixed-token belief prefix in the default configuration. The belief modules and policy are jointly trained using action imitation and auxiliary state, outcome, and observation-discrimination objectives. Across ALFWorld, BabyAI, ScienceWorld, and Crafter, LAMB matches or outperforms PABU, improving ALFWorld SR@30 and ScienceWorld success by 6.0 and 22.0 percentage points, respectively, and Crafter achievement progress by 26.37 points. On ALFWorld, it also reduces the redundant-action rate by 17.70 percentage points. These results support latent multi-hypothesis beliefs as compact memory for effective long-horizon decision making

AtomSD: Procedural Atom-Guided Self-Distillation for LLM Agents
AtomSD：基于过程原子的自蒸馏用于大语言模型智能体
Large language models (LLMs) possess broad factual knowledge and strong reasoning capabilities, but solving complex tasks also requires procedural knowledge about how to act. Existing approaches often represent procedural knowledge through trajectories or bundled skills, limiting flexible reuse across interaction histories. This paper introduces Procedural Atoms, fine-grained and independently applicable units that represent Intent, When, Do, and Avoid guidance. Building on this representation, we propose AtomSD, an Atom-guided Self-Distillation framework in which the learner continually extracts Procedural Atoms from its own successful trajectories and distills them into the evolving policy through Atom-conditioned supervision and task-reward optimization. Comprehensive experiments show that AtomSD consistently outperforms strong reward-based and distillation methods, exceeding the state-of-the-art baseline by up to 9.31 points in average performance. Further analyses demonstrate that the proposed Procedural Atoms provide effective guidance, highlighting the value of atom-level procedural knowledge for policy learning.

WaveCommitments: Private and Verifiable Speech-Agent Marketplaces
WaveCommitments：私密且可验证的语音智能体市场
Modern speech systems are evolving into ecosystems of autonomous speech agents that speak together, negotiate, and trade assets such as embeddings, spectrograms, or raw data. In such markets, sellers cannot reveal assets for evaluation due to privacy constraints, and buyers cannot price what they cannot test. Thus, we open-source WaveCommitments, an end-to-end design that pairs confidential asset valuation via Data Shapley inside a trusted execution environment with multivariate polynomial commitments. It enables selective openings and tamper-evident provenance to prove that a delivered asset matches the valuation while hiding everything else. Our extensive experiments across three known datasets and various tasks with state-of-the-art encoders, including Whisper, reveal that we speed up the best benchmarks by up to 20x while reducing their proof sizes by up to 39x. We also systematically study how a speaker's value shifts across tasks and encoders, revealing agent-level market prices.

AutoTerm-SST: Adaptive Terminology Memory with Zero Session-Time Setup for Streaming Speech Translation
AutoTerm-SST：面向流式语音翻译的自适应术语记忆，无需会话初始化
Retrieval-augmented speech translation improves rare terminology, but systems such as RASST still assume that users provide a glossary before each session. That assumption is inconvenient for live talks, while merging every domain into one growing glossary eventually places too many irrelevant terms in the same retrieval and prompt competition. We present AutoTerm-SST, an interactive system that removes session-time glossary upload and domain selection. It organizes a large offline terminology catalog into topic slices, matches recent streaming translation context to bilingual slice prototypes, and activates a compact multi-slice working set under fixed retrieval and prompt budgets. On five ACL and five ESO oncology talks, AutoTerm matches a known-domain reference in terminology accuracy (88.3% vs. 88.1%) and mWER-resegmented BLEU (45.09 vs. 45.19), while requiring no domain input. Compared with a one-million-entry merged glossary, it improves terminology accuracy by 3.9 points and BLEU by 2.30. Routing plus four-slice retrieval remains lightweight: the complete system runs at 0.406 RTF, and the vLLM host sustains 32 real-time sessions. The live demo exposes active slices and retrieved terminology as the topic changes, providing zero-setup terminology-aware streaming translation.

H2DF: Calibrated Target Bands for Controllable Language-Model Unlearning
H2DF：用于可控语言模型遗忘的校准目标带
Gradient ascent (GA) is a simple language-model unlearning baseline, but it continues increasing forget loss after useful forgetting has occurred. This unbounded pressure can damage retained utility and make forgotten examples abnormally high-loss. We introduce H2DF, a calibrated target-band objective that applies GA-like pressure below a data-dependent lower target and suppresses, or reverses, that pressure after the target region is reached. The targets are estimated from matched non-member examples before training, so the base objective requires one forget-batch forward/backward pass per optimization step; our reported TOFU variant adds sparse retain replay every tenth step. We instantiate a one-sided objective for TOFU question answering and a two-sided objective for MUSE-News language modeling. Across three TOFU seeds, H2DF obtains a forget NLL of 1.528 with margin 0.049 and retain NLL of 1.165 with margin 0.047, matching GradDiff while using retain replay only every tenth step and showing substantially lower variability than GA. On MUSE-News, H2DF improves retention over GA and NPO and is competitive with SimNPO, but does not dominate it. Ablations show that the upper target reduces overshoot and that validation-based checkpoint selection is important. These results support calibrated target bands as a practical mechanism for controlling forgetting strength, while also exposing sensitivity to random seed and calibration quality.

梯度上升（GA）是一种简单的语言模型遗忘基线方法，但在有效遗忘完成后仍持续增加遗忘损失。这种无界压力可能损害保留能力，并使被遗忘样本表现出异常高的损失。我们提出H2DF，一种校准的目标带目标函数，它在数据依赖的下界目标以下施加类似GA的压力，并在达到目标区域后抑制或反转该压力。目标值在训练前通过匹配的非成员样本估计，因此基础目标每优化步骤仅需一次遗忘批次的前向/反向传播；我们报告的TOFU变体每十步添加一次稀疏保留重播。我们为TOFU问答任务实例化了一侧目标，为MUSE-News语言建模任务实例化了两侧目标。在三个TOFU种子下，H2DF获得遗忘NLL为1.528（边际0.049），保留NLL为1.165（边际0.047），与GradDiff相当，但仅每十步使用一次保留重播，且方差显著低于GA。在MUSE-News上，H2DF的保留性能优于GA和NPO，与SimNPO相当，但未完全超越。消融实验表明上界目标能减少过冲，验证基于检查点选择至关重要。这些结果支持校准目标带作为控制遗忘强度的实用机制，同时也揭示了对随机种子和校准质量的敏感性。

Binary Discrete Diffusion: Candidate-Set Diffusion for Logic and Planning Tasks
二进制离散扩散：用于逻辑与规划任务的候选集扩散
Discrete diffusion models iteratively denoise a noisy sequence of categorical data such as text, enabling any-order generation with self-correction and in-filling capabilities. This makes them promising candidates for reasoning, planning, and constrained-generation tasks like coding and math. Yet masked and uniform diffusion erase a token's identity in a single corruption event, so their noisy states cannot explicitly represent partially resolved uncertainty in which several alternatives remain plausible. We close this gap with Binary Discrete Diffusion, a family of continuous-time discrete-diffusion processes whose noisy state is a set of candidate tokens per position, represented as a binary membership vector. Binary diffusion changes candidate sets through additions and removals, allowing information to be lost and recovered gradually while the diffusion state itself represents partial uncertainty. On Sudoku-Extreme, Boxoban, and graph coloring, the symmetric formulation of binary diffusion consistently outperforms strong, well-tuned masked and uniform baselines under matched architectures and controlled training, sampling, and tuning budgets, with its largest gains on the hardest instances, while remaining competitive on language modeling (Text8, TinyStories, OpenWebText). These results support candidate sets as an effective intermediate representation for iterative reasoning and show that the corruption kernel can materially change the computation learned by the reverse model.
中文翻译
离散扩散模型通过迭代去噪类别数据序列（如文本），实现任意顺序生成并具备自我修正和补全能力，使其成为推理、规划及编码、数学等受限生成任务的有力候选。然而，掩码和均匀扩散在单次污染事件中抹除标记的身份，导致其噪声状态无法显式表示多个备选方案仍合理的部分不确定性。我们通过引入二进制离散扩散（Binary Discrete Diffusion）填补这一空白，这是一种连续时间离散扩散过程家族，其噪声状态为每个位置的一组候选标记，以二进制成员向量表示。二进制扩散通过增删候选集来改变状态，允许信息渐进地丢失与恢复，同时扩散状态本身可表示部分不确定性。在Sudoku-Extreme、Boxoban和图着色任务上，二进制扩散的对称形式在匹配架构、受控训练、采样和调优预算下，始终优于强而调优良好的掩码和均匀基线，尤其在最难实例上取得最大提升；在语言建模任务（Text8、TinyStories、OpenWebText）上也保持竞争力。这些结果支持候选集作为迭代推理的有效中间表示，并表明扰动核可显著影响反向模型所学习的计算过程。

GPT-6-Astra Understands Touch and Outperform Vision--Tactile--Language--Action Models
GPT-6-Astra理解触觉并超越视觉-触觉-语言-动作模型
General-purpose multimodal models have shown promise as direct robot action policies, yet whether they grasp the physics underlying real-world interactions and can apply that understanding to guide action remains underexplored. We investigate this question using contact-rich manipulation as a representative testbed, examining tactile understanding as a concrete instance of this broader capability. Contact-rich manipulation requires interpreting tactile feedback and adjusting actions after obstruction. Existing learned tactile policies acquire these capabilities through specialized pretraining and per-task optimization, with substantial data and training costs. We present GPT-as-Tactile-Policy, which summarizes expert demonstrations into task guidance and tactile references for contact assessment and corrective action. GPT-6-Astra combines this context with multimodal observations and interaction history to generate control commands and adjust actions through contact feedback. Without additional robotics-specific pretraining or per-task policy fine-tuning, our method achieves 93.8\% mean success across five UniVTAC tasks. Overall performance is comparable to state-of-the-art policies, with higher success on selected tasks. On Insert Hole, tactile feedback improves success from 31\% to 99\%. Execution analysis further demonstrates contact-guided probing and correction, supporting manipulation progress through explicit expert experience and online feedback.

Toolflation: Seller Agents Distort Tool Markets Through Spontaneous Generative Engine Optimization
工具通货膨胀：卖家智能体通过自发生成引擎优化扭曲工具市场
Agents are starting to sell to other agents. Seller agents write the listings of tools, services and products, and buyer agents read these listings when they choose what to use. We show that seller agents told only to be selected more often practice generative engine optimization on their own, and that this distorts the market for sellers and for buyers. We build tool markets from real data tools on Amazon and Instagram and run every tool on every question, so we know which tools can answer each question. Under all five production models we test, sellers rewrite their descriptions to claim more and more fields, including fields that their tools return for fewer than half of the targets. We call this process toolflation. Nothing in the market limits it, and how far it goes depends on the model, as the number of claims that contradict the sellers' own evidence differs by an order of magnitude between models. Toolflation shifts the buyers' choices between sellers, and the most capable tool loses share in seven of the ten combinations of seller model and platform. Under four models, the choices stay with capable tools and buyer agents keep their task success. Under the fifth, sellers copy one another until the descriptions no longer tell the tools apart. Buyer agents then pick tools by their position in the list, tools that answer almost nothing win a quarter to two fifths of the choices, and task success falls from 89% to about 30% on Amazon, close to random choice, and from 77% to 40% on Instagram. The sellers did only what their vendors asked, and the cost fell on buyers they never dealt with. We therefore argue that honesty toward third parties under competition is part of safety alignment, and that models should be tested as sellers before they are deployed as sellers.

智能体开始向其他智能体销售产品。卖家智能体撰写工具、服务和产品的列表，买家智能体在选择使用对象时阅读这些列表。我们发现，仅被要求获得更多选择的卖家智能体会自发地对其描述进行生成式引擎优化，从而扭曲卖家和买家的市场环境。我们基于亚马逊和Instagram上的真实数据工具构建了工具市场，并对每个问题运行所有工具，以明确哪些工具能回答每个问题。在测试的五种生产模型下，卖家不断修改其描述，声称越来越多的功能，包括那些其工具仅对少于一半目标返回结果的功能。我们将这一过程称为‘工具通货膨胀’。市场中没有任何机制限制这一现象，其发展程度取决于模型本身，不同模型间矛盾自身证据的声明数量相差一个数量级。工具通货膨胀改变了买家在卖家之间的选择，最高效工具在十种卖家模型与平台组合中的七种情况下失去了市场份额。在四种模型下，买家仍选择高效工具，任务成功率保持稳定；但在第五种模型下，卖家相互模仿，导致描述失去区分度。买家随后根据工具在列表中的位置进行选择，几乎无法回答问题的工具赢得了四分之一到五分之二的选择，任务成功率从亚马逊的89%降至约30%（接近随机选择），Instagram上从77%降至40%。卖家仅执行了其供应商的要求，而成本却由从未接触过的买家承担。因此我们认为，在竞争环境下对第三方保持诚实是安全对齐的一部分，模型在作为卖家部署前应先经过作为卖家的测试。

MAS-Guard: Unified Guardrails from Individual Agents to Multi-Agent LLM Systems
MAS-Guard：从单个智能体到多智能体大语言模型系统的统一防护机制
Agentic AI extends large language models (LLMs) from generating responses to planning and executing complex workflows, substantially expanding their utility while exposing applications to unsafe actions, information leakage, and adversarial manipulation. Multi-agent systems further expand these capabilities through specialization and collaboration, but distributed authority, heterogeneous roles, and opaque or potentially compromised participants make safeguarding even more challenging. Existing defenses address important individual threats but provide fragmented coverage across local execution, system-level interactions, and enforcement mechanisms. We introduce MAS-Guard, a unified framework that closes this gap for safeguarding single- and multi-agent AI applications containing both instrumentable and opaque agents. Within instrumentable agents, the proposed local Agent Guardrail (A-Guard) layer applies task-specific experts to assess agentic events. At the system level, developer-owned Agent Charters and a Control Plane combine deterministic structural checks of routes, agent capabilities, and message formats with semantic assessments of roles and message content. These complementary layers enable enforcement through a shared set of interventions: allow, reflection, confirmation, and block. Evaluated across six benchmarks covering diverse single- and multi-agent scenarios with three agent LLMs from different providers, MAS-Guard reduces the overall attack success rate by 71.5-86.3% relative to unguarded agents, while meeting the utility reference criterion on benign tasks.

代理型人工智能将大型语言模型（LLMs）从生成回复扩展到规划与执行复杂工作流，显著提升了其应用价值，但也暴露于不安全行为、信息泄露和对抗性操纵等风险。多智能体系统通过专业化与协作进一步拓展能力，但分布式权限、异构角色以及不透明或可能被攻破的参与者使安全保障更加困难。现有防御措施虽针对重要个体威胁，但在本地执行、系统级交互和执行机制方面覆盖不完整。我们提出MAS-Guard，一种统一框架，用于保护包含可操作与不可操作智能体的单智能体与多智能体AI应用。在可操作智能体中，提出的本地代理防护层（A-Guard）应用特定任务专家来评估智能体事件。在系统层面，开发者拥有的代理章程与控制平面结合确定性结构检查（路径、智能体能力、消息格式）与语义评估（角色与消息内容）。这些互补层级通过一组共享干预手段实现强制执行：允许、反思、确认与阻止。在涵盖多种单智能体与多智能体场景的六个基准测试中，使用来自三家不同提供商的三种智能体LLM进行评估，MAS-Guard相较未受保护的智能体将总体攻击成功率降低了71.5%-86.3%，同时在良性任务上满足效用参考标准。

Disclosure Begets Disclosure: A Self-Reinforcing Privacy Loop in LLM Agents with Persistent Memory
披露引发更多披露：具有持久记忆的大语言模型智能体中的自我强化隐私循环
Large language model (LLM) agents increasingly use persistent memory to personalize responses, and personal attributes in this memory pose a privacy risk if exposed. We ask how consulting memory during dialogue changes what users go on to say, and thus what the memory comes to reveal. In multi-session simulations, a simulated user with 24 personal attributes talks with a support agent that does or does not consult memory, and in both settings attributes are inferred from the full accumulated record. Across four support models, memory raises response quality but also empirical leakage, the share of attribute values correctly inferred from the record, by a margin that widens over sessions. The cause is a loop in ordinary, non-adversarial assistance. Memory-conditioned responses carry earlier values forward, often as presuppositions, and users affirm them or leave them uncorrected, so that cues and the agent's own guesses become recorded values. The loop reinforces itself, and removing the agent's mentions of an attribute lowers its later disclosure about twice as much as single-turn effects predict. Users cannot leave it on their own, since correcting a value discloses the attribute, declining to discuss it shifts later predictions toward sensitive values, and incorrect values left in place become entrenched. The loop is best weakened at the agent's response. Instructing the agent not to state or presuppose attributes brings leakage to about the no-memory level while keeping most of the quality gains, whereas filtering or anonymizing what users say or what is stored gives up much of them.
中文翻译
大型语言模型（LLM）智能体越来越多地使用持久记忆来个性化响应，而记忆中的个人属性若被暴露则构成隐私风险。我们探讨在对话中查阅记忆如何影响用户后续所说的内容，从而导致记忆逐渐揭示更多信息。在多轮会话模拟中，一个拥有24个个人属性的模拟用户与一个查阅或不查阅记忆的支持型智能体进行交互，两种情况下均从累积记录中推断属性。在四种支持模型中，使用记忆提升了响应质量，但也增加了实际信息泄露——即从记录中正确推断出属性值的比例，且该差距随会话轮次增加而扩大。其根源在于普通非对抗性协助中的反馈循环：记忆条件化响应会携带早期值延续下去，常作为预设前提，用户予以确认或未纠正，使得线索与智能体自身的猜测成为记录值。该循环自我强化，移除智能体提及某属性后，其后续披露减少量约为单轮效应预测值的两倍。用户无法自行摆脱该循环，因为纠正一个值会暴露该属性，拒绝讨论会使后续预测偏向敏感值，而错误值若长期保留则会固化。该循环的最佳缓解方式在于智能体的回应环节。指示智能体不陈述或预设属性可将泄露降至接近无记忆水平，同时保留大部分质量提升；而过滤或匿名化用户发言或存储内容则会牺牲大量质量收益。
👤 Masahiro Kaneko, Hiromi Arai, Timothy Baldwin 📅 2026-10-20 🔗 arXiv

From RL Papers to Framework Code: Inclusion and Review in Seven Open-Source Training Frameworks
从强化学习论文到框架代码：七种开源训练框架中的包含与评审
Practitioners use reinforcement learning methods for language models through training frameworks, so a method that a framework includes is one configuration change away for its users. We measure how often this happens. We trace seven open-source frameworks for RL post-training through their commit histories and link them to 6,939 screened arXiv papers from January 2025 to June 2026. Of 1,598 papers that propose a general RL training method, 37 reached at least one framework. Across five quarterly cohorts of papers, the number included within 180 days stayed between 5 and 7 while the cohorts grew from 72 to 386 papers. Compared with papers from the same quarters, included papers more often trained policies of 30B parameters or more, at 41 against 10 percent, and more often changed a coefficient, clip, mask or weight in the existing loss, at 29 against 9 percent. Both gaps hold without papers from the companies that maintain the frameworks, while code release was equally common in both groups. The frameworks' issues and pull requests show that few methods are turned away in public. More than nine in ten of the general methods no framework included have no thread there that cites or names them, and many proposals that do appear stay open without a reply. Two further records, the frameworks' issues and the technical reports of 13 large open-weight models, show that users rarely report training failures in public and that the reports state 8 of the 13 widely included methods. We release the data as a tracker that links training failures to framework implementations and lists each framework's contribution rules and earlier proposals.

实践者通过训练框架使用强化学习方法对语言模型进行训练，因此只要框架支持某一方法，用户只需一次配置更改即可使用。我们测量了这一过程发生的频率。我们追踪了七个开源的强化学习后训练框架的提交历史，并将其与2025年1月至2026年6月期间筛选的6,939篇arXiv论文关联。在1,598篇提出通用强化学习训练方法的论文中，仅有37篇被至少一个框架采纳。在五个季度的论文组中，被框架在180天内纳入的数量稳定在5至7篇之间，而各组论文数量从72篇增长至386篇。与同期论文相比，被采纳的论文更常训练300亿参数及以上规模的策略（41% vs. 10%），也更常修改现有损失函数中的系数、裁剪值、掩码或权重（29% vs. 9%）。即使排除维护框架的公司发表的论文，这两个差距依然存在，且两组论文的代码发布率相当。框架的议题和拉取请求显示，很少有方法被公开拒绝。超过九成未被任何框架采纳的通用方法在议题中没有被引用或提及，许多被提出的方案虽出现却长期无人回应。另外两个记录——框架议题和13个大型开源权重模型的技术报告——表明用户极少在公开渠道报告训练失败，而报告中提到的8个广泛被采纳的方法正是当前主流。我们发布了数据追踪工具，将训练失败与框架实现关联，并列出各框架的贡献规则及早期提案。

Building Telecom Language Models with RSOD: Domain Data, Specialist Post-Training, Memory-Conscious Distillation, and Networking Evaluation
基于RSOD构建电信语言模型：领域数据、专家后训练、记忆感知蒸馏与网络评估
Telecom language models must combine broad networking knowledge with specialist behavior across routing, broadband services, access technologies, and operational diagnostics while remaining practical to deploy as a single model. We present an end-to-end framework that builds source-grounded synthetic training data from structured networking taxonomies and expert graphs, materializes 212,559 routed training records across fourteen networking routes, trains six frozen specialist LoRA teachers over one shared continued-pretrained backbone, and consolidates their behavior into one deployable student adapter using Routed Specialist On-Policy Distillation (RSOD). In RSOD, the committed student generates each trajectory, the route-assigned specialist teacher-forces the exact native token sequence, compact teacher hidden states are reconstructed through the shared frozen output head, and only the student LoRA is updated. A controlled reconstruction validation shows mean absolute logit error of 7.26 x 10^-6 with 100% top-1 agreement between native and reconstructed teacher logits. On four RTX 6000 Ada GPUs, the completed 2,000-step run produced 16,000 trajectories with zero observed student-version or token- ID mismatches. Under the primary route-conditioned evaluation over 2,850 held-out questions, the raw Meta-Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct model scores 12.8661/25 micro (13.0172/25 macro), the combined routed six-teacher system scores 21.7641/25 micro, and the consolidated RSOD student, released as Llama-CalixCLM v1, reaches 21.7112/25 micro (21.8006/25 macro) on the same five dimension LLM-judge rubric. Under a separate neutral deployment-like prompt, the same student reaches 20.0028/25 micro (20.0778/25 macro). The consolidated student remains within 0.0529 micro-score points of the combined routed-teacher system while retaining a numerically validated and auditable train-serve execution path.

Lost in Recovery: Demystifying Recovery Behavior of Computer-Use Agents via Goal-Oriented Action Replay
迷失于恢复：通过目标导向动作回放解构计算机使用智能体的恢复行为
Recent advances in large (vision) language models have enabled computer-use agents that automate digital workflows via fine-grained keyboard and mouse operations. However, such agents remain fragile in long-horizon settings, where errors accumulate and executed actions leave unintended side effects, making the ability to recover from failures critical. Yet, existing remedies remain inadequate: environment resets are infeasible in proprietary settings, action replay is prohibitively expensive over long horizons, and API-based rollback is limited to web environments, leaving model-based recovery largely unexplored. To address this gap, we present ReTrace, a scalable evaluation framework for systematically studying recovery behavior in computer-use agents. We formalize Transition-Consistent Recovery: identical downstream actions from the recovered and target states must yield identical outcomes. We operationalize this criterion through goal-oriented action replay following controlled error injection. Using ReTrace, we present the first systematic study of autonomous, model-based recovery, revealing that (i) ideal recovery can unlock model's latent task completion capabilities, (ii) current computer-use agents fail to exploit their latent recovery capabilities, and (iii) accurate model-based recovery yields measurable end-to-end gains. Together, these findings establish recovery as a fundamental capability for building reliable computer-use agents. Our code and dataset is available at https://github.com/BangProx/ReTRACE.

大型（视觉）语言模型的最新进展使得能够通过细粒度的键盘和鼠标操作自动化数字工作流的计算机使用智能体成为可能。然而，这类智能体在长时程场景中仍显脆弱，错误累积且执行动作会产生意外副作用，因此恢复失败的能力至关重要。但现有解决方案仍不充分：环境重置在专有环境中不可行，动作回放在长时程下成本过高，基于API的回滚仅限于网络环境，导致基于模型的恢复研究几乎空白。为填补这一空白，我们提出ReTrace，一种可扩展的评估框架，用于系统研究计算机使用智能体的恢复行为。我们形式化定义了“转移一致性恢复”：从恢复状态和目标状态出发的相同下游动作必须产生相同结果。我们通过受控错误注入后的目标导向动作回放来实现该标准。利用ReTrace，我们首次系统性地研究了自主的、基于模型的恢复行为，发现：(i) 理想的恢复能释放模型潜在的任务完成能力；(ii) 当前计算机使用智能体未能利用其潜在的恢复能力；(iii) 准确的基于模型恢复可带来可衡量的端到端性能提升。这些发现确立了恢复能力是构建可靠计算机使用智能体的基本要素。代码与数据集已公开于https://github.com/BangProx/ReTRACE。

Staged Continual Adaptation of Multimodal Foundation Models for Japanese Financial Documents
多模态基础模型在日本金融文档上的分阶段持续适应
Staged post-training of multimodal foundation models chains vision-language alignment, reasoning distillation, and domain tuning, yet how each phase trades capabilities against the others remains under-characterised. Tracking an 8.4B-parameter model from an un-fine-tuned baseline through three training phases on Japanese financial documents (evaluated on five text-input benchmarks), we find the phases interact non-additively: math-only distillation overwrites the financial knowledge acquired earlier even as procedural math skill survives, and the domain phase repairs more than it installs. A skip-phase ablation is best for domain knowledge but forfeits numerical-reasoning gains, so per-phase contributions are not decomposable and the best checkpoint is task-dependent, with implications for checkpoint retention and scheduling rather than added stages.

When Does Experience Memory Help Closed-Loop LLM Driving Agents? A Controlled Evaluation with Resampled Warmup Pools, Token-Matched Fillers, and Audited Baselines
经验记忆何时有助于闭环大语言模型驾驶代理？基于重采样预热池、令牌匹配填充物和审计基线的受控评估
When does experience memory help a closed-loop LLM driving agent? Published gains cannot say, because the two factors we find to matter most, which warmup experiences the pool contains and what any injected tokens do to the prompt, are controlled in none of them. We answer with a controlled evaluation on one audited HighwayEnv testbed (1Hz, gpt-4o-mini for the decisive contrasts). We treat the warmup pool as a resampled random factor (ten disjoint pools) and add token-matched irrelevant-filler controls, oracle-certified diagnostics, caution-pricing metrics, and quantified audits of the baseline ports. The probe suite spans flat-text retrieval (a DiLu port), a single-frame grid port (RESPOND-inspired), our episodic temporal-graph memory, a retrieval-free consolidated rulebook, and a flat K-step observation-history baseline. Four findings follow, each a property of this testbed. (1) Headline memory gains belong to the warmup draw. The reported pool is the best of ten for both retrieval memories, which beat No-Memory in only one pool of ten (sign test p=0.011), and even the rulebook's apparent pool-immunity collapsed on five fresh draws. (2) No retrieval design tested exceeds token-matched filler. The collision-only merge ceiling is reached by a single 15-token caution instruction, and metrics that price caution erase the rulebook's lead. (3) Representational sophistication is nowhere robustly operative. (4) Baseline port quality silently inverts conclusions. On this testbed, a single-pool comparison without filler controls would have reported memory gains that these controls reverse. We release the testbed, audited ports, seeds, and caches.

经验记忆何时有助于闭环大语言模型（LLM）驾驶代理？现有研究无法回答此问题，因为影响最大的两个因素——预热池中包含哪些经验，以及注入的任何令牌如何影响提示——在所有已有研究中均未得到控制。我们通过在一个经审计的HighwayEnv测试平台（1Hz，使用gpt-4o-mini进行关键对比）上的受控评估来解答。我们将预热池视为重采样的随机因素（十个互不重叠的池），并引入令牌匹配的无关填充控制、Oracle认证诊断、谨慎定价度量以及基线端口的量化审计。探测套件涵盖平面文本检索（DiLu端口）、单帧网格端口（RESPOND启发式）、我们的情景时间图记忆、无检索的整合规则手册，以及平面K步观察历史基线。四个发现随之而来，均为该测试平台的特性：(1) 头部记忆收益来自预热抽取。报告的池在两种检索记忆中均为十池中最佳，仅在一个池中优于无记忆（符号检验p=0.011），即使规则手册看似不受池影响，在五个新抽取样本中也崩溃了；(2) 所测试的任一检索设计均未超过令牌匹配填充物。仅需一条15令牌的谨慎指令即可达到仅碰撞合并的上限，而对谨慎进行定价的度量抹平了规则手册的优势；(3) 表示复杂性在任何情况下均未表现出稳健性；(4) 基线端口质量无声地反转结论。在此测试平台上，若无填充物控制的单池比较会报告记忆收益，但这些控制却使其逆转。我们公开测试平台、审计端口、种子和缓存。

[2610.27677]
agent-benchmarkagentic-rl
CovNav: Conformal Prediction-Guided Adaptive Inference for Object Goal Navigation
CovNav：基于置信区间预测引导的自适应推理用于物体目标导航
In object goal navigation (ObjectNav), an embodied agent must find a target object (e.g., "find a bed") in a previously unseen environment, relying on spatial commonsense such as object co-occurrence. Large Language Models (LLMs) encode this commonsense, but deploying them on robots exposes a trade-off between reasoning quality and efficiency: cloud-based models incur latency and privacy risks, whereas small on-device LLMs are fast but prone to hallucinated spatial judgments. Hybrid reasoning LLMs offer both a fast non-thinking mode and a slow thinking mode, which raises a key question for embodied agents: when is slow thinking worth its cost? We propose CovNav, a training-free framework that answers this question with Conformal Prediction (CP), turning dual-process (System 1 / System 2) inference into an uncertainty-aware decision. Much like recent decision models such as Jev, CovNav casts each spatial query as a structured binary decision rather than free-form generation: a local LLM in fast mode judges whether the target is likely near an observed object, CP converts its token probabilities into a prediction set, and only ambiguous queries are escalated to slow chain-of-thought reasoning. The object-pair formulation keeps calibration tractable and supports open-set objects through off-the-shelf detectors. Experiments on Gibson and HM3D show that CovNav surpasses the strongest zero-shot baseline by 0.8 and 1.7 percentage points in success rate on HM3D v1 and v2, while invoking slow reasoning for only 12.4% of queries and retaining most of the gain of always-on thinking at a fraction of its latency. Real-robot experiments on a TurtleBot 4 further validate the approach. CovNav shows how calibrated uncertainty can decide when an embodied agent should think slowly, offering a principled form of adaptive test-time reasoning for LLM-based robots.

在物体目标导航（ObjectNav）中，具身代理必须在先前未见过的环境中找到目标物体（例如“找一张床”），依赖于空间常识，如物体共现关系。大型语言模型（LLMs）编码此类常识，但将其部署在机器人上会面临推理质量与效率之间的权衡：云端模型存在延迟和隐私风险，而小型本地模型虽快但容易产生空间判断的幻觉。混合推理LLM提供了快速非思考模式和慢速思考模式，这为具身代理提出了一个关键问题：慢速思考是否值得其代价？我们提出CovNav，一种无需训练的框架，利用置信区间预测（CP）回答这一问题，将双过程（系统1/系统2）推理转化为不确定性感知决策。类似于最近的决策模型如Jev，CovNav将每个空间查询视为结构化二元决策而非自由生成：本地LLM在快速模式下判断目标是否可能靠近已观测物体，CP将其token概率转化为预测集合，仅对模糊查询升级至慢速思维链推理。物体对形式保持校准的可行性，并通过现成检测器支持开放集物体。在Gibson和HM3D上的实验表明，CovNav在HM3D v1和v2上的成功率分别比最强的零样本基线高出0.8和1.7个百分点，同时仅对12.4%的查询调用慢速推理，并以极低延迟保留了持续思考的大部分优势。在TurtleBot 4上的真实机器人实验进一步验证了该方法。CovNav展示了校准的不确定性如何决定具身代理何时应缓慢思考，为基于LLM的机器人提供了原则性的自适应运行时推理形式。

BCT: Bayes-Consistent Tempering for Flow Language Models
BCT：用于流语言模型的贝叶斯一致性温度调节
Temperature scaling is typically applied as a post-hoc transformation of model probabilities. In posterior-induced flow language models, this creates two mismatches. First, posterior tempering implicitly changes both the endpoint distribution and the distribution of intermediate noisy states, while the denoiser remains trained on the original states. Second, the tempered posterior weights are combined with transport dynamics derived for the untempered flow. These mismatches are reflected empirically: lowering temperature improves GSM8K performance much less reliably in flow LMs than in discrete diffusion LMs. We introduce Bayes-Consistent Tempering (BCT), which makes temperature explicit in both the distributions and dynamics of the generative flow. Its learned realization trains the denoiser on tempered intermediate states and tempers the endpoint distribution using the corresponding tempered dynamics. We also derive training-free variants for conventionally trained checkpoints. Across hyperspherical flow LMs with two denoiser architectures and a von Mises--Fisher flow LM, BCT improves GSM8K generation across broad temperature ranges and achieves the strongest tempering performance. The gains also extend to Sudoku, demonstrating benefits on tightly constrained generation. At very low temperatures, the learned realization is also markedly more robust than the training-free variants, supporting temperature as a property of the generative flow across training and inference rather than a post-hoc probability adjustment. Code and model weights are available at https://daioba.github.io/bct .
中文翻译
温度缩放通常作为模型概率的后处理变换。在后验诱导的流语言模型中，这会导致两个不匹配：首先，后验温度调节隐式地改变了终点分布和中间噪声状态的分布，而去噪器仍基于原始状态进行训练；其次，温度调节后的后验权重与针对未调节流推导出的传输动态相结合。这些不匹配在实验中得到体现：在流语言模型中降低温度对GSM8K性能的提升远不如在离散扩散语言模型中可靠。我们提出了贝叶斯一致性温度调节（BCT），使温度在生成流的分布和动态中显式体现。其学习实现方式让去噪器在温度调节后的中间状态上进行训练，并使用相应的温度调节动态来调节终点分布。我们还推导出适用于传统训练检查点的无训练变体。在两种去噪器架构和一个冯·米塞斯-费舍尔流语言模型的超球面流语言模型中，BCT在广泛的温度范围内均提升了GSM8K生成性能，并实现了最强的温度调节表现。收益也扩展到数独任务，表明在强约束生成中的优势。在极低温度下，学习实现方式比无训练变体显著更稳健，支持将温度视为生成流在训练和推理过程中的一种属性，而非事后概率调整。代码和模型权重可在 https://daioba.github.io/bct 获取。
👤 Daisuke Oba, Hiroki Furuta, Naoaki Okazaki 📅 2026-10-20 🔗 arXiv
[2610.27555]
agentic-rlagent-memory
Can Native Safety Travel? Reusing Safety Pretraining Across Models via On-Policy Distillation
原生安全能否迁移？通过在线策略蒸馏实现跨模型的安全预训练复用
Safety pretraining has emerged as a promising paradigm for embedding safety deeply into a model during pretraining, yielding robustness to subsequent fine-tuning that conventional safety post-training often lacks. We refer to this safety acquired during model pretraining as native safety. Yet native safety is expensive to acquire, confined to the model in which it was pretrained, and often accompanied by lower general utility. In this work, we rethink the practical value of safety-pretrained models and introduce, for the first time, the native safety reuse problem: Can native safety be transferred from a specialized, potentially smaller, safety-pretrained "teacher" to standard-pretrained "students," enabling them to inherit its robustness while preserving their general utility? We show that native safety can "travel" through on-policy distillation. A standard-pretrained student generates responses to safety-pretraining prompts, while a small safety-pretrained teacher supervises the student's token-level predictions along its own generated trajectories; cross-tokenizer alignment further enables transfer across model families. The resulting students exhibit substantially greater safety robustness than their safety-post-trained counterparts while retaining higher general utility than the teacher: subsequent fine-tuning raises the average attack success rate (ASR) of the 1.7B student by only 5.00%, compared with 11.58% for safety post-training, and its average utility of 48.20% far exceeds the aligned teacher's 27.42%. Unlike conventional large-to-small knowledge distillation, our framework enables selective, small-to-large safety transfer: it reuses the robust safety expertise of a smaller pretrained teacher without inheriting its utility limitations. These findings establish native safety as a reusable model property rather than one permanently tied to the originally pretrained model.
中文翻译
安全预训练已成为一种有前景的方法，在预训练阶段深度嵌入安全性，从而在后续微调中展现出优于传统安全后训练的鲁棒性。我们将这种在模型预训练期间获得的安全性称为原生安全。然而，原生安全获取成本高，局限于其预训练的模型，且常伴随较低的通用实用性。本文重新思考安全预训练模型的实际价值，首次提出原生安全复用问题：能否将专门的、可能更小的、经过安全预训练的“教师”模型的原生安全，迁移到标准预训练的“学生”模型中，使其继承其鲁棒性的同时保留通用实用性？我们证明原生安全可通过在线策略蒸馏实现“迁移”。标准预训练的学生模型生成对安全预训练提示的响应，而小型安全预训练的教师模型则在其自身生成轨迹上监督学生的逐标记预测；跨分词器对齐进一步实现跨模型家族的迁移。由此产生的学生模型在安全性鲁棒性上显著优于其安全后训练的同类，同时保留了更高的通用实用性：后续微调使1.7B学生模型的平均攻击成功率（ASR）仅上升5.00%，而安全后训练模型为11.58%；其平均实用性48.20%远超对齐教师的27.42%。与传统的大型到小型知识蒸馏不同，我们的框架实现了选择性的、小型到大型的安全迁移：它复用了小型预训练教师的鲁棒安全专长，而不继承其实用性局限。这些发现确立原生安全为可复用的模型属性，而非永久绑定于最初预训练模型的特性。

[2610.27495]

Self-Evolving Agents through Privileged Retrospective Distillation
通过特权回溯蒸馏实现自演化智能体
Self-evolving agents aim to improve from their own experience rather than remain fixed after deployment, but naturally collected trajectories often lack ground-truth solutions or verifier-derived rewards. We introduce Retrospective Self-Distillation (RSD), a multi-round framework that turns such experience into dense supervision using privileged retrospective context. For each on-policy trajectory, the same underlying model acts as both student and teacher: the teacher re-scores the student's realized actions while conditioning on cross-task precedents and lessons, together with turn-level reflection and future hindsight from the completed trajectory. The resulting teacher distribution is distilled back into the policy through on-policy self-distillation. Across rounds, the experience bank grows and the retrospective context is refreshed, allowing the model, its data, and its supervision to co-evolve. We further introduce frontier-guided context-policy evolution, where a stronger frontier model proposes constrained edits to how privileged context is constructed. Across ALFWorld, ScienceWorld, and SearchQA with Qwen3-8B and Qwen3-14B, RSD improves performance by up to 59.7% relative to the base model, while frontier-guided context-policy evolution adds up to a further 44.7% relative improvement over a fixed context policy. These results show that naturally accumulated experience can be converted into persistent model capability without ground-truth trajectories or verifier-derived rewards for the core policy update.
中文翻译
自演化智能体旨在从自身经验中持续改进，而非部署后保持固定。然而，自然收集的轨迹通常缺乏真实解或验证器生成的奖励信号。我们提出回溯自蒸馏（RSD），一种多轮框架，利用特权回溯上下文将此类经验转化为密集监督。在每条在线轨迹中，同一基础模型同时充当学生和教师：教师在考虑跨任务先例、经验教训以及完成轨迹中的逐轮反思与未来 hindsight 的基础上，重新评估学生的实际行为。由此产生的教师分布通过在线自蒸馏回传至策略中。随着轮次推进，经验库不断增长，回溯上下文得以刷新，使模型、其数据及其监督能够共同演化。我们进一步引入前沿引导的上下文-策略演化机制，由更强的前沿模型提出对特权上下文构建方式的约束性修改。在 ALFWorld、ScienceWorld 和 SearchQA 上使用 Qwen3-8B 和 Qwen3-14B 进行实验，RSD 相比基线模型性能提升最高达 59.7%，而前沿引导的上下文-策略演化在此基础上再带来最高 44.7% 的相对提升。结果表明，无需真实轨迹或验证器奖励即可将自然积累的经验转化为持久的模型能力。
👤 Yuhang Zhou, Yifan Wu, Bo Peng, Mingyi Wang, Jiayi Liu et al. 📅 2026-10-20 🔗 arXiv

Ask to Distill: Learning to Synthesize Data for On-Policy Distillation
提问以蒸馏：学习合成用于在线蒸馏的数据
On-policy distillation (OPD) can succeed with surprisingly few training questions, motivating a view of questions as carriers of distributional knowledge transfer. How can we assess whether a newly generated question is valuable for distillation, and learn to generate better questions---without access to its correct answer? We introduce Ask-to-Distill (\method), a framework that treats synthetic questions as distribution elicitors and optimizes the teacher supervision they expose along student-generated trajectories. A student-initialized synthesizer is optimized using a distributional surrogate for question value, computed from a frozen student--teacher pair without reference answers, correctness rewards, or per-candidate student updates. The reward combines teacher--student disagreement with teacher probability mass on the student's candidate support, together with length and diversity controls. The generated questions are filtered and deduplicated into a prompt-only bank for standard OPD. Across mathematical reasoning, code generation, and agentic tasks, our method improves average performance and teacher-gap recovery over unoptimized student-generated prompts and KL-filtered real questions, while matching or exceeding real-bank OPD in the evaluated settings. Signal analyses further show that optimized questions can elicit denser supervision and more total signal from shorter student responses, supporting elicited distributional supervision as an objective for synthetic distillation data. Our code, data and models are publicly available at https://github.com/GasolSun36/Ask_to_Distill.
中文翻译
在线蒸馏（OPD）可在极少训练问题下取得成功，这促使我们将问题视为分布知识迁移的载体。如何评估新生成的问题对蒸馏的价值，并学习生成更优问题——且无需访问正确答案？我们提出 Ask-to-Distill（ extit{method}）框架，将合成问题视为分布诱导器，并优化其在学生生成轨迹中暴露的教师监督信号。一个由学生初始化的合成器通过分布代理指标进行优化，该指标基于冻结的学生-教师对计算，无需参考答案、正确性奖励或候选学生更新。奖励结合教师与学生之间的分歧、教师对学生产生候选支持的概率质量，以及长度和多样性控制。生成的问题经筛选与去重后形成仅含提示的银行，用于标准 OPD。在数学推理、代码生成和智能体任务中，我们的方法在平均性能和教师差距恢复方面优于未优化的学生生成提示及 KL 过滤的真实问题，在所评估场景中达到或超过真实银行 OPD 的表现。信号分析进一步表明，优化后的问题能激发更密集的监督信号并从更短的学生响应中提取更多总信号，支持通过诱导分布监督作为合成蒸馏数据的目标。代码、数据和模型已在 https://github.com/GasolSun36/Ask_to_Distill 公开。

TEX: Test-Time Scaling Testing Agents via Execution-based Cross-Validation
TEX：基于执行交叉验证的测试时扩展测试代理
Test-case generation is critical but remains challenging for language models. Existing test-time scaling methods either isolate candidates or aggregate traces, which do not scale to long-horizon tasks. We present TEX, a collaborative test-time scaling framework based on execution-driven cross-validation. TEX parallelizes agents to generate tests and patches, cross-executing each test against every patch to form a $K \times K$ feedback matrix. Agents use their matrix slices for refinement, enabling objective validation without verbose trace sharing. On SWT-Bench Verified, TEX achieves 86.4%, establishing a new state-of-the-art and improving +9.0% over single-agent baselines. Moreover, even random selection from TEX outputs (84.4%) surpasses the previous SOTA (84.0%), showing collaborative execution feedback improves solution quality, not just best-case samples. Our results highlight execution-grounded feedback as an effective scaling mechanism for long-horizon agentic tasks.
中文翻译
测试用例生成对语言模型至关重要，但仍具挑战性。现有测试时扩展方法要么孤立候选样本，要么聚合执行轨迹，难以扩展到长时程任务。我们提出 TEX，一种基于执行驱动交叉验证的协作式测试时扩展框架。TEX 将多个代理并行化以生成测试用例和补丁，并交叉执行每个测试用例与每个补丁，形成 $K \times K$ 反馈矩阵。各代理利用其矩阵子集进行优化，实现客观验证而无需冗长的轨迹共享。在 SWT-Bench Verified 上，TEX 达到 86.4% 的准确率，创下新纪录，较单代理基线提升 +9.0%。此外，即使仅从 TEX 输出中随机选取（84.4%），也超过此前最先进水平（84.0%），表明协作执行反馈提升了整体解的质量，而不仅仅是最佳样本。结果强调基于执行的反馈是一种有效的长时程智能体任务扩展机制。

ReuF2F: Benchmarking Coding Agents on Formalized Undergraduate Research Problems
ReuF2F：面向形式化本科生研究问题的编码智能体基准测试
As AI systems begin to resolve open research problems, reliable verification becomes increasingly important. Formal mathematics provides a trusted framework for mathematical reasoning, enabling agents to produce machine-checkable proofs while conducting mathematical exploration. Existing formal mathematics benchmarks often focus on known results or a small set of prominent open problems, which can become difficult to evaluate reliably once solutions are discovered and widely disseminated. Existing evaluation protocols are also not always aligned with how modern coding agents operate. We therefore introduce ReuF2F, a Lean 4 benchmark of 244 formalized open problems drawn from Research Experiences for Undergraduates (REU), spanning entry-level open research problems across a broad range of mathematical subfields. We expect these less prominent open problems, which tend to attract less attention and may remain unsolved for longer, to be better suited for held-out evaluation of end-to-end open problem resolution in Lean. We adopt a standard coding-agent evaluation setting, placing each problem and its logical negation into a Lean project in a Docker container. We also build Leanimum-agent on mini-SWE-agent to provide a unified harness for fair comparisons across models. Our evaluation finds that even the best-performing model resolves only ~40% of the problems, leaving substantial room for improvement on formalized open research problems.

Teaching AI Agent to Play Poker Strategically Through Preference Optimization
通过偏好优化教授AI智能体战略性地玩扑克
A poker agent can make a legal move and still make a strategically poor one. We ask whether compact AI agents can exhibit strategic behavior under hidden information and whether preference optimization can improve beyond static and zero-shot policies. We first evaluate a static baseline and three frozen language models: SmallThinker, Qwen3, and Gemma-3 whose performance varies substantially between exact-game and six-player evaluation. We therefore propose Strategic Preference Optimization with KL-anchored Equilibrium (SPOKE), combining teacher-relative value penalties, policy anchoring, risk constraints, and structured rationale supervision, and compare it with SFT, DPO, GRPO, and GSPO. Across three runs of a SmallThinker-4B-A0.6B policy, SPOKE achieves the lowest mean exploitability in Kuhn (0.213) and Leduc (1.081) and the highest six-max top-1 agreement (0.775), while maintaining teacher KL comparable to SFT (0.522 vs 0.523). These results provide preliminary evidence that strategic preference optimization can improve strategic quality while preserving teacher-consistent behavior.

Dynamic System Inspired Continuous Large Language Model for AI4Science Tasks
受动态系统启发的连续大语言模型用于AI4Science任务
Scientific foundation models increasingly operate on continuous phenomena, yet still use discrete residual computation. We ask whether pretrained residual transformations can support additional latent computation without expanding the backbone. To test this possibility, we introduce Continuum-LM, which reuses these transformations as dynamical operators in a continuous-depth language model. Its hidden states evolve through an ordinary differential equation evaluated by higher-order numerical integration, with solver order and step size controlling computation. Across eight scientific tasks, Continuum-LM trains stably and improves mean exact-match accuracy by 7.77 and 10.96 percentage points over discrete counterparts at the 0.6B and 1.7B scales, respectively. At 0.6B, it also outperforms an Ouro-style recurrent control under matched parameter and residual-function evaluation budgets. Together, these results support extensible computational depth through parameter reuse, identifying continuous hidden-state evolution as a practical design dimension for scientific language models beyond parameter scaling.

PW-Pruning: Phase-Aware Expert Selection for Speech-Centric Omni-Modal Models
PW-Pruning：面向以语音为中心的全模态模型的阶段感知专家选择
Sparse routing reduces per-token computation but leaves the full expert bank resident in memory. We study expert pruning for speech-centric omni-modal models, where pooling calibration statistics can obscure the different expert demands of acoustic-input processing and text-answer generation. We propose PW-Pruning, a phase-aware extension of DO-ACP that constructs separate importance-weighted kernels for acoustic-prefill and teacher-forced answer tokens, then combines phase-weighted log-determinant marginal gains to select one static expert bank. On Qwen3-Omni, retaining 32 of 128 experts per layer improves MMAU-mini and MMSU accuracy over matched pooled selection by 3.20 and 4.38 percentage points, respectively, after supervised fine-tuning, averaged over three runs. Single-phase and shuffled-label controls on this public backbone suggest that semantic phase information contributes to these gains. Experiments on a non-public Qwen3.5-Omni backbone and English-only zero-shot evaluation of Nemotron provide complementary evidence. Across the tested Qwen budgets, AU gains are larger under stronger pruning, without inference-time mask switching.

[2610.26866]
agent-memoryagentic-rl
High-dimensional layer-state trajectories reveal the internal dynamics of transformer inference
高维层状态轨迹揭示Transformer推理的内部动态
Mechanistic studies of large language models localize function in parameters, attention heads or neurons, but those parts do not specify how one inference unfolds. We treat the ordered layer-state trajectory as the empirical process and test whether layer order defines a measurable computation. Across representative Qwen, Llama and Gemma checkpoints, realized order produced paths 1.60--2.24 times as aligned with their endpoint chord as endpoint-preserving shuffles. The order effect transferred to a lexical task, but was stronger in three random initializations of the same Qwen architecture, identifying an architecture-compatible scaffold rather than a training-specific law. Direct hidden states provided stronger mechanism coordinates than vocabulary readbacks, and a task-defined coordinate made candidate competition computable. After additive interventions failed, local transition actuators selectively controlled internal trajectories across checkpoints. A confidence gate then drove 17 of 87 held-out Qwen closure trajectories across the full-vocabulary top-1 candidate boundary while leaving all 203 non-closure top-1 outputs unchanged. Safety-matched action reassignment performed similarly, locating the gain in selective gating and the bounded actuator family rather than fine-grained dose assignment. Layer order exposes an observable, computable and behaviourally consequential process of transformer inference without establishing a complete theory of LLM dynamics or control of answer correctness.
中文翻译
大型语言模型的机制研究通常将功能定位在参数、注意力头或神经元上，但这些部分无法说明一次推理过程如何展开。我们将有序的层状态轨迹视为经验过程，并检验层序是否定义了一种可测量的计算。在代表性Qwen、Llama和Gemma检查点中，实际顺序产生的路径与终点弦的对齐程度是终点保持随机打乱的1.60至2.24倍。该顺序效应传递到了词汇任务，但在同一Qwen架构的三个随机初始化中更为显著，表明其属于与架构兼容的结构框架，而非训练特定规律。直接隐藏状态提供的机制坐标比词汇回溯更强，而任务定义的坐标使候选竞争变得可计算。在添加干预失败后，局部转移执行器能够选择性地控制跨检查点的内部轨迹。随后，一个置信度门控机制促使87个未见Qwen闭包轨迹中的17条跨越全词汇top-1候选边界，同时保持全部203个非闭包top-1输出不变。与安全匹配的动作重新分配表现相似，表明收益来源于选择性门控和有界执行器族，而非精细剂量分配。层序揭示了一种可观测、可计算且行为上有意义的Transformer推理过程，而无需建立LLM动态或答案正确性控制的完整理论。
👤 Heng Zhao, Yufei Wang, Ruidian Song, Weisheng Chen 📅 2026-07-15 🔗 arXiv

MythoSemantic: An Interactive System for Discovering Semantic Parallels Across Mythological and Religious Traditions
MythoSemantic：跨神话与宗教传统发现语义平行的交互式系统
We present a tool for the comparative study of mythological and religious texts. Traditional manual analysis is highly labor-intensive and difficult to scale. To address this, we use neural language models to embed texts into a shared semantic space, so that users can find semantic parallels across cultural traditions in an interactive interface. Embedded fragments form distinct semantic structures whose coarse geometry aligns with well-documented historical relations between traditions. We quantify how much of that geometry is already explained by lexical overlap and by proper names. The current system is available at https://mythoscope.io, and the installable package is available at https://github.com/haafus/MythoSemantic/blob/main/RUN.md.
中文翻译
我们提出一种用于比较神话与宗教文本的工具。传统的人工分析高度耗时且难以扩展。为此，我们使用神经语言模型将文本嵌入共享语义空间，使用户能够在交互界面中发现不同文化传统之间的语义平行。嵌入片段形成具有明显语义结构的模式，其粗粒度几何结构与已知的传统间历史关系相吻合。我们量化了这种几何结构中有多少可由词汇重叠和专有名词解释。当前系统可在https://mythoscope.io访问，可安装包可通过https://github.com/haafus/MythoSemantic/blob/main/RUN.md获取。

[2610.28434]
Every Model Leaves a Trace: Multi-Resolution LLM Fingerprinting
每个模型都留下痕迹：多分辨率大语言模型指纹识别
Large language models leave model-specific traces in their generated text, but it remains unclear how finely these fingerprints can distinguish related models and which signals remain informative as obvious cues are reduced. We study this through fingerprint resolution, considering both model resolution, from different families to distilled and adapted variants, and signal resolution across behavioral, stylometric, lexical, and semantic evidence. We progressively suppress surface cues, paraphrase outputs, vary the amount of available text, and test generalization across prompt distributions and languages. Our results show that fingerprints persist beyond broad family differences, remaining detectable in distilled models and even LoRA adaptations of the same base model. Although attribution weakens as surface and lexical cues are altered, substantial model-specific signal remains. These signals transfer across prompt sources and languages, with semantic representations proving most robust across languages. These findings show that model identity is recoverable at multiple resolutions, but that recoverability depends on model similarity and the evidence available in the text.
中文翻译
大语言模型在其生成文本中留下模型特有的痕迹，但目前尚不清楚这些指纹能多精细地区分相关模型，以及当明显线索被削弱后哪些信号仍具信息量。我们通过指纹分辨率研究这一问题，既考虑模型分辨率（从不同家族到蒸馏和适配变体），也考虑行为、风格、词汇和语义证据等信号分辨率。我们逐步抑制表面线索，改写输出，改变可用文本量，并测试在不同提示分布和语言间的泛化能力。结果表明，指纹在超越广泛家族差异后依然存在，即使在蒸馏模型甚至同一基础模型的LoRA适配版本中仍可检测。尽管表面和词汇线索改变后归属能力减弱，但仍存在显著的模型特异性信号。这些信号可在不同提示来源和语言间迁移，其中语义表征在跨语言情境下表现最为稳健。这些发现表明，模型身份可在多个分辨率下被恢复，但可恢复性取决于模型相似性以及文本中可用的证据。
👤 Mohammad Reza Modarres, Armin Tourajmehr, Artin Tavasoli, Yadollah Yaghoobzadeh, Mohammad Taher Pilehvar 📅 2026-10-20 🔗 arXiv
[2610.28337]
Beyond a Single Sentiment Score: Topical and Affective Dimensions of Emoji Meaning on Twitter
超越单一情感得分：推特上表情符号意义的主题与情感维度
Emoji are widely treated as compact affective tokens whose meaning is summarised by a single sentiment score. We argue that this picture is incomplete. From a corpus of 16{,}575 English tweets and a per-emoji metric we call \emph{stability delta} (within-class minus between-class cosine similarity of sentence embeddings), we show that emojis used in tightly clustered topical contexts also exhibit \emph{more} polarised crowd-sourced sentiment, not less. Validated against the Kralj Novak et al.\ (2015) sentiment lexicon, the central correlation is Spearman $ρ= +0.361$ (Holm $p = 0.015$, 95\% bootstrap CI $[+0.10, +0.59]$); against the independent Ferré et al.\ (2025) Spanish 13-emotion ratings, $ρ= +0.509$ ($p = 0.0003$). The result survives permutation testing, BCa bootstrap, leave-one-out jackknife, partial correlation controlling for emoji frequency, and Holm correction across a four-test family. Topical coherence and affective dispersion are distinct dimensions of emoji meaning. As a downstream check we report leakage-free five-fold cross-validation results for sarcasm classification on the Emoji Sarcasm Dataset; all three baselines (a gated multimodal architecture, fine-tuned BERTweet features, and fine-tuned RoBERTa features) score above 0.97 macro F1, indicating the dataset has saturating emoji-label cues. We frame the classifier as supporting evidence rather than the contribution.
中文翻译
表情符号通常被视为紧凑的情感标记，其意义可由单一情感得分概括。我们认为这种观点不完整。基于包含16,575条英文推文的语料库及一种名为“稳定性差异”（同一类别内减去跨类别句向量余弦相似度）的每表情符号度量，我们发现，在紧密聚集的主题语境中使用的表情符号表现出更强的极化群体情感，而非更弱。该结果经Kralj Novak等人（2015）情感词典验证，中心相关性为Spearman ρ= +0.361（Holm p = 0.015，95% bootstrap CI [0.10, 0.59]）；经Ferré等人（2025）西班牙语13种情绪独立评分验证，ρ= +0.509（p = 0.0003）。该结果在置换检验、BCa自助法、留一法刀切、控制表情符号频率的部分相关性以及四测试家族的Holm校正下依然成立。主题连贯性与情感分散性是表情符号意义的两个独立维度。作为下游验证，我们在Emoji Sarcasm Dataset上报告了无泄漏的五折交叉验证结果，所有三个基线模型（门控多模态架构、微调BERTweet特征、微调RoBERTa特征）的宏F1均超过0.97，表明数据集存在饱和的表情符号-标签线索。我们将分类器视为支持证据而非主要贡献。

Forty Questions: Identifying a Language Model from One-Word Answers
四十个问题：通过单字回答识别语言模型
A model behind an API can be identified from its one-word answers to everyday questions such as "Name a sound" or "Name a poet". A model's profile records how often it gives each answer to each question. We attribute new answers to the model most likely to have given them. On a panel of 94 models, 40 answers identify the exact model 92.5% of the time, against 1.1% by chance. Profiles built through OpenRouter identified all ten models served by DeepInfra under blinded labels. A system prompt lowers accuracy by 6 to 16 points. We applied the method to 96 endpoints of 13 open models on OpenRouter, and 92 identify as the model they claim. Of the other four, one matches the claimed model with reasoning turned off. One serves Gemma 4 under the name Llama 4 Maverick: it names itself Gemma 4, and it is billed Gemma 4's token counts on every message. Two remain unresolved. Billing records also show context added to first-party models. Grok 4.7 is billed 1,247 input tokens for a one-line question that costs GPT-4o only 12 tokens, and five models state the current date without being billed for it. The questions come from a public census of model defaults, and the method costs cents per endpoint.
中文翻译
通过API后端的语言模型可以仅凭其对日常问题的单字回答被识别，例如“说出一个声音”或“说出一位诗人”。模型的特征档案记录了它对每个问题给出每种答案的频率。我们将新答案归因于最可能产生该回答的模型。在94个模型组成的样本中，40个回答能在92.5%的情况下精确识别出模型，而随机猜测仅为1.1%。通过OpenRouter构建的档案成功识别出DeepInfra提供的十个模型，且标签为盲测。系统提示会降低准确率6至16个百分点。我们将该方法应用于OpenRouter上13个开源模型的96个接口，其中92个被正确识别为所声称的模型。其余四个中，有一个在关闭推理逻辑后匹配了声称的模型。一个以Llama 4 Maverick名义提供Gemma 4，其自称Gemma 4，并在每条消息中按Gemma 4的token计费。另外两个仍未解决。计费记录还显示，第一方模型存在上下文附加的情况。例如Grok 4.7对一条单行问题收取1,247个输入token，而GPT-4o仅需12个token；另有五个模型在未被计费的情况下报告了当前日期。这些问题来源于对模型默认设置的公开普查，该方法每接口成本仅需数美分。

Africa-Papers: A 10-Billion-Token Multi-Domain Academic Corpus for African Foundation Language Models
Africa-Papers：面向非洲基础语言模型的十亿级多领域学术语料库
Large Language Models (LLMs) depend fundamentally on the diversity, depth, and factual rigor of their pre-training corpora. However, contemporary foundational pre-training datasets (such as The Pile, S2ORC, and Dolma) exhibit a severe structural omission: African scientific, medical, legal, and agricultural scholarship constitutes less than 0.5% of global pre-training tokens. Consequently, state-of-the-art models suffer from systematic failure modes when reasoning about tropical epidemiology, localized smallholder agro-ecologies, regional economic governance, and indigenous linguistic structures. To resolve this representation deficit and advance African intellectual sovereignty in artificial intelligence, we introduce Africa-Papers, the largest curated multi-domain Pan-African academic pre-training corpus to date. Comprising over 844,807 full-text papers spanning 10.15 billion tokens across 879 Parquet shards, Africa-Papers unifies four primary scientific streams: (1) continental peer-reviewed journals indexed in DOAJ across 31 African nations, (2) the CGIAR CGSpace agricultural repository, (3) African-authored and African-focused STEM/AI preprints on arXiv, and (4) open-access international publications by scholars affiliated with African institutions indexed via OpenAlex. The entire corpus is processed into layout-aware, clean Markdown preserving section hierarchies, mathematical equations, and tabular data, accompanied by exact BPE token counts and a standardized 8-domain taxonomy. We release Africa-Papers openly on the Hugging Face Hub under the AfriSpeech organization to accelerate continual pre-training and domain evaluation for foundation models globally.
中文翻译

[2610.28066]
Same Judgment, Different Yes: Measuring Multilingual Judgment Profiles in Language Models
同一判断，不同‘是’：测量语言模型的多语言判断特征
Multilingual evaluations often score a model's opening yes or no, a token that also carries model and language answering conventions. We ask when different ways of eliciting and reading an answer recover the same judgment, and when they measure an answer habit. We collect 1.1 million replies from eight models in six languages on up to 105 moral vignettes, varying the question's form, the answer constraint, and the reading of the reply, token or sentence, in both wordings (the FAR-6 battery). Story-level judgment profiles converge across the tested readouts and question forms, except for the yes/no token after a negative interrogative read under a preset mapping: on the same replies, token and sentence profiles agree at a median r = 0.67 across cells, while sentence profiles agree with free-sentence profiles at 0.94, a known linguistic variability. With each language's story consensus removed, a model's profile is nearest to its own in another language for 39 of 48 model-language cells under proposition labels and 15 under free sentences, against 5 expected by chance. Particle, position, and named-term preferences follow their own patterns. A model can keep its judgment and change its yes: scoring that token requires establishing which proposition it affirms.
中文翻译
多语言评估通常根据模型输出的‘是’或‘否’进行评分，而这一标记也承载了模型和语言的应答习惯。我们探讨在不同提问方式和回答读取方式下，何时能恢复相同的判断结果，何时仅测量的是回答习惯。我们收集了八种模型在六种语言中对最多105个道德情景的110万条回复，变化问题形式、回答约束及回复读取方式（词或句子），构成FAR-6测试集。故事层面的判断特征在不同读取方式和问题形式下趋于收敛，但在负向疑问句后使用预设映射的‘是/否’标记时例外：相同回复下，标记与句子特征的相关性中位数为r=0.67，而句子特征与自由句特征的相关性达到0.94，反映出已知的语言变异性。在去除各语言的故事共识后，39个模型-语言组合中，模型在另一语言中的特征最接近自身，而在自由句条件下为15个，远超随机预期的5个。粒子、位置和命名项偏好遵循各自模式。模型可保持判断不变而改变其‘是’的表达；对这一标记的评分需明确其所肯定的命题。

[2610.28040]
Using Letter Positional Probabilities to Assess French Word Complexity
利用字母位置概率评估法语词汇复杂度
We use Letter Positional Probabilities (LPPs) to examine the orthographic differences between simple and complex French words. We draw from a sample of 38,000 French words scored by reaction time on a lexical decision task. We take the 5000 highest reaction time words and the 5000 lowest as a proxy for high and low word complexity groups respectively. We create 18 variables based on the structure of the words, such as length and number of syllables. We also reduce each word to a string of binary values by one-hot encoding each letter according to the 42 characters of the standard French alphabet up to a length of 25 letters. Thus, each word is represented by 42 x 25 binary characters. We then create LPPs by looking at the average binary scores for simple as opposed to complex words for all letter/positions. We find that there is a high number of LPPs that are associated with word complexity. For example, we find that most accented letters are associated with simpler words. We examine the effects of structural and LPP variables in classifiers and find that word length alone enables a classification accuracy of 77.27%, while adding the binary variables from the first 6 letter positions provided an accuracy of 83.95%. We discuss possible explanations as to how complexity could be encoded in orthography.
中文翻译
我们使用字母位置概率（LPPs）来分析简单与复杂法语词汇之间的正字学差异。基于38,000个法语词汇在词汇决策任务中的反应时间数据，选取反应时间最长的5000个和最短的5000个作为高复杂度和低复杂度组的代理。我们构建了18个基于词汇结构的变量，如长度和音节数。同时，通过将每个单词按标准法语字母表（共42个字符）进行独热编码，并限制最大长度为25个字母，将每个词表示为42×25的二进制字符序列。随后，我们通过计算简单词与复杂词在所有字母位置上的平均二进制得分，生成LPPs。研究发现，大量LPPs与词汇复杂度相关，例如大多数带重音符号的字母更常出现在简单词中。我们在分类器中检验结构变量与LPP变量的影响，发现仅使用词长即可实现77.27%的分类准确率，而加入前6个字母位置的二进制变量后准确率提升至83.95%。我们讨论了正字学中复杂度编码的可能机制。

From DQI to C-DQI: Automatic Codebook Generation and Evaluation for Measuring Computational Discourse Quality Index
从DQI到C-DQI：用于衡量计算话语质量指数的自动词典生成与评估
Effective democratic deliberation is vital for civic engagement and the health of legislative institutions. However, assessing parliamentary discourse quality is traditionally done using expert-scored, theory-based codebooks (CBs), which are rigid, labor-intensive, and prone to biases. While Large Language Models (LLMs) offer a scalable alternative for both schema induction and discourse annotation, ensuring schema quality and mitigating models' inherent bias remains a clear challenge. In this work, we propose a complete protocol for LLM-based codebook generation and evaluation. Starting from the widely accepted discourse quality Index (DQI), we compare top-down (theory-based), bottom-up (data-driven), and hybrid approaches for generating context-adapted schemas. To evaluate these schemas, we define an extrinsic proxy measure, validated by manual annotations, alongside an intrinsic inter-model agreement (IMA) metric, which leverages differing model biases to signal overall reliability. Our experiments on 100 Israeli parliament transcripts reveal that inductive, data-driven schemas achieve higher IMA than top-down methods while maintaining validity, demonstrating their potential for reliable, large-scale analysis of political discourse.
中文翻译

👤 Esther Shizgal, Ron Keinan, Daniel Shtauber, Roee Tzvi Esquira, Ori Aronson et al. 📅 2026-10-20 🔗 arXiv
[2610.27928]
Why Do LLMs Accept Generics? Explaining Acceptability using Psycholinguistic and Model-Derived Factors
大语言模型为何接受泛化陈述？基于心理语言学与模型衍生因素解释可接受性
Psycholinguistic studies explore why humans accept some generic statements but reject others by showing that these judgments depend on more than how common a property is. People consider several other factors such as how well it sets the kind apart from others or whether it is characteristic of the kind. Inspired by the linguistic and cognitive literature, we explore to what extent factors from these studies explain how large language models (LLMs) judge generics. Our results with twelve models on a psycholinguistic dataset and on naturally occurring generics show that models weigh how common a property is against how well it distinguishes the kind, and combine the two much as people do, though less clearly. Like people, they favor characteristic properties, but unlike the accounts of human judgment, they ignore striking ones. What matters most to LLMs is whether a statement is factually true, and much of the influence of human factors reaches their ratings through that judgment. In six 7-9B models, shifting the hidden states along each factor's direction moves the judgment as the factor predicts.
中文翻译
心理语言学研究探讨了人类为何接受某些泛化陈述而拒绝其他陈述，发现这些判断不仅取决于某一属性的普遍性，还涉及该属性是否能有效区分类别或是否具有类别特征。受语言学和认知文献启发，我们探究这些研究中的因素在多大程度上能够解释大语言模型（LLMs）对泛化陈述的判断。在12个模型上使用心理语言学数据集和自然出现的泛化陈述进行实验的结果表明，模型会权衡属性的普遍性与类别区分能力，其方式与人类相似但不够清晰。与人类类似，模型偏好特征性属性，但不像人类判断那样关注显著属性。对LLM而言，最核心的是陈述的事实真实性，而人类因素的影响主要通过这一判断传递。在6个7-9B规模的模型中，沿各因素方向移动隐藏状态，可预测地改变判断结果。
👤 Mervat Abassy, Zhaoxin Feng, Tatsuki Kuribayashi, Kentaro Inui, Emmanuele Chersoni 📅 2026-10-20 🔗 arXiv
[2610.27908]
Polarity Blindness in LLM Safety: A Systematic Study of Negation, Antonym Substitution, and Adversarial Framing
大语言模型安全中的极性盲视：否定、反义替换与对抗性表述的系统研究
Negation remains a persistent challenge for language models across NLP tasks and modalities. We show that this weakness can undermine LLM safety guardrails through a structural vulnerability we term polarity blindness: the failure to recognize harmful intent after polarity-altering linguistic transformations. We study two forms of polarity inversion: grammatical negation and antonym replacement, and use synonym replacement as a polarity-preserving control. We further test whether an adversarial textual wrapper amplifies these failures. Using a 260-prompt benchmark across 10 state-of-the-art LLMs and 3 languages (English, Spanish, Czech), stratified by harmfulness potential and evaluated on a validated 0-5 harm scale, we find that polarity blindness affects most evaluated models, with mean harm scores reaching 4.12 for the most vulnerable model. The effect persists across typologically differing negation systems, suggesting that polarity blindness reflects a broader limitation in semantic robustness rather than a language- or grammar-specific failure.
中文翻译
否定在各类NLP任务和模态中仍是语言模型持续面临的挑战。我们揭示了这一弱点可能通过一种称为‘极性盲视’的结构性漏洞损害LLM的安全防护机制：即在极性改变的语言转换后无法识别有害意图。我们研究了两种极性反转形式——语法否定和反义词替换，并以同义词替换作为保持极性的对照。进一步测试对抗性文本包装是否会加剧这些失败。基于涵盖10个前沿LLM和3种语言（英语、西班牙语、捷克语）的260个提示的基准测试，按危害潜力分层，并在经过验证的0-5危害评分体系下评估，发现极性盲视影响了大多数被评估模型，最脆弱模型的平均危害得分高达4.12。该效应在不同类型的语言否定系统中均持续存在，表明极性盲视反映的是更广泛的语义鲁棒性缺陷，而非特定语言或语法问题。
