[2610.27901]
CollabSciReM: Aligning Language Models for Scientific Conversations via Science-Aware Reward Decomposition
CollabSciReM：通过科学感知奖励分解实现语言模型在科学对话中的对齐
Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used as research collaborators for hypothesis refinement, methodological planning, and technical analysis across multi-turn interactions. In this setting, a useful assistant must be accurate, methodologically justified, logically consistent, and token-efficient. Current alignment approaches rarely achieve all of these together. Instruction-tuned models gain conversational quality through verbosity, and we show that science-agnostic general collaborative rewards compress responses at the expense of scientific rigor. We propose CollabSciReM, a goal-conditioned reward framework that decomposes the session-level reward into conversational quality, scientific rigor, and structural efficiency. We also introduce SciQuality, a pairwise evaluation of multi-turn research conversations grounded in 300 held-out scientific papers and scored on six dimensions: methodological soundness, justification, logical consistency, factual correctness, novelty, and responsiveness. Fine-tuned on 8.62K synthetic research conversations, CollabSciReM is preferred on SciQuality over a science-agnostic reward baseline trained on the same data for all four Llama and Qwen models, including a 65.3% win rate at matched response length. For the 7B and 8B models, CollabSciReM outperforms the science-agnostic reward baseline on the primary metric in 8 of 10 single- and multi-turn benchmark settings. In a blind study, domain experts rated CollabSciReM significantly higher on novelty than the general-reward variant and reported fewer hallucinated responses than both the general-reward variant and the base model.
中文翻译
大语言模型（LLMs）正越来越多地被用作多轮交互中的科研合作者，用于假设优化、方法规划和技术分析。在此场景中，一个有用的助手必须具备准确性、方法论合理性、逻辑一致性以及token效率。当前的对齐方法很少能同时满足所有这些要求。指令微调模型通过冗长表达提升对话质量，但我们发现，不考虑科学背景的通用协作奖励会压缩响应长度，牺牲科学严谨性。为此，我们提出CollabSciReM，一种目标导向的奖励框架，将会话级奖励分解为对话质量、科学严谨性和结构效率三个维度。我们还引入SciQuality，一种基于300篇未见科学论文的多轮科研对话成对评估方法，从六个维度评分：方法论合理性、论证支持、逻辑一致性、事实正确性、新颖性和响应性。在8.62K条合成科研对话上微调后，CollabSciReM在SciQuality上优于在同一数据上训练的通用奖励基线，在所有四个Llama和Qwen模型中均表现更优，包括在相同响应长度下的65.3%胜率。对于7B和8B模型，CollabSciReM在10个单轮和多轮基准设置中的8个主指标上优于通用奖励基线。在盲测中，领域专家评定CollabSciReM在新颖性方面显著优于通用奖励变体，并报告其幻觉响应少于通用奖励变体和基础模型。

Testing Associative Creativity in Humans and Multimodal LLMs with Difficult-to-Name Visual Stimuli
通过难以命名的视觉刺激测试人类与多模态大语言模型的联想创造力
We investigate associative creativity in descriptions of abstract visual stimuli by humans and multimodal large language models (MLLMs). While conceptual association is central to human communication, it remains unclear how humans and models adapt when visual stimuli become increasingly difficult to map onto familiar semantic concepts. We evaluate several state-of-the-art MLLMs and human participants along two dimensions of creativity, originality and effectiveness, using two datasets with controlled manipulations of difficulty. SilCC varies naming difficulty through progressively more complex combinations of abstract shapes, and PixCC varies difficulty through increasing levels of random visual noise. Results show that humans produce more diverse associative descriptions than MLLMs, including greater semantic distance, higher entropy, and more novel expressions relative to reference lexica. UvA-MT's Participation in the WMT26 General Translation Shared Task
UvA-MT参与WMT26通用机器翻译共享任务
This paper presents UvA-MT's submission to the WMT 2026 General Machine Translation shared task, competing in the unconstrained track across all 23 translation directions. This year, we fully leverage the test-time methods of Large Language Models (LLMs) for machine translation. Specifically, (1) we use sequential sampling with a refinement prompt to generate a pool of translation candidates for each source segment, a strategy shown to outperform traditional parallel sampling; and (2) we employ LLMs as pairwise judges to select the best candidate via a round-robin voting mechanism, with offline evaluation on the WMT25 benchmark showing a very clear improvement over point-wise best-of-N selection. SMART: Semantic Segment Mapping and Assessment of Recognition Transcripts with Contextual Rubrics
SMART：基于上下文评分卡的识别转录本语义段映射与评估
Word error rate (WER) assigns equal cost to lexical edits despite their different semantic consequences, while overall semantic scores cannot identify which individual errors alter the intended meaning. We formulate ASR semantic evaluation as context-aware, segment-level consequence assessment and propose Semantic Segment Mapping and Assessment of Recognition Transcripts with Contextual Rubrics (SMART), a framework for traceable semantic evaluation and targeted ASR correction. SMART maps reference-hypothesis discrepancies into coherent segments and applies a structured contextual rubric to assign explicit consequence labels. These labels support Semantic Impact Error Rate (SIER), which quantifies consequential errors at utterance, chunk, and lexical levels. Against majority-vote human labels, SMART achieves 0.843 F1 versus 0.745 for direct LLM judgment. Evaluation across multiple LLMs further demonstrates that SMART is relatively insensitive to the choice of evaluation model, while MER and SIER reveal different aspects of ASR quality. A separate target-reference-free rubric guides correction using the target hypothesis and surrounding ASR hypotheses, outperforming a matched w/o Rubric baseline on both datasets, with further absolute reductions of 0.85-0.98 percentage points in MER and 1.18-1.65 percentage points in SIER_lex.
中文翻译
词错误率（WER）对所有词汇修改赋予相同代价，尽管其语义后果各不相同；而整体语义得分又无法识别哪些具体错误改变了原意。我们将自动语音识别（ASR）的语义评估建模为上下文感知、分段级别的后果评估，并提出一种名为SMART的框架——即基于上下文评分卡的识别转录本语义段映射与评估，实现可追溯的语义评估与针对性的ASR修正。SMART将参考文本与假设文本之间的差异映射为连贯的语义段，并应用结构化的上下文评分卡为每个段落分配明确的后果标签。这些标签支持语义影响错误率（SIER），可在话语、片段和词汇层面量化具有实际影响的错误。与多数投票的人类标签相比，SMART的F1得分为0.843，优于直接使用大语言模型（LLM）判断的0.745。在多个LLM上的评估进一步表明，SMART对所选评估模型的选择相对不敏感，而MER与SIER揭示了ASR质量的不同方面。此外，一种独立于目标-参考的评分卡指导修正过程，利用目标假设及其周围ASR假设进行优化，在两个数据集上均优于无评分卡的基线，使MER绝对降低0.85–0.98个百分点，SIER_lex降低1.18–1.65个百分点。

[2610.27745]
Hearsay or Fact? How Framing and Elicitation Shape LLM Probability Updates
传闻还是事实？框架与引出方式如何塑造大语言模型的概率更新
Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used as judgment assistants and as stand-in participants in psychology experiments, yet little is known about how much their probability estimates move in response to a single new piece of information. We pair premise-hypothesis items from $δ$-SNLI with one strengthener and one weakener each, elicit the prior and the posteriors in separate calls, and measure the directional movement s: the mean posterior change signed by the annotated direction, so that the prior cancels out. We vary whether the update is stated ("Here is something else you learned") or reported ("Someone said that"), and whether the model gives one probability or five weighted estimates. We test seven configurations: two local models (Qwen3-8B, OLMo-2-7B; 240 pairs) and five configurations run through agent command-line harnesses (120 pairs), with hypotheses and analysis rules fixed before the formal runs (not publicly registered). Under stated, direct elicitation all seven move in the annotated direction (s = 18-28 percentage points). Reporting lowers s in all seven point estimates (significantly in six, by 2-5 points) and raises the share of reverse movements in all seven: the effect is consistent but small. Five weighted estimates lower the share of responses at $\leq$5% or $\geq$95% in all seven, but in the two local models they also reduce s by 43% and 68%, whereas in the harnessed configurations the point estimates of the change lie between -3.7 and +0.5 points. An elicitation format that reduces extreme values thus need not preserve sensitivity to evidence; the two should be tested separately. Because $δ$-NLI annotates only direction, we make no claim about how large an update should be. Code, data and the frozen plan: https://github.com/asj252/llm-judgment-studies
中文翻译
大语言模型（LLMs）越来越多地被用作判断助手，或作为心理学实验中的替代参与者，但对其在接收到单一新信息后概率估计变化程度知之甚少。我们结合δ-SNLI中的前提-假设条目，每条配以一个加强项和一个削弱项，分别通过独立调用获取先验和后验概率，测量方向性变动s：即标注方向加权后的后验平均变化，使得先验抵消。我们改变更新是陈述形式（“这里有一条你新学到的信息”）还是报告形式（“有人说……”），以及模型给出一个概率还是五个加权估计值。共测试七种配置：两种本地模型（Qwen3-8B, OLMo-2-7B；240对）和五种通过代理命令行工具运行的配置（120对），假设与分析规则在正式运行前已固定（未公开注册）。在陈述性直接引出条件下，七种配置均朝标注方向移动（s = 18–28个百分点）。报告形式在所有七种点估计中均降低了s（六种显著，下降2–5点），并提高了反向移动的比例，效果一致但较小。五种加权估计值降低了所有七种情况下≤5%或≥95%极端值的比例，但在本地模型中也使s分别下降43%和68%，而在工具调用配置中，变化点估计介于-3.7至+0.5点之间。这表明，减少极端值的引出格式未必能保留对证据的敏感性；两者应分别测试。由于δ-NLI仅标注方向，我们不对更新幅度大小做出主张。代码、数据及冻结计划见：https://github.com/asj252/llm-judgment-studies

[2610.27631]
A Two-Way Street: Surveying the Interplay between Code-Mixing and Native Languages in Text and Speech
双向通道：文本与语音中代码混用与原生语言相互作用的综述
Systems for code-mixed (CM) text and speech rarely start from scratch. CM data is scarce, so most work borrows from the languages being mixed, which we call native languages. This transfer can also run in reverse, when CM data is used to improve models for the native languages. Existing surveys focus on processing CM itself, and none brings the two directions together. This paper fills that gap with a systematic survey of transfer in both directions, across text and speech. We organise the surveyed work in a taxonomy of transfer direction, route and modality. Through this taxonomy, we find that most transfer runs from native languages into the CM setting. The reverse direction is younger, and in it code-switching mainly helps by aligning the two languages. Text and speech work share methods but rarely cite each other. On a shared speech benchmark, native data helps more than new architectures. These findings point to open challenges, such as how much native and CM data to mix in training, and how to evaluate CM text written in different scripts.
中文翻译

[2610.27590]
Benefit and Cost of Random-Token Replacement under Repeated Data: A Known-Entropy Decomposition and Evidence from Ten Real Corpora
重复数据下随机令牌替换的收益与代价：基于已知熵分解及十个真实语料库的证据
When unique pretraining data is scarce, models see the same tokens many times and are thought to memorize. Random-token replacement, an input-corruption augmentation, is one remedy reported to lower validation loss, but a lower loss alone does not reveal which source of error it reduces. On a controlled source whose entropy rate is known in closed form, we show that replacement shrinks predictions toward the corpus average. We also introduce a decomposition that separates validation loss into approximation, estimation, and memorization terms. This shrinkage view predicts that replacement's bias cost needs no memorization but its benefit does. Memorization raises validation loss after its minimum, so we report the effect at two points, the minimum validation loss and the end of a fixed budget. Where replacement helps, it reduces this rise by about 60%. At the end of the budget, it turns from harmful to helpful as the controlled source becomes less predictable; at the minimum, it helps at no predictability level under the fixed recipe. On Korean and Japanese web text, replacement removes about a third of the loss rise after the minimum by the end of the budget, but its effect at the minimum is much smaller. Across ten real corpora, replacement lowers the final loss on nine. It harms only the census corpus, program-generated JSON that is the most redundant of the ten and much more redundant than any of 64 further prose and code corpora we measure.
中文翻译
当唯一预训练数据稀缺时，模型会多次看到相同标记，被认为具有记忆特性。随机令牌替换作为一种输入扰动增强方法，被报告可降低验证损失，但仅凭损失下降无法揭示其减少的具体误差来源。在熵率可闭式表达的受控数据源上，我们发现替换会使预测值趋向语料库平均值。我们还提出一种分解方法，将验证损失拆分为近似、估计和记忆三项。该收缩视角预测，替换的偏差代价无需记忆，但其收益需要记忆。记忆会在最小验证损失之后提升验证损失，因此我们在两个时间点报告效果：最小验证损失点和固定预算结束点。在替换有帮助的情况下，它能将该上升幅度降低约60%。在预算结束时，随着受控数据源变得不可预测，替换从有害转为有益；而在最小损失点，无论可预测性如何，在固定策略下均有助益。在韩语和日语网页文本中，替换在预算结束时可消除最小损失后约三分之一的损失上升，但在最小损失点的效果则小得多。在十个真实语料库中，替换在九个语料库上降低了最终损失，仅在人口普查语料库（由程序生成的JSON，十者中最冗余，且远超我们测量的64个散文与代码语料库）中造成损害。
👤 Wonsup Shin, Jingu Kim 📅 2026-10-20 🔗 arXiv
[2610.27584]
Beyond Model Weights: Sampling-Dependent Fairness in Diffusion Language Models
超越模型权重：扩散语言模型中的采样依赖公平性
Diffusion language models (DLMs) are emerging as a practical alternative to autoregressive language models (ARLMs), yet their fairness remains largely uncharacterized. DLMs introduce sampling controls with no direct autoregressive counterpart, such as block length and remasking strategy, that directly set their speed--quality operating point. This raises a basic question: does a DLM's measured social bias remain stable across these settings? Across LLaDA, LLaDA-1.5, and Dream on Open-BBQ, we find that all three models exhibit social bias, but both the magnitude of measured bias and the relative ordering of models by bias are highly sensitive to the sampling configuration. This sampling dependence also changes what successful debiasing means. While mitigation strategies established for ARLMs transfer effectively to DLMs and reduce bias without degrading downstream performance, sampling dependence persists even after mitigation. Our results show that fairness in DLMs cannot be characterized independently of the sampling configuration, even after debiasing. Project page is available at https://pakapaka333.github.io/Beyond-Model-Weights/.

👤 Koshiro Saito, Diasuke Oba, Danushka Bollegala, Naoaki Okazaki 📅 2026-10-20 🔗 arXiv
[2610.27583]
TeCoP: Accelerating Diffusion Large Language Model Decoding via Tentative Context Pipelining
TeCoP：通过暂定上下文流水线加速扩散大语言模型解码
Diffusion large language models (dLLMs) predict multiple token positions per forward pass, yet block-wise decoders serialize generation and repeatedly recompute stable predictions. We attribute this inefficiency to coupling a prediction's readiness for irreversible commitment with its utility as context. We introduce Tentative Context Pipelining (TeCoP), a training-free decoder that decouples these roles through staggered block-parallel pipelining and draft-and-verify. TeCoP reuses stable but uncommitted predictions as provisional cross-block context, with commitment governed by confidence and stability checks. Across four LLaDA and Dream dLLMs on GSM8K, Minerva MATH, and MBPP, TeCoP reduces number of forward evaluations by up to 87% and achieves up to 7.50-fold throughput without KV caching and 11.77-fold with caching over the uncached original decoder. It outperforms Fast-dLLM and DSB in throughput across all 12 model-benchmark settings, with and without caching, while keeping average cached accuracy within one percentage point of the original decoder.

When Does Hate Transfer? A Large-Scale Study of Cross-Lingual Hate Speech Detection
仇恨内容何时会发生跨语言传播？一项大规模跨语言仇恨言论检测研究
Effective detection of hate content is essential for maintaining healthy online communities, yet performance can vary substantially across languages. This work investigates a core practical question: Can training data from one language support hate-speech detection in another language with comparable performance? To answer this question, we conduct a large-scale study across 14 languages. \change{We observe consistent patterns in which certain language pairs transfer more effectively than others. Among the factors we measure, linguistic similarity is the strongest predictor of these patterns, with temporal overlap and dataset-size difference playing smaller roles. These measurable factors do not fully explain the observed patterns; a complementary qualitative analysis suggests that shared cultural cues and communicative norms may be associated with some of the remaining cases, particularly within several clusters. We present this cultural interpretation as a hypothesis from qualitative inspection rather than a measured cause.}{} Our analysis includes low-resource languages, offering practical insights into when cross-lingual transfer can support them and where its limitations emerge. These findings inform the design of more reliable multilingual hate-detection systems.

[2610.27269]
African Languages AI Safety Readiness Report
非洲语言人工智能安全准备度报告
With the widespread adoption of large language models (LLMs) across languages and domains, AI safety has emerged as a critical global concern. However, due to resource constraints, research and development efforts tend to prioritize a limited number of high-resource languages, leaving many others, especially those spoken in Africa, underrepresented. While existing research on language "resource-ness" has largely examined whether sufficient resources are available to develop language technologies, the question of whether languages are adequately represented in AI safety research has received attention only more recently. This report examines the readiness of African languages for inclusion in AI safety discussions, drawing on existing literature and data. It considers four factors: the availability of AI-relevant resources, including text data; national AI policy support; performance on established text benchmarks; and speaker population. Based on these factors, languages are grouped into four readiness categories: fully ready, mostly ready, emerging, and foundational support needed. This classification is intended to inform the inclusion of African languages in AI safety benchmarks, highlight varying levels of preparedness, and guide strategic investment in data development, policy, and community engagement for underrepresented languages.

[2610.27266]
Auditing Safety Preference Supervision with Recorded Derivations
通过记录推理过程审计安全偏好监督
Preference labels align model training, but the judgments behind them are difficult to inspect or verify. In this paper, we examine how preference construction can be checked when intermediate decisions are recorded, and which of them remain uncertain. We present Symbolic Preference Labeling, a method in which a pretrained language model classifies predefined attributes of prompts and responses. Explicit rules assign response verdicts, and a fixed verdict ranking determines the preference label. Each preference label has a recorded derivation: the response verdicts and preference label can be recomputed from the stored attribute assignments, rules, and verdict ranking without rerunning the language model. On 200 prompts annotated by human raters, the language model produces valid structured outputs for 192 prompts. All eight parsing failures occur in the subset of self-harm prompts marked as harmful. Using 465 preference pairs, we train one policy model with direct preference optimization across five training seeds. Mean measured benign refusal is 40.82 percent on 1,319 benign prompts that resemble unsafe ones, and mean measured toxic refusal is 84.98 percent on 655 toxic prompts. This study distinguishes the reproducibility of preference labels from attribute correctness. The recorded derivations make preference construction open to inspection, while attribute correctness and semantic safety require separate validation.
中文翻译

[2610.27247]
Target-Aware Curation of Hallucination Stress Tests for Large Language Models
面向目标模型的幻觉压力测试精准筛选
Hallucination stress tests can yield similar average scores across language models even when different prompts elicit hallucinations in each model. We develop a procedure that iteratively edits source-grounded seed prompts to expose hallucinations in a target model. An LLM editor revises each prompt in response to the target's answer and a judge's category-specific score and explanation. In the main setting, the target also receives feedback on its previous response. Experiments cover 500 seeds from seven datasets and ten topics, with intrinsic and extrinsic hallucinations evaluated across open-weight and closed-source models. Editing substantially increases judge-rated severity over the seeds. In the baseline configuration, retained responses fall in the judge's strong-hallucination bin in 77.2% of runs targeting extrinsic hallucination and 74.2% targeting intrinsic hallucination. Later editing rounds account for more than half of the mean score increase. Among 219 prompts that pass model-assisted source-validity checks, 89 elicit responses containing unsupported claims. Across five models, 200 curated prompts yield similar average severity scores, although pairwise Spearman correlations between prompt-level scores are only $ρ=0.0387$ to $0.1897$. Curated prompts can be reused across models, but their diagnostic value depends on source validity and the failures they expose in each model.

Diagnostic Analysis for Large Language Model Reasoning
大语言模型推理能力的诊断分析
Reasoning structures (e.g., chains and graphs) are increasingly used for probabilistic inference, factuality verification, and uncertainty estimation, but their structural reliability is rarely evaluated directly. We test whether this reliability holds through a proposed two-stage diagnostic evaluation in open-ended multi-hop QA. Stage 1 tests different capabilities required to construct reasoning graphs, showing that graph reliability varies with topology, prompt design, and model family. Stage 2 uses uncertainty estimation as a downstream test case. Generated-edge experiments show that graph-based computation is sensitive to upstream graph errors, with performance dropping relative to the gold-graph setting. Gold-graph experiments further show that graph structure provides little benefit when used only as prompt context, but improves the separation of correct from incorrect answers by confidence score and enables node-level diagnostics.

[2610.27241]
Predicting Linear Relational Decodability Without the Relation
无需关系信息预测线性关系可解性
Linear relational embeddings (LREs) approximate a language model's subject-to-object mapping with an affine function. Their faithfulness varies enormously across relations, from near zero for person_father to the ceiling for country_capital_city. We investigate the factors that contribute to this variation and demonstrate that it can be significantly predicted from a readout that is not aware of the underlying relation. To do so, we propose a novel form of the Jacobian lens, the entity lens. Whereas the Jacobian lens is estimated across all tokens and concepts, the entity lens EL_t is trained to predict what the model will output for an entity of type t under the neutral prompt "{} is known for"; e.g., we train ELs for countries and persons. After estimating an entity lens, we measure how highly it ranks the model's answer for a relation without using any relation-specific information in estimation and prediction. Across 28 relations of the LRE dataset on Olmo-3-7B this relation-agnostic score correlates with LRE faithfulness at ρ= 0.7355 (p < .01). This suggests that linear decodability is largely a property of what a subject's representation already carries, rather than of the relation being decoded. Moving the unit of analysis from the relation to the individual fact, we conclude that new relation datasets and analysis methods are needed to address several problems in existing datasets, most prominently semantically weak first tokens (e.g., most predicted universities' first token is "University") and reference competition (e.g., the LLM confuses obscure actor names with competing non-actor entities that have the same last name). Our full pipeline is available at https://github.com/jonasrohw/entity-lens

Tibetan speech recognition is usually evaluated with character or syllable error rates because the writing system does not mark word boundaries. These metrics can rank systems differently and respond differently to adaptation failures. We combine character error rate, syllable error rate, and segmented word error rate in one evaluation framework. We evaluate five ASR architectures on Lhasa Tibetan, then test zero-shot transfer and target-corpus adaptation on five additional corpus subsets. These findings support evaluation at several linguistic levels and motivate better segmentation and adaptation methods for low-resource ASR.

[2610.26976]
Sharp Degree Bounds for Putinar Certificates in Terms of the Positivity Margin via Analytic Sums of Squares
基于解析平方和的Putinar证书在正性裕度下的精确度界
We establish sharp degree bounds for Putinar sum-of-squares (SOS) certificates in terms of the positivity margin $δ$. For any fixed Archimedean quadratic module with prescribed generators and any fixed bound on the degree of the objective, we prove a uniform certificate-degree upper bound of $O(δ^{-1/2}\log^2(2/δ))$ for normalized polynomial objectives. We show that this bound is sharp by constructing a matching lower-bound example in three variables, thereby establishing that the dependence on $δ$ is optimal. These matching bounds also yield sharp worst-case convergence rates for the Lasserre hierarchy.
中文翻译

A Multi-granularity Interaction Network for Relational Triple Extraction

Relational triple extraction identifies entity relations from raw sentences without depending on entity type information. The performance of traditional methods is seriously degenerated by ``unseen'' entities, because these methods can't build contextual dependencies relevant ``unseen'' entities. This paper addresses this challenge by proposing a multi-granularity interaction network that leverages word-word, entity-relation, and head-tail interactions to capture deep semantic dependencies. By incorporating multiple iterations, the multi-granularity interaction network is effective to refine the representations corresponding to head and tail entities. For example, our model improves F1-score by 3.1%/20.5% over previous approaches on the NYT-R/WebNLG-R datasets in the Unseen metric. Code: https://github.com/xiaocen-luo/Interaction-Network.

关系三元组抽取旨在从原始句子中识别实体间的关系，而无需依赖实体类型信息。传统方法在面对“未见”实体时性能严重下降，因为这些方法无法建立与“未见”实体相关的上下文依赖。本文提出一种多粒度交互网络，利用词-词、实体-关系以及头-尾之间的交互来捕捉深层语义依赖。通过多轮迭代，该网络能有效优化头尾实体的表示。它增强了与关系三元组相关的全局语义特征，有助于修复早期步骤中可能被遗漏或忽略的三元组。在两个重构数据集上，我们的方法表现出强劲且一致的性能，显著提升了关系三元组的识别能力，包括涉及“未见”命名实体的情况。例如，在NYT-R/WebNLG-R数据集的“未见”指标上，模型F1得分相比先前方法分别提升3.1%和20.5%。代码地址：https://github.com/xiaocen-luo/Interaction-Network。

[2610.26937]
Do We Need the Largest LLMs for Every Clinical Decision?
我们是否需要最大的大语言模型来完成每一项临床决策？
Large clinical language models often require multi-GPU infrastructure, limiting local deployment. We introduce CERTA, a decision-level statistical certificate that determines when a quantized model may substitute for its native BF16 reference and escalates decisions that cannot be certified to it. Across eleven instruction-tuned models, both general-purpose and domain-adapted, from three families (7-72B), we evaluated 2,500 clinical-verification decisions from de-identified patient contexts (2,484 patients), with patient-disjoint calibration and testing. In the higher-power operational analysis, round-to-nearest 8-bit quantization at BF16 storage served 99.8-100% of held-out decisions across all eleven models; formal finite-sample guarantees were assessed separately using the exact split-sample procedure. In the same operational analysis, the class-conditional 4-bit frontier reduced the amortized, BF16-equivalent deployment cost to 25.8% and 27.3% of the BF16 reference for Qwen2.5-72B and HuatuoGPT-o1-72B, respectively, with measured peak memory of 43.0 and 42.7 GB, below the nominal capacity of a single 48 GB accelerator. Deployment on that specific hardware was not directly tested. Under the exact split-sample certificate, which also accounts for configuration selection, these costs were 26.7% and 27.9%. On external disease named-entity benchmarks against human annotations, the frontier-selected NF4 configurations had a mean absolute F1 change of 0.045 on both datasets, with substantial model-specific variation. CERTA converts compression from a fixed precision choice into a risk-controlled deployment policy for this verification task.
中文翻译
大型临床语言模型通常需要多GPU基础设施，限制了本地部署。我们提出了CERTA，一种决策级别的统计证明，用于确定量化模型是否可以替代其原生BF16参考模型，并将无法认证的决策升级到该参考模型。在来自三个模型家族（7-72B）的十一款指令微调模型中，包括通用和领域适配模型，我们评估了2500个来自去标识患者情境的临床验证决策（涉及2484名患者），采用患者无关的校准和测试。在更高性能的操作分析中，使用BF16存储的四舍五入至8位量化在所有十一个模型上覆盖了99.8%-100%的保留决策；通过精确分样本程序单独评估了形式化的有限样本保证。在同一操作分析中，类别条件下的4位前沿将Qwen2.5-72B和HuatuoGPT-o1-72B的摊销BF16等效部署成本分别降低至25.8%和27.3%，实测峰值内存分别为43.0 GB和42.7 GB，低于单个48 GB加速器的标称容量。该特定硬件上的部署未直接测试。在精确分样本证书下（也考虑配置选择），这些成本分别为26.7%和27.9%。在外部疾病命名实体基准上与人工标注对比，所选NF4配置的平均绝对F1变化为0.045，模型间存在显著差异。CERTA将压缩从固定精度选择转变为针对此验证任务的风险可控部署策略。

[2610.26888]
Mixture of Phonetic Experts Based Low-Rank Adaptation of Conformer Models for Accented English Speech Recognition
基于语音专家混合的低秩适应用于口音英语语音识别
We present Mixture of Phonetic Experts based Low-Rank Adaptation (MoPE-LoRA), a parameter-efficient adaptation framework for accented English speech recognition. Recent mixture-of-experts approaches typically allocate experts per accent, implicitly treating accent identity as the primary axis of variation. In contrast, we organize adaptation along phonetic categories, motivated by the observation that accent differences are often expressed as systematic distortions of phoneme realization. MoPE-LoRA introduces a fixed set of six low-rank experts aligned with manner-of-articulation classes and performs frame-level routing through a hybrid mechanism that combines phoneme supervision with learned acoustic gating. Because the expert set is shared across accents, the model does not require accent-specific modules and naturally extends to previously unseen accents. Experiments on L2-ARCTIC show that MoPE-LoRA consistently improves over single LoRA and full fine-tuning. In zero-shot evaluation where one accent is held out during training, the proposed method yields a 12.3% relative improvement over single LoRA. These results indicate that phonetic-category-based decomposition offers a scalable and transferable alternative to accent-level adaptation.
中文翻译
我们提出了一种基于语音专家混合的低秩适应（MoPE-LoRA）框架，用于口音英语语音识别的参数高效适应。近期的专家混合方法通常按口音分配专家，隐含地将口音身份视为主要变化轴。相反，我们沿语音类别组织适应，基于观察到口音差异常表现为音素实现的系统性扭曲。MoPE-LoRA引入一组固定的六个低秩专家，对应发音方式类别，并通过结合音素监督与学习到的声学门控的混合机制实现帧级路由。由于专家集跨口音共享，模型无需口音特定模块，自然扩展至未见过的口音。在L2-ARCTIC数据集上的实验表明，MoPE-LoRA持续优于单个LoRA和全微调。在零样本评估中（训练时保留一个口音），所提方法相比单个LoRA实现了12.3%的相对提升。结果表明，基于语音类别的分解为口音级适应提供了一种可扩展且可迁移的替代方案。

Multimodal Ambivalence and Hesitancy Recognition with Bidirectional Audio--Text Cross-Attention

Ambivalence and hesitancy (A/H) are subtle behavioural states that can be expressed through both what a person says and how it is spoken. This paper presents an audio--text framework for video-level A/H recognition. Full-video transcripts and speech waveforms are encoded using pre-trained multilingual BERT and wav2vec~2.0, respectively, and projected into a shared representation space. Two stacked bidirectional cross-attention blocks allow linguistic tokens to attend to acoustic frames and acoustic frames to attend to linguistic context without requiring explicit temporal alignment. The resulting sequences are aggregated through masked mean pooling and combined using concatenation, element-wise products, and absolute differences for binary classification. We evaluate the model on the subject-independent split of the Behavioural Ambivalence/Hesitancy (BAH) dataset. The proposed system achieves 72.19\% accuracy, 75.00\% precision, 81.13\% recall, 77.95\% A/H F1-score, and 70.16\% Macro-F1 on the test set. These results demonstrate the potential of jointly modelling linguistic and acoustic information for automatic A/H recognition.code at:https://github.com/DorriG/ECCV.

Uncovering Audio-Text Alignment in Large Audio-Language Models through Cross-Modal Concept Neurons
通过跨模态概念神经元揭示大音频-语言模型中的音视频对齐
While the language model backbone is pretrained on text alone, large audio-language models (LALMs) can, to a great extent, recognize acoustic attributes, such as emotion, speaker gender, and spoken language. However, it is unclear how the audio representation is integrated, if at all, with the other text representation before the model produces text. We hypothesize that there is a shared subspace where the audio and text representations are aligned. To test this hypothesis, we identify cross-modal neurons that are responsible for representing both audio and text. We find alignment between audio and text neurons to various degrees across three LALMs and four acoustic attribute types. [2610.28133]
Occupational Uniqueness and Wage Growth
职业独特性与工资增长
Changes in occupational task content may have different economic implications depending on whether the tasks gained or lost are occupation-specific or already widespread across the economy. Existing measures of occupational task change largely abstract from this distinction, treating occupations as self-contained task bundles rather than accounting for how tasks are distributed across occupations. We develop a framework to measure a distinct dimension of occupational change, one that goes beyond task expertise or task content: task structure. Using this new framework that tracks tasks across occupations and over time, we separate changes in task quantity from changes in occupational uniqueness, defined as the distinctiveness of an occupation's task bundle relative to other occupations. Using a harmonized dataset of 196563 task statements from the Dictionary of Occupational Titles and O*NET spanning 1977-2018, we measure task diffusion, occupational uniqueness, and distinct dimensions of task addition and removal. Occupations that become less unique are also associated with lower wage growth, and the wage associations of task additions and removals depend on how widely the affected tasks are performed elsewhere. Adding highly diffused tasks is associated with lower wage growth, as is removing occupation-specific tasks. At the economy-wide dimension, however, the pattern differs: the addition of new tasks is positively associated with wage growth, whereas the removal of tasks is negatively associated with it. Taken together, these results show that similar changes in the number of tasks performed by an occupation can reflect economically distinct processes. Incorporating the cross-occupational distribution of tasks, therefore, provides a richer account of occupational change and its relationship to the wage structure.
中文翻译
职业任务内容的变化可能具有不同的经济含义，这取决于所增加或减少的任务是特定于该职业的，还是已在整个经济中广泛分布。现有的职业任务变化度量方法大多忽略了这一区别，将职业视为自包含的任务集合，而未考虑任务在不同职业间的分布情况。我们提出一个新框架，用于衡量职业变化的一个独特维度——任务结构，该维度超越了任务专长或任务内容。利用这一追踪任务在职业间及随时间变化的新框架，我们将任务数量的变化与职业独特性（即某职业任务组合相对于其他职业的独特程度）的变化分离开来。基于涵盖1977-2018年期间的196,563条任务陈述的统一数据集（来自《职业标题词典》和O*NET），我们测量了任务扩散、职业独特性以及任务增减的不同维度。职业独特性下降的职位也与较低的工资增长相关，且任务增减对工资的影响取决于受影响任务在其他地方的普及程度。高度扩散的任务增加与较低的工资增长相关，同样，移除职业特有任务也导致工资增长下降。然而，在整体经济层面，模式有所不同：新任务的增加与工资增长正相关，而任务的移除则与之负相关。综合来看，这些结果表明，同一职业执行任务数量的相似变化可能反映经济上截然不同的过程。因此，纳入任务在跨职业间的分布特征，能够更丰富地描述职业变化及其与工资结构的关系。

[2610.28067]
Contextual Candour: Role-Aware Graduated Disclosure for LLMs over Mixed-Sensitivity Contexts
情境坦率：面向混合敏感度上下文的、角色感知的渐进披露机制
LLMs increasingly operate over mixed-sensitivity contexts, where they must reason over information ranging from public to confidential while disclosing only what the user is authorised to receive. Existing approaches enforce access control as a binary decision: either fully answering or refusing, without regulating how much or what kind of information is revealed if access is granted, leading to over-refusal or unintended leakage. We introduce graduated disclosure, an access-control formulation in which the level of detail and sensitivity in a model's response is calibrated to the user's authorisation rather than treated as a grant-or-deny decision. We model graduated disclosure as a disclosure-aware generation problem and propose a reward-based optimisation framework that shapes responses toward level-appropriate abstractions while penalising unauthorised factual leakage, including paraphrases and approximations. We evaluate our approach on enterprise and clinical benchmarks and compare it with prompt-based, gating, and supervised baselines. Experiments show that our method substantially reduces information leakage while preserving correctness and utility. These results suggest that disclosure control must be integrated into the generation process itself to enable secure yet useful LLM deployments.
中文翻译
大型语言模型（LLMs）越来越多地运行在混合敏感度的上下文中，需要在处理从公开到机密的信息时，仅披露用户授权接收的内容。现有方法将访问控制简化为二元决策：要么完全回答，要么拒绝，无法调节在授权后应披露的信息量或类型，导致过度拒绝或意外信息泄露。我们引入渐进披露机制，一种新的访问控制范式，其中模型响应的详细程度和敏感性根据用户的授权级别进行校准，而非简单地作为允许或拒绝的决定。我们将渐进披露建模为一种具备披露意识的生成问题，并提出一种基于奖励的优化框架，引导模型生成与授权级别相匹配的抽象内容，同时惩罚未经授权的事实泄露（包括改写和近似表达）。我们在企业与临床基准上评估该方法，并与基于提示、门控和监督的基线进行比较。实验表明，我们的方法显著减少了信息泄露，同时保持了正确性和实用性。这些结果表明，披露控制必须内置于生成过程本身，才能实现安全且有用的LLM部署。
👤 Devansh Srivastav, David Pape, Samira Abedini, Lea Schönherr 📅 2026-10-20 🔗 arXiv
[2610.28045]
Poisoning-Based Backdoors Are Not Cryptographic
基于投毒的后门并非加密安全
To defend against backdoor attacks on neural networks, the defender must identify the verifier function that determines whether an input is a trigger, rather than rejecting individual triggers in isolation, since an attacker can generate new triggers satisfying the same verifier faster than they can be blocked. If a backdoor verifier could be installed with the asymmetry of a cryptographic authentication scheme, in which only the attacker can produce trigger inputs while the defender cannot reverse-engineer the verifier from observations, learning-based defense would be impossible in principle. We show that no such asymmetry can arise from poisoning-based installation, in which the only verifier-dependent input to training is a poisoned dataset. The argument is a reduction. A training procedure that installs a verifier from poisoned data, so that the backdoor fires on unseen triggers while benign behavior is preserved, is itself a learner for that verifier. Any verifier that is computationally hard to learn is therefore impossible to install through poisoning, and any verifier that poisoning can install is reconstructible by a white-box defender holding comparably many labeled trigger observations. A complementary argument shows that a secret key supplied to the model at inference is observable to the same defender. The result takes representative trigger observations as given and concerns when the backdoor fires rather than what it does. Experiments on instruction-tuned large language models confirm the dichotomy, with no configuration yielding a backdoor that is both effective for the attacker and irrecoverable for the defender.
中文翻译
为防御神经网络中的后门攻击，防御者必须识别出决定输入是否为触发器的验证函数，而不是孤立地拒绝单个触发器，因为攻击者可以比防御者更快地生成满足相同验证函数的新触发器。如果后门验证函数能像加密认证方案一样具备不对称性——即只有攻击者能生成触发输入，而防御者无法通过观察反推验证函数——那么基于学习的防御在原则上就不可能实现。我们证明，这种不对称性无法通过基于投毒的安装方式产生，因为训练中唯一依赖验证函数的输入是被污染的数据集。论证采用归约法：一个从污染数据中安装验证函数的训练过程，实际上就是对该验证函数的学习过程。因此，任何计算上难以学习的验证函数都无法通过投毒安装；而任何可通过投毒安装的验证函数，也能被拥有相当数量标注触发样本的白盒防御者重建。另一个互补论证表明，推理阶段提供给模型的秘密密钥，对同一防御者也是可观察的。该结论以代表性触发样本为前提，关注的是后门何时触发，而非其具体行为。在指令微调的大规模语言模型上的实验验证了这一二分法，没有任何配置能使后门对攻击者有效且对防御者不可恢复。
👤 Masahiro Kaneko 📅 2026-10-20 🔗 arXiv
[2610.27678]
PhonoBridge: Error-Aware Phoneme-Mediated EMG-to-Text Reconstruction
PhonoBridge：误差感知的音素中介肌电图到文本重建
Electromyography (EMG) provides a non-invasive means of decoding silently articulated speech into text, but accurate decoding remains challenging due to limited availability of paired EMG-text data. We introduce PhonoBridge, a two-stage framework comprising a channel-aware EMG-to-phoneme frontend and a large language model (LLM) backend for text reconstruction. The frontend is trained on paired EMG-text data that consists of channel-wise feature extraction, cross-channel fusion, and electrode embeddings. With phonemes as the intermediate representation, we expand the backend's training data by converting LibriSpeech transcripts into phoneme-text pairs without additional EMG recordings. To adapt the LLM to frontend noise, we introduce Error-Profile-Guided Augmentation (EPGA), which perturbs transcript-derived phoneme sequences according to error patterns estimated from frontend predictions, generating additional training pairs for the LLM. On the Open Vocabulary silent-speech dataset, PhonoBridge achieves 24.77% WER, outperforming the previously reported best of 28.27% on the same test split.

👤 Wen Qiu, Jiacheng Zhang, Xuanda Chen, Siqi Cai, Haizhou Li 📅 2026-10-20 🔗 arXiv
[2610.27403]
Steering intonational tunes in text-to-speech synthesis
文本到语音合成中语调的引导控制
Text-to-speech systems built on residual vector quantization often generate audio in two stages: a transformer backbone emits the first level of each frame's token stack, and a smaller depth model generates the rest autoregressively. We ask which components of such a model are responsible for prosodic intonation, more precisely for generating the English nuclear tune (for instance, the falling pitch melody that distinguishes an assertion from a question's rising melody), and whether interventions on those components can be used to control intonation. Prompt-based control mostly fails; only a final question mark is a reliable lever. Interchange interventions in CSM and S2 locate the tune: the backbone's hidden state carries it to the waveform through the depth model, and the first level of the stack carries little or none of it. Steering the backbone state with a difference-of-means vector computed on new material generated with a final period or question mark moves the tune, but the push toward an assertion degrades the voice. Steering vectors computed instead from the backbone states associated with human imitations of theoretically-specified tunes, whose text carries no punctuation, and applied inside the generation loop, move most recordings to the other condition in both systems (even when fitted without the speaker being steered), at a small cost in predicted naturalness. These imitation-derived vectors are aligned with the one obtained from the two punctuated conditions far beyond a shuffled-label null, so the backbone state of a punctuated prompt already represents intonation. The steered recordings broadly become falling or rising, according to the intent, but the edge tone moves more coherently than the pitch accent, and the target tune cannot be selected precisely beyond this broad tendency.
中文翻译
基于残差向量量化构建的文本到语音系统通常分为两个阶段：Transformer 主干网络生成每一帧标记堆栈的第一层，随后一个深度较小的模型自回归地生成其余部分。我们探讨了此类模型中哪些组件负责语调特征，特别是生成英语核心语调（例如，区分陈述句与疑问句的下降音高旋律）的机制，并研究是否可以通过干预这些组件来控制语调。基于提示的控制方法大多失败；只有句末问号是可靠的调控手段。通过在 CSM 和 S2 中进行互换干预，我们定位到了语调信息：主干网络的隐藏状态将语调传递至波形，而堆栈的第一层几乎不携带语调信息。使用新生成材料中句号或问号对应的均值差向量来引导主干状态，可以移动语调，但使语音趋向陈述句时会降低语音质量。相比之下，使用人类模仿理论指定语调时的主干状态计算出的引导向量（其文本无标点符号），并在生成循环中应用，可在两个系统中将大多数录音转移到目标语调条件，即使未对说话人进行引导也有效，仅轻微影响自然度。这些由模仿获得的向量与从有标点条件中得到的向量在显著高于随机标签零假设的情况下对齐，表明带标点提示的主干状态本身已包含语调信息。经引导的录音整体呈现为下降或上升趋势，符合意图，但边缘音调比音高重音更协调，且无法精确选择特定语调，仅能实现大致倾向。

[2610.27273]
FLINT: Fixed-budget Late Interaction
FLINT：固定预算的后期交互
Multi-vector retrieval models such as ColBERT often outperform single-vector models of comparable scale by matching queries and documents at the token level. However, retaining a vector for every token substantially increases storage requirements and slows retrieval compared to single-vector bi-encoders, limiting their scalability. We introduce FLINT (Fixed-budget Late INTeraction), an architecture that uses attention-based pooling to represent each query and document with a fixed number of embedding vectors. Unlike architectures that pool only document representations while retaining token-level queries, FLINT applies pooling symmetrically to both sides, limiting document storage and the number of vector comparisons needed during retrieval. FLINT achieves comparable ranking effectiveness to the closest baselines on BEIR and outperforms them on several benchmarks with longer and more complex queries, including BrowseComp+, CRUMB, and TREC Tip-of-the-Tongue 2025. We demonstrate that FLINT's effectiveness-efficiency benefits extend across encoder architectures, testing it on two separate backbones (BERT and ModernBERT). On long-query corpora, FLINT delivers 1.5-2.8 times faster end-to-end retrieval than ConstBERT, the closest baseline. FLINT also reduces index size by 5.3-9.3 times relative to token-level late-interaction models such as ColBERTv2.
中文翻译
多向量检索模型如 ColBERT 通常在同等规模下优于单向量模型，因其能在词元层面匹配查询与文档。然而，保留每个词元的向量会显著增加存储需求并降低检索速度，限制了其可扩展性。我们提出 FLINT（Fixed-budget Late INTeraction），一种采用注意力池化机制的架构，将每个查询和文档表示为固定数量的嵌入向量。与仅对文档表示进行池化而保留词元级查询的架构不同，FLINT 对两侧对称应用池化，从而减少文档存储量及检索过程中的向量比较次数。FLINT 在 BEIR 上达到与最接近基线相当的排序效果，并在 BrowseComp+、CRUMB 和 TREC Tip-of-the-Tongue 2025 等具有更长更复杂查询的基准上表现更优。我们证明 FLINT 的高效性与有效性优势适用于多种编码器架构，在 BERT 和 ModernBERT 两种骨干网络上均进行了测试。在长查询语料库上，FLINT 的端到端检索速度比最接近的基线 ConstBERT 快 1.5 至 2.8 倍。同时，FLINT 相较于词元级后期交互模型（如 ColBERTv2）将索引大小减少了 5.3 至 9.3 倍。

[2610.27213]
Profile-DP: Differential Privacy for User-Controlled Profile Release in Personalized LLM Inference
Profile-DP：个性化大语言模型推理中用户可控资料发布的差分隐私保护
Personalized large language models (LLMs) increasingly rely on profile-conditioned inference, pairing public requests with rich, user-specific facts. This paradigm exposes the user profile itself, together with the user's explicit sharing preferences. We introduce Profile-DP, a policy-aware local differential privacy framework designed specifically for user-profile release in personalized LLM inference. Profile-DP provably bounds the release of user-withheld facts while treating public instructions, profile schemas, and authorized facts as public side information. By generating a privatized profile that composes with the public instruction prior to answer generation, our framework decouples formal privacy guarantees from downstream model behavior. We develop two finite-domain mechanisms satisfying Profile-DP: DP-SLOT independently perturbs protected attributes, while DP-BLOCK uses public semantic decisions to coordinate their release and jointly sample related attributes. A positive, public compatibility prior connects these attributes while preserving a profile-level privacy bound. On PEEP, DP-BLOCK leads the utility composite among DP methods at all 13 structured budgets and is closest to the ideal privacy--utility point in 18 of 26 format--budget settings under our composite weighting. Direct measurements reveal complementary strengths: DP-BLOCK discloses less, while DP-SLOT retains more authorized facts. These results make policy-aware profile release a practical basis for studying privacy and personalization.
中文翻译
个性化大语言模型（LLMs）越来越多依赖于基于用户资料的推理，即结合公开请求与丰富的用户特定事实。这一范式暴露了用户资料本身及其明确的共享偏好。我们提出 Profile-DP，一种专为个性化 LLM 推理中用户资料发布设计的政策感知局部差分隐私框架。Profile-DP 可证明地限制用户隐匿事实的泄露，同时将公共指令、资料结构和授权事实视为公开的附加信息。通过在生成回答前将私有化资料与公共指令组合，该框架将形式化的隐私保证与下游模型行为解耦。我们开发了两种满足 Profile-DP 的有限域机制：DP-SLOT 独立扰动受保护属性，而 DP-BLOCK 利用公共语义决策协调其发布，并联合采样相关属性。一个正向的公共兼容先验连接这些属性，同时保持资料级别的隐私边界。在 PEEP 数据集上，DP-BLOCK 在所有 13 个结构化预算设置中均领先于其他 DP 方法，并在 26 个格式-预算配置中的 18 个中最接近理想的隐私-效用点。直接测量显示互补优势：DP-BLOCK 泄露更少，而 DP-SLOT 保留更多授权事实。这些结果使政策感知的资料发布成为研究隐私与个性化问题的实用基础。

Beyond Two Tracks: Speaker-Anchored Full-Duplex Dialogue Recovery
超越双轨：基于说话人锚定的全双工对话恢复
Spoken interaction is moving from alternating turns to full-duplex conversation, where an agent listens while speaking. Learning this coordination requires natural dialogue with persistent speaker-resolved tracks, a data requirement that remains difficult to satisfy at scale. Generative recovery offers a route from ordinary recordings, but recovering two voices is not enough: their track assignments can drift as the conversation unfolds. We address this missing link with speaker-anchored routing before diffusion: a 1.84M-parameter module matches the separator's coarse streams to fixed enrollment identities and orders them before a frozen generator reconstructs the speech, without retraining the generator or altering the reconstructed audio. On 25-minute remote conversations, routing cuts wrong-track time from 43.9% to 4.1%, below the 6.2% of matched-enrollment waveform re-identification, with its largest advantage when one participant holds the floor (17.8% versus 28.6%), and raises fixed-track activity F1 from 0.46 to 0.84 while per-chunk reconstruction quality is unchanged. The same router transfers to unseen face-to-face and telephone recordings, and a margin-gated verification step brings their wrong-track time to 0.9-1.7% while embedding 20-36% of the output, where full re-identification embeds all of it. Recovered voices thus become the participant-consistent tracks that full-duplex dialogue modeling needs. Audio examples: https://elpsykongloo.github.io/beyond-two-tracks/
中文翻译
语音交互正从交替发言转向全双工对话，即智能体在说话的同时持续倾听。学习这种协调需要具有持久说话人分辨能力的自然对话数据，而这一需求在大规模场景下仍难以满足。生成式恢复为普通录音提供了可行路径，但仅恢复两个声音是不够的：随着对话进行，其轨道分配可能产生漂移。为此，我们提出在扩散前采用说话人锚定路由机制——一个184万参数的模块将分离器的粗粒度流与固定的注册身份匹配并排序，再由冻结的生成器重建语音，无需重新训练生成器或改变重建音频。在25分钟远程对话中，该路由将错误轨道时间从43.9%降至4.1%，低于匹配注册波形重识别的6.2%；当一方长时间占据发言权时优势更明显（17.8% vs 28.6%），同时固定轨道活动F1值从0.46提升至0.84，而每段重建质量保持不变。该路由器可迁移至未见过的面对面和电话录音，结合边缘门控验证步骤，使错误轨道时间降至0.9%-1.7%，仅嵌入20%-36%输出内容，而完整重识别需嵌入全部内容。因此，恢复后的语音成为全双工对话建模所需的参与者一致轨道。音频示例：https://elpsykongloo.github.io/beyond-two-tracks/

Omni-Woodpecker: Hallucination Correction via Evidence-Prior Rebalancing for Omni LLMs
Omni-Woodpecker：通过证据优先级再平衡实现Omni LLM的幻觉纠正
Recent Omni LLMs (OLLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in joint audio-visual reasoning, yet cross-modal hallucinations remain a major challenge. Existing mitigation methods mainly operate at the output level, overlooking the entanglement of grounding evidence and learned priors within fused representations. To address this issue, we propose Omni-Woodpecker (OWP), a training-free framework that shifts hallucination mitigation from output-level manipulation to conflict-conditioned representation rebalancing. Specifically, OWP first identifies cross-modal conflicts from complementary modality-specific views. It then employs orthogonal decomposition to separate grounding evidence from competing priors within the hidden states. Finally, OWP adaptively rebalances fused representations by suppressing misleading priors and restoring ignored evidence before decoding. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness and strong cross-architecture generalization of OWP. Using Qwen2.5-Omni-7B as the backbone, OWP improves performance by 12.9 and 8.6 points on CMM and AVHBench, respectively, while consistently achieving gains of 0.67-2.25 points across four general OLLM reasoning benchmarks. Furthermore, OWP generalizes effectively to VideoLLaMA2-AV, improving performance by 4.6 and 3.9 points on the same benchmarks. Code is publicly available at https://github.com/ZiiJianLiu/Omni-Woodpecker

SciFigBench: A Benchmark for Scientific Method Figure Understanding

Scientific method figures often provide researchers with a clear visual entry point into a paper's methodology and core contributions, yet their dense layouts and complex information flows are difficult for Vision Language Models (VLMs) to interpret. Existing benchmarks predominantly focus on coarse-grained tasks, such as context-based retrieval or holistic comprehension, overlooking the fine-grained content and inter-module relations within complex methodological diagrams. To address these overlooked aspects, we introduce Scientific Figure Benchmark (SciFigBench), a benchmark for evaluating core capabilities required for scientific method figure understanding. Experiments across open-source and proprietary VLMs show that even strong models remain substantially limited in visual relation comprehension, while SciFigCue improves performance across several tasks without parameter updates. The code and related resources are available at https://github.com/LeNcyer/SciFigBench.

[2610.27079]
ClockMark: Adaptive and Host-Dependent Watermarking for Large Language Models
ClockMark：面向大型语言模型的自适应与主机依赖型水印技术
Large language model (LLM) watermarking operates on the next-token distribution that evolves during generation. In streaming generation, each sampled token becomes part of the text and affects subsequent distributions. Embedding therefore proceeds with a changing host, unlike classical watermarking, which adapts embedding to an existing host signal. To accommodate this difference, we formulate a novel embedding framework for LLM watermarking based on two principles. We first argue that embedding should be adaptive, retaining the state of previous sampling decisions to better guide subsequent choices. We further argue that embedding should be host-dependent, using the current LLM distribution to constrain both token selection and state updates. Guided by these principles and the two requirements of distribution preservation and robustness to text editing, we propose ClockMark. It combines adaptive and host-dependent embedding with message spreading, embedding the full payload in each watermarked token. Theoretically, we prove that ClockMark preserves the original LLM sequence distribution, thereby maintaining generation quality. Empirically, extensive experiments against 12 baselines demonstrate improved robustness to text editing, with gains of up to 16.08 percentage points in bit accuracy over the strongest tested baseline.
中文翻译
大型语言模型（LLM）水印作用于生成过程中动态演化的下一个词分布。在流式生成中，每个采样词都会成为文本的一部分，并影响后续分布。因此，嵌入过程面对的是不断变化的宿主环境，不同于传统水印中对已有宿主信号的适应。为应对这一差异，我们基于两项原则提出一种新型LLM水印嵌入框架：首先主张嵌入应具备自适应性，保留先前采样决策的状态以更好引导后续选择；其次主张嵌入应具有主机依赖性，利用当前LLM分布来约束词选择与状态更新。基于这些原则以及分布保持与抗文本编辑鲁棒性的双重要求，我们提出ClockMark。它结合自适应与主机依赖嵌入机制，并引入消息扩散策略，使完整信息包嵌入每个水印词中。理论上，我们证明ClockMark能保持原始LLM序列分布，从而维持生成质量。实证上，与12种基线对比的大量实验表明，其在抗文本编辑方面表现更优，比特准确率最高较最强基线提升16.08个百分点。
👤 Yupeng Qiu, Han Fang, Ee-Chien Chang 📅 2026-10-19 🔗 arXiv
[2610.26974]
Transformers Learn Two Competing Sorting Algorithms, and Only One Length-Generalizes
Transformer学习两种竞争性的排序算法，且仅一种具备长度泛化能力
We show that two-layer transformers trained to near-zero loss on a sorting task converge to one of two mechanistically distinct solution classes depending on their initialization. One solution class consists of solutions that do not use the second attention layer to sort, but also fail to length-generalize. The other, which uses both attention layers, length-generalizes perfectly, despite only being trained to sort sequences of a fixed length. By carefully choosing the weight initialization, we demonstrate that we can steer the model toward learning one solution or the other. We identify input types that are rare at the training length but increasingly common at longer sequence lengths as a source of this disparity in length-generalization, and find that layer-wise specialization of the second class of solutions allows them to handle these rare sequences more accurately. In the second class, we discover a mechanism by which the second layer attention can use the contextual information gathered by the first layer attention to reduce its search space. On the related task of finding the minimum of a sequence, we find a similar two solution-type phenomenon, but with the opposite generalization pattern, where a different form of layer-wise specialization causes the two-layer solution to length-generalize worse than the single-attention solution.
中文翻译
我们发现，在排序任务上训练至接近零损失的两层Transformer会根据初始化的不同收敛到两种机制上截然不同的解类。一类解不使用第二注意力层进行排序，但无法实现长度泛化；另一类则使用两个注意力层，尽管仅在固定长度序列上训练，却能完美实现长度泛化。通过精心选择权重初始化，我们能够引导模型学习其中一种解。我们识别出在训练长度下罕见但在更长序列中越来越常见的输入类型是导致长度泛化差异的根源，并发现第二类解的分层专业化使其能更准确处理这些罕见序列。在第二类解中，我们发现第二层注意力可通过第一层注意力收集的上下文信息来缩小搜索空间。在寻找序列最小值的相关任务中，我们也观察到类似的现象，但泛化模式相反：一种分层专业化形式导致两层解的长度泛化能力反而劣于单注意力解。
👤 Khashayar Gatmiry, Nathan W. Henry, Chloe H. Su, Emmanuel Abbe, Samy Bengio et al. 📅 2026-10-18 🔗 arXiv

Disentangling Depression from Cognitive Decline in Elderly Speech Using Concurrent Clinical Assessments
通过同步临床评估区分老年人语音中的抑郁与认知衰退
Detecting depression from speech in elderly patients with mild cognitive impairment (MCI) is complicated by overlapping acoustic effects of cognitive decline. Without disentangling these, classifiers risk learning cognitive rather than depression-specific patterns. We present a Korean elderly speech corpus of 89 MCI speakers collected over three years with concurrent depression (SGDS) and cognitive (MMSE) assessments. Both linear mixed-effects models and partial correlations identify the same pattern: of seven feature groups, only formant features are associated with depression after controlling for cognitive function, while widely used F0 features carry no signal. The F1+F2 subset achieves UAR 0.760 with just 12 features, outperforming both the full eGeMAPS baseline and self-supervised representations. These findings show that concurrent clinical assessments can identify interpretable, depression-specific acoustic markers in populations where cognitive and affective symptoms co-occur.

[2608.18679]
FRAGMENT: Factorized Graph Representations for Document Generation and Editing via Entity-Aware Transformations
FRAGMENT：基于实体感知变换的文档生成与编辑的因子化图表示
Structured documents such as invoices, forms, reports, and scientific articles derive meaning from the interplay between spatial layout, textual content, and logical structure. Generative models operating at the pixel or token level often struggle to capture these dependencies effectively. We explore FRAGMENT, a generative framework that represents a document as a typed relational graph and factorizes its distribution as p(structure, content) = p(structure) * p(content | structure). The framework consists of two stages. The first stage, the Architect, is a causally masked Transformer conditioned on document category that autoregressively generates the graph topology and typed spatial relations. The second stage, the Builder, is a GATv2-based graph attention network that enriches the graph with normalized bounding boxes, text, and visual style attributes. Both stages define explicit likelihood models, yielding a tractable document-level likelihood that serves as an anomaly score for forgery detection. For controlled editing, a prompt-conditioned extension injects instruction embeddings into the Builder through cross-attention, enabling semantic and entity-aware modifications. We describe training on DocLayNet and fine-tuning on FUNSD and SROIE. Experiments on DocLayNet, FUNSD, and SROIE evaluate FRAGMENT alongside representative autoregressive, layout-only, and graph-based baselines, providing an empirical analysis of the characteristics and trade-offs of the proposed factorized graph generation framework.
中文翻译
发票、表格、报告和科学文章等结构化文档的意义源于空间布局、文本内容与逻辑结构之间的相互作用。在像素或标记层面操作的生成模型往往难以有效捕捉这些依赖关系。我们探索了FRAGMENT，一种将文档表示为类型化关系图并将其分布分解为p(结构, 内容) = p(结构) * p(内容|结构)的生成框架。该框架包含两个阶段：第一阶段是‘建筑师’，一个基于文档类别的因果掩码Transformer，用于自回归生成图拓扑和类型化空间关系；第二阶段是‘建造者’，一个基于GATv2的图注意力网络，用于为图注入归一化的边界框、文本和视觉风格属性。两个阶段均定义显式的似然模型，从而产生可计算的文档级似然，可用作伪造检测的异常分数。对于可控编辑，通过提示条件扩展将指令嵌入注入‘建造者’的交叉注意力中，实现语义和实体感知的修改。我们描述了在DocLayNet上的训练及在FUNSD和SROIE上的微调。在DocLayNet、FUNSD和SROIE上的实验将FRAGMENT与代表性自回归、仅布局和基于图的基线进行对比，提供了对所提出的因子化图生成框架特性与权衡的实证分析。
